### 1. [Coupled or Independent? Fault Coupling in Agentic Medical Vision Systems](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40913v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40913

**作者：** Nishi Kanta Paul、Md Shihabul Islam Shovo、Md Mostafijur Rahman、Jamil Fayyad

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40913v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40913v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究医学视觉语言 agent 在传感器读数本身不可信、工具与推理通道共享同一视觉故障时，中间输出可核查性是否仍成立；作者搭建分割—检索—迭代推理的皮肤科 agent，提出单通道错误与联合错误（一致/不一致）的耦合分类，并在 PAD-UFES-20 上比较排版注入与物理损坏下的检索捕获与联合错误比例。

**相关性简评：** 直接检验「通道间分歧可作为视觉证据筛选与错误检测信号」这一隐含假设：若两通道同错则硬标签比较必然一致，分歧召回受限，对 latent 通道比较、视觉旁路监控与证据因果作用的评估可能构成反例与可复用指标。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Coupled or Independent? Fault Coupling in Agentic Medical Vision Systems

**abstract/A1：** Vision-language agents equipped with tools expose intermediate outputs for inspection and comparison with the model's reasoning, which may offer safety benefits over single-shot models. This paper tests that potential benefit when the sensor reading itself is untrusted, so that the tool and reasoning channels share a common visual fault. We consider a dermatology agent based on segmentation, contrastive retrieval, and an iterative vision-language reasoner and define a coupling taxonomy that distinguishes single-channel errors from joint errors that agree or disagree. On PAD-UFES-20, we show that typographic injection produces detectable directional retrieval capture, whereas physical corruption does not. Crop-first retrieval preserves the payload at a readable scale and produces 55% joint wrong agreement across all cases (71% of faulty cases); full-image retrieval produces 35% (43%). Under a binary decision, if both channels are wrong, their predictions must agree, so hard-label channel comparison cannot detect joint failures, limiting disagreement recall to 0.29 (crop-first) versus 0.57 (full-image). The direction of the order effect is reproduced on the independent HIBA test set.

**comment/C1：** 9 pages, 3 figures, 5 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-2"></a>

### 2. [CompoSET: A Controlled Single-Edit Benchmark for Vision-Language Compositionality](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41118v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41118

**作者：** Laure Delisle、Pietro Perona

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41118v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41118v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CompoSET 基准：每个图像-文本对配一个仅做单一受控编辑（颜色、数量、空间关系、物体状态等）的 foil，像素变化约 8%，覆盖 80 个场景、16 类编辑，并测试 22 个双编码器 VLM。

**相关性简评：** 该基准直接诊断「属性/关系绑定到哪个实体」这一视觉细节与实体关系保留问题，并指出旧基准的全局差异混淆使模型分数虚高；可供核查 LVR 内部状态是否真正保留任务必要的绑定信息，但其评测仍是图文匹配而非 latent 推理过程。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CompoSET: A Controlled Single-Edit Benchmark for Vision-Language Compositionality

**abstract/A1：** Vision-language compositionality is the ability to identify which properties, relations, quantities, and states bind to which entities in both an image and a caption, and to match those bindings across modalities. Existing benchmarks evaluate this ability through image-text matching: a model ranks candidate image-caption match similarities and succeeds when the correct match comes on top. In such datasets, paired images differ by more than a single binding: scene, layout, and content all differ, with 97% of pixels changing on average. Such pairs do not isolate compositionality: results are confounded by global image differences. We introduce Compositional Single-EdiT (CompoSET), a benchmark dataset in which each base image-caption pair has an edited counterpart, or foil, that preserves the scene but differs by a single controlled edit, such as color, count, spatial relation, or object state. Base and foil images preserve naturalistic scene complexity yet are visually near-identical: only 8% of pixels change on average. Our dataset has many foils per base scene (~22) in order to separate scene difficulty from edit difficulty, a distinction one-foil-per-base benchmarks cannot make. CompoSET contains 1,776 instances across 80 naturalistic scenes, spanning 16 edit types in 4 semantic categories, with captions at 3 verbosity levels. We test 22 dual-encoder VLMs and find that all models perform at or below chance level on CompoSET, despite scoring well on prior benchmarks. This suggests that apparent progress under prior image-text matching protocols may be an artifact arising from confounds in benchmark design, and that compositional understanding in dual-encoder VLMs is far from solved. CompoSET addresses the confounds and offers a better controlled benchmark to measure future progress.

**comment/C1：** Dataset: https://huggingface.co/datasets/CompoSET/CompoSET \| Code: https://github.com/laure-delisle/CompoSET \| 9 pages main text, 38 pages total

</details>

<a id="paper-3"></a>

### 3. [ReVEAL: Eliciting Role-Aware Latent Visual Evidence from MLLMs for Reasoning Video Object Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41257v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41257

**作者：** Zhongyuan Bao、Cunjie He、Lejun Zhang、Zenglin Xu、Yanwei Fu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41257v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41257v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** ReVEAL 面向 reasoning video object segmentation，让 MLLM 在推理中生成角色感知的潜在视觉证据：引入 Visual Evidence Tokens 把必要区域/实体区分为目标、背景或干扰物，用动态注意力层路由聚合跨层 VET-to-video 注意力，并构建 ReVEAL-47K 配合对比注意力定位损失训练，推理时融合角色证据引导冻结的 SAM2 出掩码。

**相关性简评：** 直接涉及 MLLM 内部状态如何保留并筛选视觉证据、以及答案端如何使用这些 latent 内容；其跨层注意力路由与角色约束监督可供核查深度与注意力结构对 latent 证据可靠性的影响。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ReVEAL: Eliciting Role-Aware Latent Visual Evidence from MLLMs for Reasoning Video Object Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Reasoning video object segmentation (ReVOS) requires segmenting the target specified by an implicit query throughout a video. Existing MLLM-based methods typically require costly cross-model alignment, tying MLLM training to a downstream segmentation model. We present ReVEAL, a decoupled thinking-with-video framework for ReVOS that elicits role-aware latent visual evidence from MLLM reasoning to directly guide segmentation. ReVEAL introduces Visual Evidence Tokens (VETs) into video reasoning to associate necessary visual regions or entities with target, background, or distractor roles, while a Dynamic Attention Layer Router adaptively aggregates VET-to-video attention across layers into role-specific grounding evidence. We train the MLLM to perform VET-augmented reasoning on our constructed ReVEAL-47K dataset, with a Contrastive Attention Grounding Loss as auxiliary supervision to apply role-specific constraints to VET attention and optimize layer routing for reliable latent grounding. At inference, ReVEAL identifies the target through this thinking-with-video process and fuses the resulting role-specific evidence to guide a frozen SAM2 in predicting target masks. Experiments on five benchmarks show that ReVEAL outperforms alignment-free baselines and remains competitive with alignment-based methods.

</details>

<a id="paper-4"></a>

### 4. [GenDSR: Transferring Representations from Video Generation Models for Dynamic Spatial Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41314v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41314

**作者：** Ke Yang、Zhenyu Zhang、Jun Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41314v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41314v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 GenDSR，将视频生成模型的中间表示迁入 VLM：分别保留整体生成上下文与时间变化的两条分支，经任务条件的 token 级门控融合后注入原生视觉表示，用于动态空间推理，并在 DSR-Bench 上取得 67.2%。

**相关性简评：** 直接涉及 VLM 内部是否保留场景状态与空间关系演化，其「原始/变化」双分支及门控注入为视觉证据筛选和任务必要信息保留提供了可核查线索，但注入特征是否被答案真正使用仍需阅读原文判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GenDSR: Transferring Representations from Video Generation Models for Dynamic Spatial Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** Dynamic spatial reasoning is essential for connecting visual intelligence to thephysical world, yet remains challenging for vision-language models (VLMs), which must preserve scene state while tracking how spatial relations evolve over time. Meanwhile, video generation models (VGMs), trained to synthesize temporally coherent world dynamics, offer a rich source of spatiotemporal representations. We present GenDSR, a framework that transfers intermediate representations from VGM into VLM for dynamic spatial reasoning. Rather than treating a VGM feature as a monolithic prior, GenDSR constructs two complementary branches: a raw branch preserving holistic generative context and a delta branch emphasizing temporal variation. Task-conditioned tokenwise gates then fuse both signals and inject the result into the native visual representation. On DSR-Bench, GenDSR achieves 67.2%, surpassing the previous best result and establishing a new state of the art.

</details>

<a id="paper-5"></a>

### 5. [Parse Before You Execute: Efficient Spatial Reasoning with Executable Primitives](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41389v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41389

**作者：** Xiwei Liu、Yulong Li、Xinling Zhuang、Xuhui Li、Yichen Li、Haolin Yang、Zhixiang Lu、Yutong Xie

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41389v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41389v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出免训练的智能体范式 PEPS，遵循「先解析后执行」：先将空间查询解析为符合 FESM（Frame、Entity、State、Metric）类型模式的紧凑可执行原语需求，再由执行器通过固定几何 API 与确定性计算按需落地，最后由验证器检查执行充分性，以支持提前终止或定向修补。

**相关性简评：** 论文把「欠承诺查询编译」视为失败来源，直接涉及 VLM 推理中的证据获取、充分性判定、停止决策与 API/延迟成本，可供核查 THINK / LOOK / STOP 的收益—成本权衡与动态计算终止设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Parse Before You Execute: Efficient Spatial Reasoning with Executable Primitives

**abstract/A1：** Spatial reasoning in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) has increasingly benefited from tool-integrated pipelines that ground computation in explicit geometry. However, existing systems often compile queries into relatively open-ended artifacts, such as code, task constraints, tool plans, or gathered evidence, leaving what must be resolved before execution under-specified. This can lead to unnecessary tool acquisition and iterative refinement, while still producing plausible reasoning traces from incomplete spatial commitments. We identify this failure mode as under-committed query compilation. To address it, we propose PEPS, a training-free agentic paradigm Parsing Executable Primitives for Spatial reasoning under a typed FESM schema over Frame, Entity, State, and Metric. PEPS follows a parse-before-execute principle: the Parser commits each query to a compact set of executable requirements; the Executor selectively grounds the required primitives through fixed geometric APIs and deterministic computation; and the Verifier checks execution sufficiency, enabling early termination or targeted repair. Extensive experiments across challenging spatial reasoning benchmarks show that PEPS consistently improves reasoning accuracy while substantially reducing refinement rounds, API cost, and end-to-end latency over strong tool-integrated baselines. These results demonstrate that explicit requirement commitment can make tool-integrated spatial reasoning not only more reliable, but also more execution-efficient.

</details>

<a id="paper-6"></a>

### 6. [Beyond Trial-and-Error: A Geospatial-Guided Active Reasoning Framework for Robust Aerial Vision-and-Language Navigation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41446v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41446

**作者：** Jiawei Ge、Wenkang Gong、Jiuxin Cao、Bo Liu、Lei Cui、Haoran Wang、Xuelin Zhu、Biwei Cao、Chang Liu、Chen Feng、Ioannis Patras

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41446v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41446v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 GeoReasoner，将空中视觉语言导航从被动试错转为地理空间引导的主动推理：先用区域先验约束目标假设空间，再以假设驱动探索采集判别性证据，并在停止前做一致性验证。

**相关性简评：** 其目标假设筛选、主动获取视觉证据与停止前验证，与 LVR 关注的视觉证据筛选及 LOOK/STOP 决策直接相关；但方法位于导航任务且未显式建模 latent 状态，可迁移性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Trial-and-Error: A Geospatial-Guided Active Reasoning Framework for Robust Aerial Vision-and-Language Navigation

**abstract/A1：** Aerial Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) requires UAVs to locate language-specified goals in large-scale urban environments, where objects often appear small and visually ambiguous from high-altitude viewpoints. Existing methods largely follow a reactive paradigm common in indoor VLN, repeatedly updating navigation states from local observations while underutilizing the geospatial structure that remains stable during navigation. As a result, visually similar distractors can cause a reactive goal search to repeatedly reinforce locally plausible goal hypotheses based on passively accumulated evidence, leading to unreliable localization. To address this, we propose GeoReasoner, a robust framework that shifts the paradigm of Aerial VLN from reactive trial-and-error to geospatial-guided active reasoning. Specifically, GeoReasoner first performs pre-search geospatial reasoning to derive region-level priors and constrain the goal hypothesis space. Building on these, it further conducts hypothesis-driven exploration to actively acquire discriminative evidence and refine competing goal hypotheses. Finally, consistency-aware goal verification adversarially assesses complementary evidence sources before stopping, hence reducing premature or erroneous goal confirmations. Experiments on the real-world CityNav benchmark demonstrate that GeoReasoner sets a new SOTA among zero-shot Aerial VLN methods while competing favorably with supervised approaches.

</details>

<a id="paper-7"></a>

### 7. [Attention Maps Know When to Think: Training-Free Difficulty-Aware Chain-of-Thought for Reasoning Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41618v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41618

**作者：** Chanseul Cho、Jaesung Jun、Dongheon Lee、Youngmin Ro

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41618v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41618v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文指出推理分割中无差别使用 CoT 会因「过度思考」反而损害查询意图与视觉定位的对齐，并提出免训练的 DA-CoT：用注意力图熵作为输入难度的信号，据此选择是否触发 CoT，并加入双向重思机制在初次结果失败时翻转推理决策，在基准上取得 SOTA 且提升推理效率。

**相关性简评：** 直接对应 THINK/LOOK/STOP 中的「是否思考」与「何时停止」问题，其以注意力熵作为内部不确定性信号来门控推理并可事后翻转决策的做法，可供核查在 latent 推理中用作计算分配与停止判据的可行性；但其推理仍以文本 CoT 展开，未涉及 latent 状态表示。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Attention Maps Know When to Think: Training-Free Difficulty-Aware Chain-of-Thought for Reasoning Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting has become a standard reasoning strategy in Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs), showing strong performance in complex vision-language tasks such as reasoning segmentation. However, our analysis reveals that indiscriminate CoT usage can lead to degraded performance by inducing unnecessary reasoning, a phenomenon we term overthinking. In reasoning segmentation, overthinking often leads to misalignment between the query's intent and visual grounding. To address this, we propose Difficulty-Aware Chain-of-Thought (DA-CoT), a training-free adaptive framework that selectively triggers CoT based on the input's intrinsic difficulty. We find that the entropy of the model's attention maps reliably reflects task difficulty: low entropy indicates confident, localized grounding, while high entropy suggests uncertainty and potential benefit from CoT. DA-CoT uses this entropy signal to determine whether to apply CoT prompting. Additionally, we introduce a Bidirectional Rethinking mechanism that enables the model to automatically flip its reasoning decision when the initial output fails, enhancing robustness by recovering from failures induced by overthinking and underthinking. DA-CoT demonstrates its effectiveness by achieving state-of-the-art performance on the Reasoning Segmentation benchmarks while significantly improving inference efficiency.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to WACV 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-8"></a>

### 8. [GRIT: Gradient Masking for Visually Misgrounded Tokens in Text-Rich Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41629v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41629

**作者：** Chanseul Cho、Noori Bae、Dongheon Lee、Youngmin Ro

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41629v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41629v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文指出 VLM 在富文本推理中存在「答对但视觉依据错误」（CVMR）现象，即中间步骤视觉误读仍获得最终正确答案；通过 token 级注意力分析区分误 grounding 与良好 grounding 的 token，并提出在 RLVR 中加入视觉 grounding 的 GRIT 训练框架，同时构建 GRIT-Bench 以变化文本长度与推理深度。

**相关性简评：** 直接涉及答案是否真正使用视觉证据、贡献分配与轨迹监督问题，且其 token 级注意力差异可供核查视觉证据筛选与 latent 状态中内容归因的分析。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GRIT: Gradient Masking for Visually Misgrounded Tokens in Text-Rich Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has recently been extended to vision-language models (VLMs), yet its effectiveness remains limited for text-rich reasoning tasks. We highlight a key factor contributing to this limitation: models frequently arrive at correct final answers despite misperceiving visual information during intermediate steps. We define this phenomenon as Correct-but-Visually-Misgrounded Reasoning (CVMR). Because standard RLVR rewards only final-answer correctness, it fails to differentiate between properly grounded and misgrounded reasoning, reinforcing both equally. Through token-level analysis, we reveal that CVMR tokens exhibit distinct attention patterns that differ systematically from well-grounded ones. Based on this observation, we propose GRIT, a training framework that incorporates visual grounding into RLVR to mitigate the impact of CVMR. Furthermore, to address the lack of text-rich reasoning benchmarks that allow for systematic evaluation across text length and reasoning depth, we introduce GRIT-Bench. Experiments demonstrate that GRIT significantly improves both reasoning accuracy and grounding fidelity over existing RLVR methods.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to WACV 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-9"></a>

### 9. [When Depth Lies: Benchmarking Vision-Language Models on Mirror-Induced RGB-D Ambiguity](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41651v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41651

**作者：** Hao Yin、Tianchen Guo、Heming Du、Yan Ke、Yanbin Liu、Xin Yu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41651v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41651v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文发布 MIRA-Bench：基于 525 个真实场景的 1,011 幅 RGB-D 图像与 14,140 条 QA，按反射感知、空间推理、场景决策三层评估 VLM；发现模型常混淆真实与镜像物体，且加入深度反而降低表现。

**相关性简评：** 直接关联视觉证据筛选与深度可信性：模型未能识别镜面造成的错误深度，可供核查视觉旁路与「看哪里、信什么」的证据选择失败模式。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Depth Lies: Benchmarking Vision-Language Models on Mirror-Induced RGB-D Ambiguity

**abstract/A1：** Vision-language models (VLMs) have made significant progress in spatial reasoning, particularly by integrating RGB images with depth data, which is often assumed to faithfully reflect physical distances. However, mirrors violate this assumption: they distort depth readings while reproducing object appearances, making reflections difficult to distinguish from real objects. As existing benchmarks rarely consider such reflective scenarios, VLM performance in mirror scenes remains largely unexplored. In this work, we introduce a mirror-centric RGB-D dataset from reflective scenes to evaluate the spatial reasoning capabilities of state-of-the-art VLMs in these scenarios. Specifically, we collect 1,011 RGB-D images from 525 real-world scenes, spanning diverse indoor and outdoor environments, six reflective surface types, and both daytime and nighttime conditions. All images are annotated with reflective surface region masks and mask annotations for both directly observed and mirror-reflected objects. Then, we curate a benchmark named MIRA-Bench (Mirror-Induced Reasoning Ambiguity Benchmark), comprising 14,140 QA pairs across eight sub-tasks organized into three cognitive levels: Reflection-aware Perception, Spatial Reasoning, and Scene-level Decision-making. We evaluate state-of-the-art VLMs on MIRA-Bench and find consistent failures across tasks: models misclassify whether objects are directly observed or reflected, fail to match reflected instances to their real-world counterparts, and produce erroneous spatial inferences. Remarkably, providing depth data actually degrades reasoning performance, implying that VLMs cannot identify incorrect depth in mirror scenes. The code and data are available at https://github.com/EvaYin-cloud/MIRA-Bench

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS 2026 E&amp;D Track accepted

</details>

<a id="paper-10"></a>

### 10. [Latent-Lens: Visual Perception in Small Language Models Through Latent Communication](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41751v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41751

**作者：** Mo Shahdloo、Faegheh Sardari、Mathew Salvaris

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41751v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41751v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Latent-Lens：用约 1.6M 参数（域编码器 + 形式化为 LoRA 的注意力解码器）把冻结 VLM 的隐状态投影进冻结 SLM 的嵌入空间，使视觉信息以 latent 前缀而非离散文本传给小型语言模型，并在 CLEVR、GQA、Flickr8k 上与同等 LoRA 预算的文本通信对比报告提升。

**相关性简评：** 直接触及「视觉细节是否保留在内部状态中」与视觉旁路问题，提供 latent 前缀与文本瓶颈的对照；其视觉—语言表示对齐方式、latent 前缀的可解释性及对 SLM 容量和前缀长度的依赖可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Latent-Lens: Visual Perception in Small Language Models Through Latent Communication

**abstract/A1：** Bridging independent vision-language models (VLMs) and small language models (SLMs) in a modular multi-agent system typically forces them to exchange information as discrete text, which creates a lossy bottleneck for spatial and fine-grained details. This constraint is especially acute for SLMs, whose limited capacity makes them more reliant on the quality of the input signal. Recent latent-communication methods exchange states between language models. How a VLM can pass visual information to a separately pretrained text-only SLM though remains unexplored. We propose \\textbf{Latent-Lens}, a lightweight codec that bridges the representation gap between a frozen VLM and a frozen SLM directly in latent space, enabling them to communicate purely through hidden states. The codec consists of a domain encoder that projects hidden states from the VLM into the SLM's embedding space, and an attention decoder formalised as a LoRA that teaches the SLM to attend to these visual prefixes, while training only ${\\sim}1.6$M parameters (0.2\\% of the combined model size). On visual question answering (CLEVR, GQA), Latent-Lens achieves $81.0\\pm7.9$\\% and $65.4\\pm5.4$\\%, compared with $58.3\\pm5.7$\\% and $61.8\\pm1.0$\\% for text communication with the same LoRA budget (+30.2--69.8pp relative to frozen text communication), while on image captioning (Flickr8k) it improves over the same baseline by +16.5 (+18.8 frozen). We also evaluate Latent-Lens with TinyLlama-1.1B and InternVL2-1B, which use different architectures, vocabularies, and hidden dimensions.

**comment/C1：** Published at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-11"></a>

### 11. [A Patch is More Than a Token](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41814v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41814

**作者：** Zixiong Wang、Haiping Ma

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41814v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41814v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 PMTT，把单个 patch token 扩展为以规范状态为中心的表示状态族和 patch 级局部特征空间，并在冻结 DINOv3 上通过稠密轨道视图、可见到隐状态预测及流形组织分析 patch 表示的信息量与几何结构。

**相关性简评：** 直接诊断「一个 patch 一个 token」是否压缩了任务可用的视觉细节，其状态族与低维预测流形可为 LVR 的视觉证据保留和 latent 状态组织提供参照，但尚未涉及推理期的 latent 使用，需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Patch is More Than a Token

**abstract/A1：** Vision Transformers represent each image patch through a canonical token produced under one observation condition. That token is a powerful interface, but it is still one realized state of the patch. We introduce PMTT, which establishes two foundations: a patch representation object can extend from one canonical state to a canonical-centered Representation State Family, and identity-preserving variation opens Patch-Level Local Feature Space. We develop four parts of this representation program. Patch Information Acquisition and Dense Orbital Views expose lawful same-patch states. Visible-to-Hidden prediction tests whether observed states reveal structure beyond themselves. Intrinsic analysis identifies low-dimensional, energy-concentrated, patch-specific, predictive geometry. Representation Organization separates acquiring more information from organizing that information, with an analytic Manifold as the central structured representation. We realize PMTT on frozen DINOv3 S/B/L/H+ backbones without neural post-training of the representation. Across two independent 4,096-anchor cohorts, Manifold explains 70.8-79.6% of Hidden variation, compared with 55.0-72.3% for directly Observed states, and wins on more than 95% of anchors at every scale. Effective rank falls from about 2.5 to 2.0 while dominant energy concentration rises with backbone scale. Across six visual capabilities and four scales, Observed exceeds the canonical Center in 21 of 24 conditions, Manifold exceeds Center in 23, and Manifold exceeds Observed in 16. The strongest current consequence appears in physical correspondence, where MegaDepth satisfies Center &lt; Observed &lt; Manifold at every scale. These results show that pretrained visual backbones contain richer patch information than the standard single-token interface directly exposes. Code and experimental assets are available at https://github.com/zixiongwang-ai/PMTT.

**comment/C1：** 54 pages, 16 figures, 12 tables. Code and experimental assets: https://github.com/zixiongwang-ai/PMTT

</details>

<a id="paper-12"></a>

### 12. [Open-Weight MLLMs Cannot Compare What They Cannot Name](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41841v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41841

**作者：** Wenxin Su、Anna Kreshuk

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41841v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41841v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 用合成蚂蚁图像上的身体部位大小比较作为极简基线，发现开源权重 MLLM 对部分部位比较失败、对其他部位成功，并结合 in-context learning 策略、物体识别表现与视觉编码器分析，提出「识别驱动的接地失败」解释：模型将无法识别的结构归到某个可识别的语义概念上，从而同时损害接地与比较。

**相关性简评：** 该文是 VLM 视觉比较失败的直接诊断，指出无语义支持时细粒度视觉结构在表示中保留较差，可供核查「视觉细节是否留在内部状态」以及语义识别对简单视觉判断的门控作用，与视觉证据筛选和 LOOK 的必要性判断相关。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Open-Weight MLLMs Cannot Compare What They Cannot Name

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly capable of understanding images and performing complex visual reasoning, suggesting the possibility of using them to automate biological image analysis. This need is becoming increasingly urgent as advances in technology continue to generate ever larger and more diverse microscopy datasets, already exceeding the scale at which manual visual analysis is feasible. With the final aim to use MLLMs for visual comparison of complex phenotypes, we investigate a very simple baseline: comparison of the size of body parts in synthetic images of ants. We find that state-of-the-art open-weight MLLMs fail for some body parts, but not for others, even though the comparison itself is visually trivial for all of them. We evaluate different in-context learning strategies and analyze performance on the related object recognition task. Our results suggest a recognition-driven grounding failure: structures that the models cannot recognize are instead associated with a recognizable semantic concept, impairing the grounding and comparison of both. Further analysis of the vision encoder is consistent with the hypothesis that fine-grained visual structure may be less well preserved without semantic support. Together, our results reveal a surprising limitation of current MLLMs: semantic recognition can gate simple visual comparisons that do not intrinsically require semantic understanding. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/kreshuklab/Open-Weight-MLLMs-Cannot-Compare-What-They-Cannot-Name

</details>

<a id="paper-13"></a>

### 13. [Latent Reasoning in Continuous Space for Unified Multimodal Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42149v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.42149

**作者：** Byungwoo Jeon、Yoonwoo Jeong、Hyunseok Lee、Minsu Cho、Jinwoo Shin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CL、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42149v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.42149v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 LARC，在统一多模态模型的连续隐状态空间中替代离散 token 链式推理：先用课程式 SFT 把 token 级推理逐步转为 latent 推理，再用信息增益 RL 无监督自演化；在文生图基准上提升生成质量、提示跟随与组合保真度，GenEval 较 BAGEL 提升 7%p，并呈现并行与顺序两种测试时扩展行为。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 在「连续 latent 状态取代离散推理轨迹」「课程式监督到 RL 的训练分配」「测试时扩展」上直接同构，可供对照；但其目标是图像生成，latent 状态是否承载可核验的视觉证据与关系、以及 latent 轨迹本身是否可读仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Latent Reasoning in Continuous Space for Unified Multimodal Models

**abstract/A1：** Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) achieve remarkable success on diverse multimodal understanding and generation tasks, yet they still struggle to effectively leverage language-based chain-of-thought reasoning for image generation. As a result, existing reasoning-augmented UMMs often fail to inherit the test-time scaling and fine-grained controllability observed in large language models. We attribute this failure to a fundamental modality mismatch: reasoning is performed in a discrete token space, whereas image generation operates in a continuous space. To bridge this gap, we introduce a Latent Reasoning framework in Continuous space (LARC), which alleviates the token-level bottleneck by reasoning over continuous hidden states. Specifically, the training framework consists of two stages: (i) curriculum supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and (ii) information-gain reinforcement learning (RL). First, the curriculum SFT stage gradually converts token-level reasoning into latent reasoning. Subsequently, the information-gain RL stage self-evolves latent reasoning without ground truth supervision. Experiments across diverse text-to-image generation benchmarks show that LARC consistently improves generation quality, prompt following, and compositional fidelity. Notably, LARC achieves state-of-the-art performance on GenEval with a 7%p gain over BAGEL and demonstrates clear test-time scaling behavior, outperforming token-based generation under both parallel and sequential test-time scaling strategies.

**comment/C1：** Published as conference proceeding for NeurIPS 2026. Last two authors advised equally

</details>

<a id="paper-14"></a>

### 14. [Does Recursive Knowledge Construction Help Agents Verify Consensus Claims in Science?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40906v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40906

**作者：** Lun Ai、Yu-Heng Wu、Jonathan Schaul、Leonie Küchenhoff、Denes Turei、Francesco Carli、Fabio Petroni、Aurelien Dugourd、Julio Saez-Rodriguez

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40906v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40906v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文定义「共识科学主张验证」任务，构建含 718 条 SUPPORT/REFUTE 金标的 ProClaim-eval 数据集，并提出 ProClaim：在模型上下文外持久化证据状态，以分别考察「递归构建何种证据状态」与「何物控制停止」两条设计轴，发现结构化证据状态优于上下文内累积，训练过的判据优于固定轮数停止。

**相关性简评：** 把证据状态移出上下文并用独立训练的 judge 决定何时停止，与 LVR 中视觉证据筛选、状态表示以及 THINK/LOOK/STOP 的收益成本分析高度同构，可供核查；但该工作为纯文本检索场景，无 latent 视觉状态，迁移需自行验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Does Recursive Knowledge Construction Help Agents Verify Consensus Claims in Science?

**abstract/A1：** Recent scientific agents build knowledge recursively by retrieving evidence, constructing an intermediate state, and judging whether that state contains sufficient information. These agents are evaluated end-to-end, usually without ground truth, so the effect of each design choice is unknown. Here, we examined two design axes: (1) what the recursion builds (the state of evidence) and (2) what controls it (the judge that decides when to stop). To measure them, we defined consensus scientific claim verification and measured these effects on this task, where a system must establish a claim's consensus stance from the open literature. We built a new consensus claim dataset, ProClaim-eval, including 718 claims with SUPPORT and REFUTE gold labels from five expert-curated databases spanning molecular biology, nuclear physics, and astronomy. Our instrument, ProClaim, persists an evidence state outside the model's context, so each axis can be studied with the other held fixed. Against verification systems and general-purpose agent harnesses, ProClaim ranks among the top three, with a significant advantage over the harnesses. We found that the evidence state improves performance over in-context accumulation, and it also matters what is stored in it. In addition, a trained judge that reads the evidence state performs better than fixed-round stopping. A judge trained separately and never exposed to ProClaim-eval achieves the highest agreement with the gold labels on three of five subsets, where it matches the off-the-shelf Claude Sonnet~4.6 judge and outperforms Claude Haiku~4.5. Our results support recursively maintaining a structured evidence state outside the model's context as an effective design for verifying claims in scientific agents.

</details>

<a id="paper-15"></a>

### 15. [Let LiDAR Decide Where to Look: Geometry-Measured Token Selection for Efficient Sparse Latent World Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40935v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40935

**作者：** Zeping Zhang、Zhuoya Zhao、Robert Laganière

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40935v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40935v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 GATS：在视觉 backbone 之前用 LiDAR/radar 点投影的几何信息挑选少量图像 token，并为保留 token 附上 3D anchor，送入自监督 latent world model 预测未来特征与 3D 位移，用于驾驶规划。

**相关性简评：** 「由测量几何决定看哪里」的做法可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与计算预算分配；其「随机丢弃 75% token 反超稠密模型」的观察也提示注意力稀释/冗余问题，但任务是驾驶世界模型而非 VLM 推理，迁移需验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Let LiDAR Decide Where to Look: Geometry-Measured Token Selection for Efficient Sparse Latent World Models

**abstract/A1：** Vision-centric autonomous driving models encode thousands of image tokens per frame, yet most depict sky, buildings, or empty road---content irrelevant to planning. Worse, these tokens dilute the planner's attention: we show that randomly discarding 75% of tokens already outperforms the dense model, because the planner can concentrate on the remaining driving-critical content. Building on this finding, we present Geometry-Aware Token Selection (GATS), which uses LiDAR and radar point projections to select a small fraction of image tokens before the vision backbone runs. Because the selector operates on measured 3D geometry rather than visual features, the backbone encodes only the selected patches, cutting end-to-end FLOPs by up to $18\\times$. Each kept token receives a 3D anchor from its LiDAR point and is fed to a self-supervised latent world model that predicts future features and 3D displacements without box annotations. At 25% budget, GATS achieves a 3-sec L2 displacement error of 0.520 m on nuScenes and an EPDMS of 88.9 on NAVSIM---surpassing the dense baseline (0.573 m / 85.9) at $6.6\\times$ fewer total FLOPs. At an extreme 10% budget (24 GFLOPs, $18\\times$ reduction), it still beats the dense baseline on both nuScenes (0.537 m vs. 0.573 m L2) and NAVSIM (87.9 vs. 85.9 EPDMS). On 739 intent-critical scenarios, GATS covers 95.8% of hazardous vehicles and achieves a 0.09% collision rate---73.5% lower than the dense model (0.34%).

</details>

<a id="paper-16"></a>

### 16. [CIRCE Divines LLM Task Complexity from Constraint Structure](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41089v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41089

**作者：** Chenhao Hong、Jiakai Xu、Kaushiki Singh、Yusen Zhang、Eugene Wu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41089v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41089v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CIRCE，用廉价 LLM 将问题抽取为约束中间表示并编译成约束图，在模型作答前预测其回答正确性与任务复杂度，以支持模型路由和测试时算力分配。

**相关性简评：** 「作答前由问题结构预测正确性」可为 VLM 的 THINK/LOOK/STOP 预算分配与提前停止提供外部复杂度信号，但研究对象是文本约束题，未涉及视觉 latent 状态，迁移到多模态推理尚需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CIRCE Divines LLM Task Complexity from Constraint Structure

**abstract/A1：** Estimating whether a problem is too complex for a large language model (LLM) before the model attempts it can guide model routing, how to allocate test-time compute, and suggest the need for new tools to aid the agent. While existing estimators rely on the problem text, LLM-annotated task difficulty labels, or signals obtained after a model attempts to solve the problem, such as its hidden states, none explicitly leverage the constraint structure inherent in the problem: the variables to assign and how the constraints over them interact. We introduce CIRCE, which extracts a problem's structure by using a cheap LLM to construct a Constraint Intermediate Representation that deterministically compiles into the constraint graph. Across seven reasoning benchmarks (including SAT, logic, and math) and four LLMs, even a randomly initialized GNN encoder improves predictions of LLM response correctness, reducing negative log-likelihood from 0.465 to 0.423 and increasing AUROC from 0.691 to 0.727 relative to the strongest baseline for each metric.

</details>

<a id="paper-17"></a>

### 17. [ImplicitHop: Evaluating knowledge integration across context and memory](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41169v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41169

**作者：** Alexander Detkov、Dashiel Karish、Matthew Oleynikov、Matt Thomson

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41169v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41169v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文构建图结构与真实知识图谱两类多跳演绎基准，在不使用 chain-of-thought 的隐式设定下，按推理深度比较上下文提供与预训练权重中知识的整合效果，发现两者准确率均随组合长度急剧下降，且上下文信息显著优于权重内知识。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本、无视觉输入，但其「隐式无 CoT 多跳组合随深度衰减」的评测范式可供核查 latent 多跳状态组合与长度泛化的诊断设计，上下文与权重知识的对比也可能为视觉证据与内部先验的分工提供参照。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ImplicitHop: Evaluating knowledge integration across context and memory

**abstract/A1：** The ability to integrate new information and deduce its consequences is central to learning, understanding, and reasoning in large language models (LLMs). Whether information arrives during training or in-context, models must not only store local facts, but propagate their long-range implications globally. To explore information integration in LLMs, we introduce two complementary natural language benchmarks for large-scale evaluation of deductive reasoning as a function of inference depth. The first is a formal graph benchmark enabling controlled evaluation over a wide range of graph topologies and integration tasks; the second is a knowledge composition benchmark grounded in real-world knowledge graphs. We use these benchmarks to compare knowledge integration from information encoded in context versus information encoded in model weights during pretraining, evaluating a set of open models implicitly, without chain of thought. We find that accuracy decays sharply with increased composition length for both pretrained and in-context knowledge. Information presented in-context, however, substantially outperforms deduction over information encoded in weights. These results quantify a fundamental limitation of current models with direct implications for prompt-based control of agent behavior and for continual learning strategies, and they provide a foundation for developing training, prompting, and integration methods that support deeper reasoning.

**comment/C1：** 6 pages, 2 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-18"></a>

### 18. [SPT-Flow: One Integration, Many Futures in Continuous-Time World Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41273v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41273

**作者：** Mingyang Lyu、Yinqian Sun、Gongce Cao、Yuhao Zhang、Lu Jia、Qian Zhang、Bo Wang、Pin Lyu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41273v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41273v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SPT-Flow：在连续时间世界模型中每条轨迹采样一个 latent，将自由运行的 ODE rollout 在多个读出时刻与演示状态对齐；并用匹配/交换 latent 的诊断检验 teacher-forced 基线的 latent 条件是否真被使用，在 LIBERO 上报告 PSNR/LPIPS 提升。

**相关性简评：** 「监督捷径」假设与匹配/交换 latent 探针可为核查 LVR 中 latent 内容是否被答案使用、中间状态监督是否造成视觉旁路或信息坍缩提供线索；但研究对象是机器人世界模型，迁移性待判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SPT-Flow: One Integration, Many Futures in Continuous-Time World Models

**abstract/A1：** Continuous-time world models generate a latent trajectory through a single ODE integration, but adding stochastic conditioning does not ensure that the model uses it. In our teacher-forced baseline, posterior means vary by only about 1% of the prior scale, and matched and swapped latents yield indistinguishable reconstruction PSNR at the reported precision. We study a supervision-shortcut hypothesis: ground-truth intermediate states can reduce the need for trajectory-associated latent information. We introduce SPT-Flow, which samples one latent per trajectory and matches a free-running ODE rollout to demonstration states at multiple readout times. Re-anchoring experiments support the proposed mechanism. On LIBERO, with both models trained for 30k optimizer steps, SPT-Flow improves best-of-10 PSNR over our retrained ODEWorld baseline by 2.0 and 2.4 dB at the mid-trajectory and final frames and reduces mean LPIPS by 23%, with comparable per-sample inference cost. The rollout control recovers most of the accuracy gain; learned latent conditioning adds diversity and best-of-K gains while reducing mean sample accuracy relative to this control. The diagnostics support trajectory-associated latent utilization; semantic mode coverage under matched conditions in the robotic setting remains unverified.

**comment/C1：** 19 pages, including appendices

</details>

<a id="paper-19"></a>

### 19. [Operon-S1: a fast decision controller for long-horizon biomedical agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41350v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41350

**作者：** Genoria AI Team、Yuejie Hou、Yueyuxiao Yang、Weiting Tang、Meng Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41350v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41350v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Operon-S1，把长程生物医学 agent 中的判断与执行分离：在 13 个决策点以封闭问题形式交给本地小模型（CPU 中位 0.6 s、无请求费用）作答，仅通过不含答案的固定消息干预，并记录每次决策及其后果，再用任务构建、验证器或执行标签重训并以晋升门控上线。

**相关性简评：** 其「判断层外置」把停止、错误与证据判断变成可测量、可替换、可训练的独立模块，并逐决策核算成本，可能为 LVR 中 STOP 决策与贡献分配提供可供核查的轻量控制器思路；但工作为纯文本 agent 系统，未涉及视觉细节或 latent 状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Operon-S1: a fast decision controller for long-horizon biomedical agents

**abstract/A1：** Language-model agents now carry out long scientific work in biology, but their judgements about conventions, skills, errors, evidence and stopping are made inside the reasoning that does the work, where they cannot be measured, replaced or improved on their own. Here we present Operon-S1, a fast decision system, a System 1 for the agent, that separates judging from doing. A decision harness poses the agent's recurring judgements as closed questions at 13 decision points and acts only through fixed messages that never contain an answer, so any model that answers the questions can serve as the judge. Across seven life-science benchmarks, a local decision model took over these judgements in 0.6 s at the median for an in-loop request on CPU at no request cost, with no measurable change in the agent's score on five of six benchmarks, whereas the general language model that drives the agent, asked the same questions, accounted for 8.0% to 35.2% of task cost. Every decision is recorded with its consequence. Retraining on labels from task construction, verifiers or execution raised decision accuracy on every question in each of three trained versions, and a new version enters service only through a promotion gate. Task-level results in this study use the public checkpoint. The same closed decisions serve wet-laboratory protocol troubleshooting and the design of a germline target enrichment panel. Like the regulator of an operon, which switches genes on and off without making their products, Operon-S1 steers the agent without doing its work, and makes the agent's judgements a layer that can be measured, replaced and trained.

**comment/C1：** 40 pages, 5 figures, 3 tables, 5 Extended Data figures, 6 Extended Data tables

</details>

<a id="paper-20"></a>

### 20. [Online Evidence-Containment Prediction over Text Spans under Distinct Queries](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41384v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41384

**作者：** Xiang Liu、Haochen Ding

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41384v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41384v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文将嵌套文本区域的证据验证形式化为在线证据包含预测（OECP），利用包含关系的三角二维乘积序做不同查询下的在线保序回归并给出遗憾界，再以期望搜索空间缩减把预测概率转成验证调用决策，在 NQ、SciFact、ERASER 与 DPR 层级上减少调用并提升 F1。

**相关性简评：** 其「证据包含约束＋预算内验证调用调度」可能为视觉证据筛选及 LOOK/STOP 的决策与成本建模提供可核查线索，但原文为纯文本任务，迁移到多模态推理仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Online Evidence-Containment Prediction over Text Spans under Distinct Queries

**abstract/A1：** Evidence-grounded NLP systems often verify nested text regions sequentially, so verifying one region constrains others. We formalize this as Online Evidence-Containment Prediction (OECP): each candidate is predicted before exact verification and queried at most once. Span containment is exactly a triangular 2D product order, making OECP distinct-query online isotonic regression. With multiple admissible targets, worst-case regret is near-linear, $\\widetildeΘ(m\_{\\mathrm{eff}})$, in the effective scale given by endpoint-rank compression; a single fixed target, as in standard QA, admits an explicit polynomial-time learner with optimal $Θ(\\log m\_{\\mathrm{eff}})$ regret, run on real NQ episodes. On Natural Questions, containment yields a large deductive gain plus a smaller learned gain on unresolved candidates, reproduced across two backbones and DPR hierarchies. Expected Search-space Reduction (ESR) turns these probabilities into decisions, localizing evidence in fewer verifier calls on SciFact, ERASER, and DPR hierarchies; on DPR hierarchies under call budgets it outperforms four recent external selectors and raises fixed-reader F1 over learned and generator-greedy scheduling.

</details>

<a id="paper-21"></a>

### 21. [From Representation to Readout: Mechanistic Analysis of Membership Signals in Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41445v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41445

**作者：** Wenxi Dai、Jingyu Hu、Yanting Yang、Mengnan Du

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41445v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41445v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文对 LLM 的成员推断信号做机制分析，提出由可解码信息分数、被使用信息分数和读出可达性差距组成的 DUG 指标，区分内部表示中「存在」的信息与模型读出时「实际使用」的信息，并结合 activation patching 定位相关中间层，比较 SFT、RL 类成员推断及 RLVR 污染设置下的差异。

**相关性简评：** 其把「可从隐状态解码」与「经读出被使用」分离测量的思路，可能可供核查 LVR 中视觉细节是否留在内部状态却未被答案读出（视觉旁路、信息坍缩），层定位做法也可供参考。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From Representation to Readout: Mechanistic Analysis of Membership Signals in Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Membership inference aims to determine whether a particular example was included in a model's training data. Such membership signals are often broadly described as memorization, while the underlying mechanisms can involve both how information is represented internally and how it is accessed by the model to produce behaviors. We therefore present a mechanistic study of membership signals in large language models (LLMs). We introduce Decodable Information Score, Used Information Score, and Readout Accessibility Gap (DUG), a set of complementary measures that separately quantify membership information decodable from internal representations and the extent to which it is exposed through the model's readout. Across supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL)-based membership inference methods, membership-specific effects appear in both the amount of decodable information and readout under SFT, while under RL-based membership inference methods they appear primarily in readout. Activation patching further localizes this membership-specific effect to intermediate layers. To examine whether these findings extend to a more general RL setting, we further study reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) contamination, where the training reward is independent of the reference solution. In this setting, we find that behavioral changes can emerge without a corresponding increase in likelihood-based membership inference signals.

</details>

<a id="paper-22"></a>

### 22. [Real Computation or Merely Naming? A Pre-Registered Mechanism Audit of 25 Cognitive-Architecture Modules in an LLM-Based Agent System](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41604v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41604

**作者：** Peter Chen、Ni Zhang、Ying Xu、Siyuan Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41604v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41604v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文对两个基于本地 LLM 的 agent 系统中冠以 global workspace、theory of mind 等名称的认知模块做预注册机制审计：用四问静态审计加调用链追踪、11 个模块的可反驳干预及两次修复正对照，发现 25 个模块中 18 个的计算值从未进入任何生产输出。

**相关性简评：** 其「声称-实现」审计协议与 connected-but-never-called、called-but-inert、called-but-content-blind 失效分类，可能可供核查 LVR 的 latent 状态是否真被执行路径使用、是否存在只被调用而不影响内容的视觉或推理旁路；迁移到视觉与潜变量设置的有效性仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Real Computation or Merely Naming? A Pre-Registered Mechanism Audit of 25 Cognitive-Architecture Modules in an LLM-Based Agent System

**abstract/A1：** Agent systems built on local large language models are increasingly marketed through the vocabulary of cognitive architecture: modules named global workspace, theory of mind, causal reasoning, or active inference are listed as features, and the presence of the name is taken as the presence of the mechanism. We ask the prior question -- does the named computation actually happen -- and contribute a transferable verification method for the claim-implementation gap: a four-question static audit protocol with line-number evidence and call-chain tracing, demonstrated on two systems. We audit a system claiming 25 cognitive mechanisms, pre-register falsifiable predictions for 11 modules and execute the interventions, replicate the protocol on the IIT-phi core of a second, independent system with its own pre-committed falsification criteria, and run two repair experiments as positive controls. In 18 of 25 modules the computed value never reaches any production output; three idle-wiring shapes new to the dead-code and code-smell literatures emerge (connected-but-never-called, called-but-inert, called-but-content-blind). Of 61 frozen criteria across 12 interventions, 53 were confirmed, two partially adjudicated, five falsified, one adjudication-ambiguous. Repairs raised planner task success from 0.028 to 1.000 and cut state-controller error by 52%. Module names and module computations must be audited as independent claims; the protocol and the five-class failure taxonomy are reusable for the claim-implementation gap at two-system, case-level depth.

**comment/C1：** 40 pages. Static audit of 25 cognitive-architecture modules (18 carry no computed value to any production output), pre-registered interventions on 11 modules, 61 frozen criteria (53 confirmed / 5 falsified), two repair controls (0.028 -&gt; 1.000 task success; -52% error). Artifact: https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.23433900

</details>

### 23. [Multimodal In-Context Learning with Explicit Rule Induction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41754v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41754

**作者：** Yuquan Li、Xinyang Jiang、Yuhao Xue、Cairong Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41754v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41754v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ERIM 框架：构建以规则为中心的多模态 in-context 数据集，为规则归纳加入显式监督，并用混合冷启动 SFT 加强化学习的两阶段策略微调 MLLM，使其先从示例中归纳规则再作答。

**相关性简评：** 把「规则」当作可监督的中间推理状态，可能为 LVR 中轨迹监督方式与从视觉证据中提取任务必要规则提供参照；但其规则以文本显式表达而非 latent 状态生成，迁移可行性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Multimodal In-Context Learning with Explicit Rule Induction

**abstract/A1：** A central challenge in multimodal in-context learning (MICL) arises from tasks that are ill-defined without context and require uncovering the underlying rules from demonstrations. Existing methods predominantly treat MICL as direct input-output mapping, overlooking the importance of explicitly inducing and reasoning over such rules, limiting both performance and interpretability. We propose ERIM, Explicit Rule-Induction for Multimodal In-Context Learning, a unified framework that enhances MICL by explicitly inducing the latent rules governing demonstration samples. Leveraging the reasoning ability of multi-modal large language model (MLLM), ERIM constructs rule-centric MICL datasets, introduces explicit supervision for rule induction, and fine-tunes MLLM to perform explicit rule-induction via a two-stage training strategy that combines mixed cold-start supervised fine-tuning with reinforcement learning. Extensive experiments show consistent performance improvements across diverse MICL settings and demonstrate that correctly inducing underlying rules directly leads to stronger and more stable performance.

</details>

<a id="paper-24"></a>

### 24. [There Is No Free Loop: When Does Looping a Trained Model Pay for Its Inference Compute?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42030v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.42030

**作者：** Rhea Sanjay Sukthanker、Pashmina Cameron

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42030v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.42030v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在预训练后的 Qwen3 系列模型上重新应用部分层形成循环，并与等参数无循环模型及等 FLOPs 的最优无循环基线比较，检验额外推理计算是否值得。

**相关性简评：** 其中「循环层—等 FLOPs 对照—规模依赖收益」的负结果可供核查 loop 式 latent reasoning 的计算成本与收益分配，尤其提示大模型后加循环可能不划算；但研究为纯文本，视觉证据与状态内容未涉及。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** There Is No Free Loop: When Does Looping a Trained Model Pay for Its Inference Compute?

**abstract/A1：** Looping re-applies a block of layers so that each token passes through the block more than once. Adding a loop to a trained model is attractive because it leaves the parameter count, and hence the disk size, the memory footprint and the training cost unchanged. Unfortunately, every generated token pays for the loop. Re-applying m of N layers makes each token about (N+m)/N times as expensive. Loops that a model learns during large-scale pretraining justify this price, but whether loops added after pretraining justify this cost remains unclear. We compare a looped model with two simple baselines. We first compare it with the same model trained identically without the loop (equal parameters). Secondly, we compare it with the best unlooped use of the same inference floating-point operations (equal FLOPs). Across Qwen3 and Qwen3.5 models from 0.8B to 9B parameters, only the looped 0.8B model beats both baselines. At 4B and 9B, the looped model does not beat the first baseline, i.e. the same model trained without the loop. This is not because the benchmarks are too easy, since on the same benchmarks the unlooped 9B model still scores up to 13 points higher than the 4B model, showing that there is ample room for improvement. Introducing loops in an already pretrained model therefore only pays for small models with a fixed parameter budget.

</details>

<a id="paper-25"></a>

### 25. [ACCT: Asymmetric Cross-LLM Context Transfer](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42116v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.42116

**作者：** Yuzhe Liu、Haolin Tian、Tonghan Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42116v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.42116v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** ACCT 研究同一模型族内不同规模 LLM 之间的 KV cache 迁移：先用 Cartographer 为每个目标层与 KV head 找出与 query 相关的上下文 token，再由 Allocator 在共享 KV 预算下分配各层各头的保留量，最后由 Compiler 将源模型 KV 转为目标模型可直接使用的稀疏 KV，从而复用上下文计算。

**相关性简评：** 其按层/头筛选 query 相关 token 并在统一预算下分配稀疏 KV 的做法，可能为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选、latent 状态的计算预算分配以及跨模块 latent 复用提供可核查线索；但该工作为纯文本 KV 迁移，未涉及视觉输入或推理轨迹，迁移性需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ACCT: Asymmetric Cross-LLM Context Transfer

**abstract/A1：** In real-world deployments, large language model (LLM) systems are increasingly evolving from single-model invocation toward heterogeneous deployments in which different models are dynamically invoked for inference. When the same context is reused across models, however, each newly selected target model typically has to process it again, repeatedly incurring substantial computational costs. Therefore, we propose ACCT (Asymmetric Cross-LLM Context Transfer), which targets KV cache transfer between models of different scales within the same model family. ACCT transforms the KV cache produced by a source model into sparse KV states that can be directly consumed by a target model, thereby enabling cross-model reuse of context computation and decoupling context computation from downstream inference. ACCT consists of three lightweight components: the Cartographer identifies query-relevant context tokens for each target layer and KV head; the Allocator determines how many context tokens each target layer and KV head retains under a shared total KV budget; and the Compiler transforms selected source KV into sparse target KV. The target model can therefore perform inference using the transferred KV without reprocessing the original context. We evaluate ACCT in two complementary settings comprising five general-capability benchmarks and six highly context-dependent tasks. Experiments show that ACCT matches or exceeds the target model's original inference performance even when reducing the KV cache size by 75%. At a context length of approximately 19K tokens, ACCT's KV conversion achieves a 6.5-21.2 times speedup over target context prefill. These results demonstrate the feasibility of transferring context computation across models under a limited KV budget.

</details>

<a id="paper-26"></a>

### 26. [When Does Test-Time Search Help World Action Models? An Empirical Audit of Candidate Selection and Simulator Integrity](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41970v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41970

**作者：** Jun Liu、Dajie Sun、Zhenglun Kong、Zhenjiang Fan、Peiyan Dong、Fan Yang、Wenbin Zhangg、Geng Yuan、Xue Lin、Yanzhi Wang、Dong Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41970v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41970v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文以预注册、前提优先的方式审计 Cosmos Policy 与 RoboCasa 上的 test-time scaling：检验候选选择是否含可利用信号、token 剪枝的 FLOPs 节省能否变成真实加速、评测是否可靠，并发现并修复了一处静默渲染损坏。

**相关性简评：** 其「决策信号—实现效率—评测完整性」审计链可供核查，可能用于审视 LVR 中额外计算是否带来可利用证据、算力节省能否转化为实际成本下降，以及视觉输入静默损坏对结论的影响。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Does Test-Time Search Help World Action Models? An Empirical Audit of Candidate Selection and Simulator Integrity

**abstract/A1：** World-action models increasingly apply test-time scaling, spending extra inference computation on candidate selection and adaptive allocation to improve control. Such gains are credible only when candidates carry exploitable signal, nominal compute savings become real speedup, and evaluation stays reliable. We audit all three on Cosmos Policy and RoboCasa with a premise-first, pre-registered methodology. On clean data, a learned value selector is 1.66 pp worse than random; a hindsight oracle gains only 1.40 pp, with an episode-level interval spanning zero; and 82% of states show identical candidate outcomes, leaving little selection headroom. Token pruning cuts per-forward FLOPs by up to 47% but yields at most a measured 1.15$\\times$ end-to-end speedup on the deployed path (1.25$\\times$ on a decode-excluded path), short of a 1.5$\\times$ target, and our adaptive-allocation experiment is inconclusive under a power gate. We further identify and fix a silent rendering corruption whose rate rises sharply along compute-heavy planning paths while leaving the official direct-policy result unaffected, and release a detector, fix, and scripts. These findings show why test-time scaling should be audited as a chain of premises (decision signal, realized efficiency, evaluationintegrity) rather than by success rate alone. Conclusions are scoped to the tested configuration.

</details>

<a id="paper-27"></a>

### 27. [FoAViS: Analyzing Learned Fixation Strategies in a Foveated Active Vision System](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41987v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41987

**作者：** Valentin Hassler、Jonathan Henrich、Yohaï-Eliel Berreby、Haoze Zhu、Alexander Ecker

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41987v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41987v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 FoAViS：用 log-polar 中央凹采样读取视觉输入，把信息累积到统一的 2D 空间记忆，并以与分类、语义分割任务联合训练的注视策略从 2D 图中选择注视点，随后分析涌现的注视行为及其对任务表示的影响。

**相关性简评：** 其「主动选择性采样＋外部记忆累积＋任务联合训练的策略」与 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选、LOOK 决策及状态更新有概念联系；但该工作不含语言模型与 latent 文本推理，也无显式停止机制，可供核查的只是采样策略与记忆读出设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FoAViS: Analyzing Learned Fixation Strategies in a Foveated Active Vision System

**abstract/A1：** Human vision is foveated and active: resolution is highest at the fovea and falls off towards the periphery, and fixations are actively directed to locations of interest. Building a machine system that processes information in the same way lets us study gaze behavior in a controlled setting where the system's components and objectives are known. The emergence of gaze characteristics can therefore be examined systematically, which may in turn inform our understanding of biological vision. This requires a model that can sample like the fovea, handle various tasks from classification to dense prediction, and be trained concurrently with a fixation policy. In our study, we present such a system and study its capabilities and the fixation policies that emerge from it. Our system, FoAViS, samples visual input by adopting the established log-polar foveation and accumulates information in a 2D spatial memory. Due to its 2D structure, the same memory can be read out globally for classification or densely for semantic segmentation, without any architectural change. Fixations are drawn from a 2D map by a policy trained concurrently with the task. We find that learned fixation policies select more informative, task-specific locations than non-learned heuristics. These policies also shape the task representations in a beneficial way: for both classification and segmentation, models trained concurrently with a policy outperform models trained with random locations, even when the latter are provided with learned scanpaths. Moreover, these scanpaths exhibit similarities to human gaze behavior by capturing aspects of empirical scanpath characteristics. Our work marks an important step away from standard passive, single-pass architectures, towards biologically grounded models of vision. It opens a path for studying goal-directed gaze behavior and information representation in the visual system.

**comment/C1：** 79 pages, 6 figures. Code: https://github.com/ecker-lab/FoAViS

</details>

<a id="paper-28"></a>

### 28. [Cost-Aware Stopping for Freeze-Thaw Bayesian Optimization: When Does One-Step Lookahead Suffice?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42085v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.42085

**作者：** Long Vu、Jongseong Brad Choi、Binoy Ravindran、Zhoulai Fu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、math.OC、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42085v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.42085v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究 freeze-thaw 贝叶斯优化中的成本感知停止：以「最佳观测分数减去每次增量成本」为无预算效用，提出仅在期望改进（EI）超过成本时继续的 EI 停止规则，并构造一个两步前瞻价值下界的证明书来判定该停止是否过早，在三个超参数优化基准上做了预注册比较。

**相关性简评：** 其中「单步前瞻何时足够」、效用中显式减去增量成本、以及判定停止过早的下界证明，可能为 LVR 中 THINK/LOOK/STOP 的停止决策与动态计算收益核算提供形式化参照，但需核查代理模型假设能否对应推理状态与证据获取。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Cost-Aware Stopping for Freeze-Thaw Bayesian Optimization: When Does One-Step Lookahead Suffice?

**abstract/A1：** Freeze-thaw Bayesian optimization (BO) evaluates candidates in resumable increments but usually stops at a budget or convergence test. We maximize a budget-free utility, the best observed score minus a cost per increment, with a one-step lookahead: advance the candidate with the largest expected improvement (EI) under a fixed surrogate, and stop once no EI exceeds the cost. This rule, EI stopping, never falls below stopping immediately in expected utility, yet it can stop too early. We construct a certificate, a lower bound on the value of two-step lookahead, that proves such a stop premature, and repeating this correction never lowers expected utility under the surrogate. Under the surrogate, EI stops are premature on average at low costs and lose little at high costs. On three hyperparameter optimization benchmarks, a pre-registered test finds EI stopping noninferior to the correction in most high-cost settings, while the correction gains at low costs on two of the three; more information sharing in the surrogate raises EI stopping's utility. On the surrogates of two published methods, EI stopping trades lower scores for fewer training increments. EI stopping is a simple cost-aware reference whose utility depends on the surrogate.

</details>

<a id="paper-29"></a>

### 29. [Drone-PUR: From Visual Clue Discovery to Understanding and Reasoning in UAV Scenes](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40801v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40801

**作者：** Jiahe Wu、Bing Cao、Xingyue Guo、Ruipu Zhao、Beiming Yu、Jiekang Feng、Qinghua Hu、Pengfei Zhu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40801v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40801v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Drone-PUR，一个面向无人机场景的 VLM 数据集与基准，按视觉线索发现、解释与整合组织 11 项任务（175k 训练、20k 测试），评测 22 个通用 VLM，并分阶段微调得到 UAV-PUR 4B/8B。

**相关性简评：** 其「线索发现→理解→推理」任务构造及感知与推理双向迁移分析，可为核查视觉细节是否被保留并用于答案提供诊断参考；但材料未涉及 latent 状态生成，且任务场景偏 UAV 专用，故先列为待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Drone-PUR: From Visual Clue Discovery to Understanding and Reasoning in UAV Scenes

**abstract/A1：** The reasoning capabilities of vision-language models (VLMs) can be limited by incomplete or inaccurate visual observations in the general domain. In unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) scenes, small targets, subtle attributes, and changing viewpoints further complicate perception and understanding. Therefore, we introduce Drone-PUR, a dataset and benchmark organized around visual clue discovery, interpretation, and integration, corresponding to the three capability dimensions of perception, understanding, and reasoning. Reasoning samples are designed to require models to combine multiple visual clues to derive visually grounded conclusions. Its 11 tasks provide 175k training and 20k test samples spanning single images, image sequences, cross-modal images, and multiple viewpoints. All test samples undergo human review. Evaluating 22 general-purpose VLMs reveals persistent limitations in spatial judgment and cross-modal and cross-view reasoning. Across models, stronger perception is associated with better reasoning, while single-task fine-tuning further reveals selective bidirectional transfer between these capabilities. To examine domain adaptation, we develop UAV-PUR from Qwen3-VL through staged fine-tuning with auxiliary supervision. Its 4B and 8B variants improve overall multiple-choice accuracy by 18.33 and 17.52 percentage points over their pretrained counterparts, respectively. The dataset and project resources are available at https://drone-pur.github.io.

**comment/C1：** 17 pages, 13 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-30"></a>

### 30. [Incongruence-Aware Evaluation of Multimodal Speech Emotion Recognition in Audio Large Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40883v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40883

**作者：** Fengji Jing、Guy Laban

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.MM、cs.SD、eess.AS

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40883v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40883v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把语音与转录文本映射到 valence-arousal-dominance 空间，用双成分高斯混合将二者距离转成连续的不一致分数并以人类判断验证，在 eNTERFACE'05、MELD、CMU-MOSEI 上评估三个可处理音频的模型；结果显示只要音频存在，不一致就会提高出错几率，而同特征上的监督分类器表现相反。

**相关性简评：** 属多模态证据冲突下的失败诊断，与「模型是否真正使用非文本证据、如何合并原始线索」直接相关；可核查其不一致分数与「难度源于模型组合方式而非信号本身」的结论能否迁移到视觉-文本冲突场景。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Incongruence-Aware Evaluation of Multimodal Speech Emotion Recognition in Audio Large Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Voice-interactive large language models process both what a speaker says and how they say it, yet the two channels do not always agree. We ask whether that disagreement degrades speech emotion recognition. Mapping text and audio into a shared valence-arousal-dominance space, we convert the distance between them into a continuous, threshold-free incongruence score with a two-component Gaussian mixture, and validate the score against human judgement. We evaluate three audiocapable models on acted, conversational and spontaneous speech (eNTERFACE'05, MELD and CMU-MOSEI). With the model held constant across modalities, incongruence raises the odds of error whenever audio is present, in every corpus, after controlling for the ground-truth emotion and for distance in the feature space. Supervised classifiers trained on the same features behave in the opposite way, which suggests that the difficulty lies in how these models combine raw evidence rather than in the signal itself. Text-only effects are unreliable: swapping only the model that reads the transcript reverses their sign on one corpus. Rebuilding the score without the emotion lexicon reproduces the central result, and among six simpler alternatives it is the only label-free score that predicts error in all three corpora. Code, data tables and prompts are released.

</details>

<a id="paper-31"></a>

### 31. [FieldGuideRAG: Image-Grounded GraphRAG for Fine-Grained Recognition with Vision-Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40971v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40971

**作者：** Jiacheng Ding、Yanliang Qi、Cong Guo、Peihua Mai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.IR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40971v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40971v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出免训练的 FieldGuideRAG：在知识图谱每个物种节点存真实照片并连接近缘、分布与生态事实边，用冻结 VLM 将查询与候选节点照片逐一核验，融合分数与分布边完成细粒度识别并据事实边回答问题。

**相关性简评：** 其「以图像而非文本作为候选视觉证据、并用核验分数判断目标不在图中」涉及视觉证据筛选与停止/弃答决策，可为 LVR 的外部证据获取与弃权机制提供参照，但状态仍停留在检索与打分层面，需核查对 latent 推理的可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FieldGuideRAG: Image-Grounded GraphRAG for Fine-Grained Recognition with Vision-Language Models

**abstract/A1：** We present FieldGuideRAG, a training-free GraphRAG method in which a vision-language model, or VLM, recognizes fine-grained species by comparing the query with real photographs stored on the nodes of a knowledge graph. Multimodal GraphRAG systems describe each entity in text, and text conveys little of the visual detail that separates look-alike species. Our graph stores one photograph on every species node and links each species to its relatives, its geographic range and its ecological facts. A frozen VLM verifies the query against the photograph of each candidate node. FieldGuideRAG fuses the verification scores with the range edge and answers questions about the organism from the fact edges of the verified species. On CUB-200, a Wikidata graph and four iNaturalist domains, the node photograph improves verification over the species name on every graph, while text descriptions add little. Verification of the candidate pool exceeds a SigLIP retriever by up to 16 points, the range edge adds 7 to 19 points on iNaturalist, and answering from fact edges is correct on up to 89.5% of 28,820 ecological questions. The verification scores also detect queries whose species is missing from the graph. Graphs, prompts and code will be released.

</details>

<a id="paper-32"></a>

### 32. [Hawkeye: Reading Illegible Text with Vision Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41254v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41254

**作者：** Brian Tang、Kang G. Shin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41254v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41254v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 BSTRB 基准（由 1,699 段真实视频构建的 8,398 张模糊或低分辨率文本图像），并微调出 Hawkeye，利用上下文先验与像素级对齐读取难以辨认的文本，同时刻画当前 VLM 开始失效的分辨率阈值。

**相关性简评：** 对退化文本的细粒度视觉细节保留与失效分辨率的刻画，可供核查 visual evidence 质量、像素级对齐及「何时看不清」的诊断线索；但其单次前向的识别范式与 latent 推理状态生成关联较弱。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Hawkeye: Reading Illegible Text with Vision Language Models

**abstract/A1：** While modern Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have been remarkably successful in general scene text recognition, they consistently struggle with the long-tail distribution of text that is degraded by motion blur or low resolution. These limitations are particularly apparent in egocentric applications like smart glasses, where text is often captured in noisy environments. In this paper, we introduce the \\textbf{BSTRB} (Blurry Scene Text Recognition Benchmark), a novel benchmark of 8,398 noisy image samples with high-fidelity annotations derived from 1,699 real-world video sequences. To support this, we present \\textbf{Hawkeye}, a VLM fine-tuned on BSTRB to read and reason about illegible text by leveraging contextual priors and pixel-level alignment. Our results show that \\name matches much larger VLMs on BSTRB overall and exceeds them on text at or below 20 pixels, in a single pass suited to real-time deployment. We characterize the resolution at which current VLMs begin to fail.

**comment/C1：** For datasets, code, models: https://byron123t.github.io/hawkeye/

</details>

### 33. [Don't Just Impute, Detect: Task-Aware Diffusion Pipelines for Edge Anomaly Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40732v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40732

**作者：** Jeongsu Park、Taehyeon Yun、Geonwoo Kim、Yuji Lim、Moohong Min

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40732v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40732v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对 IIoT 多传感器缺失块导致「先插补再检测」流程中的插补偏差，提出 TADR：以冻结下游检测器的损失景观训练条件扩散精修器，并用噪声预测的一步干净窗口估计承载任务感知项，避免对采样轨迹反传。

**相关性简评：** 其「插补偏差」——按 MSE 插补会把部分观测的异常平滑回常态——与 LVR 中任务必要细节在内部状态里被坍缩的问题同构；而用下游任务损失塑造扩散状态、又绕开采样轨迹反传的做法，可供核查作为 latent 状态训练监督与贡献分配的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Don't Just Impute, Detect: Task-Aware Diffusion Pipelines for Edge Anomaly Detection

**abstract/A1：** Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) edge pipelines routinely lose contiguous blocks of multi-sensor data to network dropouts and hardware faults. The standard remedy is a disjointed "impute-then-detect" pipeline whose imputer is trained only for Mean Squared Error (MSE). Such an imputer fills a missing block with the most probable normal pattern: a partially observed anomaly is smoothed back toward normality, and boundary artifacts on normal data raise false alarms. We call this imputation bias and propose the Task-Aware Diffusion Refiner (TADR), a conditional diffusion refiner trained against the loss landscape of a frozen downstream detector. Because back-propagating through the sampling trajectory is intractable, the task-aware terms act on the one-step clean-window estimate that the noise prediction already yields, so training costs no more than plain diffusion training. Under strict Point-to-Point evaluation over five seeds with paired significance tests, TADR raises F1 by $0.338$ over the unrefined detector on SMD ($p = 0.0015$), and the anomaly enhancement objective alone contributes $0.463$ in ablation. We also characterise when the method helps: the gain tracks the continuous, mutually redundant channels available and the anomaly evidence the outage removes, and it is absent on telemetry datasets dominated by one-hot command channels and on PSM. At ten sampling steps TADR runs in $41.4$ ms per window on a single CPU core, sustaining about $24$ concurrent 1 Hz streams from a $4.03$ MB model. Code and per-seed results are available at https://github.com/int-park422/TADR.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages, 6 figures. Accepted at the 33rd IEEE International Conference on High Performance Computing, Data, and Analytics (HiPC 2026)

</details>

<a id="paper-34"></a>

### 34. [PathoJev: Efficient Pathology Decisions from Shared Visual Context](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40752v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40752

**作者：** Chengyang Zhang、Wenchuan Zhang、Bo Li、Xinyu Liu、Jiaming Yang、Mengran Li、Jie Chen、Wei Ju、Yuhao Yi、Hong Bu、Jiancheng Lv

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40752v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40752v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** PathoJev 面向病理全切片与 ROI 判读，对同一视觉上下文只编码一次，批量并行评估多个问题的答案概率，并复用混合骨干的循环状态以抑制随上下文长度与问题数增长的计算量。

**相关性简评：** 其共享视觉上下文加批量问题后缀、复用循环状态的做法，可能为 LVR 中多查询共享视觉证据、缓存与注意力结构对 latent 相似度及推理成本的影响提供可核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PathoJev: Efficient Pathology Decisions from Shared Visual Context

**abstract/A1：** Pathology workflows require multiple visual judgments about the same slide, from identifying tumor type to assessing grade and invasion. Generating and parsing a separate textual response for each structured question adds latency between visual evidence and usable decisions. Building on the System One paradigm, we introduce PathoJev, a pathology model that directly predicts answer probabilities for whole-slide and region-level assessment. PathoJev encodes each slide once and evaluates independent question suffixes in batches over the shared visual context. Reusing the hybrid backbone's recurrent states further limits the computation that grows with slide-context length for each additional question. Across three whole-slide benchmarks, the 9B and 27B models achieve mean accuracies of 77.2% and 78.1%, exceeding SlideChat by 5.4 and 6.3 percentage points. Both scales also maintain competitive accuracy on five ROI benchmarks. On WSI-Bench, the 9B model runs 9.1x faster than SlideChat. Shared context accelerates eight-question workloads by 6.9x and 5.9x over separate prefix encoding, rising to 11.2x and 8.9x for 64 questions. These results provide a reusable probabilistic interface for combining multiple visual judgments in whole-slide and local tissue assessment. Code: https://github.com/ChyaZhang/PathoJev.

</details>

<a id="paper-35"></a>

### 35. [More Rules, Longer Lists? Benchmarking Self-Evolving Agents on a Hidden-Rule Curriculum](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40758v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40758

**作者：** Chenjie Qiu、Boyan Xu、Shaobin Shi、Bingfeng Chen、Ruichu Cai、Dongsheng Luo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40758v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40758v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CLR-Bench：每条链逐级叠加一条隐藏规则，规则作用于需借助网络查证的具名分组标签，用少量练习反馈检验自演化智能体能否真正学到规则、而非把纠正逐条堆成例外列表，并评测六种自演化方法。

**相关性简评：** 其用探针确认基座模型「知道却不用」、并区分真学规则与例外堆叠的设计，可能为核查 VLM 内部 latent 内容是否被答案实际使用、以及稀疏证据下的假设筛选提供可借用线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>
