**title/T1：** More Rules, Longer Lists? Benchmarking Self-Evolving Agents on a Hidden-Rule Curriculum

**abstract/A1：** Self-evolving agents rewrite a persistent state from feedback, but it is largely untested whether this lets them learn a rule nobody told them. An agent can also pass by writing down each correction as an exception, and its list then grows with every new rule. We introduce CLR-Bench, a Curriculum of Latent Rules benchmark. Each chain adds one hidden rule per level on top of the earlier ones. A rule acts on an opaque label that stands for a named grouping published on the Web, such as the founding members of an airline alliance. The task gives one example value, practice feedback reveals one more member and one non-member, and the test checks unseen members and similar non-members. Probes certify that the base model knows the members of each grouping and does not guess it without feedback, and that the evidence identifies the grouping once labels are read as named groupings. An automated pipeline builds chains from a new data domain, and we release 20 chains over 10 domains. We evaluate six recent self-evolution methods. With the certifying model they barely learn. The best gain at most 5 points over a frozen agent and exclude only 6-12% of the unseen members from the count, fewer than the 20% reached by simply keeping the raw practice history. A fixed lesson that states the named-grouping convention lifts the same methods by 14.5 to 16.5 points. Their states hold the revealed member in 75-98% of cases and almost never the class name, and when practice exposes a member the list misses, they add that member to the list. With the stronger GPT-6-Sol, ACE and Dynamic Cheatsheet learn from feedback alone. They reach 78% accuracy and solve 59-60% of the single cells, against 53% and 8% with the certifying model, and they surpass the raw history. CLR-Bench therefore measures how well an agent learns, and this ability differs across models.

**comment/C1：** 13 pages, 8 figures, 11 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-36"></a>

### 36. [EgoHSI3R: Online 4D Hand-Scene Interaction Reconstruction from Egocentric Video](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40832v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40832

**作者：** Mingzhi Pei、Zhangchen Ye、Ruoqu Chen、Liu Cao、Mengdi Xu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40832v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40832v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 EgoHSI3R，一个从第一视角单目视频因果、在线地联合重建相机运动、稠密场景几何、世界坐标双手 MANO 及逐顶点接触的 feed-forward 框架，核心是随时间累积几何证据的循环场景表示与手部提示查询。

**相关性简评：** 其「循环场景表示持续累积视觉证据 + 用任务查询读取场景特征」的结构，可供核查作为 VLM 推理中持久视觉工作区与证据筛选、更新机制的架构线索，但与推理期语言侧 latent 状态无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EgoHSI3R: Online 4D Hand-Scene Interaction Reconstruction from Egocentric Video

**abstract/A1：** Egocentric videos offer an abundant source of hand-scene interactions for embodied learning, which requires recovering hands, camera, and scene consistently in a shared 3D world. Existing methods focus on either camera-frame hands or world-space hand trajectories, without jointly recovering the scene pointmaps or interaction information. Building a complete reconstruction from such specialized predictions therefore typically requires per-sequence optimization. We propose EgoHSI3R, a unified feed-forward framework for online 4D hand-scene interaction reconstruction from egocentric monocular video. To jointly recover these components, it maintains a recurrent scene representation that accumulates geometric evidence over time and provides a shared world space and scale. We introduce hand-centric prompts that fuse pretrained hand priors and local scene context, while MANO vertex queries attend to scene features to predict dense per-vertex contact. These designs enable a single network to jointly estimate camera motion, dense scene geometry, world-space bilateral MANO hands, and per-vertex hand contact. Despite operating causally, EgoHSI3R achieves the lowest world-space hand joint error on ARCTIC among the evaluated methods, while offering faster inference than existing world-space hand reconstruction pipelines and competitive camera and scene reconstruction accuracy.

</details>

<a id="paper-37"></a>

### 37. [AquaProbe: Efficient Adaptation of Frozen Vision Models for Underwater Images](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40900v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40900

**作者：** Md Shihabul Islam Shovo、Nishi Kanta Paul、Trung Dong、Xiaomin Lin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40900v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40900v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 AquaProbe，用配对的干净图像与物理渲染水下图像，逐层测量冻结视觉编码器中新引入的域差异随水体类型与成像距离的变化，并按该 profile 分配固定的 LoRA 秩预算，另加一个基于条件聚类的三路路由器。

**相关性简评：** 逐层表征分歧的测量与按层分配适配预算，可能可供核查 LVR 中视觉细节在内部状态的层深分布、证据筛选与计算资源分配；但论文不涉及 latent 推理状态生成，联系偏间接，需进一步评估迁移价值。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AquaProbe: Efficient Adaptation of Frozen Vision Models for Underwater Images

**abstract/A1：** Reliable visual perception is essential for safe autonomous underwater exploration. Yet vision models pretrained on terrestrial images become less reliable underwater, where turbidity, backscatter, and wavelength-dependent attenuation alter scene appearance. Existing adaptation methods either spread adapters equally across the encoder depth or select layers using the divergence between two fixed domains. In this paper, we introduce AquaProbe for measuring how the location of the domain gap changes with water type and imaging range. AquaProbe separates newly introduced divergence from that accumulated through a frozen vision encoder using paired clean and physically rendered images and allocates a fixed LoRA budget according to the measured profile. The segmentation mean Intersection-over-Union (mIoU) for the proportional LoRA rank allocation, based on the measured profile, is 70.1; this is a better result than uniform LoRA rank allocation by 1.9 mIoU points while having the same parameter budget of 0.59 million adapter parameters. The three-way router, conditioned on estimated condition clusters, achieves 70.6 mIoU on the standard SUIM split and 63.4 AUDRC under held-out degradation.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 4 figures, 3 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-38"></a>

### 38. [Sparse Autoencoders for Expert-Assisted Interpretation of Histopathology Foundation Model Embeddings](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40905v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40905

**作者：** Šimon Šmída、Wanda Benesova

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40905v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40905v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文在组织病理基础模型（CONCH、UNI2-h）的 NCT-CRC 图像嵌入上训练 vanilla 与 Batch-TopK 稀疏自编码器，用重建保真度、稀疏度、一致性、标签富集与下游类别信号保留来刻画所得稀疏 latent 特征，并配合病理专家讨论提出 WSI 级特征激活可视化流程。

**相关性简评：** SAE 将高维内部表示分解为稀疏、可解释的候选概念单元，并检查其一致性、下游信息保留与上下文可视化，可能为核查 LVR 中间 latent 状态保留了哪些视觉内容、以及这些状态是否需要专家判读提供方法线索，但论文本身不涉及推理生成或停止决策。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Sparse Autoencoders for Expert-Assisted Interpretation of Histopathology Foundation Model Embeddings

**abstract/A1：** Histopathology foundation models learn rich embedding representations, but their internal structure remains difficult to inspect. Concept-level interpretability is particularly relevant in computational pathology because pathologists reason through recurring morphological and tissue-level patterns, including tissue architecture, cellular morphology, staining variation, and artifacts. In this work, we investigate whether sparse autoencoders (SAEs) can decompose embeddings from histopathology foundation models into sparse latent features suitable for expert-assisted interpretation. We train vanilla and Batch-TopK SAE variants on NCT-CRC embeddings, using CONCH as the primary foundation model and UNI2-h as an additional robustness evaluation, and characterize the resulting latent features through reconstruction fidelity, sparsity, coherence, label enrichment, and downstream class-signal preservation. The learned sparse representations preserve most downstream tissue-class information, and their latent features retrieve more coherent and label-concentrated patch groups than raw embedding dimensions or PCA components. Discussions with pathology experts further motivated a WSI-level latent visualization workflow that places feature activations into larger tissue context. Overall, the additional UNI2-h evaluation suggests that the observed behavior is not specific to CONCH, supporting the use of sparse autoencoders for expert-assisted analysis of histopathology foundation-model embeddings, while emphasizing that latent features should be treated as candidate concepts requiring pathological assessment rather than automatically validated histopathological concepts.

</details>

<a id="paper-39"></a>

### 39. [No Channels Left Behind: Understanding Diffusion Acceleration through Residual Gates](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40914v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40914

**作者：** Junha Park、Jaemyung Yu、Jaehui Hwang、Jong-Seok Lee、Dongyoon Han

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40914v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40914v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文重新审视扩散 Transformer 训练加速的来源，发现 REPA 与 dispersive 正则主要重组了 AdaLN-Zero 残差门控 α 的通路，并据此提出归一化参与率（nPR）刻画门控的通道组织，进一步给出去教师、仅做特征范数正则的径向目标以拓宽门控参与。

**相关性简评：** 研究问题属图像生成加速而非 VLM 推理，但其中「门控通道参与度指标 + 对 α 扰动的敏感性」可作为核查 latent 状态是否被真正使用、注意力/门控通路信息是否坍缩的候选分析工具，可供借鉴。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** No Channels Left Behind: Understanding Diffusion Acceleration through Residual Gates

**abstract/A1：** Training acceleration methods for diffusion transformers, such as REPA and dispersive regularization, are commonly explained by additional supervision, spatial-structure priors, or improved feature geometry. We revisit this view and uncover a striking internal change: the AdaLN-Zero residual gate $α$, which directly controls how conditioned updates enter the residual stream, is substantially reorganized by these methods. This observation motivates our hypothesis that acceleration arises, at least in part, from reshaping the residual gating pathway rather than solely from the feature structures explicitly imposed by auxiliary objectives. Supporting this view, accelerated models are markedly more sensitive to perturbations of $α$, and their gate geometry reflects semantic relationships among classes. We further introduce the normalized participation ratio (nPR) to characterize the channel-wise organization of the gates and find that, across REPA teachers, projector architectures, and radial-objective targets, broader gate participation accompanies better generation quality. Guided by this insight, we propose a simple teacher-free radial objective that promotes broader gate participation through feature-norm regularization, without pairwise feature comparisons. On ImageNet $256\\times256$ with SiT-B/2, our method improves FID over dispersive loss while increasing mean gate participation over timesteps and classes, further supporting the link between gate organization and training acceleration. Our results identify residual-gate reorganization as a measurable axis of diffusion training acceleration and suggest a simple direction for further acceleration.

**comment/C1：** Code: https://github.com/naver-ai/radialobj

</details>

<a id="paper-40"></a>

### 40. [The First Few Tokens Matter: Understanding Chain-of-Thought Controllability](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40939v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40939

**作者：** Hugh Van Deventer、Dmitrii Troitskii、Laura Ruis

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40939v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40939v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文探究 CoT 可控性受限的来源，将其归因于 thinking tag 之后最初几个 token 的熵坍缩，并用初始 token 的 prefilling 与禁用高频开头 token 配合 in-context 示例两种推理期干预来恢复可控性，同时区分 addition/style 与 suppression 两类控制。

**相关性简评：** 其「对开头 token 做干预以检验后续推理内容被锁定程度」的探测手段，可能可供迁移到 latent 推理，用于核查初始 latent 状态对后续轨迹的约束，以及可控性与可监控性的关系是否在 latent 状态上依然成立；纯文本设定，无视觉证据处理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The First Few Tokens Matter: Understanding Chain-of-Thought Controllability

**abstract/A1：** Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is a promising way to oversee increasingly capable agents. It succeeds in part because current models appear limited in their ability to control what they verbalize in their reasoning traces. We investigate the source of this limitation and link it to an entropy collapse in the first few tokens following the opening thinking tag. We exploit this mechanism to recover CoT controllability through two inference-time interventions on initial tokens: prefilling, where even a single token can substantially increase controllability, and banning frequent opening tokens combined with in-context examples, which improves average compliance by 34-41 percentage points across models. These interventions recover mainly addition and style controllability (adding or transforming information), while suppression controllability (avoiding or removing information) exhibits more varied responses, suggesting distinct underlying mechanisms. Consistent with this, the two groups behave as separate capabilities: across models, performance correlates positively within these groups, while cross-group correlations are mostly negative. Importantly, we find that the relationship with monitorability depends on the evaluation: addition and style controllability more consistently positively correlate with monitorability, while suppression often shows negative associations. Our findings motivate the development of controllability evaluations that distinguish difficulty initiating a specific opening from general limitations of the ability to alter reasoning content, alongside separate reporting of control tasks and categories.

**comment/C1：** 20 pages, 15 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-41"></a>

### 41. [Kernel-based Interpolatory Methods for Context Compression of Attention Mechanisms](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40943v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40943

**作者：** Michel Fabrice Serret、Laura Grigori

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL、math.NA

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40943v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40943v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文将 attention 的上下文压缩表述为其核对应的 Hilbert 空间中的线性映射插值问题，提出基于 GEIM 与 Q-DEIM 的两种 KV-cache 压缩方法 GAIM 与 Q-DAIM，并用 Taylor 展开、Performer、Random Fourier Features 等代理模型把压缩代价从二次降为线性，进一步扩展到 streaming attention。

**相关性简评：** 插值节点选择与残差收敛分析可能为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选、KV 压缩后的信息集中位置判断提供可核查的方法线索，但论文未涉及多模态推理或 latent 状态生成，迁移性需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Kernel-based Interpolatory Methods for Context Compression of Attention Mechanisms

**abstract/A1：** The bottleneck of attention mechanisms, which are the fundamental building block of modern language models, is their quadratic complexity in terms of context size. There has thus been an increasing interest in the compression of the context, also called KV-cache compression. In this article, we consider the problem of compressing the context of attention mechanisms by first formulating it in the Hilbert space associated with its kernel. In this formalism, attention can be represented as the ratio of two linear maps whose interpolant is also the ratio of interpolants, this allows us to consider the interpolation of the linear maps directly. We thus introduce two novel KV-cache compression methods which we call GAIM and Q-DAIM, based on the Generalized Empirical Interpolation Method (GEIM) and the Q-Discrete Empirical Interpolation Method (Q-DEIM), respectively. As the cost of these methods is quadratic in terms of context length, we propose surrogate models based on the Taylor series, the Performer and the Random Fourier Features method which allow the compression to be done in linear time. Finally, we extend this result to the streaming attention setting, providing new methods which allow one to penalize the selection of interpolation nodes based on the previously interpolated context and to update the previous interpolation based on new information. We then provide numerical results on the convergence of the residuals and the output error in future queries and preliminary results on the LongBench-E benchmark showing performance comparable to the state-of-the-art methods. These experiments suggest that the range revealed by our Q-DAIM method could help identify where information is concentrated in the KV cache, thus enabling an efficient compression.

**comment/C1：** 44 pages, 17 figures, 8 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-42"></a>

### 42. [On the Origin and Evolution of Training-Inference Mismatch in LLM Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40945v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40945

**作者：** Yu Li、Yufa Zhou、Tian Lan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40945v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40945v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把 LLM 强化学习中训练器与推理引擎的不匹配拆成引擎数值偏差与异步采样带来的策略滞后，分析两者不同的偏差传播方式，并据此提出数值居中、滞后比率裁剪与按有效样本量重同步的 DLC 修正。

**相关性简评：** 其将误差区分为近似恒定的数值偏差与经更新反馈放大的滞后、并以有效样本量阈值触发重同步，可能为 latent 轨迹训练中陈旧状态或中间内容的校正与贡献分配提供核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** On the Origin and Evolution of Training-Inference Mismatch in LLM Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** On-policy reinforcement learning for large language models assumes that training responses are sampled from the current policy. In practice, an inference engine samples the responses and a separate trainer computes the gradients, and the \\textbf{training-inference mismatch} between the two makes the training data off-policy. The mismatch has two sources, the numerics of the engine and the stale weights of asynchronous sampling, and a correction that stabilizes training under one source can fail under the other. \\textit{We ask which property of a mismatch source decides the correction it needs.} Evaluating the trainer at the sampler's weights splits each sampled-token error exactly into a \\textit{numerical discrepancy} of the engine and a \\textit{policy lag} of the trainer, and we follow both through training. The numerical discrepancy stays approximately constant, so it biases the updates by a nearly fixed amount, and centering the score at each prefix removes its leading bias. The policy lag is produced by the trainer's own updates: the bias it leaves enters the next update, which enlarges the next lag and closes a feedback loop. Importance weighting cuts this loop only while the lag ratio stays concentrated, and on Qwen3-1.7B it fails at the step where the effective sample size of the ratio falls below one half. From this account we derive the \\textbf{discrepancy-lag correction} (DLC), which centers the numerical discrepancy, clips the lag ratio against the sampler's weights and resynchronizes the sampler when this effective sample size falls below one half. Under a stale sampler, DLC keeps the KL divergence from on-policy training within twice the KL between two independent on-policy trainings on Qwen3-0.6B and Qwen3-1.7B, and its resynchronization prevents collapses that the per-token corrections leave and that a KL measured on the sampler's prefixes does not detect.

</details>

<a id="paper-43"></a>

### 43. [UFMF: Radar-Guided Kinematic Priors for Real-Time 4D Occupancy Forecasting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40947v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40947

**作者：** Zeping Zhang、Zhuoya Zhao、Robert Laganière

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40947v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40947v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出实时 4D 占据预测器 UFMF：以无注意力的对角状态空间时序核心输运由雷达引导速度先验驱动的压缩动态 latent 子空间，并附加世界坐标系动态监督与类别条件垂直剖面监督。

**相关性简评：** 其循环 latent 状态的输运机制与跨时间步辅助监督，可能为 LVR 中推理状态如何更新、如何为中间状态提供监督提供可核查线索，但属驾驶感知预测域，迁移性尚待验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** UFMF: Radar-Guided Kinematic Priors for Real-Time 4D Occupancy Forecasting

**abstract/A1：** Real-time 4D occupancy forecasting requires reliably extrapolating both ego motion and surrounding actor motion under strict latency budgets. Recent occupancy world models improve long-horizon fidelity through generative decoders and 3D volumetric modeling, yet their computational cost makes real-time deployment challenging. Light-weight BEV forecasters meet latency requirements but often blur or suppress fast-moving actors at longer horizons. We present UFMF (Ultra-Fast Multi-modal Forecaster), a real-time forecaster that keeps an attention-free diagonal state-space temporal core while injecting targeted kinematic and geometric structure where lightweight models typically fail. A radar-guided velocity-aware dynamic prior (VADP) decomposes future displacement into a linear extrapolation plus a learned horizon-dependent residual. The resulting per-cell flow fields transport a compact dynamic latent subspace during rollout. Two auxiliary training constraints complement this branch: world-frame dynamic supervision enforces inter-horizon trajectory coherence, and class-conditioned vertical-profile supervision regularizes BEV-to-3D lifting. UFMF operates at 122 FPS on a single RTX 4090 and reaches 0.45 fast-displacement mIoU on nuScenes, outperforming recent occupancy world models at a fraction of their computational cost.

</details>

<a id="paper-44"></a>

### 44. [Can Personal Requirements Become Supervision for Planning?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40992v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40992

**作者：** Junwei Wu、Runze Yan、Yuzhang Xie、Hanqi Luo、Ziwei Dong、Jiaying Lu、Xiao Hu、Carl Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40992v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40992v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建基于 NHANES 的膳食师认可周计划评测，提出 Noura：把公开临床指南与个人健康档案实例化为可执行规范，在规划中用于食物落地、份量优化、整周计划检查与修订，并将被拒计划及按上下文归因的修订转为 SFT/偏好/奖励监督。

**相关性简评：** 属非视觉的规范约束规划任务，但其「用可执行规范判定失败、并从从未完全成功的轨迹中回收监督目标」的信用分配思路，可供核查 LVR 的轨迹训练与稀疏成功信号监督问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Can Personal Requirements Become Supervision for Planning?

**abstract/A1：** Managing multiple chronic conditions through diet requires satisfying disease- and medication-specific requirements jointly across meals and days while preserving established eating habits. These requirements are public in clinical guidelines and drug labels, yet turning them into a feasible plan for one person remains difficult for language-model agents. In our dietitian-approved weekly planning evaluation built from NHANES, thirteen models, from medically fine-tuned 8B models to GPT-6.1 Sol, pass fewer than 3% of complete plans through direct generation. Explicit rules, higher reasoning effort, or model-directed tools leave GPT-6.1 Sol at no more than 8%. We present Noura, which pairs LLM planning with executable personal specifications instantiated from public clinical guidance and individual health and dietary profiles. During planning, the specification grounds foods, optimizes portions, checks the complete multi-day plan, and guides revision. Our key insight is that a specification that rejects a plan can also determine what to learn from it. Noura turns executed plans and contextually credited revisions into supervision for compact models, recovering targets even from trajectories that never achieve complete success. The same specification supplies supervised targets, preferences, and rewards for post-training. On 4,993 held-out participants, Noura-SFT raises Qwen3-14B's complete-plan pass rate from 24.0% to 72.5% within the same execution loop, cuts rule violations by 97.5% with no observed critical failures, and improves dietary quality. The locally hosted 14B planner incurs fewer rule violations than its GPT-6 Luna teacher. A matched comparison shows that executed targets reduce rule violations by 41% over model-written proposals. Across four Qwen, Phi, and Llama backbones, self-training alone reduces rule violations by 41-61% on a 1,249-person population subset.

</details>

<a id="paper-45"></a>

### 45. [Steering Vectors as a Training Signal in LLM Post-Training](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40998v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40998

**作者：** Tiejin Chen、Maunil Vyas、Huaiyuan Yao、Hua Wei

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40998v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40998v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究能否把 steering vectors 从推理期的行为控制手段转为训练信号：在 SFT/蒸馏中用 hidden-state hook 注入方向，在 GRPO 中对错误轨迹做 advantage repair，并分析方向、注入位置与组合操作三个设计轴及失效边界。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本方法，但「在隐藏状态上定向注入并用于参数更新/优势修补」可能为 LVR 的 latent 状态监督、错误轨迹贡献分配与训练信号设计提供可供核查的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Steering Vectors as a Training Signal in LLM Post-Training

**abstract/A1：** Steering vectors (SVs) are residual-stream directions that shift the behavior of LLMs when added to hidden states at inference time. While prior work has mainly used SVs as post-hoc controls, we ask whether the same directions can also guide parameter updates during post-training. We study this question across supervised learning, including SFT and distillation, and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). Our framework considers two intervention regimes: a hidden-state hook during teacher-forced supervised training, and an advantage-repair mechanism on wrong rollouts in GRPO. For each regime, we analyze three design axes, which are the steering direction, its placement, and the operation used to combine it with training. In supervised settings, SV injection improves over plain SFT and distillation, with gains scaling with the off-policy gap and reaching up to a $12\\%$ improvement when distilling from thinking-mode teachers. These gains hold across three random seeds. In RL settings, applying a correctness-contrast direction as advantage repair on wrong rollouts, including otherwise zero-gradient all-wrong groups, recovers a learning signal that transfers to held-out math benchmarks and acts as a composable auxiliary signal that adds to existing zero-variance remedies such as entropy-aware advantage shaping. We further identify three failure boundaries. In detail, code generation can break the direction, cross-family layer transfer can break the placement, and inference-time application on an SV-trained checkpoint can break the intervention regime. Together, these results establish steering vectors as a conditional but effective training signal for LLM post-training. Our code is available here.

**comment/C1：** Accept to NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-46"></a>

### 46. [MOSAIC: Addressing Disproportionate Task-Modality Dependencies via Modality-Partitioned Gradient Routing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41004v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41004

**作者：** Aryan Chowdhury、Yizheng Sun、Thomas Carroll、Riza Batista-Navarro

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41004v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41004v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对多任务多模态中某些任务过度依赖特定模态的问题，提出 MOSAIC：保留各模态独立表示并用受控跨模态 cross-attention 交互，同时以模态特定损失将梯度路由到对应分区；在日语会话语料四任务上 macro-F1 提升 7.4 点，主干参数减少 72%。

**相关性简评：** 其「保留单模态表示＋模态特定损失定向路由梯度」的做法，可供核查能否用于抑制 LVR 中的视觉旁路与信息坍缩、让视觉证据在内部状态中按任务需要保留，但原文并非推理期 latent 生成研究，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MOSAIC: Addressing Disproportionate Task-Modality Dependencies via Modality-Partitioned Gradient Routing

**abstract/A1：** In multi-task settings, combining multiple modalities allows task representations to draw on signals that a single modality alone would not capture. However, individual tasks may depend disproportionately on particular modalities, a structure that conventional fusion-based architectures do not model explicitly, instead enforcing a unified representation space. Consequently, a task's gradients can affect parameters tied to modalities it barely relies on, leaving features that compromise between competing demands. Although prior work has explored task-weighting and loss-balancing strategies, their effectiveness is limited by the absence of explicit modality separation, preventing models from optimising each modality according to its task-specific importance. To address these limitations, we propose MOSAIC (MOdality-Separated Architecture with Inter-modal Cross-attention), which preserves unimodal representations while enabling controlled interaction between them, and uses modality-specific losses to route gradients to their corresponding partitions. On the Corpus of Everyday Japanese Conversation (CEJC), MOSAIC improves average macro-F1 by 7.4 points across four tasks with a 72% reduction in backbone parameters relative to a fusion-based baseline. Our results demonstrate that modality partitioning yields consistent gains across tasks while requiring far fewer backbone parameters than a fusion-based design.

**comment/C1：** 9 Pages, 1 Figure, 3 Tables

</details>

<a id="paper-47"></a>

### 47. [Do Language Models Know What Compelling Scientific Evidence Looks Like?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41015v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41015

**作者：** Conor Igoe、Gabriel Grand、Zane Koch、Albert Bou、Eli Moss、Michaela M. Hinks、Samuel G. Rodriques、Andrew White、Siddharth Narayanan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41015v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41015v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 构建 EDB-Bio 基准，用 96k 条生物学真实实验结果序列要求模型预测目标假设成立的概率，发现开权重与前沿模型普遍过度保守，并用 strictly proper scoring rule 做后训练，显著改善校准与区分度；还用 Bayes factor 量化证据权重、追踪信念更新，并在序贯实验选择任务中检验效率。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本科学证据场景，但其中「以 Bayes factor 追踪信念更新」「按 proper scoring rule 训练校准」「序贯选择证据以提升效率」三条线索，可能可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据加权、置信校准与 STOP／试探决策的设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Do Language Models Know What Compelling Scientific Evidence Looks Like?

**abstract/A1：** Expert scientists excel at weighing empirical findings from multiple sources that vary in both statistical reliability and epistemic relevance. To what extent are language models, which are increasingly driving autonomous discovery systems, capable of such evidence deliberation? We introduce EDB-Bio, a scientific reasoning benchmark curated from real-world data in three biological domains: drug-perturbation transcriptomics, CRISPR screens, and human genome-wide association studies (GWAS). Each of EDB-Bio's 96k episodes presents a sequence of experimental results from published studies and asks the model to predict the probability that a target hypothesis is true. Out-of-the-box, open-weight and frontier models alike demonstrate systematic underconfidence and attain Brier scores comparable to a trivial base-rate forecaster. Post-training against strictly proper scoring rules significantly improves both calibration and discrimination. This calibration pays off downstream: in a sequential experiment selection task, our Ibis0 model is 2.5 times more efficient than the best frontier model. Throughout, quantifying evidence weights as Bayes factors reveals how models update their beliefs over time, whether they agree with expert biologists on relevance, and where they exhibit inconsistencies and biases. Our work extends calibration training and uncertainty analysis to a realistic scientific forecasting setting with applications to human cancer biology. We release all code, data, and trained models.

</details>

<a id="paper-48"></a>

### 48. [ReplayRCA: Measuring Post-hoc Diagnosis and Live Oversight of Agent Failures by Counterfactual Replay](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41025v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41025

**作者：** Jeremy Canale、Fabien Soulis

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41025v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41025v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ReplayRCA 基准，通过反事实回放（恢复所选步骤前状态、用诊断模型给出的动作替换原动作后由同一 agent 继续）并以安慰剂对照扣减，量化失败诊断的因果修复分数，区分事后复盘与在线监督两种设定。

**相关性简评：** 其干预式评分加安慰剂对照可供核查如何为中间推理步骤或 latent 状态做贡献分配，事后诊断与在线监督的落差也可能类比 THINK/STOP 的评价；但任务为文本后台 agent，视觉仅出现在最高难度级，迁移线索需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ReplayRCA: Measuring Post-hoc Diagnosis and Live Oversight of Agent Failures by Counterfactual Replay

**abstract/A1：** When a language-model agent fails a multi-step task, can another model find the decisive mistake and fix it with a single action? We introduce ReplayRCA, a benchmark that scores such diagnoses by intervention rather than by agreement with annotations: the environment is restored to the state preceding the chosen step, the proposed action replaces the agent's own, and the same agent continues. The score, causal repair, is the continuation success rate minus that of a paired placebo that replays the agent's original action through the same mechanism. Two tracks share this protocol: a post-hoc autopsy, where the diagnoser sees the whole failed run and is told that it failed, and live oversight, where a supervisor watches the run unfold and may intervene once, before an irreversible action, without knowing whether the run will fail. Tasks come from OpsWorld, a deterministic, procedurally generated back-office environment with six difficulty levels, the last of which is multimodal (QR codes, handwriting). On 62 calibrated systematic failures, the best of 13 frontier models (Grok 4.7) reaches a causal repair of +51.3 points; the top eight models are statistically indistinguishable, the flagship models of the two largest vendors rank 8th and 12th, and 5 items are repaired by no model. Forcing a strong model to intervene at the last step costs it 17.1 points, so the last-step shortcut of annotation-based attribution benchmarks does not transfer. On 87 runs, the two best live supervisors are statistically tied and one of them is a small open-weight model (DeepSeek V4.1 Flash); the supervisor that is among the best post-hoc diagnosers (GPT-6.1 Sol) is the weakest live one (-14.5 points on the same runs, p = 0.002), so post-hoc diagnostic performance can be a poor proxy for live oversight. The environment and the evaluation code are released at https://github.com/jeremy1392/RCAreplay.

**comment/C1：** 29 pages, 10 figures, 16 tables. Code: https://github.com/jeremy1392/RCAreplay

</details>

<a id="paper-49"></a>

### 49. [WebOverlay: High-Throughput Vision-Only Distillation for Extreme Aspect-Ratio Web Overlay Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41037v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41037

**作者：** Jiannan Huang、Jerry Ding、Li Yu、Zhenting Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.IR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41037v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41037v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 WebOverlay：以强 VLM 为教师蒸馏一个纯视觉检测器来识别网页遮挡层，用保长宽比的动态切块加边界仲裁处理极端纵横比整页渲染，从而避免 DOM 解析并大幅提升吞吐。

**相关性简评：** 其保长宽比切块与边界裁决可供核查「极端分辨率下视觉细节如何保留」这一表示问题，纯视觉旁路加教师蒸馏的监督方式也可能对 latent 视觉证据筛选与模态旁路有借鉴，但属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** WebOverlay: High-Throughput Vision-Only Distillation for Extreme Aspect-Ratio Web Overlay Detection

**abstract/A1：** Web overlays, such as cookie consent banners, newsletter prompts, and age gates, obstruct automated web crawlers and degrade downstream document representations. While proprietary multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) can accurately identify overlays, their latency and inference cost preclude web-scale ingestion. Conversely, lightweight vision models struggle with full-page renderings characterized by extreme vertical aspect ratios, where standard isotropic resizing collapses critical text features and causes false positives. In this work, we present WebOverlay, a high-throughput vision-only detector trained via distillation from a frontier vision-language teacher. We establish a dynamic tiling strategy that partitions extreme aspect-ratio web renders into aspect-preserving patches with spatial overlap, coupled with an aspect-ratio-aware boundary adjudication mechanism to eliminate boundary artifacts. To eliminate cross-modal latency overheads, our student model relies purely on visual cues, bypassing DOM tree parsing. WebOverlay significantly outperforms DOM-based heuristics and achieves competitive precision and recall against heavy proprietary MLLM baselines while delivering multiple orders of magnitude higher throughput and substantially reduced deployment costs.

**comment/C1：** 9 pages, 2 figures, 2 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-50"></a>

### 50. [Characterizing Recoverable Expert-Utilization Signatures in Fine-Tuned Mixture-of-Experts LLMs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41039v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41039

**作者：** Fei Wang、Baochun Li、Bo Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41039v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41039v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究微调 MoE 大模型的「结构记忆」漏洞：模型会记住处理训练样本所用的专家路径并形成路由指纹，作者仅凭路由与输出 logits 即可从无条件生成中恢复这些路径，并提出结合参考模型尾部似然与复杂度归一化的检测指标。

**相关性简评：** 其把路由路径当作可恢复的内部状态并设计检测与归因指标，可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态的「内容与路径」解耦、内部状态可解码性及贡献分配评价；与视觉推理本身无直接关系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Characterizing Recoverable Expert-Utilization Signatures in Fine-Tuned Mixture-of-Experts LLMs

**abstract/A1：** As Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) large language models are increasingly deployed in distributed environments, from federated learning to edge-cloud systems, their architecture harbors an underexplored landscape of security vulnerabilities. Moving beyond textual analysis, we uncover structural memorization: a novel vulnerability where a fine-tuned model memorizes not only its training data but also the internal pathway used to process it, creating a characteristic routing fingerprint unique to the training process. We provide comprehensive empirical evidence that structural memorization emerges consistently even from a few samples, and that its intensity is directly influenced by the choice of trainable modules. We show that these memorized expert pathways can be stealthily recovered through the model's unconditional generation, using only routing and output logits, with no knowledge of the fine-tuning data or procedure. In distributed deployments, this vulnerability is amplified as routing information is exposed at multiple trust boundaries, including federated aggregation points, edge-cloud interfaces, and multi-tenant monitoring systems. Recovery exposes a critical blind spot: traditional membership inference metrics, viewed through the lens of expert routing, fail to identify memorized data corresponding to these structural pathways. To close this gap, we devise composite metrics combining reference model tail likelihood with complexity-aware normalization, significantly improving detection of memorized routing structures. This enables adversaries to pinpoint critical experts shaped by fine-tuning, whether dominant or dormant, and execute targeted attacks such as router manipulation or backdoor injection in realistic distributed settings where attackers lack training data but can observe routing patterns across system components.

</details>

<a id="paper-51"></a>

### 51. [Capability-Grounded Agentic Planning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41040v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41040

**作者：** Shikha Shrestha、William Yeoh、Ning Zhang、Yevgeniy Vorobeychik

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41040v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41040v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 将工具目录确定性编译为抽象 PDDL 模型，由 LLM 把请求转成目标、经典规划器求解，执行期再由 LLM 补充实体级调用，并用确定性检查与 LLM 验证器判定结果是否交付，在多个工具调用基准上优于标准工具调用 agent。

**相关性简评：** 其「只跟踪输入是否可用、不跟踪取值」的抽象状态，以及执行期校验与结果交付判定，可能为 LVR 的潜状态更新、证据可用性跟踪与停止决策提供可核查线索，但论文本身不涉及视觉或多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Capability-Grounded Agentic Planning

**abstract/A1：** LLM agents serve natural-language requests through tools, data sources, and other agents, and even simple requests often need several interdependent calls. Typically, the LLM picks these calls by trial and error, with no guarantee of an executable plan. We present Capability-Grounded Agentic Planning (CGAP), which instead reasons about what a request requires and what tools provide. CGAP compiles the tool catalog deterministically into a PDDL model, uses an LLM to turn the request into goals, and solves the model with a classical planner. Since tool outputs are unknown in advance, the model is abstract, tracking which inputs are available, not their values. During execution, an LLM adds entity-specific calls that abstract plans omit, and deterministic checks reject any that break the plan. Every argument is drawn from a cited source, such as a conversation span or an earlier tool response, and each requested result counts as delivered only after an LLM verifier checks it against the request. With three LLMs on ToolSandbox, ComplexFuncBench, BFCL multi-turn, and $τ^2$-bench retail, CGAP outperforms a standard tool-calling agent by up to 28.9 points.

**comment/C1：** 25 pages, 3 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-52"></a>

### 52. [Before We Adapt: The Zero-Shot Generalization Bottleneck in Continual World Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41041v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41041

**作者：** Amir Mesbah、Di Wu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41041v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41041v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究持续强化学习中的世界模型零样本复用瓶颈：将上下文分解为行为与物理成分，在FourRooms中用Dyna-Q和TD-MPC检验物理上下文变化下的直接复用，并通过消融与潜空间诊断分析观测表示的影响。

**相关性简评：** 其「学习表示不会自动具备复用所需不变性」的潜空间诊断，可供核查 LVR 中视觉细节保留与任务必要信息筛选的评价思路，但该工作属强化学习世界模型，迁移线索需谨慎验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Before We Adapt: The Zero-Shot Generalization Bottleneck in Continual World Models

**abstract/A1：** Continual reinforcement learning (CRL) requires agents to operate under environmental changes while reusing prior knowledge whenever possible. We argue that model-based reinforcement learning provides a promising framework for this setting because learned world models (WMs) can, in principle, support change detection, knowledge reuse, adaptation, and acquisition of new knowledge. However, such benefits depend heavily on whether the learned model can generalize beyond the contexts in which it was trained. In this work, we propose to formulate this problem with a contextual Markov decision process in which context is decomposed into behavioral and physical components. This view organizes continual WM learning into reuse, adaptation, and acquisition of new knowledge. As a first step, we study direct reuse when behavioral rules remain unchanged. Using Dyna-Q and TD-MPC in controlled FourRooms tasks, we find that zero-shot reuse can degrade substantially under physical-context changes and that this behavior depends strongly on the observation representation used by the WM. Additional ablation studies and latent-space diagnostics suggest that learned representations do not automatically provide the invariances needed for reuse. These results identify representation generalization as an important bottleneck for reliable world-model reuse in CRL.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to Continual World Models workshop @ NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-53"></a>

### 53. [Localizing predictive concepts encoded in neural representations via hierarchical testing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41046v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41046

**作者：** Viktoriia Huryn、Marco Simnacher、Jim Berend、Wojciech Samek、Christoph Lippert、Sonja Greven、Uwe Ohler

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、q-bio.GN

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41046v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41046v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CUTTIE：把每层单元聚类成树，自上而下做条件独立性检验，在控制族错误率下定位携带目标信息的单元簇，再用条件归因图揭示其编码的 motif；在模拟数据和染色质可及性预测的卷积模型上演示。

**相关性简评：** 其分层条件独立性检验与多重比较错误控制可能可供核查，用于判断 LVR 中哪些内部状态单元真正携带任务必要信息（而非仅相关），提供了表示归因与信息定位的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Localizing predictive concepts encoded in neural representations via hierarchical testing

**abstract/A1：** Understanding which regulatory sequence motifs a genomic deep learning model has learned, with statistical error guarantees, is essential for trusting its use in downstream tasks such as variant effect prediction. Existing approaches to identifying which units in a trained network encode human- understandable concepts fall into two groups. Exploratory methods rank and visualize units but offer no statistical guarantee that the selected units are associated with the prediction target across the population of inputs. Testing-based methods test only single units or one pre-defined set of them, and do not control the error across the many sets of units. We introduce CUTTIE (Cluster-tree Unit Top-down Testing of Independence with Error control), which clusters the units of each layer into a tree and tests the clusters top-down with conditional independence tests, localizing information about the target while controlling the family-wise error rate. Thereby, we identify clusters that carry information about the target beyond the rest of the layer. For the units in the identified clusters, conditional attribution maps then reveal what motifs they encode. In a controlled simulation, the procedure identifies the clusters whose concepts affect the target. We apply the procedure to a convolutional model predicting cell-type-specific chromatin accessibility from DNA sequence, where the concepts encoded in the identified clusters map onto known regulatory motifs.

</details>

<a id="paper-54"></a>

### 54. [Single-Annotation, Multi-Hypothesis Segmentation with Semantic-Spatial Residual Banks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41069v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41069

**作者：** Armin Dadras、Robert Sablatnig、Franziska Proksa、Markus Seidl

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41069v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41069v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究单标注下的多假设分割：每张训练图仅有一个掩码，模型需输出有限假设库以覆盖测试时其他标注者的掩码，并提出冻结语义库加图像残差的 SSRB。

**相关性简评：** 其「冻结语义结构＋空间残差」的多假设表示与覆盖率评价，可供核查 LVR 中多种 latent 假设的多样性、结构信息保持及视觉细节补充。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Single-Annotation, Multi-Hypothesis Segmentation with Semantic-Spatial Residual Banks

**abstract/A1：** Multi-rater segmentation datasets show that a single image can admit several valid masks, yet most training sets expose only one annotation per image. We study single-annotation multi-hypothesis segmentation: each training image exposes one fixed mask, while the model must return a finite bank that covers held-out human annotations at test time. Our experiments reveal a representation tradeoff. Banks learned directly from images recover variations in local boundaries and extent but lose structural hypotheses, whereas frozen semantic features preserve structural grouping but underrepresent spatial variation. We introduce the Semantic-Spatial Residual Bank (SSRB), which freezes a semantic bank trained with balanced assignments and learns a residual from the image for each hypothesis. Across five medical endpoints, SSRB improves coverage of held-out masks at $K=8$ over a Probabilistic U-Net under the same one-mask constraint. On LIDC and LIDC-hard, it also produces substantially more distinct useful masks. On BSDS, it preserves the semantic bank's performance in grouping regions. Its advantage grows as more masks are returned, showing that residuals learned for corresponding heads improve coverage of local annotation variation while preserving how regions are grouped.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at WACV 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-55"></a>

### 55. [bf16 Rounding Silently Discards Most RL Policy Updates: A Hidden Training-Inference Mismatch and an Unbiased One-Line Fix](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41073v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41073

**作者：** Andrew Gao、William Gao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41073v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41073v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文指出 bf16 导出把大多数小于半个 bf16 间距的 RL 权重更新静默丢弃，使采样策略滞后优化器 50–150 步；提出 Fair Cast，在导出时改用随机舍入或误差反馈并在服务副本上算损失，在 Qwen3-0.6B–4B 上取得平均 6.1 点提升。

**相关性简评：** 其「低精度转换丢弃微小状态更新、训练副本与推理副本隐式不一致」的度量与无偏舍入/误差反馈思路，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态、KV 缓存与深度/位置相似度实验是否被数值舍入掩盖了真实更新。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** bf16 Rounding Silently Discards Most RL Policy Updates: A Hidden Training-Inference Mismatch and an Unbiased One-Line Fix

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training of language models keeps fp32 master weights in the optimizer and serves rollouts from a bf16 copy produced by round-to-nearest conversion. At the learning rates in common use, most per-step updates are smaller than half a bf16 spacing and the conversion discards them: on Qwen3-1.7B only 0.4-0.5% of served weights change per step, and the policy that generates the training data lags the optimizer by 50-150 steps. Existing mismatch corrections cannot see this lag because they compare two copies downstream of the same rounding. We propose Fair Cast: export the master with an unbiased rounding rule (stochastic rounding or error feedback) and compute the training loss on the served copy. The change is one line at the export call, adds no hyperparameters and costs 22-38 ms per export of 1.7B parameters. Across 25 matched seed-pairs on Qwen3-0.6B to 4B with AdamW and Muon, Fair Cast is ahead on 18, tied on 6 and behind on 1, with a mean gain of 6.1 points; on zebra puzzles it reaches 0.57 +/- 0.16 over four runs where round-to-nearest collapses to 0.33 +/- 0.10. It also makes the saved fp32 checkpoint agree with the served model, and it exposes the implicit lag of round-to-nearest as a regularizer that can be restored explicitly when a task needs it.

</details>

<a id="paper-56"></a>

### 56. [Grounding Image Composition with Label-Conditioned Visual Evidence](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41080v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41080

**作者：** Armin Dadras、Tingyu Lin、Robert Sablatnig、Franziska Proksa、Markus Seidl

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41080v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41080v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 构建 KU-PCPE 基准：给定图像与已判定适用的构图标签，要求分割出支持该标签的可见区域，含 4,093 个单标注训练掩码与 1,327 个专家测试任务，并评估 VLM 作为候选区域选择器的表现。

**相关性简评：** 其「识别标签易、定位支持标签的视觉证据难」的落差及区域与语义引导线孰更有效的标注，可能可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与答案是否真正依赖具体证据的评测设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Grounding Image Composition with Label-Conditioned Visual Evidence

**abstract/A1：** Image composition labels such as center, diagonal, or rule of thirds describe visual structure rather than object categories. KU-PCP provides image-level labels and class-specific geometric elements, but does not show which visible regions people associate with each of its nine classes. We introduce KU-PCP Elements (KU-PCPE), a label-conditioned region-evidence benchmark for photographic composition. Given an image and a label already judged applicable, the task is to segment the visible regions that explain why the label applies. KU-PCPE contains 4,093 single-annotator training masks and a 1,327-task expert test split with three masks per image-label task. The annotations also include exploratory segment-versus-line ratings that describe whether region or semantic-guideline evidence better explains the label. A held-out expert reaches .867 Dice against the other experts, while the strongest deployable model reaches .664, a proposal oracle .672, and the best vision-language model (VLM) proposal selector .395. KU-PCPE exposes the gap between recognizing a composition label and grounding the human-aligned visual evidence that makes it plausible in a particular image.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at WACV 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-57"></a>

### 57. [MUON-RT Reusing the Teacher's Trajectory for Momentum-Free Muon for LLM Self-Distillation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41104v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41104

**作者：** Aryan Das、Vinay Kumar Verma、Aakansha Mishra

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41104v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41104v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Muon-RT，一种 LLM 自蒸馏优化器：用 EMA 教师在训练中与学生权重的位移充当动量缓冲的替代，去除径向分量后做谱归一化并与当前梯度混合，在 Qwen2.5-7B、Olmo-3-7B 的自蒸馏设定下报告精度提升与显存下降。

**相关性简评：** 论文本身不涉及视觉或多模态推理，但「以师生位移累积的指数加权历史替代额外状态」这一做法，可供核查能否用于 latent 蒸馏训练中复用教师信号、降低状态开销；迁移可能性低，仅属方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MUON-RT Reusing the Teacher's Trajectory for Momentum-Free Muon for LLM Self-Distillation

**abstract/A1：** Self-distillation methods for large language models with an exponential moving average (EMA) teacher already retain a history of student updates. Muon nevertheless stores a separate momentum buffer, increasing persistent training memory. We propose Muon with Reused Teacher (Muon-RT), which reuses the teacher-student displacement to eliminate momentum buffers for hidden weight matrices. We show that this displacement encodes an exponentially weighted history of applied updates and smooths temporally uncorrelated noise under a local model. Muon-RT removes its radial component, normalizes it spectrally, and mixes it with the current gradient after Newton-Schulz (NS) orthogonalization. Under explicit regularity and alignment assumptions, we establish linear contraction of an expected objective-gap bound toward a residual error level. With Self-Distillation Fine-Tuning (SDFT) on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, Muon-RT improves average accuracy over AdamW by 2.6 points with 42% less persistent memory, and over momentum Muon by 5.4 points with 27% less persistent memory. With Self-Distillation Policy Optimization (SDPO) on Olmo-3-7B-Instruct, it improves average accuracy over AdamW by 2.0 points.

**comment/C1：** Under Submission

</details>

<a id="paper-58"></a>

### 58. [TalentRL: Strategy-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Hierarchical Skill Routing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41143v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41143

**作者：** Huancheng Chen、Xiaodi Sun、Zhaoqiong Huang、Michelle Chang、Jingwen Lu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41143v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41143v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 TalentRL：在 LLM agent 的程序性记忆上做分层路由，由粗粒度 talent 选择策略引导细粒度 skill 的相似度检索，并用同一任务下对 talent 条件上下文做匹配干预的 rollout 奖励对比来训练路由，下游执行则用 group-relative 优势优化，在多个 QA 与 ALFWorld/WebShop 上评测。

**相关性简评：** 任务结果混合了路由、检索上下文与执行三方面影响，其「匹配干预下的奖励对比」为隔离单一决策贡献提供了可核查的监督思路，可能迁移到 LVR 中 latent 内容对答案贡献的分配与训练；但该工作为纯文本 agent，无显式视觉状态或 latent 推理过程。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TalentRL: Strategy-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Hierarchical Skill Routing

**abstract/A1：** Natural-language skills enable large language model (LLM) agents to reuse procedures across tasks, yet most systems retrieve individual skills from a flat SkillBank without explicitly modeling the high-level strategy that governs their selection and use. We introduce TalentRL, a strategy-guided reinforcement learning framework for hierarchical routing over procedural memory. Coarse-grained talents represent recurring strategies for skill use, while fine-grained skills provide executable procedures. For each task, the policy model selects a talent that guides execution and softly prioritizes its associated skills during similarity-based retrieval. However, since task outcomes depend jointly on talent selection, retrieved skill context, and downstream execution, terminal rewards alone cannot isolate the contribution of the routing decision. TalentRL therefore trains talent routing with reward contrasts between rollouts of the same task under matched interventions on the talent-conditioned context, and optimizes downstream execution with group-relative advantages. Across seven open-domain and multi-hop question-answering benchmarks, talent-aware supervised fine-tuning improves average success rate by 7.53%, 6.71%, and 9.21% over the corresponding zero-shot baselines for Qwen2.5-7B, Qwen3-8B, and Llama-3.1-8B, respectively. Reinforcement learning further improves these SFT checkpoints by 7.58% to 9.32%, and TalentRL achieves the highest average success rate among all compared methods on all three backbones. On the agentic benchmarks ALFWorld and WebShop, TalentRL achieves the highest ALFWorld success rate (99.3%) and the highest WebShop score (90.7) among all compared methods.

</details>

<a id="paper-59"></a>

### 59. [Counterfactual Pairwise Augmentation Improves Small Pointwise LLM Judges](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41149v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41149

**作者：** Mohammad Zangooei、Peyvand Teymoori、Mohammad Robati Shirzad、Soroosh Shahtalebi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41149v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41149v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 CPA：为每个 pointwise 评分样本合成并审核一个同源、反事实最小的对立标签回答，构成 pairwise 任务，与 pointwise 数据联合微调小型评审模型，但仅以 pointwise 方式部署；并用二阶分析说明其优于分别训练后合并专家。

**相关性简评：** 其「构造反事实最小对以显式暴露决定标签的特征」可供核查如何设计对比监督来定位 LVR 中的关键视觉证据与贡献分配，但对象为纯文本判官、无 latent 状态，迁移性尚待判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Counterfactual Pairwise Augmentation Improves Small Pointwise LLM Judges

**abstract/A1：** Agentic systems increasingly gate individual steps with a pointwise judge that scores a response against a criterion. Small language models are attractive for this role because they can be served and specialized efficiently. Such a judge must locate the criterion's decision boundary, but pointwise labels under-determine that boundary in fine-tuning: labeled responses to different queries differ along many dimensions at once, so they do not reveal which features decide the label. We introduce CPA (Counterfactual Pairwise Augmentation), which makes the deciding difference explicit in training. For each pointwise item, CPA synthesizes and audits a counterfactual response to the same query intended to carry the opposite label. It frames the accepted pair as a pairwise task asking which of the two responses satisfies the criterion. The judge is fine-tuned jointly on both pointwise and pairwise datasets and deployed in pointwise mode only. A second-order analysis shows when this should outperform the prevailing strategy of merging separately trained pointwise and pairwise experts: merging moves every parameter direction toward the pairwise expert by the same fraction, whereas joint training can hold the pointwise optimum wherever the pairwise loss is flat and absorb its curvature where it is sharp. This is the regime that CPA's same-source, counterfactually minimal pairwise view is designed to produce. On separate Qwen3-8B LoRA adapters for six binary criteria, CPA improves criterion-averaged pointwise macro-F1 by 2.5 points over pointwise-only training and outperforms model merging on all six criteria at every tested merge weight. It also surpasses the strongest zero-shot frontier judge we evaluate, Gemini 3.6 Flash, by 11.8 points.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at Neurips'26 workshop of small language models for agentic systems

</details>

<a id="paper-60"></a>

### 60. [MATS: A Multi-Agent Framework for Controllable and Reflective Tumor Synthesis](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41159v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41159

**作者：** Zhongnan Shi、Keyang Zhou、Siyi Li、Qichao Ying、Xinchen Ma、Shuyi Gong、Wei Tang、Sheng Li、Zhenxing Qian

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41159v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41159v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 MATS，一个面向医学图像肿瘤合成的多智能体框架：先按细粒度属性刻画目标疾病形态分布并制定生成计划，再定位器官、用两阶段扩散分别生成肿瘤掩码与纹理，并在三个检查点做闭环属性对齐校验与定向反馈重生成。

**相关性简评：** 任务本身是图像增强而非视觉推理，但「多检查点属性对齐验证 + 定向反馈重生成」可供核查其对 LVR 中答案验证、回退重生成与停止决策的借鉴价值。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MATS: A Multi-Agent Framework for Controllable and Reflective Tumor Synthesis

**abstract/A1：** Synthesizing realistic tumors on medical images helps address data scarcity for clinical AI. Existing diffusion-based methods, however, lack fine-grained attribute-level controllability, closed-loop quality verification, and awareness of which morphological patterns are underrepresented in the target distribution. We present MATS, a multi-agent framework for text-controllable, cross-organ tumor synthesis. Given a target disease, MATS first profiles its morphological distribution over a fine-grained attribute space covering tumor geometry and appearance, and constructs a generation plan that amplifies underrepresented subtypes while keeping common ones dominant. It then localizes the target organ, proposes anatomically valid placement, and executes two-stage diffusion to produce the tumor mask and render texture, each conditioned on the corresponding attributes. A closed-loop reflective mechanism verifies attribute alignment at three checkpoints throughout synthesis and provides targeted feedback to guide re-generation when misalignment is detected. Trained exclusively on a single-source pancreatic neuroendocrine tumor dataset, MATS consistently improves downstream segmentation both in-domain and across LiTS, KiTS, and MSD-Pancreas benchmarks, whereas most prior generative augmentation methods fall below real-only training.

**comment/C1：** IEEE BIBM 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-61"></a>

### 61. [LLM Detoxification via Transition-Aware Reward-Guided Representation Sampling](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41165v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41165

**作者：** Aishan Liu、Yisong Xiao、Xianglong Liu、Yongxin Huang、Siyuan Liang、Zonghao Ying、Yang Qu、Jian Yang、Yaodong Yang、Dacheng Tao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41165v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41165v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 TARGRS 测试时去毒框架：沿毒性方向渐进引导构造从无毒到有毒的表示轨迹，用轨迹上的毒性分数训练 token 级奖励模型，推理时反向沿无毒方向做由粗到细的表示采样并取最小扰动解。

**相关性简评：** 其「在 latent 表示空间中用奖励模型引导由粗到细采样与扰动最小化」的做法，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的筛选、干预与验证提供可核查的方法线索，但任务本身为安全去毒而非视觉推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LLM Detoxification via Transition-Aware Reward-Guided Representation Sampling

**abstract/A1：** Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate toxic content, motivating effective test-time detoxification. Existing representation steering methods often rely on static or coarse interventions and provide imprecise guidance because they inadequately model transitions between toxic and non-toxic generations. We propose Transition-Aware Reward-Guided Representation Sampling (TARGRS), a test-time framework that learns reward guidance over toxicity transitions in latent representation space. During exploration, progressive steering along the toxicity direction constructs trajectories from non-toxic to toxic states. Toxicity scores along these trajectories turn sparse annotations into dense, listwise supervision for a transition-aware token-level reward model. During inference, TARGRS uses this model to guide coarse-to-fine sampling along the opposite, non-toxic direction: coarse sampling identifies the intervention needed to reach non-toxic regions, and refinement selects a detoxified representation with minimal perturbation. Experiments on 10 LLMs across RealToxicityPrompts, BOLD, and AttaQ show that TARGRS consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, reducing toxicity by up to 69.38% on RealToxicityPrompts. TARGRS runs nearly as fast as the original LLM while preserving its core capabilities with minimal impact. By intervening in the language decoder space, TARGRS also extends to Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs), reducing toxicity by up to 64.23% across 3 representative LVLMs.

</details>

<a id="paper-62"></a>

### 62. [Shifting Focus by Reliability: Geometric Fusion Modulation for Robust RGB-T Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41204v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41204

**作者：** Liuyin Shi、Chengqi Li、Wenbo He

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41204v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41204v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对 RGB-T 目标检测中的信号损坏问题，先用受控损坏扫描说明不同损坏类型在轻度时影响相近、重度时明显分化，据此提出 PG-FM：按各模态估计的损坏类型与严重度，用提示引导特征调制做逐输入自适应，在 FLIR、KAIST 上提升 mAP50 且无需重训即可迁移到自然采集数据集。

**相关性简评：** 其「按估计的信号质量逐输入调制并区分模态可靠性」的做法，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与可靠性加权的融合调制提供方法线索，但其为检测任务、不涉及 latent 推理状态，实际可迁移性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Shifting Focus by Reliability: Geometric Fusion Modulation for Robust RGB-T Detection

**abstract/A1：** RGB-thermal (RGB-T) object detectors combine visible and thermal imagery for robust detection, but signal corruption in either stream can substantially degrade accuracy. Restoration-based methods incur significant latency and do not consistently improve downstream accuracy, raising a more direct question: rather than restoring corrupted inputs, can we efficiently adapt the detector to their varying signal quality? Conventional PEFT offers an efficient route, but its input-independent corrections cannot track diverse test-time corruptions. Our controlled corruption sweeps show that corruption types have similar effects near clean conditions but diverge increasingly with severity, motivating corruption-aware, per-input adaptation. We therefore propose PG-FM (Prompt-Guided Feature Modulation), which adapts RGB-T object detectors to the estimated corruption type and severity of each modality. Under controlled RGB-T corruption simulations, PG-FM preserves clean-set performance, improves mAP50 by up to 20.8 points on FLIR and 19.9 points on KAIST, and transfers without retraining to unmodified, naturally acquired external RGB-T datasets.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to WACV 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-63"></a>

### 63. [Beyond Scarcity: When Encoder Adaptation Is Worthwhile](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41213v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41213

**作者：** Zexing Zhang、Jichao Li、Tianyang Lei、Yiyin Zhang、Kewei Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41213v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41213v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究长尾识别中分类器重平衡后编码器是否仍值得继续适配，提出受控评测框架，将「独特样本支撑量」与「表征分配」解耦，并用匹配的分类器-only 与全网络续训来估计编码器更新的增量价值。

**相关性简评：** 其「匹配续训 + 受控变量」的增量价值估计思路，可能可供核查 LVR 中收益究竟来自 latent 状态更新还是答案读出端，但从纯视觉分类任务迁移到多模态推理仍需较多改造验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Scarcity: When Encoder Adaptation Is Worthwhile

**abstract/A1：** Long-tailed recognition often learns a shared representation before correcting the classifier for class imbalance. Once the classifier has been rebalanced, however, final accuracy does not reveal whether further encoder adaptation remains worthwhile. Standard imbalance benchmarks obscure this question because they jointly vary the number of distinct examples available to rare classes and the frequency with which those examples shape the representation. We introduce a controlled evaluation framework that separates unique support from representation allocation and estimates the incremental value of encoder updates through matched classifier-only and full-network continuations from a common corrected model. The framework combines stage-specific controls, multiple representation and adaptation objectives, whole-distribution metrics, and hierarchical inference over class assignments, support reconstructions, and optimization runs. Across the controlled study, the same low-support regime moves from one in which classifier-only training is adequate to one in which encoder adaptation yields a practically meaningful benefit solely as representation allocation becomes more imbalanced. The transition persists across classifier corrections, representation objectives, normalization and augmentation controls, and ImageNet-scale evaluation, while preserving frequent-class accuracy. Under the prespecified one-percentage-point operating margin, the two regimes are separated by simultaneous confidence bounds. These results show that benchmark construction can alter a post-correction training decision and that support size alone does not characterize the remaining value of representation adaptation.

</details>

<a id="paper-64"></a>

### 64. [A Positional Realizability Theory of Length Generalization in Single-Head Attention](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41218v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41218

**作者：** Jiasong Han、Yunkai Chai、Jintao Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41218v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41218v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对单头仅位置注意力建立「可实性」理论，用模拟转移算子 T\_L 与标量 α\_L 解释绝对／相对位置编码在长度泛化上的分化，并给出深度行刻画、表达力界与实验验证。

**相关性简评：** 位置编码、深度与注意力结构对长度泛化的刻画，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的位置结构及支持组合外推提供核查线索；但其仅位置单通道设定明确排除内容依赖任务，迁移到视觉推理仍需较多验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Positional Realizability Theory of Length Generalization in Single-Head Attention

**abstract/A1：** Why does length generalization (LG) depend so sharply on the positional encoding (PE)? Relative schemes extrapolate fixed offsets, absolute schemes fix positions, and neither handles growing offsets. We give a realizability theory for a deliberately controlled abstraction, a single positional-only attention head, that derives this well-documented empirical pattern and says precisely where it holds and breaks. The device is a simulation transfer operator $T\_L$ that re-evaluates a selected length-$L\_0$-realizing positional kernel on the longer grid, with a scalar $α\_L \\in \[0,1\]$ read off before any training from $(Φ, t\_n)$ alone. Here $α\_L$ serves as the computable witness of that taxonomy's derivation, not as a stronger empirical predictor. Our results are a relative-head deep-row characterization for kernels with a strict unique global maximizer (Theorem 2), an absolute/relative duality that derives the pattern (Proposition 4), a learned-absolute underdetermination result, and a static expressivity bound; a sufficiency theorem (Theorem 1) bridges $α\_L$ to LG, and the general deterministic case is a precise open conjecture (correcting a tempting but invalid "outside-colspan" argument). Experiments confirm the structure: in the controlled single-head regime $α\_L$ exactly separates the taxonomy's generalizing and non-generalizing (PE, task) cells (2625 trained-model measurements; stable at vocab 64/d=128). Together, these results turn the received PE taxonomy into a positional-kernel transfer theorem, and identify the content and depth mechanisms that escape it. The theory concerns the positional channel alone: tasks whose targets depend on token content, including the standard length-generalization suite such as addition and sorting, fall outside Theorem 2.

**comment/C1：** 26 pages, 6 figures. Published in Transactions on Machine Learning Research (TMLR), October 2026. Code available at https://github.com/superajgklasjglaks/positional-realizability

</details>

<a id="paper-65"></a>

### 65. [Corroborative Learning: Mutual Enhancement through Task-Response Reconstruction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41222v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41222

**作者：** Zexing Zhang、Jichao Li、Tianyang Lei、Yiyin Zhang、Kewei Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41222v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41222v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Corroborative Learning：让协作模型从对方表示重建任务响应增量，以跨组可重建性施加方向性正则，并在多语言及低标签视觉任务上验证互补增强。

**相关性简评：** 其「按表示可重建性选择性约束方向」的做法可能为 LVR 的 latent 内容监督与贡献分配提供核查线索，但研究的是模型间协作训练，并未涉及视觉推理状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Corroborative Learning: Mutual Enhancement through Task-Response Reconstruction

**abstract/A1：** In pattern analysis, models trained independently for the same task often develop complementary capabilities through differences in architecture, data, and optimization. Exploiting complementarity while retaining independent deployment is central to collaborative learning. Most methods establish transfer by reducing differences in predictions, relations, or representations between target and collaborator, although these differences also underlie complementarity. This creates a structural trade-off between reducing differences for knowledge exchange and preserving them for complementarity. We therefore propose Corroborative Learning (CL). Each model forms task-driven responses, with cross-group support from its collaborator. Source-excluded reconstruction of response increments from collaborator representations separates fitting and evaluation across disjoint groups and selectively constrains directions by cross-group reconstructability. The process requires no direct output or feature matching or additional inference dependency. Furthermore, with linear reconstruction and fixed collaborator representations, the loss equals a positive semidefinite directional regularizer, constraining less reconstructable directions more strongly. Signal and noise conditions characterize when directional shrinkage reduces response-estimation error. Across six language tasks, 24 models, and 36 pairs, CL improves over capacity-matched independent adaptation on every task, with positive gains across most directed model combinations. Pairwise comparisons show broad advantages over transfer and mutual-learning methods and different gains from complementarity across collaboration rules. Low-label language and vision results support cross-domain applicability. Task-relevant differences can support mutual enhancement without eliminating disagreement.

</details>

<a id="paper-66"></a>

### 66. [Coarse3D: Controllable and Faithful Sparse Structure Generation for Image-to-3D](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41231v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41231

**作者：** Gaige Wang、Bowen Pan、Hao Ren、Jiangjing Lyu、Chengfei Lyu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41231v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41231v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 Coarse3D，用于从任意无位姿图像生成可控朝向且与输入图像结构一致的稀疏体素几何；通过首帧锚定规范空间、双塔 DiT 结合前馈重建辅助任务，以及像素到体素的对应关系引导 cross-attention 来实现。

**相关性简评：** 其以辅助前馈重建与像素—体素对应正则化 cross-attention，使生成特征保持空间与视觉依据，可供核查「latent 状态是否保留视觉细节」及用重建监督约束 grounding 的线索；但任务是 3D 生成、无推理过程，迁移性待判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Coarse3D: Controllable and Faithful Sparse Structure Generation for Image-to-3D

**abstract/A1：** Coarse-to-fine 3D-native generation relies heavily on the first-stage sparse structure, which determines the global orientation and overall geometry for subsequent refinement. However, existing coarse generators suffer from two key limitations: their orientation is difficult to control due to ambiguous semantic canonicalization, and their structures are often inconsistent with input images due to weak 2D-3D grounding. We propose Coarse3D, a sparse-structure generation model that produces orientation-controllable and structurally faithful voxel geometry from arbitrary unposed images. Coarse3D introduces three designs: a first-frame anchored canonical space that aligns generation with the first input view while preserving object-centric normalization; a dual-tower DiT that couples generation with auxiliary feed-forward reconstruction to learn spatially grounded features; and spatial-prior guided attention that regularizes cross-attention with pixel-to-voxel correspondence. Coarse3D can directly replace the coarse stage of existing pipelines. Experiments show that it substantially improves sparse structure quality and consistently enhances final 3D generation, achieving state-of-the-art performance.

</details>

<a id="paper-67"></a>

### 67. [A Unified Framework for Probability Estimation of Data-Dependent Events](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41244v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41244

**作者：** Vitaly Feldman、Satyen Kale、Vinod Raman、Kunal Talwar、Ambuj Tewari

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41244v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41244v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究「数据依赖事件」的概率估计：在只能访问样本 S 与映射的条件下分析经验 plug-in 估计与 leave-one-out（Good–Turing）估计的误差，并用于「基分类器＋记忆例外」预测器的测试误差估计，提出受限区域的混合估计及基于 VC 维与互信息的两条界。

**相关性简评：** 「先用同一样本选出区域、再估计其概率」的偏差框架与互信息界，可能可供核查 LVR 中先筛选视觉证据或 latent 再评估其贡献时的估计偏差，以及区分记忆例外与可泛化部分；但该文非多模态工作，迁移线索需人工核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Unified Framework for Probability Estimation of Data-Dependent Events

**abstract/A1：** We study the general problem of estimating $D(\\dde(S))$, the probability that an unknown distribution $D$ assigns to an event determined by a sample $S$ of $n$ i.i.d.\\ draws from $D$ through a known mapping $\\dde$. The estimation algorithm knows $\\dde$ and $S$ but does not have access to any additional samples from $D$. This formulation captures classical missing mass, the data-dependent targets of adaptive data analysis and post-selection inference, and the test error of a data-trained predictor, whose estimation was recently studied by \\new{Gastpar et al.\\ (2024a,b)}. Within this framework we examine the two classical approaches to estimation. The error of the empirical plug-in estimator is exactly a generalization gap, so generalization bounds transfer to it directly. The leave-one-out estimator, which recovers the Good--Turing estimator, is accurate for every mapping that is stable under the deletion of one sample point, including new structured examples such as neighborhoods of the sample and pierced sets. The main application of our framework is to error estimation for a predictor that combines a base classifier with memorized exceptions, a structure common in theory and in practice. For it, the test error is exactly the missing mass restricted to the error region of the base classifier, a region selected by the same sample. We formulate the general problem of \\emph{adaptive missing mass estimation} to address such dependence and propose a natural hybrid estimator, the Good--Turing estimator restricted to the selected region. We show that the generalization of the base classifier alone does not guarantee the accuracy of this estimator, and derive two bounds on its error: the first one based on the VC dimension of possible error regions and another based on the mutual information of the selected region with the sample.

**comment/C1：** 39 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-68"></a>

### 68. [Rome Wasn't Built in One Turn: Replay-Grounded Reward Signals for Long-Horizon Freeciv Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41264v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41264

**作者：** Yanchen Liu、Xinyu Che、Brihi Joshi、Renzo Fukuda、Junhua Liu、Ruifeng Li、Yunfei Ge、Hang Yan、Xiang Ren

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41264v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41264v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对 Freeciv 长时程策略游戏中奖励稀疏、局内分数不可靠（「scoreboard traps」）的问题，构建可保存状态、分支决策并向前重放以取得长时程反馈的 CivHarness，并训练 LLM Judge（CivTelescope）判断两个局面的后续优劣，将其作为稠密奖励训练游戏 agent。

**相关性简评：** 「保存状态—分支重放—学习判断器」的流程可能可供核查 LVR 中过程监督与贡献分配的线索，即以反事实后续结果衡量中间状态价值；但对象为纯文本游戏状态，与视觉证据和 latent 表示无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Rome Wasn't Built in One Turn: Replay-Grounded Reward Signals for Long-Horizon Freeciv Agents

**abstract/A1：** Training language-model agents to play long-horizon strategy games is hard because deterministic feedback is sparse and often arrives only at the end of a game.
