In-game scores can offer dense signals, but they do not reliably predict future outcomes: in 8-68% of position pairs across eight strategy games, the higher-scoring position leads to the worse outcome. We term this phenomenon scoreboard traps. In Freeciv, an open-source turn-based empire-building game where several players manage units, cities, and diplomacy over hundreds of turns, we look into better reward signals for gameplay agents. To facilitate this, we build CivHarness, an open system that saves current game states, branches on different decisions, and replays the game to future turns to provide measurable long-horizon feedback from saved positions. We use CivHarness to train CivTelescope, an LLM Judge that predicts which one of two game positions will yield the better outcome. On scoreboard-trap pairs, CivTelescope is 1.3-2.0 times more accurate than off-the-shelf language models, and it generalizes to new game starts, later turns, and unseen rules. We then train a Freeciv gameplay agent with CivTelescope as a dense reward, and call this recipe CivMarshmallow. When used in the middle stage of the game where scoreboard traps are common, CivMarshmallow reaches an average training phase gain of 8.94 points, 77% more than the best baseline, the end-of-game reward. Over the full 120-turn game, CivMarshmallow uses a hybrid reward that starts from the scoreboard or the end-of-game reward and shifts to CivTelescope as the game advances; this hybrid reward scores highest among the rewards we compare.

**comment/C1：** 22 pages, 3 figures, 12 tables. Code: https://github.com/jamesnulliu/CivMarshmallow ; Checkpoints: https://huggingface.co/collections/CivMarshmallow/civmarshmallow

</details>

<a id="paper-69"></a>

### 69. [SAT-ACT: Learning CDCL Branching from Outcome-Derived Action Preferences](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41290v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41290

**作者：** Zhenchao Sun、Chongyang Tao、Ping Lu、Tongkai Lu、Jianling Gao、Shuai Ma

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41290v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41290v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 SAT-ACT，从同一求解器状态下独立分支动作的 rollout 结果中，用三种结果比较准则提取动作偏好，并保留证据不足时的不可比情形；再以状态条件网络学习分支优先级，直接指导 CDCL 搜索而无需在线 rollout，同时引入非支配启发式动作监督。

**相关性简评：** 非视觉工作，但其从同一状态多次采样结果反推动作偏好、并在证据冲突时不做强制排序的做法，可能为 LVR 中轨迹贡献分配与停止/分支决策的监督提供可供核查的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SAT-ACT: Learning CDCL Branching from Outcome-Derived Action Preferences

**abstract/A1：** Learning-based approaches have increasingly been explored for SAT solving, with particular attention to improving branching decisions in conflict-driven clause learning (CDCL) solvers. These approaches derive branching guidance either from static structural properties of SAT formulas or from dynamic signals obtained through solver feedback and execution traces. However, these forms of supervision provide limited evidence about state-dependent branching effects, while different outcome measures may favor different branching choices. To address these limitations, we propose SAT-ACT, an outcome-derived learning framework for CDCL branching. We design three complementary outcome comparison criteria to extract action preferences from independent branching action rollouts initiated at the same solver state. The criteria identify preferences supported by the outcomes and leave comparisons unresolved when the evidence is insufficient or conflicting, instead of forcing an ordering using a predefined scalar metric. A state-conditioned network learns branching priorities from the extracted preferences and uses them to guide CDCL search directly without online action rollouts. We further introduce non-dominated heuristic action supervision, which retains the solver-selected action as a training target only when it remains non-dominated under these preferences. Solver-level experiments show that SAT-ACT consistently reduces CDCL search costs on three SAT families and improves PAR-2 on the SAT Competition benchmarks.

**comment/C1：** Under review

</details>

<a id="paper-70"></a>

### 70. [Self-Improvement with Auxiliary Rewards: Learning from Verified Traces and Evaluating Transfer](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41296v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41296

**作者：** Minju Gwak、Alan Ritter

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41296v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41296v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究在只有答案级奖励、无法区分同样正确推理轨迹的情况下，用置信度（SC）与熵（UID-Conj.，结合全局熵变化与局部平滑度）两类辅助轨迹偏好，在 Qwen3-1.7B 上尝试在线奖励增强与离线加权 SFT，并评估小模型自提升与迁移效果。

**相关性简评：** 论文关注「结果同样正确但学习价值不同」的轨迹如何加权，其熵/置信度信号可能为 LVR 中推理轨迹的训练监督与贡献分配提供可核查线索；但工作纯文本、无视觉或内部状态分析，迁移到 latent 推理轨迹需自行验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Self-Improvement with Auxiliary Rewards: Learning from Verified Traces and Evaluating Transfer

**abstract/A1：** Answer-level rewards cannot distinguish reasoning traces with equally correct outcomes. We study whether auxiliary trajectory preferences improve small-language-model self-improvement and transfer under limited supervision. We compare confidence-based (SC) and entropy-based metrics (UID-Conj.), which combines global entropy variation and local smoothness, through online reward augmentation and offline training on correct responses. Online experiments adapt Qwen3-1.7B on 30 AIME problems without reference answers; offline experiments use historical AIME problems. UID-Conj. improves the online macro-average across the adaptation benchmark and three transfer benchmarks by 2.08 percentage points over unmodified TTRL. The strongest evidence comes from offline weighted SFT: UID-Conj. exceeds uniform weighting on the same response pool by 1.93 points, with a paired prompt-bootstrap 95\\% CI of \[0.44, 3.44\]. Benefits are task-dependent: SC rewards judged trace quality and instruction following, while UID-Conj. rewards yield stronger training outcomes in several settings. We find that auxiliary preferences can expose learning value among equally correct traces not captured during inference time, motivating their evaluation through downstream gains.

**comment/C1：** 28 pages, Work in Progress

</details>

<a id="paper-71"></a>

### 71. [Subliminal Reinforcement Learning: traits travel through a judge's scores](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41301v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41301

**作者：** Keuntae Kim、Yong Suk Choi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41301v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41301v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究「subliminal RL」：学生模型仅依靠自身输出与 LLM judge 的分数做 GRPO 后训练，judge 对某动物的偏好即可经由无该语义痕迹的字符串传递给学生；在 48 个设计单元中多数复现该效应，并给出四阶段链条（偏好须体现在分数中、偏好能推动学生的字符串、累积、到达答案），去掉 judge 间奖励差即无迁移。

**相关性简评：** 其「四阶段链条 + 逐环消融」可作为一种核查范式，供检验 LVR 中 latent 内容是否真正影响答案、即贡献如何分配与监督信号是否旁路泄漏，但纯文本设定与视觉推理的对应关系仍需人工判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Subliminal Reinforcement Learning: traits travel through a judge's scores

**abstract/A1：** Subliminal learning, the transfer of a model's trait through data with no semantic trace of it, has been shown for students trained on a teacher's text. Post-training relies on group relative policy optimization with a language-model judge, where the student learns only from its own outputs and one judge's scores. We show this suffices: a student writes invented syllables or numbers, a judge made to prefer one animal scores them, and the student's preference moves toward that animal relative to a run whose judge prefers another. Of 48 design cells, two fail the validity gate and 17 establish the trait at 5% false-discovery rate, numbers-only cells included; elsewhere, our account gives the reason. Removing only the reward difference between judges removes transfer. We establish the principle as a chain of four stages: the trait must show in the judge's scores, favour strings that move the student, accumulate and reach the answers. Judges with the same preference built from other data, or on another model, place it on other strings, and transfer is not detected. Carried unchanged to AI-safety dispositions, the chain holds: undesirable dispositions reach the student's answers, and stages measured before training anticipate where, helping to prevent part of this risk.

**comment/C1：** 37 pages, 4 figures, 2 tables, 2 Extended Data figures, 6 Extended Data tables; Supplementary Information included. Code and data: https://github.com/kkt94/subliminal-rl

</details>

<a id="paper-72"></a>

### 72. [STAR-FER: Adaptive Selective Restoration for Edge-Based Low-Resolution Facial Expression Recognition](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41310v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41310

**作者：** ZhongYang Wu、ZhenFeng Gan、Di Zhao、XiaoJun Zhu、HouDe Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41310v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41310v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 STAR-FER，用轻量门控在多个退化尺度下联合预测像素级空间排序与连续恢复预算，经精确支撑投影得到输入相关的自由形状掩码，仅在选中的稀疏区域做恢复并保留完整人脸上下文，以少量修改与计算提升低分辨率表情识别。

**相关性简评：** 其输入自适应的稀疏支撑选择与连续预算预测，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选、局部细节保留与动态计算分配提供可核查的方法线索，但任务是单图低分辨率分类，与多模态 latent 推理状态生成尚缺直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** STAR-FER: Adaptive Selective Restoration for Edge-Based Low-Resolution Facial Expression Recognition

**abstract/A1：** Variable interaction distances continuously degrade facial resolution in edge-based affective interaction, making low-resolution facial expression recognition (FER) difficult under limited computation. Full-face image or feature restoration incurs substantial overhead, whereas lightweight recognition pipelines may miss local cues that remain discriminative after degradation. We propose STAR-FER, a scale-and-task-adaptive selective restoration framework that balances recognition performance and resource constraints. A lightweight image-scale conditioned Gate jointly predicts a pixel-wise spatial ranking and a continuous restoration budget, then applies exact-support projection to obtain an input-dependent free-form mask. The mask-conditioned restorer retains complete facial context while concentrating sparse restoration and residual insertion on the selected support. Across seven degradation scales from x2 to x8 on the complete RAF-DB test set, STAR-FER achieves 80.92% mean accuracy and 71.64% Macro-F1, improving the low-resolution ResNet-18 baseline by 2.25 and 2.71 percentage points, respectively. It surpasses SR-FER by 1.04 accuracy points while requiring 8.84 GFLOPs, a 54.29% reduction, with 28.01% mean mask coverage, and maintains effective recognition at unseen non-integer scales. These results show that spatial-ranking and continuous-budget driven selective restoration reduces spatial modification and computation while maintaining low-resolution FER performance, offering an efficient path to resource-constrained affective interaction.

</details>

<a id="paper-73"></a>

### 73. [StrictPRMBench: A Replayable Evaluation Benchmark for Auditing Fixed-Score PRM Aggregation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41333v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41333

**作者：** Pengfei Zheng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41333v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41333v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文把 PRM 步级分数的聚合规则（乘积 vs 几何平均）当作独立评估对象，固定数学推理题池、评分证据与删除干预轨迹，构建可重放基准 StrictPRMBench，并报告不同聚合器在同一 N=32 记录上的选择不稳定性差异。

**相关性简评：** 其「删除干预后选择是否翻转」的可重放审计协议，或可供核查 LVR 中答案与停止决策是否真正依赖中间 latent 证据、以及贡献分配是否稳健；但研究对象是纯文本数学推理的 PRM 打分聚合，与视觉状态表示无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** StrictPRMBench: A Replayable Evaluation Benchmark for Auditing Fixed-Score PRM Aggregation

**abstract/A1：** Process reward models (PRMs) score reasoning steps, but best-of-N selection also requires a rule that aggregates those scores. We study that rule as a separate evaluation object. StrictPRMBench fixes mathematical reasoning pools, scorer evidence, and the source trace of a deletion intervention, so that different reducers face the same recorded selection problem. Across seven scoring checkpoints and two benchmarks, the stored N=32 outcomes give higher deletion-induced selection instability for product than geometric mean (GM) in all 14 cells. The equal-cell difference is +0.0965; a shared-problem bootstrap, conditional on these checkpoints and frozen pools, gives a 95% interval of \[0.0846,0.1086\]. A pre-injection-defined sensitivity excludes replacement slots that are either reducer's clean winner and retains 4,459 of 4,622 cell-problem records. The retained difference is +0.0986 \[0.0875,0.1098\]. Effects are heterogeneous: the two Qwen cells have positive percentile-bootstrap intervals on retained support but two-sided exact paired p-values of 0.0625 and 0.0703. Clean-pool Qwen accuracy does not detect a product advantage, but this is not an equivalence result. We provide versioned outcome records, CPU reanalysis, and an explicit evidence-availability contract. The contribution is a diagnostic evaluation protocol and a reproducible audit of recorded selector behavior, not a new PRM, a universally optimal reducer, or evidence of deployment-wide harm. Raw-score replay and outcome-record reanalysis are distinguished: missing primary edited-score vectors limit independent evaluation of new reducers under the same stress.

</details>

<a id="paper-74"></a>

### 74. [LEAP: Efficient LLM Inference Scheduling with Generalizable Length Prediction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41359v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41359

**作者：** Kwan Yan、Jialun Zhong、Yanzeng Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41359v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41359v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 LEAP 长度预测系统：复用服务模型 prefill 阶段的隐状态，通过线性读出预测输出长度，并用递归最小二乘在测试时以已完成请求为监督在线更新；预测与更新异步运行于 CPU，与 GPU prefill 重叠，用于长度感知调度。

**相关性简评：** 「从 prefill 隐状态线性探针读出输出长度」加「无梯度在线更新」可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态的读出方式与停止/长度决策的测试时自适应，但论文目标是服务调度而非推理机制，联系需人工判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LEAP: Efficient LLM Inference Scheduling with Generalizable Length Prediction

**abstract/A1：** Length-aware scheduling reduces request latency in LLM serving by prioritizing shorter requests. However, despite their large model sizes, length predictors can suffer degraded ranking performance under domain and task shifts, and adapting them can require costly retraining. Their prediction overhead can further offset the benefits of scheduling. To improve the generalization and efficiency of length-aware scheduling, we propose \\textbf{LEAP}, a length prediction system with fast online adaptation and asynchronous CPU execution. LEAP reuses the serving model's prefill hidden states, which contain information about the output length, and extracts this signal through a linear readout. To adapt to substantial domain and task shifts, it updates the readout at test time through recursive least squares, using completed requests as supervision without gradient computation or historical-data replay. Prediction and updates run asynchronously on CPU, while prediction and initial batch ordering overlap the remaining GPU prefill. Across the evaluated cross-domain transfers, LEAP retains strong ranking performance, and online adaptation improves Spearman correlation by 64.7\\% under a larger domain shift. Serving experiments show that LEAP reduces average per-token latency by 15.9--40.8\\% compared with the evaluated length predictors, while achieving 1.9\\% higher throughput with $30{,}569\\times$ fewer predictor parameters than the strongest evaluated baseline.

</details>

<a id="paper-75"></a>

### 75. [Task-Performance Routing for Multi-Teacher Distillation in CT Medical Image Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41362v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41362

**作者：** Jiaye Yang、Yao Liu、Die Dai、Hanwen Zhang、Peiyuan Jiang、Qiao Liu、Yutong Xie、Peng Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41362v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41362v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Task-Performance Routing（TPR），在 CT 器官分割的多教师蒸馏训练中，依据区域级分割误差为各教师分配权重——困难区域偏向低误差教师，简单区域用均匀权重，并用分层空间划分实现浅层细粒度、深层粗粒度路由；推理仅用学生模型。

**相关性简评：** 其「按区域误差自适应分配教师贡献、无需学习路由参数」的思路，可能为 LVR 中依据视觉证据区域进行监督分配与贡献分配提供线索，但属医学分割训练侧工作，与 VLM 推理状态生成无直接关联，可供核查迁移可行性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Task-Performance Routing for Multi-Teacher Distillation in CT Medical Image Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Pre-trained models offer complementary strengths in CT organ segmentation, but their relative accuracy varies across organs and spatial regions. Multi-teacher knowledge distillation can combine these strengths in a single student, yet global or instance-level teacher weights overlook variation within a 3D volume. We propose Task-Performance Routing (TPR), which uses region-wise segmentation errors to determine teacher weights during training without learned routing parameters. In hard regions, TPR favors teachers with lower prediction errors; in easy regions, it uses uniform weights to combine their knowledge. A hierarchical spatial partition provides fine-grained routing in shallow encoder stages and coarse-grained routing in deep stages. Experiments on BTCV, MSD-Liver, and MSD-Pancreas with two teacher pairs and two backbone architectures show higher Dice scores than the individual teachers, adaptive ensembles, and evaluated multi-teacher distillation baselines. On MSD-Pancreas, TPR achieves Dice scores of 65.40% with SwinUNETR and 68.25% with U-Net, compared with 64.38% and 67.45% for the corresponding adaptive ensembles. Inference uses the student alone, with no routing or teacher overhead. Code is available at https://github.com/EdvinCecilia/TPR\_MultiTeacher.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at MICCAI 2026. 11 pages, 3 figures, 4 tables. Code: https://github.com/EdvinCecilia/TPR\_MultiTeacher

</details>

<a id="paper-76"></a>

### 76. [Ask on Demand: Targeted Peer Communication for Parallel Agentic Systems](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41378v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41378

**作者：** Haofu Yang、Hongyang Yin、Ran Bi、Faguo Wu、Peng Xue、Feng Zhou、Han Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41378v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41378v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Ask on Demand 框架，让并行 LLM agent 在出现具体信息需求时向同伴发起定向请求，从对等执行历史中检索证据并生成定制回复；同时用配对团队延续结果与推理成本训练轻量门控，在 AppWorld 和 WebShop 上验证性能与成本收益。

**相关性简评：** 其“何时值得请求外部信息”的门控与证据检索，可能为 LVR 中的 LOOK/STOP 决策及成本感知的证据筛选提供方法线索，但该工作面向文本多 agent 通信，视觉状态迁移仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Ask on Demand: Targeted Peer Communication for Parallel Agentic Systems

**abstract/A1：** Parallel LLM agents tackle complex tasks by exploring multiple trajectories, yet useful discoveries remain isolated in individual execution histories. Existing sharing methods often select or summarize information before other agents' needs become clear, potentially overlooking details that become important later. We propose Ask on Demand (AOD), a communication framework that enables agents to seek targeted peer assistance when specific information needs arise. Upon an approved request, AOD retrieves relevant evidence from peer execution histories, selects a suitable peer, and generates a response tailored to the request. To determine when such assistance is worthwhile, we train a lightweight gate using paired team continuations, with supervision derived from final team outcomes and inference cost. Experiments on AppWorld and WebShop demonstrate that AOD consistently improves task performance over independent parallel execution while achieving lower inference cost than existing information-sharing baselines. These findings highlight the benefits of aligning peer communication with agents' evolving information needs to achieve more effective and efficient collaboration.

**comment/C1：** Equal contribution: Haofu Yang and Hongyang Yin. Corresponding authors: Ran Bi and Faguo Wu

</details>

<a id="paper-77"></a>

### 77. [SACQ: Structure-Aware Codebook Quantization for Ultra-Low-Bit Vision-Language-Action Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41408v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41408

**作者：** Haesung Lee、Insu Han

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41408v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41408v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** SACQ 提出面向 VLA 的超低比特权重向量码本量化框架，结合结构感知的码本拟合、按最终动作误差增量分配层间比特，以及两阶段量化后精化，在 LIBERO 与 SimplER 上报告成功率、内存和速度结果。

**相关性简评：** 其按下游动作误差增量做层级精度分配的敏感度度量，可能可供核查用于评估 LVR 中哪些内部状态或计算步骤对最终答案贡献更大；但论文本身不涉及 latent 推理状态及其生成与使用。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SACQ: Structure-Aware Codebook Quantization for Ultra-Low-Bit Vision-Language-Action Models

**abstract/A1：** Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models enable capable robot behavior, but their large memory footprint limits deployment on resource-constrained robotic platforms. Weight quantization is a natural remedy, yet post-training quantization (PTQ) recipes inherited from large language models degrade when applied to VLAs at ultra-low precision. Existing low-bit PTQ methods often fail to explicitly account for the structure of downstream operations and allocate precision without considering its effect on the policy actions. We introduce SACQ, an ultra- low-bit weight-only vector-codebook quantization framework for VLAs. SACQ combines (1) structure-aware codebook fitting with role-specific quadratic metrics that reflect input statistics and downstream sensitivity; (2) action-aware mixed-bit allocation based on the increase in final-action error as individual layers are compressed; and (3) two-stage post-quantization refinement that reassigns codeword indices with second-order error compensation and calibrates codebooks and row-group scales against the decoded action. Using GR00T N1.7, SACQ achieves 93.85% average success across four LIBERO suites at 1.5 bits per language/action weight while reducing model memory by 4.83x. With INT8 activation quantization and low-precision attention, it retains 93.55% success while running 1.51x faster than BF16. On SimplER, SACQ further achieves 63.76% and 52.27% success under Visual Matching and Variant Aggregation at 2 bits per language/action weight, compared to 58.90% and 53.45% for BF16, respectively.

</details>

<a id="paper-78"></a>

### 78. [SparseGDN: Chunk Sparse in Linear Attention](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41420v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41420

**作者：** Jiedong Zhuang、Peng Zhao、Jialin Tang、Haoji Hu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41420v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41420v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** SparseGDN 是面向 Gated DeltaNet 长上下文预填充的硬件感知加速框架，用块内结构化近似与输入相关的循环事件动态选择，减少局部 Delta 求解、循环状态更新与中间状态存储，在 Qwen3.5-4B/9B 的 RULER 任务上报告 1.50–1.94x 预填充加速。

**相关性简评：** 按输入动态分配循环状态更新预算、并以紧凑状态快照重建输出的做法，可能为 LVR 中「何时及多大程度更新 latent 状态」与状态压缩的内存–计算取舍提供借鉴线索，但该工作针对纯文本效率，与视觉推理的关联需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SparseGDN: Chunk Sparse in Linear Attention

**abstract/A1：** Gated Delta Networks (GDNs) offer linear-time sequence modeling, yet long-context prefill remains costly due to dense intra-chunk computation, recurrent-state updates, and intermediate state storage. We introduce SparseGDN, a hardware-aware framework that combines structured intra-chunk approximation with input-dependent recurrent-event selection while producing outputs for every token. A block-adjacent solver reduces local Delta-solve computation, while a dynamic selector allocates recurrent-update budgets across head pairs. The GPU implementation materializes updates only for selected events and reconstructs token outputs using compact state snapshots and group-local interactions, reducing computation and memory traffic. We evaluate SparseGDN on Qwen3.5-4B and Qwen3.5-9B across RULER tasks and context lengths from 4K to 128K. Retaining approximately 29% of recurrent events on average, SparseGDN achieves 1.50-1.94x GDN-core prefill speedups over native FLA kernels, including online selection overhead.

</details>

<a id="paper-79"></a>

### 79. [FlexReader: Towards Adaptive Decoding Paths for Efficient Arbitrary-Length Scene Text Recognition](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41426v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41426

**作者：** Zhibin Ma、Pengwen Dai、Zhigang Yao、Xugong Qin、Wenqi Ren、Xiaochun Cao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41426v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41426v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** FlexReader 提出一种支持多种解码步长的场景文本识别模型：先用子串感知模块从视觉特征中定位目标子串，再由多个并行解码块独立解码其中每个字符，并在推理时用基于字符置信度的自适应前缀解码为每个输入选择解码步长，以在准确率与速度间取得折中。

**相关性简评：** 其置信度驱动的解码步长自适应选择与「动态计算、停止决策」问题有形式上的对应，可供核查能否迁移为 LVR 中按证据充分性调整推理长度或提前停止的机制；但任务是 OCR 字符识别，不涉及多模态推理状态，联系较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FlexReader: Towards Adaptive Decoding Paths for Efficient Arbitrary-Length Scene Text Recognition

**abstract/A1：** Existing Scene Text Recognition (STR) models are inherently coupled with a specific decoding strategy by their training paradigms, limiting their flexibility in deployment across diverse recognition scenarios. In this paper, we propose FlexReader, an STR model that supports multiple decoding strides, enabling a single model to achieve an optimal accuracy-efficiency trade-off across different datasets. Specifically, we first introduce a Sub-string Perception module to perceive the target sub-string from visual features, followed by multiple parallel decoding blocks that independently decode each character in the sub-string. During inference, FlexReader supports decoding with arbitrary strides within the perceived sub-string range. Furthermore, we propose Adaptive Prefix Decoding, which adaptively selects the decoding stride at each step based on the model's confidence in the decoded characters, thereby identifying the optimal decoding path for each input. Experimental results demonstrate that FlexReader exhibits robust recognition capability across a diverse range of benchmarks, including those featuring long text, curved text, and occlusions. It consistently outperforms existing decoding strategies in recognition accuracy while achieving up to 6-flod decoding speedup over autoregressive decoding.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages, 5 figures

</details>
