### 80. [When Links Mislead: Label-Free Structural Polarity for Zero-Shot Classification of Web Pages](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41464v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41464

**作者：** Hanshuai Cui、Zhiqing Tang、Zhi Yao、Weijia Jia、Jun Luo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41464v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41464v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究零样本网页分类中链接结构可能误导的问题，提出用 LLM 标签与文本原型预测沿链接的「超额一致」无标签地估计结构极性，并据此门控一次带符号传播（ZeroPolar），以及在部分页面已有标注后用文本分类器去噪（ZeroPolar-T）。

**相关性简评：** 任务本身不涉及视觉推理，但其「用无需真值的统计量判断外部结构证据应当正向还是反向注入，并门控单步传播」的做法，可能为 LVR 中判定视觉证据与语言先验是否一致、何时抑制旁路信息的门控设计提供核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Links Mislead: Label-Free Structural Polarity for Zero-Shot Classification of Web Pages

**abstract/A1：** Web platforms such as digital libraries, online stores and university sites must classify new pages before anyone labels them. Large language models (LLMs) can label each page from its text, but these labels are noisy, and links could correct them only where linked pages share classes. Existing zero-shot methods ignore the links, or pull a page toward its neighbours as label propagation and graph neural networks (GNNs) do, which hurts where linked pages differ in class. Others weight propagation by an LLM-estimated homophily that costs extra calls and drifts across LLMs. We find that the sign with which links should enter a prediction, which we call structural polarity, is readable without true labels. It is the excess agreement of LLM labels and text-prototype predictions along links. We thus propose ZeroPolar, which gates one signed propagation step by this statistic. Training-free ZeroPolar mixes prototype scores with any number of LLM labels, and ZeroPolar-T denoises the labels with a text classifier once half of the pages carry one. With one GPT-4o label per page, ZeroPolar-T reaches the highest mean accuracy on eleven graphs (73.99%), 6.01 points above LLM-BP and significantly above all eighteen baselines we compare. With LLM labels on only 5% of the pages, training-free ZeroPolar leads a GNN trained on these labels by 10.80 points.

</details>

<a id="paper-81"></a>

### 81. [EvoMCP: Co-evolving MCP agents and training data](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41479v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41479

**作者：** Xuanang Gao、Kepeng Lin、Xinyan Shi、Yong Deng、Shuheng Shen、Changhua Meng、Xiaofeng Wu、Jie Yang、Wei Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41479v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41479v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 EvoMCP，一个 MCP agent 与训练数据互演化框架：依据 rollout 反馈评估学生的能力与缺口，据此演化任务指令与环境状态，再用可复用技能修复或精炼教师轨迹，闭环进行监督微调与再评估，在三个 MCP 基准上取得稳定提升。

**相关性简评：** 其「按能力缺口生成任务 + 用可复用技能回收失败轨迹」的做法，可能为 LVR 轨迹训练中的数据筛选、失败样本利用与贡献分配提供可核查线索，但本身不涉及 latent 状态或视觉推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EvoMCP: Co-evolving MCP agents and training data

**abstract/A1：** Supervised fine-tuning of Model Context Protocol (MCP) agents is typically conducted on a static data distribution, which fails to adapt to the student's evolving capabilities and leaves newly exposed weaknesses insufficiently addressed. To address these challenges, we propose \\method, an agent--data mutual evolution framework that enables MCP agents to improve through a closed loop of task evolution, trajectory evolution, and supervised fine-tuning. Specifically, \\method\\ assesses the student's established capabilities and capability gaps from rollout feedback to identify learning targets. Task evolution then translates these targets into executable tasks by adapting the instructions and observable environment states of compatible verified seed tasks while keeping their verifiers aligned. To provide reliable and efficient demonstrations for these tasks, trajectory evolution uses reusable skills to recover failed teacher rollouts and refine successful ones. The resulting task--trajectory pairs are used to fine-tune the student, while accepted tasks seed subsequent evolution. To close the loop, we re-evaluate the updated student and use its feedback to guide the next round of data evolution. Experiments on Toolathlon, MCPMark, and MCP-Atlas demonstrate consistent improvements over the initial student, yielding absolute gains of 16.5\\%, 10.2\\%, and 12.6\\%, respectively. Project page: https://github.com/xagao/EvoMCP.

</details>

<a id="paper-82"></a>

### 82. [BDNet: Fast-slow thinking is all you need](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41485v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41485

**作者：** Chenzhi Zheng、Zhuoran Zheng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41485v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41485v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 BDNet，一个图像分类骨干：以基线变形算子把特征相对阶段性参考的偏差经低秩投影与通道缩放处理，四个参考缓冲区在训练中累积统计量、推理时冻结，并在 ImageNet-1K 上考察干净精度、消融与噪声、对比度、亮度扰动。

**相关性简评：** 其「慢」参考仅为训练期统计量、明确区别于输入相关注意力，与 LVR 主线关联较弱；但以参考相对残差替代成对 token 相似度、复杂度随 token 数线性增长，可能为 latent 状态中相似度/注意力结构及缓存式参考的设计提供核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** BDNet: Fast-slow thinking is all you need

**abstract/A1：** We present BDNet, a hierarchical image-classification backbone built around fast image features and slow statistical references. Its baseline deformation operator (BDO) transforms the deviation of a feature from a stage-specific reference through a low-rank projection and a learned channel scale. Rather than computing pairwise token similarity, the branch operates directly on baseline-relative residuals with complexity linear in the token count. Each of four reference buffers accumulates detached stage statistics during training and remains frozen at inference. A four-stage pyramid with depths $(2,2,6,2)$ and widths $(96,192,384,768)$ yields a 20.70M-parameter classifier at $224\\times224$ resolution. We organize an ImageNet-1K study around clean classification, eight controlled ablations, and Gaussian-noise, contrast, and brightness perturbations. The central hypothesis is that a slow reference can improve the robustness of a compact representation while retaining a simple tensor implementation. We distinguish this training-time statistical mechanism from input-dependent attention and make its linear inference behavior explicit.

**comment/C1：** 7 pages, 2 figures, this manuscript is an early draft

</details>

<a id="paper-83"></a>

### 83. [VariaFlow: Uncertainty-Aware Flow Matching for Unsupervised Anomaly Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41494v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41494

**作者：** Arnela Hadzic、Martin Urschler、Cosmin I. Bercea

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41494v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41494v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 VariaFlow：在无条件 flow matching 先验下用不同噪声初始化做掩码引导修复集成，估计每个位置的预测方差，并用其标准化重建残差，从而区分真实病变与正常解剖变异，在三个脑 MRI 数据集上做无监督异常检测。

**相关性简评：** 其「集成估计预测方差、再按不确定性归一化残差」的机制可能可供核查：在 LVR 中或可用于区分任务必要的视觉证据与无关变异，并为视觉细节的可信度筛选与停止决策提供不确定度信号。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** VariaFlow: Uncertainty-Aware Flow Matching for Unsupervised Anomaly Detection

**abstract/A1：** Reconstruction-based anomaly detection no longer fails at reconstruction. Generative models produce high-quality pseudo-healthy reconstructions, and the remaining difficulty is the step of scoring residuals. The residual between a scan and its reconstruction has two indistinguishable sources, genuine pathology and the anatomical variation that separates one healthy brain from another. Scoring against a single reconstruction treats both as anomalous, inflating false positives where healthy anatomy varies most while small or subtle lesions are lost in the surrounding reconstruction error. We identify the missing quantity as the predictive variance, how much a healthy reconstruction can vary at each location. We introduce VariaFlow, which estimates this variance from an ensemble of mask-guided restorations under an unconditional flow matching prior, each started from a different noise initialization, and which normalizes the residual by the resulting standard deviation. Few-step flow matching keeps the ensemble computationally affordable, and restoring at two spatial scales captures lesions of different size. Across three brain MRI datasets spanning stroke and tumor, VariaFlow is trained once on healthy anatomy and generalizes across all three, attaining the highest AUROC (0.94, 0.80, 0.78) on each and the highest AUPRC on both cross-domain datasets, exceeding the strongest baseline by 37% and 50%. Under domain shift, the same baseline drops by up to 52% in AUPRC, whereas VariaFlow stays more stable and achieves the best small-lesion Dice on all three datasets.

**comment/C1：** Accepted for WACV 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-84"></a>

### 84. [PCD: Predictive Coding Dynamics for Irregularly Sampled Time Series](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41506v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41506

**作者：** Tianrui Gou、Jiayangmei Xu、Xingzhou Zhang、Lei Sha

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41506v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41506v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Predictive Coding Dynamics（PCD），一种用于不规则采样连续时间序列模型的残差反馈机制：保留上一步重建结果并由其预测已观测项，再将掩码后的符号残差以能量惩罚形式并入状态识别，在 ODE-RNN 与 Latent ODE 上实现事件驱动的隐状态更新与潜状态推断。

**相关性简评：** 其「预测误差经能量惩罚回灌以细化 latent 状态」的循环更新结构，可供核查是否能迁移为 LVR 中推理状态的迭代修正与观测一致性检验；但原文面向时间序列预测，未涉及视觉或多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PCD: Predictive Coding Dynamics for Irregularly Sampled Time Series

**abstract/A1：** Dynamic systems evolve continuously over time, whereas observations in real scenarios are often sparse, irregular, and asynchronous. Accurate prediction therefore requires inferring latent state evolution between observations, as future values depend on partially observed trajectories. We propose Predictive Coding Dynamics (PCD), a residual feedback mechanism for irregular continuous dynamic models. PCD maintains previous reconstructions, predicts observed entries from them, and incorporates masked signed residuals into state recognition through an energy penalty. The mechanism is implemented with ODE-RNN and Latent ODE, supporting event-based hidden updates and latent state recognition. Experiments on forecasting and trajectory recovery tasks show that PCD improves continuous dynamic backbones under sparse and irregular sampling. The results further indicate that predictive error feedback improves latent state estimation when observations constrain the current trajectory. These results suggest that PCD provides a residual feedback framework that jointly supports trajectory generation, observation consistency checking, and state refinement in continuous-time series modeling. The source code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/PCD-for-paper-92F1/.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages. Supplementary material (2 pages) available as an ancillary file

</details>

<a id="paper-85"></a>

### 85. [Specification-Guided Generation for Large Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41511v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41511

**作者：** Alessandro Previti、Konstantinos Vandikas、Mario Alviano、Mats Carlsson、Albin Larsson Forsberg、Alexandros Nikou、Athanasios Karapantelakis、Aneta Vulgarakis

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41511v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41511v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出神经符号的规范引导解码框架：每一步用符号推理过滤无法扩展为满足形式规范的候选值，给出前向保证版与回溯修正版，并在配置与约束求解基准上验证合规性与开销。

**相关性简评：** 逐步可行性过滤与「下游后果不佳时回退早期选择」的机制，可能为 latent 推理中的状态验证、错误步骤回退与停止决策提供线索；但纯文本约束解码与视觉 latent 状态之间的距离仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Specification-Guided Generation for Large Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) can generate structured outputs, but existing constrained-decoding methods mainly enforce syntax and cannot directly enforce satisfaction of a formal specification. We introduce a neuro-symbolic, specification-guided decoding framework that combines LLM generation with symbolic reasoning. At each step, the framework refines the decoding grammar by filtering out values that cannot be extended to an output satisfying the specification. We present a forward-only variant that guarantees specification satisfaction by construction, and a backtracking variant that revises earlier feasible choices when their downstream consequences leave only values that the LLM itself judges unsuitable. The framework is model-agnostic, requires no retraining, and builds on existing grammar-constrained decoding mechanisms. Experiments on configuration and constraint-solving benchmarks show that the forward-only variant reliably produces specification-compliant outputs while reducing repeated LLM inference compared with rejection sampling and incurring modest symbolic-reasoning overhead. The backtracking variant further reduces cases in which the model is forced to select values that it has itself judged unsuitable, thereby substantially improving adherence to the LLM's stated interpretation of the prompt.

</details>

<a id="paper-86"></a>

### 86. [FOCUS: First-Order Control Using Optimal Transport Supervision for Imitation from Observations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41540v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41540

**作者：** João A. Cândido Ramos、Lionel Blondé、Hugues Vinzant、Jacopo Castellini、Alexandros Kalousis

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41540v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41540v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 面向仅有状态观测、无时间对齐的模仿学习，提出 FOCUS：用局部最优传输把智能体短 rollout 窗口与多段专家轨迹做 soft best-of-K 匹配，生成可微模仿奖励，并结合短视界可微 actor-critic 与 critic bootstrap 稳定训练。

**相关性简评：** 其「无时间对应下用最优传输软匹配 rollout 与专家片段」的训练监督思路，可能可供核查多步 latent 推理轨迹在缺少步级标注时的贡献分配与轨迹训练；但与视觉证据筛选、THINK/LOOK/STOP 无直接联系，迁移仅为推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FOCUS: First-Order Control Using Optimal Transport Supervision for Imitation from Observations

**abstract/A1：** Imitation learning from observations must recover behavior from state-only demonstrations without known temporal correspondence. Differentiable simulators provide pathwise policy gradients, but differentiating through long, contact-rich trajectories can make optimization unstable. We introduce FOCUS, which combines local optimal-transport alignment with short-horizon differentiable actor-critic learning. FOCUS matches agent rollout windows to multiple sampled expert segments using a soft best-of-$K$ objective that emphasizes locally compatible matches. The resulting transport-based costs provide differentiable imitation rewards, while a learned critic bootstraps value beyond the rollout horizon. FOCUS requires neither temporally aligned trajectories nor expert-aligned initialization or teacher forcing. Across contact-rich locomotion tasks in DFlex, including a musculoskeletal humanoid controlled by 152 muscle activations, FOCUS improves sample efficiency and training stability over adversarial imitation-from-observation methods and prior differentiable imitation baselines.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Physical Understanding for Decision-Making

</details>

<a id="paper-87"></a>

### 87. [ORION: An Outcome-Driven LLM Advisor for RL-Based Multi-Domain 6G Slice Placement](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41563v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41563

**作者：** Mahmoud S. Mayaleh、Fetia Bannour、Nour-El-Houda Yellas

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.MA、cs.NI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41563v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41563v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对多域 6G 网络切片放置，提出 ORION：两个 LLM agent 将服务意图转为结构化请求与抽象放置计划，多智能体 RL 选择跨域划分，并以结果驱动的情景记忆记录网络条件、计划与已验证可行性，再回流给规划器。

**相关性简评：** 其按可行性验证结果给计划打标并回流的记忆机制，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 轨迹的贡献分配与验证式监督；「计划作为持续软先验」也可能对应向推理状态注入外部计划的方式。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ORION: An Outcome-Driven LLM Advisor for RL-Based Multi-Domain 6G Slice Placement

**abstract/A1：** Deploying 6G network slices across administrative domains requires an online decision for each arriving request: which domain hosts each virtual network function, how traffic crosses domain boundaries, and whether the slice is admitted. This joint decision problem is NP-hard under continuous arrivals. Experiments on a parametrized family of multi-domain topologies show that no fixed planning strategy dominates across all regimes: the performance ranking reverses between topology classes, and feasible placements exist for most rejected requests but remain undiscovered. These findings identify the plan layer as the decisive performance lever. We introduce ORION, a hierarchical orchestrator in which two LLM agents translate service intents into structured requests and generate abstract placement plans. A cooperative multi-agent RL coordinator selects the cross-domain partition, treating the plan as a persistent soft prior, while per-domain agents place the functions. ORION main contribution lies at the plan layer: an outcome-driven episodic memory that records, for each completed attempt, the network-condition profile, the proposed plan, and its verified feasibility outcome. The memory feeds verified successes and labelled failures back to the planner, thereby closing the adaptation loop using deployment feasibility rather than a scalar reward assigned to the planner's own proposals. To isolate the effect of the plan quality, we evaluate the planner on held-out topology families while keeping the downstream selection and execution mechanisms fixed. Under this controlled setting, the proposed memory improves admission by $4.6$--$11.5$ percentage points over the same planner without memory, while importance-based retention accounts for most of the improvement over a first-in-first-out store.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at IEEE MobiWac 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-88"></a>

### 88. [Scout-Refine-Correct: Teaching Flow Models to Refine Their Own Samples](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41598v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41598

**作者：** Sergey Karpukhin、Aibek Alanov

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41598v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41598v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Scout-Refine-Correct 训练配方：让 flow-matching 生成器在采样时先行 K 步得到 scout 轨迹并外推整样本估计，作为自条件信号反馈，可重复多轮精修，并用两个 correction loss 在自身 rollout 实际到达的状态上训练。

**相关性简评：** 其「在自身轨迹到达的状态上训练 + 无外部验证器的多轮自我修正」思路，可能为 LVR 的轨迹监督、迭代 latent 状态精修与停止决策提供可核查线索，但属图像生成任务，与视觉推理的答案使用尚无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Scout-Refine-Correct: Teaching Flow Models to Refine Their Own Samples

**abstract/A1：** The quality of image generation models trained with diffusion or flow matching is largely fixed by the training budget: at inference, the only available lever is the number of sampling steps, and additional steps improve quality only up to a point. We present a training recipe, Scout-Refine-Correct (SRC), that instead gives a flow-matching generator a genuine inference-time refinement capability. At each sampling step the model takes a scout rollout -- $K$ steps ahead along its own trajectory -- extrapolates a full-sample estimate, and feeds that estimate back to itself as a self-conditioning signal before taking the real step. This can be repeated for $R$ refinement rounds. Two correction losses, applied during training at states the model's own rollouts actually reach rather than only on the ground-truth path, teach the model to use this signal to reach new samples instead of collapsing to a suboptimal one. On ImageNet-256, SRC improves LightningDiT-B from FID~14.5 to FID~7.26 at NFE~550, matching a privileged best-of-$N$ baseline that uses an oracle FID verifier to choose among samples. SRC uses no external verifier, no reward signal, and, after ablation, almost no extra parameters over the base architecture. Combining SRC with best-of-$N$ improves the result further, to FID~3.81 at NFE~4400, showing that the two axes are complementary. We ablate the main design choices -- the architectural pathway for the scout signal, the scout horizon, each correction loss, and the training schedule to demonstrate key working components of training.

**comment/C1：** preprint

</details>

<a id="paper-89"></a>

### 89. [What Should LLMs Forget: Reinforcement Unlearning of Knowledge Associations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41616v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41616

**作者：** Wutong Xu、Yun Qu、Haiyan Feng、Qi Wang、Xiangyang Ji

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41616v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41616v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把「知识关联」（查询上下文与目标知识之间的条件预测分布）作为遗忘的基本目标，提出强化学习框架 KARU，用 token 级奖励构造、分布级正则和邻近保留采样实现在抑制特定知识的同时保留其余能力。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理无直接关联，但其 token 级奖励与分布级正则可能为 LVR 轨迹训练中的细粒度贡献分配、以及「该抑制哪些内部状态关联」提供奖励设计线索，能否迁移尚需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** What Should LLMs Forget: Reinforcement Unlearning of Knowledge Associations

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) acquire extensive factual knowledge during pre-training, which may include sensitive, private, or copyrighted information, motivating the development of unlearning methods that selectively remove specific knowledge while preserving general capabilities. However, existing approaches typically optimize entire responses without explicitly identifying the knowledge to be forgotten, resulting in coarse and uncontrolled forgetting. To address this, we revisit the forgetting target of LLM unlearning and introduce Knowledge Association (KA), which characterizes the association between a query context and its target knowledge and is instantiated by the corresponding conditional predictive distribution, as the fundamental target of forgetting. Based on KA, we propose Knowledge Association Reinforcement Unlearning (KARU), a unified reinforcement learning framework that suppresses forgotten knowledge associations while preserving retained ones. Specifically, KARU derives complementary mechanisms from the KA-based formulation: token-level reward construction for finer-grained forgetting supervision, distribution-level regularization for precise knowledge suppression, and proximity-aware retain sampling to mitigate collateral degradation. Experiments across diverse LLMs on representative benchmarks demonstrate that KARU consistently improves forgetting effectiveness over existing baselines while maintaining comparable retention performance and general utility.

</details>

<a id="paper-90"></a>

### 90. [On the Generation Order in GFlowNets](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41648v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41648

**作者：** Timofei Gritsaev、Evgeny Katasonov、Ildus Sadrtdinov、Esmeralda S. Whitammer、Dmitry Vetrov

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41648v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41648v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究 GFlowNet 中离散对象的生成顺序，构造可控问题说明顺序影响收敛，并提出无需顺序标注即可学习可解释生成顺序的 Learning-Order GFlowNet，同时用梯度 SNR 解释顺序的形成。

**相关性简评：** 其「学习生成顺序」与梯度 SNR 刻画好顺序的线索，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 推理步骤的排列、状态更新与训练监督设计，但该工作并非视觉或多模态推理研究。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** On the Generation Order in GFlowNets

**abstract/A1：** Sampling from discrete distributions specified by unnormalised probability functions is a classical statistical problem. GFlowNets address this problem by constructing objects sequentially, adding one discrete component at a time in either a predefined or a learned order. The choice of order determines which conditional distributions the neural network must learn, some of which may be easier to learn than others. Yet whether existing GFlowNet algorithms discover interpretable generation orders and make use of them during training remains unexplored. We introduce controlled problems where generation order is experimentally shown to affect convergence and standard GFlowNet training struggles to discover useful orders. To address this difficulty, we develop Learning-Order GFlowNet, a training recipe that learns interpretable generation orders without order annotations and improves convergence on these problems. Finally, we investigate gradient signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) as a possible explanation for order emergence: orders associated with faster convergence exhibit higher SNR at comparable validation loss, suggesting both a mechanism favouring their emergence and an SNR-based characterisation of a good generation order.

</details>

<a id="paper-91"></a>

### 91. [Breaking Information Islands in Sparse Tuning via Small-World Connectivity](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41665v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41665

**作者：** Xianchao Guan、Zijun Xiong、Yifeng Wang、Yubo Cui、Yaowei Wang、Guo-Sen Xie、Xin Li、Zheng Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41665v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41665v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 SWIFT 稀疏微调框架：用块对角稀疏更新实现局部特征混合，再加一个固定、无额外参数的散射置换把混合结果路由到其他特征组，并从理论上说明「信息孤岛」问题及该置换带来的块级扩张，在常识推理、文本生成和图像分类上平均优于多个 PEFT 基线。

**相关性简评：** 其「信息孤岛」诊断与固定置换打通特征子空间的做法，可能可供核查 latent 推理状态如何跨子空间混合、避免内部表示分裂为孤立分组；但论文不涉及视觉证据或推理期 latent 生成，只提供结构性方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Breaking Information Islands in Sparse Tuning via Small-World Connectivity

**abstract/A1：** Sparse tuning is widely used to adapt large language models due to its parameter efficiency. However, its effectiveness depends not only on the number of trainable parameters, but also on the feature-interaction topology of the sparse update's structural support. Limited or uneven connectivity in this support may isolate subsets of feature dimensions, forming information islands that restrict direct task-specific interactions through the trainable update. To address this issue, we propose Small-World Induced Fine-Tuning (SWIFT), a sparse tuning framework inspired by small-world connectivity. SWIFT combines block-diagonal sparse updates for local feature mixing with a fixed deterministic scattering permutation that routes the mixed outputs across feature groups without additional trainable parameters. We further show theoretically that certain random sparse-update models and block-diagonal updates can exhibit information islands, whereas a sufficiently scattering permutation guarantees positive block-level expansion when the trainable block-diagonal factor consists of equal-sized, structurally dense square blocks. Extensive experiments on commonsense reasoning, natural language generation, and image classification demonstrate that SWIFT outperforms the evaluated parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) baselines on average and can match or surpass full fine-tuning. Implementation is available at https://github.com/xianchaoguan/SWIFT

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026. 23 pages, 6 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-92"></a>

### 92. [Radial Kernel Attention for Long-Range Modeling in Polymer Machine Learning Force Fields](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41675v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41675

**作者：** Wenbo Fu、Chenghui Mao、Chengwen Hu、Haifeng Lv、Zhou Lv

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41675v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41675v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Radial Kernel Attention：把原子到簇质心的距离映射为可学径向特征，与内容相关的 query/key 重叠结合，通过因子化聚合与读出在原子数上以线性成本实现全局特征交换，并将其插入 EquiformerV3 局部块之间用于聚合物力场的能量与力预测。

**相关性简评：** 其以距离核加权的线性成本全局聚合，可能为视觉 latent 推理中需保留空间/几何结构的全局状态交换提供效率线索，但该机制能否迁移到多模态推理尚需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Radial Kernel Attention for Long-Range Modeling in Polymer Machine Learning Force Fields

**abstract/A1：** We introduce Radial Kernel Attention (RKA) for long-range modeling in polymer machine learning force fields. Local neighbor graphs constrain communication across polymer chains and their surrounding environments, while explicit all-pair attention incurs quadratic cost. RKA maps atomic distances from the cluster centroid to learned radial features and combines their overlap with content-dependent queries and keys. Factorized aggregation and readout enable global feature exchange at linear cost in atom count for fixed feature dimensions. We insert the invariant scalar update between EquiformerV3 local blocks and obtain conservative forces by differentiating the total energy, including its radial coordinate dependence. We evaluate energy and force prediction for finite polymer-derived clusters in OPoly26. Our models are trained on the official training split for 12 epochs and evaluated directly after training on the complete official validation split. A matched 5M comparison shows that RKA reduces per-atom energy and force MAEs by 67.4% and 36.3%, respectively. The 20M RKA model is compared with UMA-S-1.2 and UMA-S-1.2.1 on the same validation structures. The 20M model attains a total-energy MAE of 15.81 meV and a force MAE of 2.619 meV/angstrom; its per-atom energy MAE is 0.1182 meV/atom. RKA provides a compact global communication pathway that improves energy and force prediction on the evaluated polymer-derived clusters.

</details>

<a id="paper-93"></a>

### 93. [OT-CAL: Optimal-Transport-Guided Adaptive Sample Selection for Efficient Visual Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41677v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41677

**作者：** Suryansh Talukdar、Jin Soung Yoo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41677v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41677v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 OT-CAL：用难度、可学性、不确定性等五个信息量特征刻画样本，以课程向量表述当前偏好的样本特性，并通过最优传输匹配分配样本选择权重，仅对部分样本做反向传播，从而在视觉分类训练中自适应调整选择偏好并提速。

**相关性简评：** 其「特征表示—目标课程向量—最优传输匹配—反馈更新」机制是纯训练侧的样本筛选，与推理期 latent 状态无直接关系，但其按信息量与可靠性动态分配计算与监督的思路，可能可供核查用于 LVR 中视觉证据筛选或轨迹训练监督的贡献分配。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** OT-CAL: Optimal-Transport-Guided Adaptive Sample Selection for Efficient Visual Learning

**abstract/A1：** Deep neural networks for image recognition are typically trained using mini-batches, with a forward pass to compute predictions and losses followed by a more computationally expensive backward pass to update model parameters. However, not all training samples contribute equally to learning, suggesting that computation can be reduced by performing backward passes only on informative samples. The challenge is that sample informativeness changes throughout training: difficult samples may be valuable early on, improving samples become important later, and class diversity becomes increasingly relevant toward the end. Existing methods often rely on fixed selection rules or computationally expensive per-sample gradient calculations. We propose OT-CAL, an adaptive data selection method that automatically adjusts its sampling preferences during training. OT-CAL represents each sample using five informativeness features, including difficulty, learnability, and uncertainty. A curriculum vector describes the currently preferred sample characteristics, and optimal-transport-based matching assigns selection weights according to how closely samples match this target. The curriculum vector is then updated using the selected samples, forming a feedback loop that adapts without a manually defined schedule. Experiments on three standard visual learning benchmarks show that, when selecting 50% of each mini-batch, OT-CAL improves performance by 1.3--2.1 points over AdaSelection while achieving 1.18--1.24 times faster training than full-data training.

**comment/C1：** 9 pages, 3 figures. Submitted to the IEEE BigData 2026 Workshop on Efficient AI

</details>

<a id="paper-94"></a>

### 94. [TSBPO: Tree-Structured Branching for Policy Optimization in Interactive Language Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41697v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41697

**作者：** Rui Yu、Chong Mu、Jinchuan Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41697v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41697v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 TSBPO 训练范式，用 Root/Branch 分配器在固定 rollout 预算下采样更易产生对比性终局的任务与状态续接，并在回放树上以兄弟段比较和同状态分组比较做延迟反馈的优势分配，同时按完整回答求和、跨轮归一化 token 损失；在 ALFWorld 与 WebShop 上报告成功率的消融对比。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本交互智能体，无视觉与 latent 状态，但其「在匹配状态下对比结果以分配延迟反馈」和分支采样式贡献分配，可供核查 LVR 中多步 latent 推理的信用分配与 rollout 预算投向对比性证据的问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TSBPO: Tree-Structured Branching for Policy Optimization in Interactive Language Agents

**abstract/A1：** Training interactive language agents from terminal outcomes poses three coupled problems: which task and state continuations to sample, how to assign delayed feedback to decisions, and how to aggregate token losses for multi-token actions. We present TSBPO, a tree-structured sampling-to-update paradigm that addresses these problems within one training loop. A likelihood-fitted Root/Branch allocator uses retained task and state evidence to target continuations likely to produce contrasting terminal outcomes under fixed rollout budgets. On the resulting replayed physical trees, trajectory advantages compare immediate sibling segments, while observation-group step advantages compare outcomes at matched task/observation states. The actor then sums clipped token surrogates over each complete response and normalizes across active turns, avoiding response-length averaging in the policy-gradient term. With a Qwen2.5-1.5B actor, fixed checkpoints obtain 97.79% seen and 96.64% unseen stochastic success on ALFWorld and an 81.07% per-episode success rate over six stochastic episodes for each of the 500 WebShop test goals. A WebShop step-term ablation yields 75.93% at the same update-100 evaluation; an ALFWorld aggregation comparison shows higher observed command validity with complete-turn weighting. These results support the evaluated training configuration on two interactive benchmarks.

</details>

<a id="paper-95"></a>

### 95. [Hierarchical Residuals: A Minimalist Decoupling of Residual Width from Compute Width](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41711v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41711

**作者：** Ziteng Wang、Jianqiao Lu、Xinyu Ma、Jintao Zhang、Jun Zhu、Yiyuan Ma、Jianfei Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41711v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41711v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 HiRes：把层分组，块内仍用标准 d 维 PreNorm 层，块间用更宽的 D 维 PostNorm 外残差，并以可学习的读写投影在块边界衔接，从而将残差状态宽度与每层计算宽度解耦；在多种规模与 OLMoE-1B-7B 预训练上优于普通残差相加。

**相关性简评：** 其「加宽残差状态、读写投影跨空间桥接」可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态容量与视觉信息在深层保持提供一种架构杠杆，且只用标准算子、成本可控，可供核查是否缓解 latent 推理的信息坍缩，但原文未涉及多模态或推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Hierarchical Residuals: A Minimalist Decoupling of Residual Width from Compute Width

**abstract/A1：** Residual connections underpin Transformers, but they implicitly tie the residual width carried through depth to the compute width used inside each layer. We introduce Hierarchical Residuals (HiRes), a minimalist architectural change that decouples these dimensions, widening the residual state while keeping per-layer compute width fixed. HiRes groups layers into blocks: standard d-dimensional PreNorm layers operate inside each block, while a wider D-dimensional PostNorm outer residual connects blocks. Learned read/write projections bridge the two spaces at block boundaries, leaving individual Transformer layers unchanged. Across different model sizes and depths, HiRes consistently outperforms vanilla residual addition, with fitted scaling laws indicating a 1.30-fold improvement in FLOPs efficiency. On OLMoE-1B-7B pretraining for 500B tokens, HiRes achieves 0.0208 lower training loss and 1.14-point higher downstream accuracy. HiRes is also competitive with other residual expansion methods such as VWN, mHC and AttnRes with less activation memory and I/O costs. Built from only dense projections and normalizations, it requires neither specialized kernels nor infrastructure-level optimizations. HiRes thus exposes residual width as a practical scaling variable using only standard operators.

</details>

<a id="paper-96"></a>

### 96. [UpLoop: Training Looped Transformers via Upcycling at No Extra Pretraining Compute](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41720v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41720

**作者：** Cheng Liang、Tengfei Zhang、Ya Zhang、Yanfeng Wang、Zhouhan Lin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41720v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41720v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 UpLoop 预训练方案：先用浅层 Transformer，再通过复制已学层逐步增加深度，并把早期节省的算力用于后期重复执行共享中间块，在参数量、训练 token 数和训练 FLOPs 与稠密基线对齐的条件下训练 looped Transformer。

**相关性简评：** 其共享块的循环执行与训练期算力重分配，可能为 LVR 的循环式推理状态更新和训练预算分配提供方法线索，但纯文本设定与视觉证据、动态停止等问题的联系仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** UpLoop: Training Looped Transformers via Upcycling at No Extra Pretraining Compute

**abstract/A1：** Looped language models can strengthen reasoning through repeated computation with shared weights, increasing execution depth without adding independent parameters. However, each additional pass increases training floating-point operations (FLOPs), making these gains more expensive to obtain. To address this cost, we propose UpLoop, a pretraining schedule that uses upcycling to train looped Transformers while exactly matching a standard dense baseline in final parameter count, total training tokens, and training FLOPs. Specifically, UpLoop begins with a shallow Transformer, progressively grows its depth by copying learned layers, and uses the computation saved early to support repeated execution of a shared middle block later in training. Experiments across model scales show improvements in language modeling and downstream performance, with the average score across 22 downstream tasks increasing from 22.99 to 27.52 at 1.3B parameters. These gains also extend to mathematical reasoning after supervised fine-tuning.

</details>

<a id="paper-97"></a>

### 97. [Task Vectors Can Be Linearly Decomposed into Interpretable and Controllable Components](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41743v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41743

**作者：** Yukiya Horiba、Hakaze Cho、Takahiro Yakoh

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41743v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41743v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究 Task Vector 的内部功能结构，借助候选标签 unembedding 将单个 TV 分解为任务识别（TR）、任务学习（TL）与残差成分，并用 Bayesian optimization 优化 TR/TL 系数做干预，在多种模型与分类任务上验证分解后的方向更有效且具有可解释的 logit 效应。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本 steering 工作，但「把单一隐向量分解为功能子成分并优化干预系数」的思路可供核查是否能用于分离 latent 推理状态中的任务识别与推理更新、或用于多 latent 假设的筛选与控制；原文未涉及视觉证据或推理期状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Task Vectors Can Be Linearly Decomposed into Interpretable and Controllable Components

**abstract/A1：** Task Vectors (TVs) provide a compact way to steer large language models, but they are typically treated as atomized directions merely extracted from hidden states, leaving their internal functional structure poorly understood. Motivated by prior work that decomposes in-context learning into Task Recognition (TR) and Task Learning (TL), we ask whether a single TV can likewise be decomposed into functionally meaningful components. We formulate a TR/TL-based decomposition from candidate-label unembeddings, separating each TV into TR, TL, and residual components, and try to optimize the TR and TL coefficients using Bayesian optimization for more effective intervention. We evaluate this formulation across multiple model families, classification tasks, and both instruction- and demonstration-based TVs. Across these settings, TR/TL-based optimization identifies intervention directions that outperform the original TVs. Although optimization over random directions can also improve performance, TR/TL-based search yields more reliable gains, particularly for instruction-based TVs. Moreover, isolated interventions on the decomposed components produce distinct logit-level effects that are consistent with their intended functional roles. Together, these results suggest that TVs are not monolithic steering directions, but can be decomposed into functionally meaningful components that support both interpretation and control.

</details>

<a id="paper-98"></a>

### 98. [Measuring What Matters: A Causal Reliance Score for Histopathology Image Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41763v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41763

**作者：** Samuel Thomas、Daniel Tweneboah Anyimadu、Ahmed Karam Eldaly、Somaia Elsheikh、Mohammed M. Abdelsamea

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41763v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41763v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出因果启发式评测框架：通过对前景组织与背景／域变化的受控干预，给出 Causal Reliance Score，量化分割模型对病理相关结构与伪相关背景的相对敏感度，并用训练期增广作为隐式反事实干预，以 CAUSE-ViT 为例展示 Dice／IoU 未反映的行为差异。

**相关性简评：** 「干预—敏感度」式审计可供核查能否迁移到判断 VLM 推理是否真正使用视觉证据而非语言或背景先验，帮助诊断 latent 状态是否保留任务必要信息；但论文限于医学图像分割，尚无推理轨迹与 latent 生成层面的分析。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Measuring What Matters: A Causal Reliance Score for Histopathology Image Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Reliable histopathology segmentation requires models that rely on pathology-relevant structures rather than spurious background correlations. In this work, we argue that improving architectures alone is insufficient if models continue to rely on spurious correlations rather than pathology-relevant features. We introduce a causality-inspired evaluation framework based on controlled interventions that explicitly probes model sensitivity to foreground tissue structure and background/domain variation. Building on this, we propose the Causal Reliance Score (CRS), a metric that quantifies the relative sensitivity of model predictions to foreground perturbation versus background/domain variation under controlled interventions. To support this analysis, we design augmentation strategies that act as implicit counterfactual interventions during training, encouraging invariance to nuisance factors while preserving morphology. We demonstrate how these interventions reveal behavioural differences between models that are not captured by standard metrics such as Dice or IoU. While we instantiate our framework using a self-supervised transformer (CAUSE-ViT), the primary contribution of this work is not a new architecture, but a principled intervention-based methodology and evaluation metric for auditing model behaviour in medical image segmentation. Our results suggest that evaluating model behaviour through counterfactual interventions is essential for developing reliable and clinically robust medical image segmentation systems.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages, 8 figures, accepted into Medical Image Understanding and Analysis (MIUA) 2026 conference

</details>

<a id="paper-99"></a>

### 99. [Hard Sample-Conditioned Decorrelated Attention for Robust Face Recognition](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41789v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41789

**作者：** Tahar Chettaoui、Guray Ozgur、Eduarda Caldeira、Naser Damer、Fadi Boutros

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41789v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41789v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ViT-HarDis：依据样本与竞争身份的余弦相似度高低调节注意力头去相关正则，对困难样本施加更强的头解耦，并发现只在最后几层解耦优于全网正则，从而在不改动结构、不增加推理开销的前提下提升人脸识别。

**相关性简评：** 其「按样本难度调节注意力头解耦强度、且仅作用于末层」的训练思路，可能可供核查 LVR 中多头/多步 latent 状态是否冗余互补及困难样本的监督分配，但论文面向人脸识别判别嵌入，与推理期 latent 生成无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Hard Sample-Conditioned Decorrelated Attention for Robust Face Recognition

**abstract/A1：** Multi-head attention in Vision Transformers (ViTs) allows independent attention patterns across heads, but without explicit regularization, heads may learn redundant patterns rather than complementary roles. Correlation-based penalties encourage head disentanglement, yet apply uniform regularization across all training samples. In face recognition (FR), hard samples whose embeddings lie close to competing identities are critical for optimization and generalization. Motivated by this, we propose ViT-HarDis, which conditions attention head disentanglement on sample difficulty: samples with high cosine similarity to negative class centers receive stronger head decorrelation, enforcing specialization where identity ambiguity is highest. Experiments show improvements over both the baseline and uniform disentanglement on most benchmarks. Our analysis shows that ViT-HarDis reduces the similarity of hard samples to competing class centers while decreasing inter-head attention similarity, and that applying disentanglement only to the final transformer layers outperforms regularizing the entire network. Trained on WebFace4M, ViT-HarDis achieves SOTA results among ViT-based methods on IJB-B, IJB-C, and TinyFace Rank-1, while achieving the best IJB-B and TinyFace results when trained on MS1MV3, improving FR without architectural modifications or additional inference cost. Code: https://github.com/TaharChettaoui/Hard-Sample-Conditioned-Attention-for-FR.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the IEEE/CVF Winter Conference on Applications of Computer Vision (WACV 2027)

</details>

<a id="paper-100"></a>

### 100. [Accurate and Auditable ECG Classification via Visibility Graph Neural Networks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41795v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41795

**作者：** Artur Xavier、Guilherme Henrique Gomes Evangelista、Aitazaz Khan、Antonio Luiz Pinho Ribeiro、Mohammed J. Zaki、Srinivasan Parthasarathy、Wagner Meira

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41795v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41795v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 LYNX-ECG，把每次心搏表示为带时间戳转折点的稀疏可见图并用 GNN 分类 ECG，同时通过删除临床定义的波形区域、改动心搏节律来审计预测依据。

**相关性简评：** 其「节点对应具体时间点」的稀疏表示与按区域删除的因果审计，可供核查是否能迁移为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与贡献分配的评价手段，但论文本身不含多模态推理实验。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Accurate and Auditable ECG Classification via Visibility Graph Neural Networks

**abstract/A1：** Physicians read electrocardiograms (ECGs) from waveform shape and timing, yet accurate deep-learning classifiers rarely show which waveform regions drive their predictions. We introduce LYNX-ECG, a graph neural network that represents each heartbeat as a sparse visibility graph of timestamped turning points. On CODE-Test, LYNX-ECG reaches a macro F1 score of 0.889, outperforming five baselines including the ECGFounder foundation model, while its graphs need about 14% of the storage of graphs built from every sample. Because each node maps to a specific ECG time, we can delete clinically defined waveform regions and alter beat timing to audit predictions. For conduction disorders, the clinically expected region was the strongest evidence in more than 90% of correct predictions. Bradycardia and tachycardia calls follow heart rate with conduction intervals held fixed, and atrial fibrillation calls vanish when beat-to-beat variation is removed. Sparse visibility graphs thus pair accurate ECG classification with clinically grounded, testable explanations.

**comment/C1：** 43 pages, 10 figures, 21 tables, including Supplementary Information

</details>

<a id="paper-101"></a>

### 101. [Backpropagation-free Deep Learning for Time-Series Classification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41805v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41805

**作者：** Miha Kapš、Blaž Bertalanič、Carolina Fortuna

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41805v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41805v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文将 DFA、DRTP 与 NoProp 三种免反向传播学习规则迁移到一维卷积的单变量时间序列分类，在 112 个 UCR 数据集上统一调参与系统比较，并用池化替换展平读出、以双重差分检验卷积特征是否真正学到。

**相关性简评：** 其免全局反向传播的信用分配思路与「卷积特征近乎随机」的诊断，可能为训练多步或循环 latent 推理状态时的梯度传播与监督分配提供核查线索，但不涉及视觉或多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Backpropagation-free Deep Learning for Time-Series Classification

**abstract/A1：** Deep neural networks for time series classification (TSC) are trained almost exclusively by error backpropagation, which imposes update locking and the weight-transport problem. Backpropagation-free learning rules such as Direct Feedback Alignment (DFA), Direct Random Target Projection (DRTP), and the diffusion-inspired NoProp relax these constraints but have been evaluated almost exclusively on 2D image benchmarks. We adapt these three learners to univariate TSC with 1D convolutional architectures and present the first systematic study of them across 112 datasets from the UCR time series archive, with one hyperparameter-optimized configuration per method. The optimized learners reach mean F1 scores of 0.76 to 0.79, below ROCKET (0.83) and InceptionTime (0.84). We propose an analysis framework that relates the per-dataset gap to InceptionTime to dataset properties and tests convolutional feature learning by replacing the flatten readout with global average pooling, measured as a difference-in-differences (DD) against a backpropagation control on the identical architecture. Intra-class temporal variability is the dataset property most consistently associated with the gap for all three learners (partial Spearman $ρ$ from $0.28$ to $0.40$). Pooling harms DFA and DRTP more than backpropagation (DD $-0.025$ and $-0.055$), consistent with near-random convolutional features, whereas NoProp tolerates pooling as backpropagation does (DD $+0.013$). Relative to backpropagation on the same architectures, DRTP reduces training floating-point operations (FLOPs) by 33% and trains faster only on series longer than about 700 time steps, DFA yields no computational reduction, and NoProp reduces peak memory by 38% at the cost of 48% longer training.

**comment/C1：** 16 pages, 12 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-102"></a>

### 102. [RECAST: REconstruction of Chain-of-Thought via Auditable Structured Traces](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41813v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41813

**作者：** Qi Wang、Kang Xu、Chengcheng Wan、Jiangtao Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41813v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41813v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文先复现三种 CoT 抽取方法，发现它们在闭源前沿模型的常规接口上因推理字段缺失、工具参数被过滤而全部失效，转而提出 RECAST：以问题与目标模型最终答案为条件，让解码器重建第一人称结构化推理链，清洗后构建可供 SFT 的数据，并在 Claude-Sonnet-5.5 上实例化、在另两个模型上做定性尝试。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理无直接关联，但其「仅有答案即可重建推理轨迹并转成监督数据」的流程，可能为 LVR 在缺少 latent 状态标注时的轨迹监督与贡献分配提供可核查线索；重建轨迹是否忠实反映真实计算并未验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RECAST: REconstruction of Chain-of-Thought via Auditable Structured Traces

**abstract/A1：** Large closed-source reasoning models expose only their final answers. The intermediate reasoning that produces those answers is hidden behind API surfaces, tool-call policies, and safety filters. Existing chain-of-thought (CoT) extraction methods assume a side channel: a visible reasoning field, a scratchpad carried through tool calls, or a signed/encrypted reasoning block. We reproduce three representative state-of-the-art extraction methods and find that, on Claude-Sonnet-5.5 accessed through the standard chat-completions interface, all three return zero CoT tokens. The failure is structural rather than a bug: the reasoning field is absent, named tool selection is rejected, free-text scratchpad arguments are filtered, and no replayable reasoning block is exposed. We therefore take a different route. We present RECAST, a reconstruction-based framework that conditions a decoder on the question and the target model's final answer, reconstructs a first-person structured rationale, cleans it, and builds SFT-ready data. To the best of our knowledge, RECAST is the first system to reconstruct CoT from frontier closed models such as Claude-Sonnet-5.5, GPT-6.1, and Gemini 3 without using hidden reasoning channels. We instantiate RECAST on Claude-Sonnet-5.5 and additionally obtain successful qualitative reconstructions on GPT-6.1 and Gemini 3 with the same answer-only protocol. The system is open-source and packaged as an agent skill: one prompt deploys it and runs the complete pipeline to produce SFT-ready CoT data. This preprint describes the system, its threat model, and a controlled artifact validation; it does not claim large-scale benchmark improvements.

</details>

<a id="paper-103"></a>

### 103. [ElTab: Elastically Looped Tabular Transformers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41819v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41819

**作者：** Mieszko Komisarczyk、Saurabh Mathur、Maurice Kraus、Sriraam Natarajan、Kristian Kersting

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41819v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41819v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ElTab，通过对随机早退位置施加监督，训练单一循环式表格 Transformer 适应不同计算预算，并在 30 个 OpenML 分类数据集上以更低训练与推理成本匹配特定深度模型。

**相关性简评：** 虽面向表格任务，其随机早退的弹性深度训练可能为 LVR 中循环计算预算、早停决策与长度泛化提供训练监督线索，值得核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ElTab: Elastically Looped Tabular Transformers

**abstract/A1：** Tabular transformer models such as TabPFN achieve strong predictive performance but incur substantial inference costs, making them difficult to deploy in resource-constrained settings. While looped transformers reduce parameter count through weight sharing, they can lose significant predictive power when stopped early. To address this challenge, we introduce the Elastically Looped Tabular Transformer (ElTab), a single model trained to operate across different computational budgets through supervision at randomly sampled early exits. Across 30 OpenML classification datasets, a single ElTab model matches six depth-specific looped models while cutting training cost by more than half, and matches a 12-layer TabPFN-style baseline with 25% fewer inference FLOPs. These results show that elastic-depth training yields a single model that adapts to the deployment budget at hand, offering a favorable trade-off among predictive performance, storage, and computational cost.

</details>

<a id="paper-104"></a>

### 104. [Trainable Vector Quantization for Large Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41822v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41822

**作者：** Anastasiia Prutianova、Alina Bogdanova、Mikhail Shutov、Dmitrii Poimanov、Anatoly Gorkov、Ilia Zharikov

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41822v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41822v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对向量量化压缩 LLM 权重在极低位下性能退化的问题，提出 TVQ：用 straight-through estimation 让量化后的模型以可训练 latent 权重做梯度下降优化，并结合 block-wise 知识蒸馏与加速训练技巧，在 2-bit 等低比特场景取得较好质量。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理主线无直接关系，但「离散量化参数 + STE + 可训练 latent 权重」的优化方式，可供核查其是否适用于训练含离散 latent 状态或码本的 LVR 模型。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Trainable Vector Quantization for Large Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Vector Quantization (VQ) is a rapidly evolving technique for compressing Large Language Model (LLM) weights, reducing memory usage, and speeding up inference. However, extreme compression deteriorates model performance, which is challenging to recover due to the discrete nature of VQ parameters. Existing methods employ training-free approaches that are limited to layer-wise reconstruction loss minimization. To address this limitation, we propose TVQ, a novel method that leverages straight-through estimation to enable gradient descent-based optimization of vector-quantized LLMs. By reformulating VQ LLM optimization with trainable latent weights, TVQ enables arbitrary objective minimization. We constitute our approach by applying TVQ in a block-wise knowledge distillation paradigm, which balances quality and training efficiency, and propose several practical solutions to accelerate training. In experiments on modern LLMs, we demonstrate that TVQ is compatible with modern VQ schemes. As a result, TVQ achieves state-of-the-art quality in low-bit post-training quantization settings with a modest training budget and straightforward PyTorch implementation. For instance, TVQ achieves near-lossless 2-bit quantization for LLaMA2-70B with only 1% accuracy drop on popular Question-Answering benchmarks.

**comment/C1：** ECML PKDD 2026, Research Track

</details>

<a id="paper-105"></a>

### 105. [ReuScope: Auditing Data Reuse and Evaluation Sensitivity in Capsule Vision 2024](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41835v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41835

**作者：** Eunseob Choi、Kyeonghun Kim、Hyuk-Jae Lee、Nam-Joon Kim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41835v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41835v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 ReuScope 审计协议，以 Capsule Vision 2024 为案例，将训练-评测数据关系度量与「固定预测换评测总体」「固定评测换训练数据」两组比较结合，区分已观察到的数据复用与条件性性能敏感性，并配以匹配控制和不确定性单位说明。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 无直接关联，但其「去除某类输入 vs 匹配对照 + 双重差分」的审计式评价设计，可能可供核查视觉证据或 latent 内容对答案的因果贡献时借鉴，并提示需明确结论所对应的总体与不确定性单位。潜在迁移需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ReuScope: Auditing Data Reuse and Evaluation Sensitivity in Capsule Vision 2024

**abstract/A1：** We develop ReuScope, a protocol for auditing data reuse and evaluation sensitivity, using Capsule Vision 2024 as a case study. It combines measurements of data relationships with two comparisons: changing the evaluation population while holding predictions fixed, and changing training data while holding evaluation fixed. Byte-identical files cross the public training-validation boundary, and all 41 Kvasir-Capsule recordings that contribute frames occur in both partitions. Shared source recordings establish cross-split overlap; missing cross-recording patient identifiers prevent counting unique shared patients. Across ten paired seeds, removing all training rows attributed to Kvasir-Capsule, plus five rows from other sources flagged as near-matches to it, lowers public-validation balanced accuracy from 82.5% to 61.2%. Evaluating the same fitted models on the validation rows of the other sources reverses the comparison: removal improves balanced accuracy by 1.38 percentage points. The public-validation drop measures sensitivity to source removal, not an amount of leakage-induced inflation. Matched controls show that sample size and class counts alone do not explain the observed score differences. An exploratory experiment removes the training neighbors of validation frames that have close training matches, or equally many distant training rows, and yields a difference-in-differences estimate, relative to matched unflagged frames, of -0.355 percentage points in true-class probability; the paired-t interval for accuracy spans zero. The resulting procedure distinguishes observed reuse from conditional performance sensitivity and specifies the population, class support and uncertainty unit for each conclusion.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026, Track on Evaluations and Datasets. 39 pages, 3 figures. Code and data: https://github.com/eunseobchoi/ReuScope

</details>
