### 106. [Fuzzy selective state model for forecasting the dynamics of consumer preferences](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41879v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41879

**作者：** P. A. Golovinski、A. A. Drobyshev、A. O. Shatalova

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41879v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41879v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文用三角模糊数刻画零售客户偏好，构建 Mamba 型选择性状态空间模型：隐藏状态为三角模糊数，在 Matveev 二元模糊数代数下递推，参数由清晰特征计算，并证明状态始终保持为过去观测的凸组合、模糊度不随时间累积，最终在合成切换场景上做了预测与语言化读出。

**相关性简评：** 其「隐状态为模糊量、以非负线性递推保持凸组合从而不累积不确定性」的状态更新机制，可能为 LVR 中承载不确定性的 latent 工作区状态与长链推理下的信息保持提供可核查线索，但该约束能否迁移到多模态视觉状态尚待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Fuzzy selective state model for forecasting the dynamics of consumer preferences

**abstract/A1：** We consider forecasting the dynamics of consumer preferences of retail chain customers. A customer's preference is described by a triangular fuzzy number. The evolution of the preference over time is governed by a Mamba-type selective state space model whose hidden state is a triangular number, with computations carried out in M.G.~Matveev's two-component algebra of fuzzy numbers. The recurrence parameters are computed from crisp features, while the fuzzy part of the model remains linear with non-negative coefficients. We show that the state then always remains a proper triangular number and is a convex combination of past observations, so that the fuzziness of the forecast does not accumulate over time and the algebraic computations coincide with Zadeh's extension principle. On a synthetic example with competitor promotions and customer switching, a lightweight 107-parameter model reaches a mean absolute error of the forecast centre of 0.154 versus 0.163 for the constant-inertia evolution operator and 0.179 for the naive forecast, matches a crisp model of the same architecture and additionally provides a linguistically interpretable estimate of forecast vagueness. We also show how the linguistic readout of the forecast can be used to detect a permanent switch of a customer to a competitor.

</details>

<a id="paper-107"></a>

### 107. [ReX-GG: a LLM Ensemble Pipeline for Relation-extraction and Graph Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41901v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41901

**作者：** Giacomo Magnifico、Eduard Barbu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41901v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41901v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出一个可定制的轻量级 LLM 集成流水线，用 JSON 结构化输出与匿名互评排序来抑制幻觉和单模型失败，并在英文科普文章的关系抽取与因果图生成任务上用用户研究评估感知准确率。

**相关性简评：** 其「匿名互评排序」与结构化输出约束可作为候选推理状态验证、评分与选择的借鉴线索，供核查能否用于 latent 内容的筛选与停止决策；但任务本身为文本关系抽取，未涉及视觉或多模态推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ReX-GG: a LLM Ensemble Pipeline for Relation-extraction and Graph Generation

**abstract/A1：** Current LLM ensemble frameworks focus on multi-step setups with additional modules for answer ranking, often opting for token and span analysis rather than structured outputs, leading to heavyweight architectures with potential fail states along the pipeline. Faster, lighter solutions are more vulnerable to hallucination propagation and can lack output control in more complex pipelines. This paper proposes a customisable, lightweight ensemble workflow of coordinated Large Language Models that leverages JSON-structured outputs and anonymous peer-review ranking to mitigate hallucinatory outputs and single-model failure points. The pipeline is demonstrated on a relation extraction task applied to popular science articles in English, targeting four ontologically-grounded relation types (strong causation, weak causation, contrastive, and compositional), with semantic node canonicalisation and interactive, colour-coded HTML causal graphs as the final output. Performance is evaluated through an anonymous user study, achieving an average perceived accuracy of 0.778 against a human-annotated gold standard. The modular architecture supports flexible deployment across both API-based and in-house LLM setups, and the full framework is released under an open license to foster reproducibility and collaborative research.

**comment/C1：** 9 pages, 4 figures, accepted at KG&amp;LLM @ LREC 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-108"></a>

### 108. [Optimizing Stochastic Policy Geometry for Few-Step Flow Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41949v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41949

**作者：** Zhuguanyu Wu、Congjia Chen、Ruihao Gong、Yang Yong、Shiqiao Gu、Yi Xu、Xianglong Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41949v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41949v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对少步 flow 模型的 GRPO 训练，指出当单次去噪跨越很宽噪声区间时随机策略的选择缺少准则，提出以「给定速度更新下条件 KL 最小」为选择原则，在保系数的高斯族内化为单一几何因子，给出闭式最优解，并配合梯度尺度匹配的权重，在图像与视频生成 4 步设置下提升奖励。

**相关性简评：** 其随机转移几何、KL 与梯度尺度归一化的选择准则，或可为扩散式 latent 推理状态的随机更新设计与训练稳定性提供可核查线索，但原文面向生成模型 RL，与 VLM 推理无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Optimizing Stochastic Policy Geometry for Few-Step Flow Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Few-step flow models generate samples through a handful of large denoising transitions. Applying GRPO to such models requires introducing stochasticity into the denoising transitions, and when a single transition spans a wide range of noise levels, the choice of the resulting stochastic policy becomes a major factor in optimization. Recent constructions constrain the policy to preserve the target flow coefficients, but this constraint admits a family of policies and offers no criterion for choosing among them. We introduce Geo-FSRL (Geometry-Optimized Few-Step Reinforcement Learning), built on a selection principle: at a fixed conditioning state and timestep pair, choose the admissible policy that minimizes the conditional KL divergence induced by any given nonzero velocity-output update. Within a coefficient-preserving family of isotropic Gaussian transitions, this criterion reduces to minimizing a single scalar geometry factor that determines the conditional KL for every velocity-output update. The same factor governs velocity-space Fisher information and conditional transition log-likelihood-ratio variance. We derive the unique closed-form optimizer within this family, shared across all nonzero update magnitudes and directions, with transition coefficients determined by both the source and target noise levels. Because the minimized geometry factor still varies across timesteps, we further adopt gradient-scale matching from prior work and derive closed-form policy-gradient and KL weights that normalize explicit score scales and reference-KL curvature. Geo-FSRL trains on the original few-step schedule and retains the deterministic solver at inference. Our method achieves higher final reward scores than Flow-GRPO and Flow-CPS on both image generation and video generation models under the 4-step setting. Code is available at https://github.com/ModelTC/LightX2V/tree/main/lightx2v\_rl

</details>

<a id="paper-109"></a>

### 109. [MoETok: Sparse Expert Routing for 1D Visual Tokenizers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41992v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41992

**作者：** Jiahao Guo、Bao Tang、Haoxiang Cao、Jingfeng Yao、Bo Li、Wenyu Liu、Kun Gai、Changqian Yu、Xinggang Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41992v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41992v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 MoETok，一种 1D 视觉 tokenizer：发现可学习 query 在语义监督下分化为少量 Content query 与多数 Detail query，并用稀疏专家路由按 token 角色进行差异化处理，联合重建与语义目标训练，以提升重建、语义表示和自回归生成。

**相关性简评：** 其内容/细节 token 分工与稀疏路由可能为 LVR 中分离语义抽象与细粒度视觉证据、按 token 角色分配计算提供线索，但需核查路由信号是否能迁移到推理期 latent 状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MoETok: Sparse Expert Routing for 1D Visual Tokenizers

**abstract/A1：** Modern visual tokenizers aim to preserve fine-grained visual details while learning high-level semantic representations for downstream generation and understanding. Query-based 1D visual tokenizers encode images into flexible latent sequences, yet how individual learnable queries gather and encode image information remains poorly understood. We reveal that these queries spontaneously develop complementary roles under semantic supervision: a small set of Content queries captures high-level semantic information, while a larger set of Detail queries preserves fine-grained visual details for reconstruction. To adapt token processing to these heterogeneous roles, we introduce MoETok, a 1D visual tokenizer that routes tokens through sparsely activated experts. Jointly optimized with reconstruction and semantic objectives, the router learns role-specialized processing paths adapted to each token's representation. Across model scales and token budgets, MoETok consistently improves reconstruction, semantic representation, and autoregressive generation over dense tokenizers under matched supervision and active computation. On ImageNet, it combines strong reconstruction and semantic representation with state-of-the-art class-conditional generation within the 1D tokenizer family. We further validate MoETok at scale in a unified autoregressive model jointly trained for understanding and generation with a next-token cross-entropy objective. The resulting model combines competitive visual understanding with state-of-the-art generation among unified discrete models, matching or surpassing diffusion-based models such as BAGEL and Qwen-Image on benchmarks including DPG-Bench (87.61), GenEval2 (40.04 GM), and WISE (0.49).

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://gjhhust.github.io/moetok-project

</details>

<a id="paper-110"></a>

### 110. [DAMI: Dual-Axis Memory Initialization for Enhanced Long-Context Retrieval in Gated Linear Attention](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41994v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41994

**作者：** Zhangchi Feng、Harry Jiang、Yangyifei Luo、Ray Zhang、Richong Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41994v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41994v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对门控线性注意力有限状态记忆限制长上下文检索的问题，提出 DAMI：在训练前联合配置门控参数 A\_log 与 dt\_bias，按头分配几何时间尺度、按层设定低-高-低输入敏感度，并用少量激活样本校准实现，在 GDN、KDA、Mamba-2、RWKV-7 上提升 RULER 类检索准确率。

**相关性简评：** 其按头、按层分配状态衰减时间尺度与输入敏感度的做法，可能与 LVR 中 latent 状态的保留时长、视觉证据跨步留存及长度泛化相关，可供核查；但工作为纯文本架构，未涉及多模态验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DAMI: Dual-Axis Memory Initialization for Enhanced Long-Context Retrieval in Gated Linear Attention

**abstract/A1：** Linear attention scales linearly with sequence length, but its finite-state memory can limit long-context retrieval. In gated linear recurrences, memory retention depends on state decay, which is shaped jointly by gate parameters A\_log and dt\_bias and by input activations. In controlled Gated DeltaNet (GDN) experiments, both parameters remain highly correlated with their initial values after training, highlighting the importance of initialization. Therefore, we introduce Dual-Axis Memory Initialization (DAMI), which jointly configures these parameters before training. DAMI assigns geometric timescales across heads for varied retrieval distances and a low-high-low input-sensitivity profile across layers. A small activation sample calibrates the GDN-family implementation to match both behavioral targets without adding trainable parameters. Across GDN, KDA, GDN2, Mamba-2, and RWKV-7, DAMI improves average RULER-style retrieval accuracy by 5.29 to 16.71 percentage points over the corresponding random-initialization baselines. Performance on language modeling, commonsense reasoning, and 2K real-world retrieval remains broadly comparable to those baselines. Our code is available at https://speedai.com/research/dami.

</details>

<a id="paper-111"></a>

### 111. [Less Loss, Fixed Weights: Logit Corrections for Forgetting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42000v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.42000

**作者：** Hanyu Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42000v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.42000v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 在14对1B–7B基座与派生模型上，拟合固定词表偏置（单独或配合温度）以检验简单的输出端 logit 修正能否挽回微调或继续预训练带来的损失；结果显示恢复有限（偏置不足10%，加温度最高36%），数学继续预训练几乎无法恢复。

**相关性简评：** 其「固定偏置+温度」的输出端校正可作为核查 LVR 收益是否仅源于输出分布校准的对照基线，负结果也暗示损失主要来自内部状态而非输出层；但属纯文本、非多模态工作，迁移线索待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Less Loss, Fixed Weights: Logit Corrections for Forgetting

**abstract/A1：** When a language model is fine-tuned to follow instructions, or further pretrained on a new subject, it often gets worse at predicting standard web text. This is known as "forgetting." We ask whether this loss can be undone by simple, fixed adjustments to the model's final token predictions. Across 14 pairs of base and derived models, from 1B to 7B parameters, we fitted a fixed bonus or penalty for each token (a vocabulary bias), alone or together with a temperature. On text held out from correction fitting, the largest top-4K bias recovery estimate is under 10%, with loss conditional on the retained vocabulary. Combining the bias with a temperature recovers up to 36%. Models further pretrained on math lose the most and recover almost nothing. Finally, in a controlled simulation where a model is damaged at random, with no planted bias or temperature change, the bias-plus-temperature fix still recovers 29%. Most of the real-model loss increase stays out of reach of our restricted fits.

**comment/C1：** 8 pages, 3 figures, 2 tables. Code and data: https://github.com/SeverinVisionary/prior-shift-diagnostic

</details>

<a id="paper-112"></a>

### 112. [Know When the Equation Is Wrong: Structural Uncertainty for LLM-Guided Symbolic Regression under Extrapolation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42024v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.42024

**作者：** Mostafa Cham、Bayu Adhi Tama、Jianwu Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42024v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.42024v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 LLM-SR-UQ，在 LLM 引导的符号回归中保留多个候选方程结构，用结构间分歧构造「结构区间」，以改善外推条件下的不确定性覆盖。

**相关性简评：** 保留多个竞争假设并以分歧量化不确定性、据此判断何时扩大区间的做法，可能为 LVR 中多 latent 假设的分歧度量与停止/证据筛选决策提供可核查的方法线索，但任务领域与视觉推理相距较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Know When the Equation Is Wrong: Structural Uncertainty for LLM-Guided Symbolic Regression under Extrapolation

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) can help discover mathematical equations from data, but most LLM-guided symbolic regression methods focus on a single best equation without quantifying uncertainty over alternative functional forms. This is challenging because distinct equations can fit the observed data similarly while producing substantially different extrapolations. Our experiments on the LLM-SRBench dataset show that standard conformal intervals achieve nominal in-distribution coverage but frequently fail under extrapolation. To tackle this challenge, we introduce LLM-SR-UQ, a framework that retains competing equation structures and uses their disagreement to characterize uncertainty about the underlying functional form. We extend the standard fixed-width conformal approach to quantify structural intervals, which measure disagreement among candidate equations and identify where prediction intervals should widen. Extensive experiments show our framework substantially improves coverage attainment by incorporating structural disagreement while preserving in-distribution coverage. Our approach also shows good generalizability when it is applied to other symbolic-regression methods.

</details>

<a id="paper-113"></a>

### 113. [Any Forcing: Any-Step Generality and One-Step Stability for Autoregressive Video Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42031v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.42031

**作者：** Jiahua Xu、Yijie Fang、Nannan Wang、Xunhao Lin、Li Cao、Lei Hao、Mingrui Zhu、Fei Gao、Xinbo Gao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42031v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.42031v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Any Forcing 框架，用统一对抗判别器兼顾真实分布对齐、步间一致性与首帧质量，实现任意步采样下的一步生成质量；并提出免训练的自适应长期记忆选择机制，按全局语义相关性与局部价值贡献挑选历史状态并重映射其时间位置，以稳定长视频的一步自回归生成。

**相关性简评：** 该工作属自回归视频生成，与视觉推理无直接关联；但其「按相关性与贡献筛选历史状态＋位置重映射」的免训练缓存机制，可能为多步 latent 推理中的状态与缓存筛选、以及步间一致性监督提供可供核查的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Any Forcing: Any-Step Generality and One-Step Stability for Autoregressive Video Generation

**abstract/A1：** Autoregressive video generation enables efficient inference through streaming prediction. However, existing methods are typically restricted to fixed sampling steps, limiting the flexibility to balance generation quality and efficiency, while one-step generation suffers from degraded visual quality and error accumulation over long sequences, leading to content drift and generation collapse. To address these issues, we propose Any Forcing, a unified framework for achieving Any-Step Generality and One-Step Stability. Specifically, we introduce a unified adversarial discriminator that jointly performs real-distribution alignment, inter-step consistency alignment, and first-frame quality enhancement, enabling flexible sampling while preserving one-step generation quality. Furthermore, we propose a training-free adaptive long-term memory selection mechanism that selects historical states based on global semantic relevance and local value contribution, while remapping their temporal positions to preserve temporal relationships within the memory cache, thereby enabling stable one-step long-video generation. Experiments demonstrate that Any Forcing achieves high-quality and stable one-step autoregressive video generation, achieving 20.9 FPS and 24.7 FPS for short and long video generation on a single H100 GPU, respectively.

**comment/C1：** Autoregressive video generation

</details>

<a id="paper-114"></a>

### 114. [Rethinking Asymmetry in Self-Supervised 3D Geometry Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42092v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.42092

**作者：** Gyeongjin Kang、Freeman Cheng、Botao Ye、Weijie Lyu、Ming-Hsuan Yang、Eunbyung Park

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42092v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.42092v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文以几何精度而非渲染质量为目标，重新审视无位姿图像的自监督 3D 学习：把非对称约束从输入空间移到 3D 表示空间，让共享骨干利用全部视角做对应、仅在重建场景中排除留出视角，并用稀疏/密集上下文特征对齐及冻结视觉基础模型特征对齐作为额外训练信号。

**相关性简评：** 其「表示空间施加不对称」与「稀疏—密集特征对齐提供无教师监督」的思路，可能可供核查 LVR 中如何用内部表示对齐作为训练信号、以及几何信息是否真正保留在 latent 状态中；但论文面向 3D 重建，与 VLM 推理的联系需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Rethinking Asymmetry in Self-Supervised 3D Geometry Learning

**abstract/A1：** Self-supervised learning relies on asymmetry between what a model observes and what it must explain. Recent methods learn 3D geometry from unposed images by predicting cameras and scene structure, then rendering held-out views. To avoid trivial solutions, they impose this asymmetry in the input space, restricting how views interact through separate branches or masked attention. We revisit this design with geometric accuracy, rather than rendering quality, as the main objective. When a held-out view lies between context views, the context already constrains the geometry it observes. We therefore impose the asymmetry in the 3D representation space, excluding held-out views only from the reconstructed scene while a shared backbone uses all views for correspondence. This improves depth and pose estimation over input-space designs. We further exploit view density: sparse and dense observations depict the same scene but provide different amounts of correspondence. Aligning sparse-context features with dense-context features supplies a geometric training signal alongside rendering, without a pretrained geometric teacher. We additionally align features with a frozen vision foundation model, which brings further gains. None of these signals requires geometric annotation, and the resulting model improves geometry estimation over prior self-supervised methods. Controlled experiments isolate the contribution of each component, and self-supervised pretraining also improves subsequent supervised training.

**comment/C1：** Website: https://gynjn.github.io/rethinking-asymmetry/

</details>

<a id="paper-115"></a>

### 115. [CausalWAM: Learning the Causes of Actions in World Action Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42096v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.42096

**作者：** Juntao Cheng、Jinzhou Tang、Fan Feng、Henrik I. Christensen、Yujia Zheng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.RO、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42096v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.42096v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究 world action model 中如何把真正驱动动作的因果 latent 与仅为场景预测服务的冗余变量分离：作者证明在稀疏约束下，生成动作所用的 latent 是 action-relevant latent 的可逆函数且结构可识别，并据此在预训练 WAM 的动作头上加一条稀疏路由路径（CWAM），在合成数据与真实机器人上验证理论与闭环成功率。

**相关性简评：** 其用稀疏路由让输出端只依赖部分 latent、并给出可识别性论证，可供核查是否能迁移为 LVR 中检验「答案是否真正使用 latent 视觉内容」、抑制无关信息与做贡献分配的约束手段；但该工作面向机器人动作生成，与视觉推理状态的联系仍需人工判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CausalWAM: Learning the Causes of Actions in World Action Models

**abstract/A1：** World action models (WAMs) learn latent variables from observations and use these to both predict the evolution of the scene and to generate actions. The prediction has to be of the whole scene, whereas only a portion of the scene causes actions. WAMs fitted to demonstrations do not, in general, guarantee that they will generate actions solely from the latent causes of the demonstrated actions (the action-relevant latent variables), as the model could just as well learn to imitate in this way whilst entangling the action-relevant latent variables with the nuisance variables. We therefore separate out what causes action from what prediction depends on. We prove that under a sparsity constraint the latent variables used to generate actions are an invertible function of the action-relevant latent variables alone and the true structure is identified up to a permutation of the latent variables. This requires neither sufficient temporal dependence nor any assumptions on how the unobserved regimes evolve and holds even when the action-relevant latent variables are statistically dependent on the rest. We therefore propose CWAM (Causal WAM), which incorporates the constraint into a pretrained WAM by adding a sparse routing path to its action head, with the world modeling objective remaining unchanged. Experiments on both synthetic data and real-world robots validate the theory as well as showcase the improved efficiency in closed-loop success.

</details>

<a id="paper-116"></a>

### 116. [Towards Understanding the Impact of Plasticity Loss on Reinforcement Learning in Stochastic Environments](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42100v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.42100

**作者：** Philipp Bordne、André Biedenkapp

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42100v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.42100v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文系统考察随机环境下的可塑性丧失问题：在三个 DeepMind Control 任务上加入可控的观测与动作噪声训练 SAC，比较保持可塑性的方法，并分析噪声水平、重置与 LayerNorm 组合以及 TD 不稳定的影响。

**相关性简评：** 可塑性丧失、重置与归一化的组合以及「后期由 TD 不稳定主导失败」等结论，或可为 LVR 中循环/长序列训练的稳定性与贡献分配提供可核查的训练侧线索，但本文不涉及视觉或 latent 推理状态，联系较弱。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Towards Understanding the Impact of Plasticity Loss on Reinforcement Learning in Stochastic Environments

**abstract/A1：** Deep reinforcement learning (RL) is a powerful framework for sequential decision-making. Despite many high-profile successes in recent years, deep RL is known to be brittle. An emerging line of research attributes this brittleness to a loss of plasticity in neural networks. However, plasticity loss has so far been understudied in stochastic environments. We study this interplay systematically, training SAC agents on three DeepMind Control tasks augmented with controlled levels of observation and action noise. We find that plasticity-preserving methods increase learning robustness on the two harder tasks, with their advantage growing as observation noise increases, while combining resets with layer normalization underperforms resets alone at every observation noise level tested. Further, stochasticity can accelerate the loss of plasticity in harder environments, whereas in easier settings noise can act as a regularizer that sustains plasticity. Lastly, plasticity loss is most pronounced in the early stages of training. In the most challenging stochastic settings, TD instability rather than gradual plasticity loss becomes the dominant failure mode.

**comment/C1：** 9 pages main paper, 15 pages references and appendix, 17 figures. Presented at the Nineteenth European Workshop on Reinforcement Learning (EWRL 2026)

</details>

<a id="paper-117"></a>

### 117. [A Better Critic Matters for Distribution Matching Distillation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42135v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.42135

**作者：** Zongze Wu、Xianbang Wang、Zhengyang Geng、Mingdeng Cao、Zhoutong Zhang、Richard Zhang、Eli Shechtman、Ruiming Cao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42135v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.42135v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究 DMD 少步蒸馏中 critic 需要多少容量与信息：发现 critic 不随 teacher 扩展会导致崩溃，因而提出 joint scaling，并用按学生生成步条件化的 step-aware critic（STAC）替代步无关的混合分数估计。

**相关性简评：** 其「按生成步条件化的评分器」及「步特定状态 vs 步无关混合」的对比，可能与 latent 推理轨迹中的逐步信用分配与监督信号设计相通，可供核查；但本文属扩散蒸馏，与 VLM 推理无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Better Critic Matters for Distribution Matching Distillation

**abstract/A1：** Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) trains a few-step student using the difference between a teacher's score and an online critic's estimate of the student distribution's score. We investigate what the critic needs, in capacity and in information, to provide a reliable training signal. On the capacity side, controlled experiments across seven SiT model sizes on ImageNet show that increasing teacher size while keeping a small critic fixed can degrade student quality and cause training collapse. Scaling the critic alongside the teacher, a strategy we call joint scaling, prevents collapse in our experiments and allows the student to benefit from larger teachers. On the information side, rather than forcing the critic to model a step-agnostic mixture of student outputs, we introduce a step-aware critic (STAC) that conditions on the student's generation step to estimate step-specific scores. Together, joint scaling and STAC consistently improve few-step distillation across class-conditional ImageNet generation, text-to-image generation with SDXL and FLUX.2, and text-to-video generation with Wan2.1.

**comment/C1：** 35 pages, 16 figures. Project page and code: https://peppaking8.github.io/critic-matters/

</details>

<a id="paper-118"></a>

### 118. [ReLift: Dense 3D Tracking as Lifted Trajectory Repair](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42136v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.42136

**作者：** Assaf Singer、Maroun Khriesh、Noam Rotstein、Yoni Kasten、Or Litany

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42136v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.42136v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把单目视频的密集3D跟踪重构为「提升轨迹修复」：先用预训练2D跟踪器和3D几何模型生成含遮挡插值的轨迹，再训练一个前馈轨迹修正器，沿轨迹与跨轨迹交替注意力以保留可靠观测、修复错误位置并补全缺失运动。

**相关性简评：** 其「筛除受污染观测、修复并补全状态」的修正机制与训练时注入模拟误差的做法，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的证据筛选与迭代更新提供借鉴线索，但论文本身不涉及视觉推理或语言模型。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ReLift: Dense 3D Tracking as Lifted Trajectory Repair

**abstract/A1：** Dense 3D tracking from monocular video requires establishing point correspondences over time, estimating their depth, and inferring their trajectories during occlusion. Existing methods typically learn these tasks jointly, a demanding objective given the scarcity of 4D supervision. We show that strong pretrained 2D trackers and 3D geometry models provide an effective foundation: lifted trajectories, with occluded segments simply interpolated, already rival dedicated 3D trackers using the same geometry. However, lifted trajectories inherit correspondence and depth errors, and under occlusion, their depth comes from the occluder rather than the tracked point. We introduce ReLift, an efficient feed-forward framework that recasts dense 3D tracking as geometric trajectory repair and completion. At its core, a trajectory corrector alternates attention along and across trajectories to preserve reliable observations, repair corrupted positions, and complete missing motion. Using only geometric inputs allows us to train directly on 3D trajectories with simulated lifting errors, decoupling training from images and from any specific tracker or geometry model. Concentrating 4D supervision on trajectory refinement yields a reusable model that consistently improves lifted trajectories across trackers and geometry providers without retraining, so it benefits directly from future advances in either component. On WorldTrack, ReLift achieves state-of-the-art mean accuracy, improving on the strongest baselines by 2.1 APD on all points and 3.0 on dynamic points, with faster configurations remaining competitive with leading 3D trackers while running faster than 4RC. Visit our supplementry page for more examples and code.

</details>

<a id="paper-119"></a>

### 119. [Latent Sparse Attention: Towards Subquadratic Ultra-Long Context Attention](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42147v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.42147

**作者：** Ales Kubicek、Andrea Caciolai、Liangzu Peng、Sukrit Sumant、Benjamin Hoover、Fraser Greenlee、Ruairidh M. Battleday、Radim Urban、Torsten Hoefler、James C. R. Whittington

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42147v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.42147v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Latent Sparse Attention（LSA）：在学到的 latent 空间中对 key 做层次聚类，让 query 只对簇层级打分而非逐 key 打分，从而在超长上下文下降低 prefill、decode 与训练成本，并给出蒸馏版 D-LSA 与原生训练版 N-LSA 的加速与精度结果。

**相关性简评：** 其「latent 空间层次索引 + 稀疏证据选择」这一注意力结构线索，可能与 LVR 中长视觉/上下文证据筛选及注意力结构对 latent 相似度的影响有关，可供核查；但论文本身不涉及推理状态生成或 latent reasoning。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Latent Sparse Attention: Towards Subquadratic Ultra-Long Context Attention

**abstract/A1：** Agentic workflows are increasingly shifting towards long-horizon tasks, such as open-ended scientific work, where models benefit from long context windows. However, at ultra-long context (above 1M tokens), full attention is prohibitively expensive. A promising approach is sparse attention, in which a dedicated indexer selects a subset of keys for the attention. Yet current methods of indexing either still have quadratic cost in context size or lose accuracy at ultra-long context scale. We address this with Latent Sparse Attention (LSA), an efficient attention method that maintains accuracy at ultra-long context. In LSA, keys are hierarchically clustered in a learned latent space, and queries score the hierarchy of clusters instead of scoring every key, making prefill, decode and training faster at long context. We propose two variants, Distilled-LSA (D-LSA), which can be applied to pre-trained models via distillation, and Native-LSA (N-LSA), which is trained natively on the next-token loss alone. On GLM-5.3-FLASH (QWEN-3.8-27B) at 10M tokens, D-LSA speeds up one attention layer by 22.7x (72.4x) in prefill and 4.6x (42.5x) in decode over its original sparse attention (full attention), while keeping similar accuracy across a variety of tasks. N-LSA matches the accuracy of full attention with a training speedup of 1.6x per layer at 128k tokens. LSA offers practical and efficient attention for models to benefit from ultra-long context.

</details>

<a id="paper-120"></a>

### 120. [GazeRIB: Systematic Evaluation of Multi-Reader Gaze-Guided Chest X-ray Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42152v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.42152

**作者：** Nattakorn Kittisut、Roy M. Gabriel、Marly van Assen、Mohammadreza Zandehshahvar、Carlo N. De Cecco、Elizabeth A. Krupinski、Ali Adibi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.HC、eess.IV、eess.SP

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42152v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.42152v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文系统评估 GazeRIB：训练时用放射科医生注视掩码遮挡未被注视区域、推理时使用原图，在 80 张胸片、1/3/5/8 位读片者组合、7 种架构与 10 个随机种子上训练 910 个模型，考察四类严重度预测及外部验证的迁移表现。

**相关性简评：** 其「以注视掩码筛选视觉证据」的训练期监督思路可能为 LVR 的视觉证据筛选与贡献分配提供参照，但任务是医学影像分类、不涉及 latent 推理状态，联系需人工核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GazeRIB: Systematic Evaluation of Multi-Reader Gaze-Guided Chest X-ray Learning

**abstract/A1：** Radiologists' visual attention can supervise medical image learning, but how reader panel composition affects its value remains untested. We evaluated GazeRIB (Gaze-guided Radiologist-Informed Blackout), which masks unviewed regions during training and uses unmasked images at inference. Eight experts repeatedly read the same 80 chest X-rays (CXRs). We compared individual readers with three-, five- and eight-reader panels across seven architectures and 10 seeds, evaluating 910 models on a separate 2,897-CXR cohort for four-class severity prediction. Every panel improved macro F1 over the no-gaze baseline by 5.33-7.47 percentage points (pp; Bonferroni-adjusted p &lt; 0.007); no individual reader reached significance on this endpoint. Three readers captured 73-80% of the eight-reader gain in macro F1, the area under the receiver-operating-characteristic curve (ROC-AUC) and the area under the precision-recall curve (AU-PRC). On external validation without retraining, all four panels improved ROC-AUC on SIIM-FISABIO-RSNA by +1.93 to +4.24 pp (p &lt;= 0.004); on MIMIC-Eye no panel differed significantly from baseline, whereas the single-reader average declined (-1.40 pp, p = 0.008). Reader composition therefore influenced performance, although transfer depended on the cohort.

**comment/C1：** 20 pages on main manuscripts (3 tables + 4 figures), 14 pages supplementary (10 tables + 8 figures)

</details>
