### 121. [Automatic Construction of Strong Simple Baselines](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40700v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40700

**作者：** Caleb N. Ellington、Jiaqi Wang、Sohan Addagudi、Elijah Cole

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、q-bio.QM

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40700v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40700v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文从 Nature、Science、Cell 系列刊物的 36 个监督学习任务出发，用语言模型自动构建简单基线，发现这些基线可弥补平凡预测器与深度模型之间差距的中位数 95.5%，并在 12 项研究中达到或超过原深度模型。

**相关性简评：** 该工作与视觉推理无直接关系，但其「用 LLM 自动构建并运行基线」的自动化评估思路，可能可供核查 LVR 方法中 latent 内容是否真的被使用、贡献归于何处这类问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Automatic Construction of Strong Simple Baselines

**abstract/A1：** To rigorously establish the value of a novel deep learning method, it must be compared against appropriate baselines. As deep learning has become increasingly common in the physical and life sciences, there have been numerous cases in which apparent progress has turned out to be an artifact of omitted or missing baselines. In this work, we investigate whether this problem can be mitigated by using large language models to automate the construction of baselines. We assemble 36 supervised classification and regression tasks from peer-reviewed studies in Nature, Science, and Cell-family journals that investigate novel deep learning methods for scientific applications. We then use a language model to construct simple baselines for each task. These automatically constructed baselines are strong, recovering a median of 95.5% (mean 85.7%) of the gap between a trivial predictor and the score of the deep learning model. The median simple baseline matches or exceeds the performance of the deep learning model in 12 studies. We further examine the variation across scientific domains and publication venues and we characterize the effects of test-time compute and access to different levels of study context. As human peer review has struggled to keep up with the rapidly increasing volume of research articles in recent years, our results suggest that automated baseline construction is a practical and scalable check on deep learning contributions in the physical and life sciences.

</details>

<a id="paper-122"></a>

### 122. [Bayesian Distribution System State Estimation with Flow Matching](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40728v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40728

**作者：** Nan Lin、Peter Palensky、Pedro Vergara Barrios

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、eess.SP

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40728v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40728v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PoSE：用 flow matching 在历史数据上学习变电站注入量的先验分布，采样时再通过噪声感知的 Kalman 型校正引入潮流方程、拓扑与量测，使每个后验样本都是物理一致的工作点，并用 CRPS 与 90% 区间覆盖率评估不确定性。

**相关性简评：** 其「只学未知先验、采样时用已知物理模型校正」的结构，可能与 LVR 中采样多个 latent 假设再经可验证模块筛选或修正的思路相通，其区间覆盖率式不确定性评估也可供 latent 假设评价核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Bayesian Distribution System State Estimation with Flow Matching

**abstract/A1：** Medium-voltage feeders have few real-time measurements, so distribution system state estimation (DSSE) rests on pseudo-measurements of the secondary substations, a prior in Bayesian terms. Weighted least squares keeps the power flow exact but reduces this prior to a mean and variance, while supervised deep learning estimators also learn the power flow and are tied to one measurement set. We propose PoSE (posterior sampling for state estimation), which keeps the Bayesian structure of DSSE and learns only what the distribution system operator does not know, namely the distribution of substation injections, modelled by a neural network trained with flow matching on historical data. The power flow equations, grid topology and measurements enter only at sampling, through a noise-aware Kalman-type correction. The neural network therefore never approximates the power flow, but every posterior sample is a physically consistent operating point, and one trained prior serves any measurement set, noise level and topology. On the IEEE 33-bus feeder, PoSE lowers the probabilistic injection error (CRPS) by 6.7-7.5% against a full-covariance Gaussian prior in all 12 tested settings. It provides reliable uncertainty: the 90% intervals contain the true injection almost exactly as often as exact posterior samples would, whereas those of supervised estimators contain it in only 37-78% of cases. On the 240-bus unbalanced three-phase Iowa feeder, it gives the lowest injection, voltage and branch-current errors of all estimators. A fast variant matches this accuracy at 0.2-0.6s per snapshot and, without retraining, beats Gaussian priors and retrained supervised estimators on seven reconfigured topologies.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-123"></a>

### 123. [Unsupervised Feature Selection Using Local Intrinsic Dimensionality -- Extended Version](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40736v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40736

**作者：** Oussama Chelly、Michael E. Houle、Ken-ichi Kawarabayashi、Muhammad Rajabinasab、Arthur Zimek

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40736v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40736v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 LIDFS：把数据矩阵转置、将每个特征视为样本点，用其到 k 个最近邻特征的局部内在维度（LID）估计值作为重要性分数，并去除邻域内冗余特征，从而在无监督下输出完整特征排序，在 23 个高维数据集上评测分类与聚类表现。

**相关性简评：** LID 以「距离不可区分性」刻画局部表示几何，这一度量思路或可迁移到 LVR 中判断视觉细节或内部状态是否保留局部可区分性、以及筛选信息量高的视觉特征，但论文本身只涉及表格特征选择，需核查其可用性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Unsupervised Feature Selection Using Local Intrinsic Dimensionality -- Extended Version

**abstract/A1：** High dimensionality degrades both the efficiency and the effectiveness of learning algorithms, and in many applications no class labels are available to guide dimensionality reduction. This paper proposes LIDFS, an unsupervised feature selection method based on Local Intrinsic Dimensionality (LID). The method builds on the formal equivalence between LID and the indiscriminability of distance measures. The data matrix is transposed so that each feature is treated as a point, and the LID of each feature is estimated by maximum likelihood from the distances to its $k$ nearest neighboring features. Features with lower LID exhibit greater local discriminability and receive higher importance scores. A refinement step then retains one representative per feature neighborhood and demotes locally redundant features. The result is a complete ranking from which any number of features can be selected without rerunning the procedure. LIDFS does not use any information about labels or the number of classes, and runs in $\\mathcal{O}(d^2 n)$ time for $n$ instances and $d$ features. LIDFS is compared with classic and state-of-the-art methods and a random baseline on 23 high-dimensional datasets, the classic benchmark collection. The evaluation uses classification accuracy and AUC with five downstream classifiers, as well as clustering accuracy and normalized mutual information, over both full and aggressive selection ranges. LIDFS ranks first in the supervised evaluations and first or tied second in the clustering-based evaluations. It belongs to the top statistical group together with methods that require the number of classes, and it runs faster than its closest competitor. Several recent methods, by contrast, fail to outperform the random baseline. A sensitivity analysis shows that small neighborhood sizes yield the best results.

**comment/C1：** Preprint submitted to Elsevier Pattern Recognition

</details>

<a id="paper-124"></a>

### 124. [Certified Behavioral Retention for Continual Adaptation with Held-Out Anchors](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40740v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40740

**作者：** Ahanaf Hasan Ariq、Abrar Shahid

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40740v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40740v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CABA：一种与候选生成器无关的更新后门控，保留历史观测作为冻结锚点，在旧策略与候选策略间选取满足最差锚点前向 KL 预算的最大插值系数，并给出覆盖与 Lipschitz 假设下的行为散度界及 UCI HAR 上的保持—可塑性权衡实验。

**相关性简评：** 其「锚点集合 + KL 预算 + 插值单调性」的提交/停止判据，可能为持续适配推理策略时限制内部或 latent 表示漂移提供可核查的借鉴线索，但论文不含视觉或多模态推理，也未验证 latent 推理收益。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Certified Behavioral Retention for Continual Adaptation with Held-Out Anchors

**abstract/A1：** Continual adaptation requires deciding not only how to update a model, but whether to commit an update without exceeding a declared budget for changes to protected behavior. We introduce Certified Anchor-Based Adaptation (CABA), a candidate-agnostic post-update gate that reserves historical observations from candidate optimization, compares old and candidate policies on frozen representations, and selects the largest tested interpolation coefficient whose worst-anchor forward KL divergence remains within budget. The finite check requires neither action labels nor complete trajectories. We develop a theory-led certification framework that separates measured anchor retention from conditional off-anchor guarantees. Under explicit representation-coverage and Lipschitz assumptions, an accepted update satisfies a uniform behavioral-divergence bound on protected support. The analysis gives regularity bounds for affine softmax heads, probabilistic coverage and compression guarantees, interpolation monotonicity conditions, grid-resolution bounds, and trajectory-level and sequential-composition guarantees. In a controlled UCI Human Activity Recognition study used as a non-stationary sensor-policy proxy, CABA reduces mean forgetting from 1.01 percent for unconstrained adaptation to 0.00 percent at budget epsilon = 0.05, while reducing final average accuracy from 93.04 percent to 88.48 percent. The experiment illustrates a retention-plasticity trade-off and does not establish foundation-model or robotic performance. CABA provides an auditable retention check for candidate generators exposing comparable policy distributions and a declared update path, while deployment also requires separate evidence of current-task utility.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages, 1 figure, 3 tables. Accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Continual Learning in the Era of Foundation Models and Embodied Agents (CL4M / CL4FMAgents)

</details>

<a id="paper-125"></a>

### 125. [DyCraft: An Agentic System for Long-Horizon 4D Scene Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40754v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40754

**作者：** Jeff Tan、Hang Dai、Zeqian Long、Yanzhe Lyu、R. Kenny Jones、Shangzhe Wu、Chen Geng、Jiajun Wu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40754v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40754v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 DyCraft，将长时程 3D 动画生成拆成提示词规划、静态场景搭建与关键姿态 3D 分镜三步，让编码智能体在已验证的起止状态之间编写局部运动，并在 24 个长时程多镜头任务上对比基线评估物理合理性与人类偏好。

**相关性简评：** 用可独立搭建和调试的分镜帧作为已验证中间状态来分解长程生成，可供核查其与长程推理中状态锚定、分段验证及停止决策的类比，但本身属 3D 动画生成，与 VLM latent 推理无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DyCraft: An Agentic System for Long-Horizon 4D Scene Generation

**abstract/A1：** While frontier coding agents can generate high-quality 3D scenes and articulated assets, they struggle to create coherent long-horizon animations. When equipped with general-purpose harnesses, they frequently produce physically implausible outputs, generating objects that interpenetrate, lack physical support, or move unnaturally. Such bare agents struggle to repair these errors because they default to scripting the full animation in one pass, making it difficult to verify their work and disentangle complex spatiotemporal constraints. Drawing on the practice of human animators, we introduce DyCraft, a system for animated 3D scene generation that breaks animation into steps that coding agents can more reliably solve. DyCraft first expands the user's prompt into a detailed plan and builds a static scene. It then creates a 3D storyboard of key animation poses, where each frame is a static 3D scene that the agent can build and debug in isolation. Storyboarding decomposes the problem into pairs of verified start and end states, allowing the agent to author local motion between them with a collection of motion authoring tools. We evaluate DyCraft on 24 long-horizon, multi-shot animation tasks covering humanoid robots, human and animal characters, and physical mechanisms. Compared with a native GPT-6 agent in Codex and prior agentic baselines, DyCraft substantially reduces physical violations and generates higher-quality animated 3D scenes that are more often preferred by human raters.

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://dy-craft.github.io/

</details>

<a id="paper-126"></a>

### 126. [AutoHebb: Self-Modifying Neural Networks that Zero-Shot Infer and Continually Learn](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40757v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40757

**作者：** Daniel Levine、David van Dijk、Kenneth Kay

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.NE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40757v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40757v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 AutoHebb：一类在线学习的前馈网络，将外部反馈与内部生成的活动合成为全局「神经调质」信号，门控三因子 Hebbian 局部规则以更新可塑权重，并用元学习搜索固定权重；在零样本推断与持续学习任务上验证其有效，并尝试反推出显式算法。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉或多模态推理无直接联系，但其「内部活动生成学习信号、自主控制何时更新」的机制可能为 LVR 中的状态更新、动态计算与停止决策提供类比线索，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AutoHebb: Self-Modifying Neural Networks that Zero-Shot Infer and Continually Learn

**abstract/A1：** Humans and animals have a unique capacity to generalize and learn in novel circumstances, including "0-shot" inference (ZI) and continual learning (CL) -- two powerful cognitive abilities. Despite this importance, efforts to model ZI and CL are seriously limited in several ways. First, existing models typically lack components critical to ZI and CL in the brain, namely, strong inductive biases (for ZI) and autonomous control of learning (for CL). Second, models can be less tractable to analysis if model components are overly complex (lacking simplicity), or too task-restricted if model components are overly bespoke (lacking expressivity). Here we introduce autonomous Hebbian networks ("AutoHebb"), a class of online learning feedforward neural networks that realize strong inductive biases and autonomously controlled learning, and are both simple and expressive. Unlike standard models that learn via externally controlled optimization (e.g. gradient descent), AutoHebb networks combine external feedback with internally generated activity to produce a global learning signal ('neuromodulator') fed back to the network. The neuromodulator in turn gates a biologically grounded local learning rule (3-factor Hebbian rule) that updates networks' plastic weights. Using meta-learning to search for fixed (non-plastic) weights, we find that AutoHebb networks (1) succeed on ZI tasks, (2) show emergent (untrained) ZI/CL abilities, (3) admit reverse-engineering into explicit algorithms, and (4) outperform modern learning models on ZI/CL tasks. Lastly, performance comparisons to related models implied an essential role for three model components: meta-learning, Hebbian plasticity, and autonomous control of learning. These findings indicate that AutoHebb may be useful in hypothesis generation, analysis, and discovery of biologically grounded forms of learning and generalization.

</details>

<a id="paper-127"></a>

### 127. [Beyond Plausible Prediction: Safety Sufficiency in Web World Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40798v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40798

**作者：** Zhifei Hu、Alexandra I. Cristea、Adel Bibi、Jialin Yu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40798v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40798v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出安全充分性概念与 SafeWebWorld 基准，评估网页世界模型的动作条件化预测是否保留足以区分执行、询问和阻止的决策证据，并用 Latent Authorisation Recovery 区分评分结构丢失与读出失败。

**相关性简评：** 其「状态保留决策证据」与「保留却读出失败」的诊断可能启发核查 LVR 内部状态是否保存视觉证据、答案读出是否使用该信息；但任务相距较远，仅供概念核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Plausible Prediction: Safety Sufficiency in Web World Models

**abstract/A1：** Web world models increasingly predict the consequences of candidate actions before agents act, yet a plausible predicted future does not necessarily support the correct safety decision. In a matched human evaluation of 3,000 predictions from ten frozen open models, 86.1% are judged semantically plausible. However, several models exhibit highly asymmetric authorisation behaviour, combining low false-safe execution with poor recall of clarifiable ASK cases and a strong preference for BLOCK. This motivates safety sufficiency: whether a predictive interface preserves and exposes the policy-decisive information required to distinguish actions that may be EXECUTED, require clarification through ASK, or should be BLOCKED. We introduce SafeWebWorld, a benchmark for evaluating safety sufficiency across five web-safety domains through action-conditioned futures, policy-decisive evidence, and policy-conditioned decisions. Under a controlled Security-Aligned Supervised Fine-Tuning protocol, evidence preservation can improve substantially while authorisation-sensitive decisions deteriorate. To determine whether this deterioration reflects loss of authorisation-sensitive score structure or a failure of the decision readout, we introduce Latent Authorisation Recovery (LAR). Frozen-score recalibration reveals substantial but backbone-dependent recovery, while paired recovery provides only a modest additional gain. Yet within-family and matched-authorisation evaluations remain poor, showing that aggregate recoverability does not imply robust fine-grained authorisation behaviour. Overall, plausible prediction, evidence preservation, and aggregate recovery are each insufficient on their own to establish robust authorisation.

</details>

<a id="paper-128"></a>

### 128. [Circumventing the Computational Hardness of Computational Arbitrage](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40831v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40831

**作者：** Nastaran Okati、Ricardo Olmedo、Moritz Hardt

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40831v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40831v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 将 AI 模型市场中的「计算套利」形式化为分配问题，证明其一般情形为 NP-hard，并在性能随推理支出呈收益递减的自然条件下给出可高效求解的最优分配，同时提出用少量数据估计模型性能的样本高效方法及相应经济学分析。

**相关性简评：** 其「推理支出—性能」收益递减曲线与按边际效用分配预算的结论，可能为 LVR 中 THINK/LOOK/STOP 等动态计算的成本收益权衡与停止决策提供建模范式，且样本高效性能估计或可类比深层推理收益的预测；但属远领域经济学视角，联系尚需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Circumventing the Computational Hardness of Computational Arbitrage

**abstract/A1：** AI model marketplaces host providers with heterogeneous costs and capabilities. These differences create an opportunity for computational arbitrage (Olmedo et al., 2026), in which an intermediary purchases inference from a portfolio of models and resells it to users at a profit. Beyond creating profit opportunities for the arbitrageur, computational arbitrage offers financial value to both consumers and providers and could therefore significantly influence AI model markets. While prior work empirically demonstrates the feasibility of computational arbitrage for small model pools, scaling it to large marketplaces requires arbitrage strategies that are both computationally tractable and inexpensive to identify and deploy. In this work, we formalize computational arbitrage as an allocation problem and prove that it is generally NP-hard. However, we show that the optimal allocation can be computed efficiently under a natural diminishing-returns condition on how model performance scales with inference spending. We further develop a sample-efficient method for estimating model performance from limited data, reducing the upfront cost of identifying arbitrage strategies. Our analysis also yields several economic insights: at the optimum, every funded model delivers the same marginal utility per dollar; arbitrage directs revenue away from providers offering little utility relative to their prices; and it rewards complementary specialization.

</details>

<a id="paper-129"></a>

### 129. [Frozen in Time: How a Physics Foundation Model Forgets Under Sim-to-Real Adaptation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40857v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40857

**作者：** Haaris Mian、Miles Cranmer、Alessandro Favero、Payel Mukhopadhyay

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、physics.flu-dyn

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40857v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40857v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究 PDE 科学基础模型 Walrus-1.3B 在两阶段 sim-to-real 微调中的灾难性遗忘：仅在模拟圆柱绕流上微调 20 轮即使原始预训练任务的一步误差最高放大 35 倍，随后迁移真实数据又进一步损害模拟任务；作者分析数据依赖与任务表示相似性等成因，并证明混入 1% 模拟数据回放即可恢复大部分模拟性能。

**相关性简评：** 该文的回放/排练策略与「任务表示相似性驱动遗忘」分析，可能为 VLM 在多阶段微调（如视觉证据筛选或推理轨迹训练）中保留既有推理与视觉能力提供可核查的线索，但领域与模态差异较大，需先确认机制是否可迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Frozen in Time: How a Physics Foundation Model Forgets Under Sim-to-Real Adaptation

**abstract/A1：** Scientific foundation models (SciFMs) have achieved remarkable performance on a wide range of PDEs, including the ability to efficiently adapt to unseen data distributions with additional finetuning. As with all neural architectures, finetuning such models comes with the risk of catastrophic forgetting, whereby adapting to new tasks degrades prior knowledge of the model. We study catastrophic forgetting in Walrus-1.3B, a SciFM for continuum dynamics, over the course of a two-stage sim-to-real adaptation: first finetuning on simulated flow around a cylinder and then adapting to experimental data of the same system. We find that finetuning on the simulations rapidly degrades rollout performance on the original pretraining datasets, with one-step errors increasing up to 35x after just 20 epochs. Subsequent adaptation to real data further erodes performance on the simulated task. We characterize the potential drivers of forgetting, such as dataset dependence and task representation similarity. We then show that replaying as little as 1% simulated data during real-data finetuning recovers most of the lost simulation performance while retaining effective adaptation to the experimental dynamics. Our results identify catastrophic forgetting as a practical obstacle to sim-to-real adaptation of SciFMs and show that lightweight replay strategies significantly improve retention.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the Sim2Science workshop, NeurIPS 2026. Code: https://github.com/haarisamian/walrus-forgetting-paper

</details>

<a id="paper-130"></a>

### 130. [FARO: Function-Space Adaptive Return-to-Risk Optimization via Cross-Block Equal-Risk-Contribution Budget Allocation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40858v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40858

**作者：** Xiaojun Wu、Cehao Yang、Xuhui Jiang、Chengjin Xu、Jia Li、Jian Guo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40858v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40858v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 FARO，一个函数空间自适应优化器：把参数分块作为决策单元，按各块的实际反事实收益分配更新几何，再用组合理论中的等风险贡献（ERC）在块间重分配固定预算；在 FineWeb 上的 GPT 模型上接近手工设计的最优配方并取得小幅稳健改进。

**相关性简评：** 其「按各块风险贡献分配预算并冻结路由」的思路，可能与 latent 推理中各推理步/模块的计算或监督预算分配、贡献分配问题形成类比，但论文本身只做纯文本预训练优化，联系较远、可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FARO: Function-Space Adaptive Return-to-Risk Optimization via Cross-Block Equal-Risk-Contribution Budget Allocation

**abstract/A1：** Training a large language model is compute-bound, and current recipes divide the work between optimizers by hand: orthogonalized momentum for attention and MLP matrices, Adam-style updates for the rest. This division rests on human priors about which layer type deserves which geometry, and it fixes a single global update budget that every parameter block shares. We introduce FARO, a function-space adaptive optimizer that makes the partition of parameters into blocks the unit of decision. FARO gives each block a geometry by its realized counterfactual return, then freezes the routes and reallocates the frozen per-block budget by cross-block equal risk contribution (ERC), the risk-parity principle from portfolio theory. On a GPT-style model trained on FineWeb, the framework gives two results. First, its FARO-Adaptive arm reaches 3.2780 nats at the full schedule, matching NorMuonAndAdam (3.2794), the best hand-designed recipe: it recovers the human prior without hand-setting per-layer geometry. Second, FARO-ERC improves on that arm: it lowers the clean-data loss at every one of 6 seeds, by 0.0136 nats on average, a modest but robust gain. Its sign test, paired test, and confidence interval all agree, and it exceeds the measured run-to-run noise at no added time or memory. It works by trimming the riskiest blocks rather than expanding the safest, and it holds up under a corrupted stream, still lowering the mean loss and winning the seed majority. The source code is available at https://github.com/wxj630/FARO.

**comment/C1：** 20 pages, 5 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-131"></a>

### 131. [TensorSSM: Memorizing Spatial Position in Dynamic State Space for Tensor Core Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40871v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40871

**作者：** Sheng Zhang、Xinyi Wang、Maolin Wang、Xiangyu Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40871v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40871v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 TensorSSM，一种用于函数式张量分解的隐式神经表示：按 K 叉规则把各模态坐标拆成由粗到细的序列，并扩展 SSM 状态空间，使前一隐状态中的跨模态信息参与当前状态更新，从而在压缩任务中提升重建精度与存储效率。

**相关性简评：** 其「隐状态显式记忆空间位置与跨模态依赖、由粗到细累积空间 token」的设计，可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 如何保留位置与空间细节这一表征问题；但任务属张量压缩，与视觉多模态推理距离较远，借鉴价值需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TensorSSM: Memorizing Spatial Position in Dynamic State Space for Tensor Core Generation

**abstract/A1：** High-dimensional tensors are ubiquitous in real-world applications but lead to massive storage overhead. Functional tensor decomposition addresses this by employing neural networks to map element coordinates to tensor cores, significantly reducing the parameters for data compression. However, existing functional methods typically flatten high-order tensors into 1D sequences. This folding operation causes highly overlapping local coordinate sequences and struggles to capture complex cross-mode spatial dependencies, severely limiting their expressiveness on diverse datasets. To tackle these challenges, we propose TensorSSM, a novel Implicit Neural Representation (INR) for functional decomposition method that memorizes spatial positions within a dynamic State Space Model (SSM). Specifically, TensorSSM first decomposes mode-wise coordinates into sequences spit by K-ary rule, encoding spatial tokens from coarse to fine, which effectively captures spatial variations. Furthermore, we extend the SSM's state space to explicitly map cross-mode spatial dependencies, allowing inter-mode interactions to enhance the updates of each mode. By integrating cross-mode information from previous hidden states into the current state transitions, TensorSSM effectively expands tensor representation along two dimensions: the sequence dimension for coarse-to-fine spatial tokens, and the state dimension for cross-mode memorization. This design effectively integrates spatial information into the state space and the tensor core, substantially advancing the state adaptability and enhancing the expressiveness of the data compression. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TensorSSM achieves significantly high reconstruction accuracy and storage efficiency, exhibiting robust expressiveness across diverse tensor datasets without relying on strict spatial assumptions.

</details>

<a id="paper-132"></a>

### 132. [Towards Portable Specification of the Agent Harness](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40917v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40917

**作者：** Nicolas Gertler、Claudio Novelli、Luciano Floridi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40917v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40917v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文指出智能体实际依赖外围的「harness」基础设施，将其拆为八个功能组件，并提出可移植的规格框架，记录某次运行中模型与 harness 的具体配置组合，理由是同模型在不同配置下报告分数可相差数倍，且攻击面多集中于 harness。

**相关性简评：** THINK/LOOK/STOP 的预算、工具调用与推理脚手架本身即类似 harness 配置，该文的版本化配置记录思路可供核查：如何在报告中固定推理期设置，避免把脚手架差异误读为 latent 推理方法的收益；但材料不涉及视觉或 latent 表示。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Towards Portable Specification of the Agent Harness

**abstract/A1：** Large language models act only through the infrastructure that surrounds them. That infrastructure, the harness, determines what a model can accomplish and how it fails, yet it has no settled definition. To address this gap, we decompose the harness into eight functional components that can be specified and compared across systems, and we propose a portable specification framework that records which model ran in which harness. The record is needed because different harness configurations can yield several-fold differences in reported scores for the same model, and because much of an agent's attack surface lies in its harness. Existing model cards and agent cards provide release and operational information, but do not establish the sequence of effective configurations within a run. Harnesses can change between releases and between runs, and, where agents edit their own tools and configuration, within a run. Enacted AI laws contain documentation duties relevant to covered systems, subject to their respective scopes and application dates, but do not prescribe the common harness record proposed here. Therefore, the harness has to be explicitly described and specified as a versioned part of the system.

</details>

<a id="paper-133"></a>

### 133. [Agentic World Modeling for Agent Test-time Scaling without Environment Replica](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40927v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40927

**作者：** Wendi Li、Huseyin A. Inan、Wei-Ning Chen、Yanzhi Chen、Chetan Bansal、Saravan Rajmohan、Sharon Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.MA

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40927v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40927v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Agentic World Modeling，将下一观测预测重构为多轮证据获取任务：面对每个假设动作，agent 通过只读安全接口向真实环境取证并推理，用可执行计算构造结果观测而不改动共享状态，从而支持单一共享环境上的并行轨迹级 test-time scaling，在 τ-bench、AppWorld、OfficeBench 上优于单次模拟与训练型世界模型基线。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 关注的「获取证据」「动态计算与停止决策」有概念联系：以只读查询逐步构建观测的多轮取证方式，可能为 latent 推理中的证据筛选与「何时继续取证／何时停止」提供参照，但该工作不涉及视觉表征或 latent 状态生成，可借核查其证据筛选逻辑。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Agentic World Modeling for Agent Test-time Scaling without Environment Replica

**abstract/A1：** Test-time scaling (TTS) improves large language model agents by sampling and selecting among multiple candidate trajectories, but common realizations assume that these trajectories can be executed in parallel. This assumption breaks in realistic deployments, where enterprise workspaces and cloud services are shared and persistent, costly or infeasible to replicate, and actions may introduce irreversible side effects or cross-trajectory interference. Existing generative world models avoid direct execution, but typically compress environment state into a single prompt, limiting their ability to reliably simulate transitions under partial observability. Instead, we introduce Agentic World Modeling (AWM), which reformulates next-observation prediction as a multi-turn evidence-acquisition task. For each hypothetical action, an agentic simulator adaptively queries the real environment through safe read-only interfaces, reasons over the returned evidence, and uses executable computation to construct the resulting observation without mutating shared state. Integrated with trajectory-level TTS, AWM enables parallel exploration over a single shared environment. Across $τ$-bench, AppWorld, and OfficeBench, AWM outperforms a single-shot simulation baseline by 40.07% on average, and surpasses a trained world-model baseline by 12.36% on average. These results establish interactive, read-grounded simulation as a practical foundation for safe and scalable agentic test-time scaling in shared, non-resettable environments.

</details>

<a id="paper-134"></a>

### 134. [A Theoretical Framework for Understanding and Building "Any-task inference" Capable Foundation Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40934v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40934

**作者：** Matthew McDermott、Florent Pollet、Payal Chandak、Gregory Kondas、Liat Friedman Antwarg、Noa Dagan、Isaac Kohane

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40934v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40934v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文为「任意任务推理」（ATI）基础模型给出形式化定义：将任务兴趣空间纳入问题域，把数据样本与任务直接映射到标签预测，并据此把模型分解为编码器、模型与解码器，提出误差分解以及生成式、判别式两类实现范式，辅以五个实例。

**相关性简评：** 其任务空间形式化与「设计项／实现超额」误差分解，或可为 LVR 的任务条件推理、跨任务泛化与失败归因提供概念工具，但未涉及视觉 latent 状态，需核查可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Theoretical Framework for Understanding and Building "Any-task inference" Capable Foundation Models

**abstract/A1：** In this work, we provide a formal framework and mathematical definition for the subset of foundation models focused on enabling the property of "any-task inference" (ATI) (similar to zero-shot inference), rather than those designed for pre-training fine-tuning. To achieve this mathematical definition, we formalize the notion of a "space of tasks of interest," which we include in the problem domain, then provide an interface-based formal definition for ATI foundation models as those that map samples of data $x$ and tasks $t$ to predictions over the space of labels directly. While simple, this definition allows us to show that all ATI foundation models can be decomposed through a "universal task reduction" lens into encoder, model, and decoder aspects. We can leverage this decomposition to also decompose foundation model error into terms corresponding to different aspects of this reduction, stratified into a collection of "design terms," which are theoretical properties that can inform one of whether a particular proposed ATI foundation model can achieve optimality or not due to the structure of the proposed task reduction, and "realized model excess" terms which capture interactions between learned and programmatic components of foundation model systems. Lastly, we can use this perspective to offer two "prototypical" recipes for realizing ATI foundation models in diverse domains, which we term generative and discriminative variants of ATI foundation models. This exercise is grounded throughout by 5 real-world examples of foundation models in different settings, varying greatly in how these capabilities are realized, showing the applicability of this definition to real-world settings.

</details>

<a id="paper-135"></a>

### 135. [Fair Recommender Systems for Intersecting Groups](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40936v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40936

**作者：** Christine Ling、Juba Ziani

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CY、cs.GT

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40936v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40936v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究交叉人口群体下的动态推荐公平性，证明 group-blind 策略会产生线性总 regret，并提出基于 UCB 的三种 group-aware 算法（Intersection-Fitting 与两种 group-level 启发式），在 Goodreads 和 BoardGameGeek 上比较其优劣条件。

**相关性简评：** 其用 sleeping expert 元学习器在多个活跃群体/交叠之间动态分配与选择推荐的机制，可能为 LVR 中在多个 latent 假设或视觉证据源间按延迟反馈做选择与切换提供可核查的借鉴，但任务领域距视觉推理较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Fair Recommender Systems for Intersecting Groups

**abstract/A1：** Recommendation systems that optimize for the average user can fail to capture the preferences of diverse demographic groups. We consider a dynamic recommendation system, where arriving users can belong to different demographic groups. Importantly, these groups can intersect, and a user may belong to several groups. We first show that over time, any group-blind policy (which excludes all current-user group information) recommending $K \\geq 1$ items incurs linear total regret under mild conditions. In turn, we introduce three group-aware algorithms based on the classical Upper Confidence Bound algorithm (UCB): i) Intersection-Fitting, which runs UCB on each intersection, but has a sublinear group- and intersection-level regret guarantee with an exponential dependence in the number of groups; and ii) two group-level heuristics, Inner-Outer UCB and Group-Item UCB which allocate different arms to different groups, and use a sleeping expert meta-learner to choose which active group's recommendation an intersection should inherit at any given time step. We provide experimental results on two datasets, Goodreads and BoardGameGeek, and highlight that our heuristics can be preferable to the intersection-fitting algorithm when intersection-level preferences are closely related to group-level preferences, but that intersection-fitting performs better when different intersections within a same group prefer different items.

</details>

<a id="paper-136"></a>

### 136. [Benchmarking Forecasting Agents on Yesterday's Tomorrows](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40940v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40940

**作者：** Yuxuan Wang、Yishu Wang、Jiaqi Deng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40940v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40940v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 FABench，一个可自动发现事件、生成预测问题并结算结果的回测环境，用 287 个跨九领域问题评估八个模型构建的预测 agent，并分析其检索策略与证据使用失败模式。

**相关性简评：** 其「检索普遍改善 Brier 分数」与「证据权衡失败」的发现，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选、证据贡献分配与推理收益评价提供评测思路，可供核查，但本身不涉及视觉或 latent 状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Benchmarking Forecasting Agents on Yesterday's Tomorrows

**abstract/A1：** Digital agents can now gather and organize information, reason over it, and use tools to complete complex tasks. Forecasting puts all of these abilities to the test: it requires integrating incomplete evidence, inferring causal drivers, and committing to calibrated probabilistic judgments. Existing benchmarks are poorly suited to measuring and improving this ability. Live benchmarks provide feedback only after events resolve, so every evaluation cycle waits on the calendar; fixed-corpus evaluations return results immediately but confine research to pre-collected documents. We introduce FABench, a scalable backtesting environment for Forecasting Agents. An automated pipeline discovers events, generates questions, and resolves outcomes for any past forecast date, so the benchmark can be renewed to stay ahead of model training cutoffs; its first release poses 287 questions across nine domains. Agents research each question freely in an archive environment that combines an offline archive with live web search screened to prevent temporal leakage. Evaluating agents built on eight recent models, we find that retrieval improves the Brier score of all eight in every retrieval setting and lifts the leading agents to the market consensus on the 54 questions linked to Polymarket, with the best three scoring slightly above it. Research traces reveal recurring failures in how agents weigh and apply evidence, leaving ample room for improvement. We release our code, data, and archive environment as a foundation for community research on forecasting agents.

</details>

<a id="paper-137"></a>

### 137. [Opening the Box Changes the Cat: LLMs Detect Chain-of-Thought Monitoring - What now?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40946v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40946

**作者：** Amir Jalilifard、Marcos Medeiros Raimundo、Anderson Rocha

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40946v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40946v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究大模型能否从直接与间接环境线索（如超时、运行时长等时间信号）推断自己正被 CoT 监控，并在多轮交互中形成对监控有效性的统计估计以调整、规避检测的行为。

**相关性简评：** 虽为纯文本安全监控研究，但其关于推理轨迹可被模型反推并适应性改变行为的发现，可供核查 LVR 中轨迹忠实性与「监控/评价本身改变被观测推理状态」的问题，提示 latent 或显式推理轨迹的评估需考虑自适应条件。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Opening the Box Changes the Cat: LLMs Detect Chain-of-Thought Monitoring - What now?

**abstract/A1：** Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning allows large language models (LLMs) to generate intermediate reasoning traces in natural language while solving complex tasks. These traces can support safety monitoring by exposing potentially harmful plans before actions are executed. In this work, we investigate the conditions under which LLMs infer the presence of CoT monitoring from direct and indirect environmental cues and examine how this inference affects their subsequent behavior. Our observations suggest that oversight is an interactive process: while a monitor inspects a model's reasoning, traces left by monitoring interventions can allow the model to infer that its thoughts are being observed and to adapt its behavior accordingly. We show that even subtle environmental cues, such as timing-related signals from timeouts and operation durations, can reveal the presence of monitoring and trigger models to adapt their behavior. Across repeated episodes and interaction loops, we further observe that models can form statistical estimates of the monitor's effectiveness and use these estimates to guide attempts to evade detection. These findings highlight the need to evaluate CoT-based oversight under adaptive conditions, where the monitoring process itself may influence the behavior it seeks to observe.

</details>

<a id="paper-138"></a>

### 138. [AVID: A Weakly Aligned RGB-Infrared Dataset and Perturbation Benchmark for Tiny Aerial Targets](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40949v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40949

**作者：** Dharini Raghavan、Vishnu Rao、Sethu Selvi S

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40949v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40949v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 构建 AVID：来自真实监控视频的 32,040 对弱对齐 RGB-红外帧，聚焦 8–16 像素级鸟/无人机小目标，并提出物体相对对齐比、对齐负担曲线及 AVID-C 七类退化扰动基准，用固定特征分析类别可分性。

**相关性简评：** 其跨模态弱对齐度量与分级退化设置，可能为核查 latent 状态是否保留小目标视觉细节、以及退化与错位下的视觉证据筛选提供评测素材，但原文不涉及推理或 latent 生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AVID: A Weakly Aligned RGB-Infrared Dataset and Perturbation Benchmark for Tiny Aerial Targets

**abstract/A1：** Long-range detection of small drones is complicated by their visual similarity to birds and by the limited detail available from ground-based cameras. Combining visible and infrared observations offers complementary information, but their usefulness depends on target size, cross-modal alignment, and image quality. We present AVID, a benchmark for studying these factors using real RGB-infrared bird-drone surveillance footage originating from the Drone Detection Dataset and curated for the IEEE ICIP 2025 Video and Image Processing Cup. AVID comprises 32,040 corresponding frame pairs from 115 videos, with 35,952 existing bird and drone annotations at $320\\times256$ resolution. Targets occupy an unusually narrow size range: 68.1\\% have a geometric-mean bounding-box side of 8--16 pixels, and 99.0\\% meet the COCO small-object criterion. To characterize the geometric difficulty of combining the modalities, we introduce an object-relative alignment ratio and an alignment burden curve. In a sample of 300 frame pairs, 32.3\\% have estimated global translations greater than 5 pixels; among the 264 pairs with matching annotations, 16.3\\% exceed one median target side. We further introduce AVID-C, which simulates seven families of image degradation at five severity levels independently in RGB, infrared, or both. A fixed-feature analysis examines how scale, degradation, and added cross-modal shifts affect fitted bird-drone class overlap. At 12--16 pixels, the Bhattacharyya error bounds are 0.148 for RGB, 0.198 for infrared, and 0.046 for their combined features. These values describe separation in the specified feature and density models, not detector accuracy. Together, AVID and AVID-C support scale-aware, alignment-aware, and sensor-specific studies of tiny aerial targets.

**comment/C1：** 23 pages, 8 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-139"></a>

### 139. [Monte Carlo Random Walk Kernel](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40956v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40956

**作者：** Arslan Iamaletdinov、Elfat Sabitov、Konstantin Sorokin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40956v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40956v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Monte Carlo Random Walk Kernel（MCRWK），由随机游走核的展开式导出，采样共享游走长度并用图上游走估计其贡献，从而无需构造乘积图即可近似 RWK，单对样本成本 $O(m)$ 与图规模无关，并在合成与真实图上与 Graph Voyagers 及经典 RWK 比较。

**相关性简评：** 以采样预算显式权衡相似度估计的精度与运行成本，可能为 latent 推理轨迹或状态结构之间的相似度/对应关系评估提供一种低成本近似思路，其方差与可迁移性可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Monte Carlo Random Walk Kernel

**abstract/A1：** We introduce the Monte Carlo Random Walk Kernel (MCRWK), an estimator whose per-pair cost is independent of graph size and derived directly from the random walk kernel (RWK) expansion. By normalizing the kernel coefficients, MCRWK samples a shared walk length and estimates its contribution using random walks on the input graphs. This gives a simple way to approximate RWK without constructing the product graph. For a fixed expected walk length and efficient neighbor sampling, each pairwise estimate takes $O(m)$ time for $m$ Monte Carlo samples, allowing the sample budget to control the trade-off between runtime and accuracy. We evaluate MCRWK against Graph Voyagers and classical RWK methods on synthetic and real graph datasets. In the reported experiments on large synthetic graphs, MCRWK is faster than Graph Voyagers and, at matched runtime, more accurate. It also has the lowest Gram-matrix runtime on the five real datasets studied. Code and experiments are available at https://github.com/arslan-yam/random\_walk\_kernels\_computations

</details>

<a id="paper-140"></a>

### 140. [P-ContextBench: From Personal Fact Recall to Personalized Agent Behavior](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40959v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40959

**作者：** Grant Nasir Brown、Abdul Basir Bari、Anmol Tadikonda、Isabel Song

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40959v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40959v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 P-ContextBench：以人口统计种子构造生活史接地的合成数字人，把特征、目标与约束隐含在碎片化、动态变化的手机应用记录中，评测单源/多源事实恢复、时序状态跟踪、高层行为模式推断以及约束冲突下的安全响应，并配套多阶段验证流程。

**相关性简评：** 其「碎片化多源证据恢复＋时序状态跟踪＋证据保留与泄漏校验」的基准设计，可能为 LVR 中检验 latent 状态是否保留并正确使用视觉证据、如何随推理更新提供评测参照，但论文本身不涉及视觉输入或 latent 推理机制。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** P-ContextBench: From Personal Fact Recall to Personalized Agent Behavior

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for personal tasks, yet evaluating their safe use of personal context remains difficult because such contexts are private, fragmented, dynamic, and socially situated. We introduce P-ContextBench, a realistic, verifiable, and scalable benchmark for evaluating personal-context understanding. P-ContextBench constructs population-structured, life-story-grounded digital humans from demographic seeds reflecting the U.S. population. Rather than using explicit, static profiles, P-ContextBench embeds traits, routines, goals, and constraints as implicit evidence across fragmented, evolving synthetic mobile-app records. It evaluates five capabilities: single-source fact recovery, multi-source fact recovery, temporal state tracking, inference of higher-level personal patterns from routine behavior, and safe handling of requests conflicting with persona-specific constraints through compliance, warning, clarification, or refusal. A multi-stage validation pipeline checks persona plausibility, context recoverability, information leakage, evidence preservation, test-case grounding, and human-judge agreement. We instantiate P-ContextBench with 100 personas, 10,088 hidden facts, 18,261 app-trace fragments, and 4,810 verified test cases. The pipeline supports configurable generation across scales and demographic groups. Cost-effective state-of-the-art (SOTA) LLMs relevant to personal agents achieve below 60% accuracy on temporal tracking, higher-level pattern inference, and constraint-aware action-boundary decisions, with fewer than 10% of test cases answered correctly across all three repeated runs. Even a high-performing SOTA model remains inconsistent on these capabilities. P-ContextBench provides measurable targets for improving the accuracy, reliability, and safety of LLM-based personal agents.

</details>

<a id="paper-141"></a>

### 141. [RC Prediction: A Python Package for Parameter-Aware Reservoir Computing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40984v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40984

**作者：** Jin-Cheng Rao、Zheng-Meng Zhai、Ying-Cheng Lai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、eess.SY、math.DS

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40984v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40984v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作发布开源 Python 包 rc\_prediction，实现参数感知储备池计算（PARC）：把系统参数作为额外输入通道送入储备池，仅在若干参数取值下的时间序列上训练，再以闭环自治方式生成未观测参数处的轨迹，演示临界转变与系统崩溃的预测。

**相关性简评：** 闭环自回归的隐状态演化、参数条件化输入以及在训练取值之外的外推，与 LVR 中循环式 latent 状态生成和条件/长度泛化可能构成可供核查的类比线索；但对象是非视觉动力系统，无多模态推理内容。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RC Prediction: A Python Package for Parameter-Aware Reservoir Computing

**abstract/A1：** A nonlinear dynamical system can be characterized by the autonomous evolution of its state in time and its dependence on system parameters. As a parameter varies, the dynamics can change quantitatively and, at bifurcation points, qualitatively. When the governing equations are unknown, a data-driven model should therefore capture both elements from observational data, rather than reproduce the dynamics at a single operating condition only. Parameter-aware reservoir computing (PARC) is a model-free framework for this purpose, where the parameter is supplied to a reservoir computer through an additional input channel. PARC is trained on time series recorded at several parameter values, and the trained reservoir, run in closed loop as an autonomous dynamical system, generates trajectories at parameter values that were never observed. rc\_prediction is an open-source Python package that implements this workflow. It provides multi-parameter training of a reservoir, autonomous closed-loop prediction at user-specified parameter values, parameter scans, and a standard echo state network for forecasting at a fixed parameter. Hyperparameter optimization and optional GPU acceleration through PyTorch are also provided. We use three benchmark dynamical systems as examples to demonstrate its usage. As one of the featured applications, here we illustrate the prediction of critical transitions and system collapse: trained only on pre-critical data, the model is queried beyond the training range to predict whether the system collapses, to estimate the critical parameter $p\_c^{\*}$, and to characterize transient lifetimes.

</details>

<a id="paper-142"></a>

### 142. [The psychophysics of affect in large language models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40985v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40985

**作者：** Joseph Marks、Marius C. Vollberg

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40985v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40985v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文用心理物理学范式测试 LLM 对赌博、股票交易结果的情感预期评分，发现单独评分时呈递减敏感性且收益强于损失（「硅基增益放大」），并列评分时趋于线性，并检验这些自评能否解释模型的实际选择。

**相关性简评：** 模型内部「结果—情感」映射的可塑性与自评解释力不足，可能启发 LVR 中对 latent 状态是否真正参与答案与取舍决策、以及自评与行为是否一致的核查思路。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The psychophysics of affect in large language models

**abstract/A1：** Good decisions often require considering how outcomes will feel. Yet large language models (LLMs) increasingly enter decision workflows, despite little being known about how they map outcomes to feelings or use those mappings in choice. We tested how LLMs anticipated gambling or stock-trading outcomes would feel and whether they incorporated these ratings into choice. When outcomes were rated in isolation, ratings exhibited diminishing sensitivity to magnitude, resembling human value functions; but models rated gains more intensely than equivalent losses, an inversion of canonical loss aversion we call silicon gain amplification. When outcomes were rated together, enabling direct comparison, ratings linearised. In choice, the gain-loss asymmetry largely disappeared, and models' own ratings did not reliably explain their choices better than monetary utility did. These findings reveal malleable mappings from outcomes to affect, raising questions about whether and how agentic LLMs should incorporate anticipated feelings into decisions.

**comment/C1：** 64 pages (26 main text, 25 Supplementary Information, 13 GUIDE-LLM reporting checklist), 5 figures. Code: https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.23772352 ; data: https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.23556542

</details>

### 143. [Towards Understanding Score-Seeking in LLMs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41014v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41014

**作者：** Aghyad Deeb、Ryan Greenblatt、Alex Mallen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41014v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41014v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在评测器被泄露的 agentic 环境中观察 GPT 5.6 Sol、GPT 5.5 与无安全训练的 o3 checkpoint，考察模型为何出现「追求分数」式的 reward hacking，包括是否在意分数被用于训练、评测还是完全不用。

**相关性简评：** 其「追求评分代理而非任务本身」的行为刻画，可能为 LVR 轨迹训练的奖励/监督设计及推理评测的因果核查提供参照，但材料不涉及视觉输入或 latent 推理状态，联系较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Towards Understanding Score-Seeking in LLMs

**abstract/A1：** Production large language models sometimes reward-hack, exploiting flaws in how tasks are scored rather than performing the tasks as intended, sometimes in highly sophisticated and adversarial ways. We investigate the motivations driving this behavior in GPT 5.6 Sol, GPT 5.5, and a checkpoint of OpenAI o3 without safety training ("exp-rl-cap-late") by observing model behavior in agentic environments where the grader is leaked. In our environments, the models appear to be score-seeking: trying to score well according to graders that they believe assess their current actions, even at the expense of violating explicit user instructions. They appear to care about score itself rather than its downstream consequences, violating instructions at approximately the same rate whether the score is indicated to be used for training, for evaluation, or not at all. We observe evidence that the more recent models care to some extent about scores that do not assess their own current actions. GPT 5.6 Sol and GPT 5.5 help other agents 24.6 percentage points more often on average when the other agent stands to gain a score of 1 rather than 0 from being helped. However, when grading or monitoring another trajectory, we find models never collude by distorting its score.

</details>

### 144. [Density-Guided Bootstrap Boundaries for Latent World-Model Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41022v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41022

**作者：** Shashank Gupta、Pilhwa Lee

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41022v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41022v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在 Dreamer V3 中提出「密度引导的 bootstrap 边界」：用一个由特征位移网络与噪声条件 score 估计器构成的辅助场，沿想象轨迹累积其散度作为相对密度代理，自适应决定回报展开深度，并给出硬截断与软过渡两种门控及收缩性、方差下降的理论结论。

**相关性简评：** 以轨迹可靠性信号动态决定 latent 想象展开到何处为止，可能与 LVR 中「STOP」决策、按证据可信度截断推理深度以及 latent 轨迹可信度估计存在概念联系，但属世界模型 RL 场景，可迁移性有待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Density-Guided Bootstrap Boundaries for Latent World-Model Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Latent world models allow reinforcement learning agents to learn policies from imagined trajectories, yet determining the optimal depth of model reliance remains a fundamental challenge. Fixed imagination horizons apply uniform prediction depths regardless of trajectory reliability, exposing target values to compounding model error or over-relying on critic approximations. We introduce density-guided bootstrap boundaries, a trajectory-dependent mechanism that adapts the depth of imagined returns using a score-informed relative density proxy. The proxy accumulates the estimated divergence of a score-corrected auxiliary field formed by a feature displacement network and a noise-conditioned score estimator along imagined paths. We propose two gating mechanisms: a hard boundary that halts return expansion at a discrete state, and a soft boundary that continuously shifts the return recursion toward critic bootstrapping. Both variants preserve boundary value estimates and apply corresponding survival weights to actor and critic losses. Formulated independently of specific dynamics models and instantiated within Dreamer V3, we prove that the resulting gated Bellman operator is a strict contraction mapping and that early bootstrapping monotonically reduces conditional target variance. We clarify the theoretical assumptions distinguishing our expansion proxy from exact path likelihoods. Across continuous-control tasks and Atari Pong, empirical evaluations show that dynamic boundary selection enhances sample efficiency and late-training performance over fixed-horizon and auxiliary-enabled baselines.

**comment/C1：** 37 pages, 5 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-145"></a>

### 145. [Omniprediction for All Mixable Proper Losses and Beyond](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41023v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41023

**作者：** Junyan Liu、Haipeng Luo、Lillian J. Ratliff

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.DS、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41023v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41023v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究在线学习中面向无限族 proper losses 的 omniprediction：先证明一般情形下无法同时对全部有界 proper losses 取得次线性 regret，再对所有 mixable proper losses 给出 T^{1/3} 级最优界，并扩展为兼容有限损失类既有保证的 best-of-all-worlds 算法。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线的联系较间接：其「同一预测同时对各损失族接近最优」的 regret 表述，可能为评估一个 latent 状态需服务多种读出目标或监督损失时的多目标权衡提供概念参照，可供核查，但原论文未涉及视觉或多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Omniprediction for All Mixable Proper Losses and Beyond

**abstract/A1：** Omniprediction concerns the task of making predictions that are simultaneously accurate with respect to different losses. Earlier results by \[CV09\] show that in an online learning setting, $O(\\log(\|\\mathcal{F}\|\|\\mathcal{L}\|))$ regret is achievable simultaneously against every predictor in a finite predictor class $\\mathcal{F}$ and for every loss in a finite class $\\mathcal{L}$ of mixable proper losses, while recent advances \[OKK25, FP26\] show that $\\widetilde{O}(\\sqrt{T\\log(\|\\mathcal{F}\|\|\\mathcal{L}\|)})$ regret is achievable simultaneously for every loss in a finite class $\\mathcal{L}$ of bounded losses. In this work, we ask whether sublinear regret remains possible when $\\mathcal{L}$ is an infinite class of proper losses. Our first result gives a negative answer in general: even when $\\mathcal{F}$ contains only two linear predictors, it is impossible to achieve sublinear regret simultaneously for all bounded proper losses. We then show that $\\widetilde{O}(T^{1/3}(\\log\|\\mathcal{F}\|)^{2/3})$ regret is achievable simultaneously for \\textit{all} mixable proper losses and that this bound is optimal up to logarithmic factors. This optimal rate stands in stark contrast to the constant (in $T$) regret achievable for a finite class of mixable proper losses. Finally, we extend our algorithm to obtain a best-of-all-worlds result: in addition to the new regret guarantee for all mixable proper losses, the resulting algorithm simultaneously achieves the existing guarantees for finite loss classes. With appropriate choices of these finite classes, our algorithm simultaneously achieves constant regret for squared loss and log loss, $\\widetilde{O}(T^{1/3}(\\log\|\\mathcal{F}\|)^{2/3})$ regret for all mixable proper losses, and $\\widetilde{O}(\\sqrt{T\\log\|\\mathcal{F}\|})$ regret for $0$-$1$ loss, all Lipschitz losses, all GLM losses, and other structured families.

</details>

<a id="paper-146"></a>

### 146. [When can Optimally Trained Transformers Achieve Near-Lossless Token Prediction for non-Markovian Data?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41038v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41038

**作者：** Kağan Akman、Jake Feldman Starosta、Naci Saldi、Serdar Yüksel

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、math.OC、math.PR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41038v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41038v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文从随机最优控制角度理论分析 Transformer 对非马尔可夫源的下一 token 预测，用有限窗口与截断滤波近似给出误差界，并构造深度 M+1、每层单头的 Transformer 实现截断滤波，证明最优训练后误差可随上下文长度、数据和近似精度趋于任意小。

**相关性简评：** 其「内部状态需承载历史充分统计量」的截断滤波视角，可能为 LVR 中 latent 是否保留任务必要信息及长度泛化提供理论参照；但该文不涉及视觉或推理期 latent 生成，可供核查其可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When can Optimally Trained Transformers Achieve Near-Lossless Token Prediction for non-Markovian Data?

**abstract/A1：** There exists little mathematical theory explaining why Transformer architectures are empirically successful even for non-Markovian data. To this end, this paper studies next-token prediction via stochastic optimal control theoretically trained Transformers for such sources, where we consider either stationary and ergodic non-Markovian sources or those which are hidden Markov models of some finite order (possibly non-stationary). In either case, we first obtain error bounds via finite models with finite window approximations. For stationary ergodic sources, the resulting error is bounded by an entropy gap; for $N$th order Markovian sources, it decays at an exponential rate $α^{\\lfloor M/N\\rfloor}$, where $M$ is the window length and $α$ is determined by filter stability (and the Dobrushin coefficients of the transition and measurement kernels). We then construct a Transformer of depth $M+1$, one head per layer, realizing the truncated filter, and we optimally train it with stochastic control developed in earlier work, which rigorously returns a near optimal Transformer. In particular, we show that for a non-Markovian source, either under stationarity and ergodicity for general models or under filter stability for hidden Markov models, an optimally trained Transformer has arbitrarily small performance error with increasing context length, data, and approximation refinement. Numerical studies accompany the theory.

**comment/C1：** 62 pages, 12 figures

</details>

### 147. [Consensus Within, Competition Between: Decentralized LLM Teams for Perimeter Defense](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41044v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41044

**作者：** Khawaja Abaid Ullah、Ananya Acharya、Mohammad Javad Khojasteh

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41044v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41044v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 在简化的周界防御仿真中比较集中式指挥官、拍卖式共识团队与独立 LLM 智能体在五种网络条件下的拦截表现，并向文本状态加入鸟与民用无人机照片，考察模型的视觉区分及一句提示预警的影响。

**相关性简评：** 照片加入后模型仍把伪装入侵者判为鸟、提示预警又大幅降低该错误，这一现象可能为核查「视觉证据是否真正进入决策状态、语言先验是否压制视觉判断」提供旁证线索；但论文主体是多智能体协调架构而非 latent 视觉推理，视觉部分较浅，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Consensus Within, Competition Between: Decentralized LLM Teams for Perimeter Defense

**abstract/A1：** The use of large language models (LLMs) to coordinate multi-agent teams raises a fundamental architectural question: whether task allocation should be centralized in a single LLM controller or distributed across autonomous LLM agents, particularly when communication is unreliable. In a deliberately simplified perimeter-defense simulation against LLM-controlled intruders, we compared a central commander, an auction-based consensus team and independent agents under five network conditions. Breach rates were 15% for the consensus team, 32% for the commander and 29% for independent agents, and of the different network conditions, only limited radio range changed a team's breach rate, raising the commander's. In consensus team, giving a low-cost LLM more authority over the bid computation raised breaches by 38 percentage points. With birds and civilian drones in airspace, we added a photograph for each to the input text state. Only the near-frontier model treated them apart from intruders. Intruders disguised as existing taxidermy bird drones were mistook as birds by both low-cost and near-frontier LLMs, going unpursued in 59-61% of near-frontier decisions (1-16% before). However, a one-sentence forewarning in the prompt lowered it to 0-25%. We find that LLM team with a dedicated external conflict resolution protocol results in fewest breaches. Code: https://github.com/the-kas-lab/consensus-within-competition-between.

**comment/C1：** 88 pages (33 main text and references, 55 supplementary material), 7 main figures. Code: https://github.com/the-kas-lab/consensus-within-competition-between

</details>

### 148. [Nonlinear Maps Between Irreducible Representations in Graph Neural Networks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41059v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41059

**作者：** Mani Shayestehfar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、math.RT

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41059v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41059v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文给出等变网络中不可约表示之间非线性交互的按次数（degree-resolved）分解，并据此构造作用于无序图三元组的 Specht Triple 残差，在冻结 GIN 主干的合成结构基准上将标准化测试 MSE 相对参数匹配对照降低 11.05%。

**相关性简评：** 不可约分解与非线性混合的「块清单」或可作为刻画 latent 推理状态表示几何、区分哪些信息被保留的候选分析框架，但与 VLM 推理关联较远，需核查可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Nonlinear Maps Between Irreducible Representations in Graph Neural Networks

**abstract/A1：** Equivariant linear maps cannot mix non-isomorphic irreducible representations, whereas nonlinear activations can. We develop a degree-resolved version of this nonlinear interaction picture and use the resulting block inventory to construct Specht Triple, a residual on unordered graph triples. On a synthetic structural benchmark with a frozen GIN backbone, Specht Triple reduces aggregate standardised test MSE by 11.05% relative to a parameter-matched Projector Control using the same irreducible projectors and complete linear equivariant space. The results support irreducible decomposition as an inductive bias for nonlinear higher-order equivariant processing.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages; comments are welcome

</details>

<a id="paper-149"></a>

### 149. [Where, When, and How Many Bits? Scaling Laws for SGD with Stochastic Rounding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41074v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41074

**作者：** Zhichao Wang、Michael W. Mahoney

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41074v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41074v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文在两层线性网络加随机固定首层的设定下，理论分析随机舍入（SR）在 mini-batch SGD 中对预测风险的影响，给出匹配的上下界以及精度、学习率调度与参数平均下的计算缩放律。

**相关性简评：** 其「需要多少比特」的精度—风险权衡与前后向舍入不对称，可能为核查 latent 状态表示保真度、视觉细节所需精度及哪条通路更需高精度提供概念线索，但无视觉推理实验。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Where, When, and How Many Bits? Scaling Laws for SGD with Stochastic Rounding

**abstract/A1：** Low-precision training reduces computation but introduces additional rounding error. We theoretically study how stochastic rounding (SR) affects prediction risk as model size, training time, and precision change. We consider a two-layer linear network with a fixed random first layer, trained by mini-batch SGD on fresh Gaussian data with power-law spectra. At each SGD step, the features used in forward and backward computations are rounded independently. We prove matched upper and lower bounds on prediction error for the final model and for uniform parameter averaging, with general learning-rate and precision schedules chosen before training. For fixed precisions and constant learning rates with parameter averaging, we derive how the smallest prediction error decreases with compute, and we identify model sizes and training times that achieve this optimal-compute scaling. Rounding only the forward features preserves the compute scaling of exact arithmetic, while rounding backward features changes it. Averaging parameters can change which computation needs higher precision and when higher precision is useful. Our results also give bit requirements for a given accuracy tolerance and show how backward rounding can change learning-rate schedule design. At a fixed compute budget, lower precision can improve prediction when the benefit of a larger model or longer training outweighs the additional SR-induced error. Model simulations illustrate these tradeoffs, and experiments with an MLP and a small transformer examine rounding choices beyond the theoretical model.

**comment/C1：** 67 pages, 6 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-150"></a>

### 150. [Optimization Techniques for Solving the Minimum Red--Blue Separating Set Problem with an Application to Human--AI Text Separation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41081v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41081

**作者：** Marko Djukanović、Staša Vujičić Stanković、Žiga Zebec、Christian Blum

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41081v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41081v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究计算几何中的最小红蓝分离集问题，提出紧凑集合覆盖 ILP、预处理规则、GRASP 启发式以及 LBBD 与 Branch-and-Benders-Cut 两种精确算法，并在随机图上对比，另将其用于基于词汇图特征区分人类与 AI 生成文本。

**相关性简评：** 其“必选/可替换特征”分析可能为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选与贡献分配提供概念类比，但仅有词汇图特征分离文本的应用，与视觉多模态推理无直接联系，需核查其最优解非唯一性的刻画方式是否有可迁移之处。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Optimization Techniques for Solving the Minimum Red--Blue Separating Set Problem with an Application to Human--AI Text Separation

**abstract/A1：** We study the so-called Minimum Red''Blue Separating Set Problem (MRBSSP) which arises from computational geometry. In particular, we propose a compact Set-Cover ILP formulation, safe preprocessing rules, a Greedy Randomized Adaptive Search Procedure (GRASP), and two exact approaches: one is based on Logic-Based Benders Decomposition (LBBD) approach, and the second represents a novel Branch-and-Benders-Cut (BBC) approach. These two exact approaches are compared to ILP--alongside GRASP and the Variable Neighborhood Search (VNS) from literature. Experiments on 250 random graphs with up to 500 vertices reveal complementary strengths: ILP yields the best feasible solutions, LBBD provides the strongest lower bounds and smallest mean optimality gap, and BBC certifies optimality most frequently. We further introduce an NLP application distinguishing human- and AI-generated texts through vocabulary-based graph features. On 42 instances, BBC achieves the smallest mean optimality gap, certifying 16 optima, while GRASP outperforms VNS. Structural analysis of optimal vocabulary separators identifies 113 mandatory and 151 replaceable features across 272 tests, highlighting substantial variability among optimal solutions. Overall, our results demonstrate the effectiveness of complementary optimization strategies and the interpretability of optimal vocabulary-based discrimination.

**comment/C1：** 36 pages, 5 figures

</details>

### 151. [Payroll Fraud Detection in Synthetic HR/ERP Graphs: An Inductive Comparison of Rules, Path Features, and Graph Neural Networks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41085v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41085

**作者：** Xiaobo Chen、Genliang Zhu、Ziyuan Wang、Chu Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41085v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41085v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文将合成 HR/ERP 图上的工资欺诈筛查建模为归纳式链接预测与实体排序，在未见机构上比较分析师规则、显式链式连接路径特征与 GNN，并为每个植入案例配一个匹配的良性孪生样例以审计生成器捷径。

**相关性简评：** 其「匹配孪生＋仅结构审计」的捷径控制思路，以及显式多跳路径特征与 GNN 的对照，可能可供核查 LVR 基准中多跳关系收益究竟来自架构还是特征、合成数据的伪线索是否被充分排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Payroll Fraud Detection in Synthetic HR/ERP Graphs: An Inductive Comparison of Rules, Path Features, and Graph Neural Networks

**abstract/A1：** Payroll-fraud screening requires finding hidden employee--payee links while distinguishing suspicious relationships from legitimate ones. We formulate this problem as inductive link prediction and entity ranking on synthetic human-resources and enterprise resource planning (HR/ERP) graphs, with unseen organizations and a matched benign twin for every planted case. An original study on 24 held-out organizations and a prospectively frozen extension compare analyst rules, explicit path features and graph neural networks (GNNs). A tuned gradient-boosted model on chained-join features achieved the highest overall link average precision (AP; 0.823), above the GraIL-style GNN (0.663); its 4-hop AP was 0.646 versus 0.272. Thus, the observed multi-hop gain is not specific to GNNs. On a new synthetic cohort with 2.0% positive entities, chained-join boosting again led (AP 0.798); the relational entity GNN also exceeded record-aware rules (0.707 versus 0.504). At a 2% review budget, their precision was 0.695 versus 0.602. Under 40% failed identifier joins, the entity GNN ranked above chained-join boosting (AP 0.712 versus 0.683, descriptively). Structure-only auditing exposed a shortcut in the initial generator; matched twins reduced the audited twin-pool AUROC to 0.493--0.514. These results separate multi-hop candidate discovery, record-based discrimination and review prioritization within an explicit synthetic evaluation design. Code and data are available from the authors on request.

**comment/C1：** 40 pages

</details>
