### 152. [Verifying Text-to-SQL Queries under Changing Business Semantics](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41090v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41090

**作者：** Genliang Zhu、Xiaobo Chen、Ziyuan Wang、Chu Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41090v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41090v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究业务定义（退款归因、税务处理、适用人群、汇率日期）变更后，固定 text-to-SQL 查询的再验证问题；提出可执行的验证剖面对照，在 32 个新编修订族、192 个查询-定义单元格上做预注册确认，并在八次台账预算下比较义务引导检查与通用排序的差异检出率。

**相关性简评：** 「执行成功不等于语义正确」的区分以及预算受限下按义务排序的证据检查与覆盖优先结论，可能可供核查 LVR 中答案正确但 latent 未承载证据的评价方式与证据获取/停止预算设计；但该工作无视觉输入与 latent 状态，联系属较远领域的类比线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Verifying Text-to-SQL Queries under Changing Business Semantics

**abstract/A1：** In text-to-SQL and AI-agent analytics, successful SQL execution does not establish semantic correctness under changing business definitions. We study revalidation of fixed queries after changes to refund attribution, tax treatment, eligible populations and exchange-rate dates. An executable verification profile binds the requested revision and knowledge cutoff to legal counterexample data and exact monetary comparison. A preregistered confirmation on 32 newly authored revision families and two local producer bundles retains 192 query-definition cells: 144 nonconforming, 36 finite-suite agreeing and 12 unresolved. Of the nonconforming cells, 63 agree on the current snapshot. At an eight-ledger budget, obligation-guided checking detects 137 discrepancies versus 127 for generic ordering, a paired difference of 6.9 percentage points (family bootstrap 95% interval \[2.1, 13.6\]; exact family sign-flip p=0.0156). Every ledger arm rejects zero of the 36 agreeing cells. Current-obligation and equal-weight scoring match the guided result, identifying coverage rather than revision-specific priority as the observed source of benefit. Controlled assays, a natural-query pilot and repeated samples provide separate mechanism and robustness evidence. A legal discrepancy refutes conformance; agreement remains restricted to the recorded suite. The study establishes an auditable business-semantic revalidation profile within its authored schema and local-model scope.

**comment/C1：** 22 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-153"></a>

### 153. [Differential rank preservation manifold learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41107v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41107

**作者：** Henry Han、Luyu Niu、Diane Li、Jiacun Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41107v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41107v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作指出 t-SNE、UMAP 等流形学习只保留近邻集合而丢失近邻排序，提出在 t-SNE 损失中加入基于 sigmoid 秩近似的可微排序项（SR-tSNE），并提出只在近邻回到原始名次时才计分的 O-DLP 指标，在八个基准及标签迁移、细胞类型发现、交易等下游任务上验证排序保持的收益。

**相关性简评：** 其「近邻集合一致但排序不一致」的观察与排序保持指标，可能可供核查 latent 表示中细粒度视觉相似结构是否被保留；但研究对象是通用降维与嵌入可视化，与 VLM 推理状态生成无直接关系，迁移价值需进一步判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Differential rank preservation manifold learning

**abstract/A1：** Manifold learning methods like t-SNE and UMAP preserve local neighborhoods, but they do not preserve the ranking of the neighbors inside them. Two embeddings can return the same neighbor set while disagreeing on which point is closest. This loss of order degrades downstream applications that value the order in the embedding. We propose Smooth Rank t-SNE (SR-tSNE), which adds a differentiable rank term to the standard t-SNE loss. To enable efficient training, this term uses a sigmoid rank approximation to explicitly force a point's true high-dimensional neighbors to outrank unrelated intruders and retain their original order. We also introduce Ordinal DLP (O-DLP), an extension of the Degree of Locality Preservation (DLP) metric that credits a neighbor only when it returns at its original rank. Across eight benchmarks spanning image, text, speech, remote-sensing and single-cell data, SR-tSNE outperforms 12 baselines in rank preservation and neighborhood overlap. Ablation studies demonstrate our rank term alone boosts O-DLP by 5% to 28%, a gain not achievable using standard pairwise loss. This advantage transfers to downstream applications. SR-tSNE achieves the best label-transfer performance across seven vision datasets. For cell-type discovery across six single-cell datasets, it ranks first in 17 of 18 dataset-metric combinations, whether clustering resolution is selected with or without cell-type labels. In intraday trading, SR-tSNE achieves higher returns, lower risk, and lower turnover than the baselines. To our knowledge, SR-tSNE is the first t-SNE-family method with explicit order preservation.

**comment/C1：** 67 pages, 10 figures, 28 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-154"></a>

### 154. [An Empirical Study on Learning Rooftop Solar Potential from Satellite Imagery Directly Without Explicit Geometry](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41111v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41111

**作者：** Joshua Lee

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41111v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41111v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 DRSP，把卫星影像与粗略区域日照信息直接映射到屋顶太阳能输出，跳过屋顶朝向、坡度、高度或 DSM 等显式几何中间量，并构建约 6 万与 8.7 万屋顶的两个区域数据集，系统分析训练地理分布、模型深度、图像分辨率和热力图回归目标的影响。

**相关性简评：** 其「直接端到端预测、无需显式中间表示监督」的设定与 latent 推理是否必须依赖显式中间监督的问题形式相近，分辨率与深度消融或可供核查视觉细节保留与任务必要信息的关系，但属遥感应用、未涉及推理状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** An Empirical Study on Learning Rooftop Solar Potential from Satellite Imagery Directly Without Explicit Geometry

**abstract/A1：** Rooftop solar potential estimation often relies on LiDAR, digital surface models (DSMs), or pipelines that explicitly reconstruct roof geometry before estimating solar potential. We propose Direct Rooftop Solar Prediction (DRSP), an end-to-end formulation that maps satellite imagery and coarse regional sunlight information directly to application-level solar outputs. DRSP neither requires explicit geometric representations as input nor predicts roof orientation, slope, height, DSMs, or other geometric quantities as intermediate outputs, and therefore requires no geometric intermediate supervision. To enable large-scale study of this formulation, we develop a programmatic data-curation pipeline that converts per-panel information from the Google Solar API into usable-rooftop masks and spatial solar-energy targets, yielding two geographically distinct datasets containing approximately 60K rooftops from the U.S. Midwest and 87K from Texas. We demonstrate the feasibility of DRSP and systematically characterize the effects of geographic training distribution, model depth, image resolution, regional sunlight conditioning, and heatmap regression objectives. Overall, our results suggest that useful rooftop-scale solar information can be learned directly from 2D imagery without explicit geometric intermediate representations, offering a simpler path toward large-scale solar assessment. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/joshualee124/direct\_rooftop\_solar\_prediction.

</details>

<a id="paper-155"></a>

### 155. [Predicting Human-Gain Curves under Local Proxy Optimization from Repeated Ratings](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41114v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41114

**作者：** Moonwon Choi、Kisung Nam、Seunggeun Lee

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41114v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41114v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 PACE 诊断方法，利用重复人工评分估计代理目标与人类感知质量的一阶对齐和二阶曲率，从而在优化前预测随优化强度变化的人类收益曲线。

**相关性简评：** 其「代理优化强度—真实收益」曲线与曲率分析，可能启发 LVR 中推理计算投入、代理监督与真实任务收益之间关系的刻画和收益预测，但尚无直接视觉推理证据。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Predicting Human-Gain Curves under Local Proxy Optimization from Repeated Ratings

**abstract/A1：** Optimizing a proxy objective can improve human-perceived quality, but the resulting gain can vary with optimization strength. Average proxy-human correlation alone does not reveal how human-perceived quality changes as optimization strength increases. We propose PACE (Proxy Alignment and Curvature Expansion), a pre-optimization diagnostic that uses repeated human ratings to predict the human-gain curve induced by a fixed proxy. The method models proxy optimization as a KL-regularized exponential tilt. At KL radius $d$, the local gain is approximated by $A\\sqrt{d}+Bd$, where $A$ measures first-order proxy-human alignment and $B$ captures the leading second-order correction along the proxy-optimization path. We estimate $A$ and $B$ from repeated human ratings on calibration items and test the resulting gain-curve prediction on held-out evaluation items. Across SummEval, analysis-eligible WMT MQM clusters, an OpenMEVA-MAGS ROC/WP repeated-rating slice, and a GPT-4o judge proxy experiment, PACE consistently reduces held-out gain-prediction error relative to $A$-only, zero-gain, and reliability-only baselines. With the same pre-pool information, it is competitive with flexible proxy-conditional calibration, and a LoRA-DPO experiment provides a modest bridge to actual optimization. Transfer experiments across datasets and evaluation slices show that source coefficients are useful priors, but target calibration further improves prediction. The resulting diagnostic is local and one-sided: it characterizes gentle optimization along the positive tilt branch of a fixed item pool.

**comment/C1：** 10-page main paper, 65 pages including references and supplementary material. Accepted as a Spotlight at NeurIPS 2026. Code: https://github.com/pacifiic/local-proxy-diagnostics

</details>

<a id="paper-156"></a>

### 156. [MotorSense: A Video-EMG Dataset of Motoric Representations for Action Understanding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41127v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41127

**作者：** Eadom Dessalene、Michael Maynord、Amir-Hossein Shahidzadeh、Botao He、Yasamin Tabatabaee、Ruoshi Liu、Ruohan Gao、Paul Pu Liang、Yiannis Aloimonos

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41127v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41127v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 构建首个大规模第一视角视频与双臂表面肌电（EMG）配对数据集（约 100 小时无脚本操作），通过视频-EMG 跨模态预训练学习感觉运动对齐，并迁移到动作识别、3D 手部与接触重建及 EMG 条件视频生成。

**相关性简评：** 把无遮挡的稠密肌电信号当作视觉表示的辅助监督，可能为「手部与接触等细节如何在内部表示中保留」提供训练期线索，但论文不涉及 latent 推理状态与停止决策，可供核查其对齐是否真能支撑细粒度推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MotorSense: A Video-EMG Dataset of Motoric Representations for Action Understanding

**abstract/A1：** Computer vision models learn hand action only from its visual consequences, without access to the underlying embodied motor signals. We address this absence with MotorSense, the first large-scale paired egocentric video and surface electromyography (EMG) dataset. MotorSense pairs bimanual EMG bands with egocentric video and yields synchronized video, bimanual EMG and IMU, and audio across 100 hours of unscripted manipulation. EMG offers a powerful scalable alternative to manual annotation: it captures finger movement, hand configuration, and the forces exerted between hand and environment as a dense signal free of occlusion and labeler bias, whereas manual annotation is costly, subjective, and unreliable under occlusion. We use MotorSense to learn sensorimotor alignment, producing an organization of action that is shared across the visual and motoric spaces, through cross-modal pre-training between video and EMG. The resultant vision models are then transferred to downstream tasks that benefit from sensorimotor alignment: in this case, action recognition, 3D hand reconstruction, and 3D contact reconstruction. We further demonstrate a generative application in which EMG motor signals are used to condition a video diffusion model, yielding videos of fine-grained hand manipulation. Across four action-centric computer vision tasks and eight popular video datasets, we demonstrate that sensorimotor alignment provides a stronger foundation for visual representation learning than conventional self-supervised learning methods over video, outperforming every baseline across all tasks and datasets evaluated.

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS 2026. 27 pages, 9 figures, 5 tables. Project page: https://www.cs.umd.edu/~edessale/motorsense/

</details>

<a id="paper-157"></a>

### 157. [When Should AI Agents Ask? Clarification Requests Under Limited Human Attention](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41129v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41129

**作者：** Hanzhao Wang、Shengjie Liu、Zhengchao Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.HC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41129v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41129v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究多个 AI agent 共享一个注意力有限的人类时如何决定「自行检索、向人提问、还是先做不依赖缺失信息的部分」，将其建为动态规划问题，并提出基于双任务精确求解的 Pairwise Coordination Policy（PCP），在合成基准与 LLM 案例中评估成本。

**相关性简评：** 其「检索／提问／继续」三类动作与共享注意力预算的建模，可能为 LVR 中 LOOK 与 STOP 的收益—成本权衡、外部证据获取顺序提供问题表述与评价线索；但该设定无视觉输入与 latent 状态，迁移性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Should AI Agents Ask? Clarification Requests Under Limited Human Attention

**abstract/A1：** AI agents increasingly carry out delegated tasks in parallel, with one person overseeing several of them at once. In practice, an agent often lacks information its task requires, such as an unstated preference, constraint, or specification. It can ask the human to clarify, or it can search for the information itself, for example in past records, which takes time and effort and may fail. In this paper, we study how to coordinate these decisions across several agents that share one human with limited attention. We build a stylized model in which each agent can search, ask, or continue the parts of its task that do not depend on the missing information, and we formulate the problem as a dynamic program. We first show that whether an agent should ask depends on more than its own uncertainty: an agent with other work to do can be better off asking earlier, and which question the human should answer first depends on all waiting questions, so no ranking computed agent by agent is optimal. We then propose the Pairwise Coordination Policy (PCP), which uses exact one- and two-task problems to estimate how pairs of tasks delay each other when they share the human. Theoretically, PCP is exactly optimal when at most two requirements remain unresolved and costs at most twice the optimum when every unresolved requirement must be resolved by the human. We evaluate PCP on 864 synthetic cases and in a case study with LLM agents. On the synthetic benchmark, PCP's mean gap to optimal cost is 0.07%, and it reduces cost by an average of 18-31% relative to three standard rules for when to ask. In the LLM case study, agents completed every task correctly, and PCP achieved the lowest mean cost in all tested settings.

</details>

<a id="paper-158"></a>

### 158. [FrontierMat: Benchmarking Language Models at the Frontier of Materials R&amp;D](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41162v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41162

**作者：** Feng Chen、Jianguo Huang、Yushan Xiao、Yue Li、Bijun Tang、Xuyu Dong、Xingguo Wang、Feiyang Xie、Jianhui Gu、Dongfeng Cao、Hongda Chen、Yongji Gong、Ruiqi Li、Shuxin Li、Xingji Li、Ruitao Lv、Li Song、Lei Wang、Ye Zhang、Xiaoxu Zhao、Xitao Zheng、Bo An、Zheng Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41162v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41162v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文构建 FrontierMat 基准，包含 330 个由领域专家校验的前沿材料研发问题，要求模型依据给定实验证据推断被隐藏的结论；其中 130 题涉及跨尺度机制，并附一项基于元加权继续预训练与尺度模块化自蒸馏的领域适配实验。

**相关性简评：** 其「模型倾向认可看似合理而非实际观测到的结论」这一失败模式，可能与 LVR 中视觉或实验证据筛选、答案是否真正使用所提供证据的问题相通，跨尺度机制或可供核查多尺度内部状态保留的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FrontierMat: Benchmarking Language Models at the Frontier of Materials R&amp;D

**abstract/A1：** To accelerate materials discovery, language models must be able to address problems at the research frontier. Yet textbook- and examination-derived benchmarks primarily assess established knowledge, leaving performance on frontier research problems largely untested. We introduce FrontierMat, a benchmark of 330 problems drawn from frontier academic and industrial research in fields including aerospace, semiconductors, embodied intelligence and nuclear fusion. Every problem is validated by leading experimentalists and industry experts in its field. Each problem presents a real study and asks the model to infer a withheld outcome or draw a conclusion from selected experimental evidence. Problems are annotated by functional domain, material system and the scale of the governing mechanism; 130 require cross-scale reasoning. The strongest general-purpose model scores only 53.7%, and on matched problems, cross-scale problems score 8.7 points below single-scale ones. Half of the problems are solved by at most one model, and models fail chiefly by endorsing what is plausible rather than what was observed. A domain-adaptation study with meta-weighted continued pretraining and scale-modular self-distillation raises an open-weight model from 36.7% to 55.4%, matching the strongest proprietary model, with improvements on public materials benchmarks and no decline in aggregate performance on general benchmarks. FrontierMat provides a testbed for measuring progress toward language models that support frontier materials R&amp;D.

**comment/C1：** 23 pages, 4 figures, 13 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-159"></a>

### 159. [CAP: Learning to Optimize from Sparse Observations of Related Tasks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41173v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41173

**作者：** Lin Fu、Siwei Fu、Dong Ni

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41173v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41173v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 CAP，从相关任务已完成的稀疏历史记录中构造交互练习函数，学习一个由反馈条件化、面向固定评估预算下最终性能的查询策略，并在 BOPTEST、SU2 等基准上验证。

**相关性简评：** 其「每次观测更新下一次查询、在固定预算下权衡最终收益」的顺序查询策略，可能为 LVR 中 LOOK/STOP 决策与证据获取的预算分配提供概念类比，但任务与视觉推理无关，可迁移性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CAP: Learning to Optimize from Sparse Observations of Related Tasks

**abstract/A1：** In experiment-driven black-box optimization, each evaluation can consume materials, equipment time, and other resources, limiting the number of experiments available for a new task. Records from completed related optimization campaigns could help, but learning a sequential query policy remains challenging when task-specific experience consists of only a few sparse histories. We propose CAP (Candidate Acquisition Policy), which models shared structure and task-specific variation in fixed historical records to sample interactive practice functions. On these functions, CAP learns a feedback-conditioned query policy aimed at final optimization performance under a fixed evaluation budget. At deployment, its parameters remain fixed while each new observation informs the next query. Across five synthetic task families spanning 6 and 20 dimensions and two engineering simulation benchmarks, BOPTEST and SU2, CAP attains the lowest median final-budget value among the reported methods on four synthetic families and both engineering benchmarks. These results indicate that sparse records from completed related tasks can support adaptive query decisions on new tasks, making more effective use of limited, costly experimental evaluations.

</details>

<a id="paper-160"></a>

### 160. [General Frequency Principle for Linear Approximation Classes](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41179v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41179

**作者：** Yujun Wang、Yiqi Gu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、math.NA

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41179v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41179v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文在有限线性逼近类上研究梯度下降训练中的一般频率原理，证明高频处学习器振幅相对目标振幅在训练全程一致趋于零，并给出低频优先的充分条件与“学习更慢的分量通常频率更高”的比较结论，用 Legendre 与三角基数值实验验证。

**相关性简评：** 其频率优先/谱偏置结论可能为 LVR 中视觉细节等高频信息能否进入内部状态、以及表示学习的收敛顺序提供分析视角，但需核查该线性逼近框架与深度 latent 推理动态的可迁移程度。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** General Frequency Principle for Linear Approximation Classes

**abstract/A1：** In this paper, we study a general frequency principle under gradient descent training for the finite linear approximation classes. Under suitable assumptions, we prove that the learner amplitude relative to the target amplitude vanishes at high frequencies, uniformly throughout training. We also establish sufficient conditions for low-frequency priority, covering single-frequency targets trained separately and two frequency components trained in the same run. This general frequency principle does not imply that lower frequencies always converge faster. Instead, for two frequencies that satisfy certain comparison conditions, one component will take longer to learn than the other; and in common cases, the longer component has higher frequency. Numerical experiments with Legendre and trigonometric bases validate the theoretical results.

</details>

<a id="paper-161"></a>

### 161. [Researcher-in-the-Loop Evolutionary Discovery of Entanglement-Assisted Quantum Code Families](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41183v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41183

**作者：** Jae-Hyun Baek、Byung-Sun Won、Jon-Lark Kim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.IT

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41183v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41183v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出「研究者参与循环」的 LLM 程序进化发现流程，结合精确评估、数学推广、不存在性验证与 Lean 证明，用于纠缠辅助量子纠错码，得到族构造并填补 54 个表内空缺。

**相关性简评：** 其「程序进化＋验证器筛选＋形式化证据」链路，以及在有标注的对照中度量对未见长度 n=13/15 的泛化成功率，可能为 LVR 的 latent 轨迹搜索、验证式选择与长度泛化评价提供类比线索，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Researcher-in-the-Loop Evolutionary Discovery of Entanglement-Assisted Quantum Code Families

**abstract/A1：** LLM-guided program evolution can produce mathematical constructions, but validity, generality, and reproducibility require distinct evidence. We develop a researcher-in-the-loop discovery pipeline that combines program evolution, exact evaluation, mathematical generalization, certified nonexistence checks, and formal proof checking. We apply it to entanglement-assisted quantum error-correcting codes (EAQECCs). Starting from tasks that did not specify a family formula, evolved programs produced finite qubit constructions whose structure researchers generalized to the family $\[\[n,1,n-1;n-q-1\]\]\_q$ for prime powers $q\\ge3$ at $n\\ge2q+1$ and for $q=2$ at odd $n\\ge5$. Lean checks the algebraic certificate underlying this family. Together with entanglement lifting, the family closes 54 gaps in archived EAQECC tables. The released evidence includes 115 verified code records, ten nonexistence results with checked DRAT proofs, family implementations, and preserved search programs for exact checks and seeded replay. In a controlled cohort of eight paired generation blocks, validation-selected iterative programs achieve success in 46.1% and 31.6% of held-out executions at unseen lengths $n=13,15$, compared with 19.5% and 3.1% for independently generated programs. In this setting, program evolution and researcher analysis turn finite computational discoveries into a proved family with independently checkable evidence.

**comment/C1：** 25 pages, 6 figures. Code, data and Lean proofs: https://github.com/LeGenAI/llm\_guided\_EAQECC\_search

</details>

### 162. [From Evaluation to Evolution: Benchmarking Image Editing and Evolving Image Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41210v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41210

**作者：** Jun He、Canmiao Fu、Ying Zhang、Zhipeng Huang、Chenyu Mu、Yazhen Xie、Chen Li、Jing Lyu、Weijia Li、Xiang Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41210v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41210v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出图像编辑基准 WeGenEditBench，用预生成清单将请求拆成原子要求并逐项扣分、附视觉证据，同时构建 WeGenEvoStudio 多智能体流程做失败诊断、数据构造、监督适配与再评估，验证针对性迭代带来的提升。

**相关性简评：** 虽聚焦图像编辑而非 LVR 内部状态，其原子要求清单、逐项视觉证据和失败驱动再训练，可能为诊断视觉推理中「哪条证据或要求未被满足」及监督分配提供评价设计参考，需核查可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From Evaluation to Evolution: Benchmarking Image Editing and Evolving Image Models

**abstract/A1：** Real-world image editing requires coordinating multiple visual transformations with content preservation and reference constraints, yet aggregate scores can readily obscure unmet local requirements. Effective evaluation must therefore identify local failures and translate them into priorities for subsequent model improvement. We introduce WeGenEditBench, comprising 2,740 evaluation instances and 148 task types across single-image, text-intensive, and multi-reference editing. Pre-generated checklists shared across models decompose each request into atomic requirements; item-level deductions and visual evidence are established first and then summarized into scores for edit execution, preservation, visual quality, and task-specific correctness. To support the experience-acquisition component of recursive self-improvement (RSI), we develop WeGenEvoStudio, a unified multi-agent harness for RSI-oriented learning that integrates model-conditioned failure diagnosis, retrieval-grounded data construction, supervised adaptation, and re-evaluation. Among 15 evaluated configurations, GPT-Image-2 leads single-image editing with an Overall score of 4.076, while Seedream-5.0-Pro leads text-intensive editing at 4.291. Evolution experiments across four generation and editing benchmarks demonstrate substantial targeted gains: on their report-selected Top-5 single-image targets, FLUX.2 \[dev\] improves Overall from 1.083 to 3.517, while Qwen-Image-Edit-2511 gains 13.2%. Together, our benchmark and harness provide a foundation for developing more reliable image models through interpretable evaluation and feedback-driven learning.

**comment/C1：** 58 pages, 42 figures

</details>

### 163. [TRIDENT and SHAKTI: A Framework and Benchmark for Hindi AI-Text Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41232v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41232

**作者：** Shifali Agrahari、Sujit Kumar、Sanasam Ranbir Singh

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41232v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41232v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文构建了面向印地语的 AI 生成文本检测基准 SHAKTI，并提出基于往返翻译语义一致性的检测框架 TRIDENT，比较静态阈值、动态阈值与多距离特征回归三种变体。

**相关性简评：** 「往返变换后语义一致性」可作为评价指标的思路，可能启发对 LVR 中隐状态内容在改写或重渲染等可逆变换下是否保持一致的核查，但与视觉推理主线距离较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TRIDENT and SHAKTI: A Framework and Benchmark for Hindi AI-Text Detection

**abstract/A1：** As Large Language Models (LLMs) become increasingly multilingual, distinguishing human-written from AI-generated text is essential for maintaining information integrity. However, research remains English-centric, with limited benchmarks for low-resource languages such as Hindi. To address this gap, we introduce SHAKTI, a large-scale Hindi benchmark covering four genres, eight LLM families, multiple prompting strategies, and adversarial transformations. We evaluate its quality through human annotation and analyze linguistic, grammatical, and sentiment-based differences between human- and AI-generated texts. Building on these findings, we propose TRIDENT, a round-trip translation-based detection framework with three variants: TRIDENT-S (static threshold), TRIDENT-D (dynamic threshold), and TRIDENT-C (logistic regression using multi-distance RTT features). TRIDENT leverages semantic consistency between original and round-trip translated texts to identify AI-generated content. Extensive experiments show that TRIDENT outperforms conventional and supervised baselines, with TRIDENT-S achieving the best overall performance. Further evaluations across seven languages, adversarial settings, and unseen LLMs demonstrate its multilingual applicability and robustness, while highlighting sensitivity to the translation model.

</details>

<a id="paper-164"></a>

### 164. [Experts Exhibit Structured Redundancy: Structure-Preserving Expert Pruning for Mixture-of-Experts Large Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41236v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41236

**作者：** Tian Wang、Yongding Tao、Yihong Dong、Xiaoming Wu、Ying Guo、Ge Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41236v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41236v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文指出 MoE 层专家输出存在结构化冗余：稳定秩与熵有效秩远低于名义专家数、有效模态数偏少；据此把专家剪枝重述为结构保持的子集选择问题，用贪心策略提出 PEEP（最大化加权投影能量以保留功能子空间）与 DCEP（最小化加权覆盖误差以保留分布几何），并把 router 权重作为先验，在 DeepSeek-V2-Lite 与 Qwen3-30B-A3B 上验证。

**相关性简评：** 其以稳定秩、熵有效秩刻画内部表示的有效维度与冗余，可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 推理状态是否同样低有效秩且存在结构化冗余，而按子空间能量与分布覆盖选取保留成分的做法或可启发动机 latent 工作区的表示筛选与容量分配；但论文本身属纯文本 MoE 压缩，与视觉多模态推理无直接关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Experts Exhibit Structured Redundancy: Structure-Preserving Expert Pruning for Mixture-of-Experts Large Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Sparse MoE mechanism significantly improves the trade-off between model capacity and inference efficiency. However, practical deployment still requires storing all experts, resulting in substantial memory overhead. We show that expert outputs in MoE layers exhibit structured redundancy. Across different models and task categories, the stable rank and entropy effective rank of expert outputs are consistently far below the nominal number of experts, while expert output distributions also exhibit fewer effective modes. Motivated by these observations, we reformulate expert pruning as a structure-preserving expert subset selection problem and efficiently optimize it via a greedy strategy, rather than only independently scoring and ranking individual experts or exhaustively searching over expert combinations. We propose two expert pruning methods under the unified structure-preserving framework: Projection Energy Expert Pruning (PEEP), which preserves the functional subspace by maximizing weighted projection energy, and Distributional Coverage Expert Pruning (DCEP), which preserves the distributional geometry by minimizing weighted coverage error. Both methods incorporate router-derived weights as priors, enabling them to jointly preserve the global structural relationships among experts while accounting for the local importance of individual experts. Experiments on DeepSeek-V2-Lite and Qwen3-30B-A3B demonstrate that, at the expert retention ratios of 75% and 50%, our methods outperform the state-of-the-art baselines on the majority of benchmarks and consistently achieve the best average performance across both general tasks and mathematical reasoning tasks.

</details>

<a id="paper-165"></a>

### 165. [Generative SDEs with Trainable Diffusion and Density-Based Losses](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41243v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41243

**作者：** Angxiu Ni

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、math.DS

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41243v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41243v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出一种 divergence-kernel 方法，用直接依赖终端密度的损失联合优化神经 SDE 的漂移场、扩散场和初始高斯分布；在 2 维与 8 维高斯混合实验中学习时间无关场，失模样本明显少于同等参数量的 score-based 生成器。

**相关性简评：** 其“直接优化终端密度、考察模式覆盖”的训练目标，可能可供核查 diffusion 式 latent 推理状态生成中如何避免状态塌缩到少数模式；但实验限于低维高斯混合，与视觉推理尚无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Generative SDEs with Trainable Diffusion and Density-Based Losses

**abstract/A1：** We develop a divergence-kernel method for jointly optimizing neural SDE drift and diffusion fields and the initial Gaussian distribution, using losses that depend directly on the terminal density. In two- and eight-dimensional Gaussian-mixture experiments, we learn time-independent fields and obtain far fewer off-mode samples than score-based generators with similar parameter counts. The drift attracts samples toward the modes, while the diffusion helps disperse excess probability between them.

**comment/C1：** 17 pages, 5 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-166"></a>

### 166. [MEMIG: Self-evolving Memory Construction for Antibody Optimization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41245v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41245

**作者：** Yunhui Jang、Zach Wang、Jai Doshi、Kangway Chuang、Sidney Lisanza、Taylor Joren

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41245v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41245v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 MEMIG，一种面向抗体优化的自进化智能体记忆构建框架：用领域标识符索引经验条目、累积属性预测器测得的定量效应，并通过分解评估集合测量共同干预间的上位效应，以替代自由文本重写式记忆。

**相关性简评：** 非视觉、非推理期 latent 生成工作，但其「可索引、定量、结构化」记忆与精确查表检索，或许可为 LVR 中证据缓存结构、跨干预的贡献分配提供问题表述上的启发，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MEMIG: Self-evolving Memory Construction for Antibody Optimization

**abstract/A1：** Memory has become central to self-evolving LLM agents, which store and retrieve experience from past rollouts to guide future decisions. Existing systems represent this experience as free-form natural language and maintain it by rewriting, which fits tasks whose experience is narratable. However, in scientific discovery and optimization, experience is often a quantity measured by an oracle under a specific context. Rewriting it as text leaves no index for where a record should be, replaces a measurement with a description, and cannot express how interventions interact. To address this, we argue that agent memory for biomolecule optimization should instead be indexable, quantitative, and structured, and we introduce MEMIG. This memory construction framework realizes these three properties. In detail, MEMIG addresses each insight with a domain-grounded identifier and accumulates the quantitative effect measured by a property oracle, so that updates remain local and retrieval becomes an exact lookup. Next, MEMIG connects insights whose interventions are applied together and measures their epistasis by decomposing each evaluated set. Our results show that MEMIG improves performance in most settings across in silico antibody optimization tasks.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-167"></a>

### 167. [SketchDirector: Complex Scene Sketching via Agentic Layout Reasoning and Detail Refinement](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41260v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41260

**作者：** Yiqi Liang、Xiaodong Cun

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41260v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41260v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SketchDirector，用 LLM 将文本场景拆解为多个 Bézier 曲线草稿资产，再通过带自演化记忆的 agent 循环规划空间布局，最后用扩散先验细化细节并保持几何结构，同时发布 MCSketch8K 数据集。

**相关性简评：** 其显式布局状态与自演化记忆驱动的迭代规划循环，可能为 LVR 中结构化中间状态、多步更新与细节细化时保结构的问题提供类比线索，但任务属图像生成，仍需核查状态表示是否可迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SketchDirector: Complex Scene Sketching via Agentic Layout Reasoning and Detail Refinement

**abstract/A1：** Existing sketch generation methods often rely on image guidance and are largely limited to individual objects or simplified scenes, leaving text-guided complex scene sketching underexplored. We present SketchDirector, an agentic framework that mimics the human sketching workflow for automatic multi-concept scene sketching. Given a scene-level text prompt, SketchDirector first employs a large language model~(LLM) to decompose the scene into multiple draft assets, where each is represented by a set of Bézier curves. Then, an agentic reasoning loop iteratively plans and executes their spatial arrangement, guided by self-evolving memory. Finally, a detail refinement module leverages diffusion priors to enhance sketch quality while preserving geometric structure. To support future research and applications in multi-concept scene sketch generation, we further introduce the \\textit{MCSketch8K} dataset, comprising editable vector assets, compositional layouts, and object-level manipulation annotations. Extensive experiments show that SketchDirector outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in semantic and compositional quality while effectively preserving overall contour organization during refinement. The project page is: \\href{https://yiki77.github.io/SketchDirector/}{https://yiki77.github.io/SketchDirector/}.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages, 13 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-168"></a>

### 168. [From 1D Benchmarks to Complex 3D Geometries: Can Agents Reliably Specify Problems for Automated PDE Simulation?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41267v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41267

**作者：** Akshat Jani、Sakhinana Sagar Srinivas、Venkataramana Runkana

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41267v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41267v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究 agent 能否为自动化 PDE 仿真可靠构造完整可执行的 JSON 规格（几何、物理、边界条件与数值设置），将模型抽取与确定性科学检查、显式澄清、保留无关字段的局部修补结合，并分别评估规格正确性、执行完成度与数值验证。

**相关性简评：** 其「false-ready」判定与缺失信息触发澄清的流程，可能可供核查 LVR/VLM 推理中的 STOP 决策与证据获取；规格是否保留任务必要信息也类比内部状态保真度，但领域距离较远，需较多迁移核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From 1D Benchmarks to Complex 3D Geometries: Can Agents Reliably Specify Problems for Automated PDE Simulation?

**abstract/A1：** Turning an engineering request into a simulation requires consistent geometry, physics, boundary conditions, and numerical settings. Recent agent-assisted systems automate simulation setup, but successful execution alone does not establish whether the specification preserves the intended problem or correctly handles missing information. We study whether agents can reliably construct complete executable JSON specifications for automated geometry creation, meshing, PDE solving, and result export. Our workflow combines model extraction with deterministic scientific and capability checks, explicit clarification, and validated local patches that preserve unrelated accepted fields. We evaluate specification correctness, execution completion, and numerical verification separately. On 120 template-related requests, the full workflow achieves 98.3% correct first-turn specification and decision outcomes, compared with 92.5% for schema-assisted single shot and 82.5% for model-only: gains of 5.8 and 15.8 percentage points, respectively. It makes no false-ready decisions among the 42 requests requiring clarification or rejection. Component ablations identify contributions from scientific checks and conditional repair. Separate downstream studies cover 1D, 2D, and 3D steady and unsteady, linear and nonlinear problems, including flow and elasticity with complex three-dimensional wing geometries. A 30-case gallery demonstrates automated execution from retained JSON specifications, while 9 of 12 selected live user-to-results tests achieve joint specification, execution, export, and numerical-reference success (75.0%). These results support traceable, engineer-reviewed simulation assistance within the tested scope, while fresh-response regressions identify consistency as a priority for further improvement.

</details>

<a id="paper-169"></a>

### 169. [SciGraphSelect: Auditable Scientific Data Selection through Knowledge--Reasoning Graphs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41280v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41280

**作者：** MiHyeon Kim、Ji-Eun Han、Eunkyeong Lee、Jeehyun Lim、Hwijung Ryu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41280v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41280v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SciGraphSelect，用类型化的「知识—推理」图组织领域知识、可复用推理操作与参考文献支持的关系，再结合概念覆盖、关系与路径共支持、方向对齐为候选科学文档打分，使数据筛选结果可追溯到具体证据。

**相关性简评：** 其「参考侧保留丰富关系结构、对候选侧嵌入作保守解释」的审计式证据筛选思路，可能为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选与训练语料/轨迹取舍提供概念参照；但与 latent 推理状态生成无直接关联，可迁移性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SciGraphSelect: Auditable Scientific Data Selection through Knowledge--Reasoning Graphs

**abstract/A1：** Scientific data selection is typically driven by relevance or utility scores, providing limited insight into why a document is retained. We introduce SciGraphSelect, a reference-grounded framework for auditable scientific data selection. SciGraphSelect organizes domain-specific knowledge, reusable reasoning operations, and reference-attested relations into a typed knowledge--reasoning graph, then scores candidate documents using concept coverage, relation and path co-support, and directional alignment. To avoid over-interpreting document embeddings, rich relational structure is preserved on the reference side while candidate-side evidence is treated conservatively without assuming relation entailment. By exposing the reference-grounded evidence behind each selection, SciGraphSelect brings traceability to the upstream construction of scientific training corpora and complements downstream scientific verification.

**comment/C1：** 40th Conference on Neural Information Processing Systems (NeurIPS 2026). Workshop: AI4Science

</details>

### 170. [Quantile Propagation Planning for Budgeted Tail-Risk Evaluation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41351v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41351

**作者：** Weijia Han、Zhi Huang、Lisha Qu、Yuanyuan Tan、Jiayue Meng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、eess.SY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41351v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41351v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 quantile propagation planning（QPP）：把少量固定扰动分位点沿动力学传播并积分所得代价曲线，在有限评估预算下同时评估均值与尾部风险，给出传播层精确性、网格细化收敛与单侧保守性保证，并在过载与库存任务上验证。

**相关性简评：** 用固定少量分位点做确定性信念／扰动传播，可能为 LVR 中有限算力下的 latent 状态传播与计算分配提供概念类比，其与粒子滤波式状态抽象、预算受限评价的具体对应可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Quantile Propagation Planning for Budgeted Tail-Risk Evaluation

**abstract/A1：** Risk-aware planning must identify rare, costly outcomes using only a limited number of model evaluations. Monte Carlo rollouts spend this budget on noisy tail estimates, while planning from the mean can miss the tail entirely. We introduce quantile propagation planning (QPP), which propagates a small, fixed set of disturbance quantiles through the dynamics and integrates the resulting cost curve to evaluate both mean and tail risk. The method is exact at the propagated levels, converges as the quantile grid is refined, and admits a one-sided conservatism guarantee under stated structural conditions. In controlled overload and inventory tasks, QPP reduces realized tail cost and constraint violations relative to mean-rollout planning. On overload, it remains competitive with sampled evaluators that use substantially more trajectories. These results position QPP as a transparent, budget-controlled approach to tail-risk evaluation.

**comment/C1：** 60 pages, 8 figures, 34 tables; includes appendices

</details>

<a id="paper-171"></a>

### 171. [What We Keep of What We Forget: Replacement Operators and Routing Semantics in Training-Free MoE Expert Pruning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41366v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41366

**作者：** Ezza Ali、Sehrish Bibi、Momina Moetesum、Faisal Shafait

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41366v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41366v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在 OLMoE、DeepSeek-V2-Lite、Moonlight 等模型上固定剪枝掩码，系统比较三类「被剪 MoE 专家替代算子」，指出幸存专家 gate 缩放这一常被隐式处理的选择影响最大，并提出低秩的 tilted novice 映射与五项报告清单。

**相关性简评：** 替代算子与低秩输入映射或可类比 LVR 中视觉旁路、被移除计算路径的替换与贡献分配，可供核查；其评测发现（选择题保留 92–97% 而 GSM8K、代码分数骤降）也提示单一基准可能掩盖推理能力损失。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** What We Keep of What We Forget: Replacement Operators and Routing Semantics in Training-Free MoE Expert Pruning

**abstract/A1：** Training-free expert pruning makes two decisions: which experts to remove, and what the layer computes when its router still selects a removed expert. Published methods change both at once. Holding the mask fixed, we compare replacement operators from three families on OLMoE, DeepSeek-V2-Lite and Moonlight, with Granite as a fourth router, using paired, multiplicity-corrected tests. The largest effect is a choice usually left implicit: the survivors' gate scale. On routers trained without top-k renormalization, renormalizing the survivors' gates, a common implementation choice, costs 0.8-8.5 points and reverses whether rerouting beats MoNE's mean-output replacement; on two routers trained with renormalization, the trained scale wins at 50% pruning. We introduce the tilted novice, a closed-form low-rank map of the pruned expert's input whose rank-0 case is MoNE's constant. At 50% pruning it adds 1.2-1.8% parameters and gains 3.45 and 1.37 points over the constant on OLMoE and Moonlight, stable across calibration draws and largely unchanged by 4- and 3-bit quantization. No operator dominates: trained-scale rerouting is best on DeepSeek-V2-Lite, and a partial copy of each pruned expert is statistically indistinguishable from the tilted novice on two models. Evaluation can change conclusions: at 25% pruning MoNE's constant keeps 92-97% of dense multiple-choice accuracy but only 5-65% of GSM8K and 0-17% of code scores, and the calibration domain moves the mask 4-9 times more than resampling. We propose a five-item reporting checklist.

**comment/C1：** 56 pages, 8 figures, 76 tables

</details>

### 172. [Queryable Gaussian View Index: Text Retrieval and Selective Camera Localization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41373v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41373

**作者：** Tianyu Xiong、Rui Li、Jiaqi Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41373v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41373v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 QGVI，在已有 Gaussian 场景资产上构建可文本检索的视图索引，把结构化描述绑定到已知相机位姿与地点标识，并用关键点内点筛选实现选择性 RGB 相机定位，在九个场景上报告检索与位姿成功率及延迟。

**相关性简评：** 预先构建的视图—描述索引支持离线语义检索，配合内点门控的候选接受机制，可能为 LVR 中「该取哪份视觉证据」及证据验证与接受决策提供线索，但其目标是位姿估计而非推理，需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Queryable Gaussian View Index: Text Retrieval and Selective Camera Localization

**abstract/A1：** We present Queryable Gaussian View Index (QGVI) for querying existing Gaussian scene assets and their associated calibrated reference data. Text-view retrieval and selective RGB camera localization return poses in the asset's coordinate frame. QGVI-T turns Gaussian-rendered views into a language-searchable index of scene locations, binding structured captions to known camera poses and place identifiers. Semantic and lexical retrieval select views from distinct places without online rendering, VLM calls, or a scene-specific pose network. The image route, QGVI-I, filters LoGS-based geometric estimates using distinct inlier query keypoints. It omits photometric refinement and preserves accepted pose coordinates. On nine development scenes, QGVI-T achieves 58.3% Top-1 and 78.3% Top-5 joint relevance-and-validity success, compared with 26.1% and 34.6% for our CityLoC reimplementation with refinement. QGVI-I achieves 97.3% scene-mean pose success at 1 m/2 degrees, with pooled false-acceptance and false-rejection rates of 0.707% and 1.142%. A paired cached evaluation retains all 1,775 initializer poses correct at this threshold. Recorded median text query latency is 0.020 s. Matched controls show competitive BM25 retrieval and slightly lower false acceptance for thresholded full LoGS; grid augmentation suggests modest gains on novel-view-derived descriptions. We report model-judged semantic success separately from source-camera recovery, with source-verified or estimated scales for geometric evaluation.

**comment/C1：** Includes supplementary material; 3 figures, 47 references

</details>

<a id="paper-173"></a>

### 173. [From the Zoo to the BULLPEN: Sample-Efficient LLM Profiling via Multi-Channel Model Embeddings](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41375v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41375

**作者：** Russell Ro、Qiming Cui、Reza Abbasi-Asl

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41375v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41375v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 BULLPEN 框架，用模型答案文本联合正确性与问题文本构建模型级定长嵌入，并新增 20 个行为、风格与来源基准，使数百道探测题即可低成本预测模型多种属性。

**相关性简评：** 其「少量探测输入压缩为向量并预测多重属性」的建模范式，可能为 LVR 中低成本预测模型行为或诊断表示所含信息提供参照，但属模型级画像而非实例级 latent 状态，可迁移性待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From the Zoo to the BULLPEN: Sample-Efficient LLM Profiling via Multi-Channel Model Embeddings

**abstract/A1：** Choosing among the hundreds of thousands of available large language models (LLMs) requires a thorough understanding of each candidate model's properties. In practice, each property is measured by its own benchmark suite, so profiling a model on many properties means paying for many separate evaluations. To address this, model embeddings compress each model into a fixed-length vector from which many properties can be predicted. Existing methods, such as EmbedLLM, build these vectors from correctness patterns and question features, and often need large probe batteries to do so. However, many behaviors that are important when choosing a model, such as its verbosity, safety and style, show up more in how it answers than in whether it is correct. We introduce BULLPEN (Behavioral Understanding of Large Language models via Profile ENcodings), a framework that extends EmbedLLM in three ways. First, BULLPEN organizes the downstream tasks that prior work uses and adds 20 task benchmarks we built on behavior, style and provenance. Second, it is the first framework to read a model's own answer text jointly with its correctness and the question text. Third, it encodes models from a few hundred questions instead of the full 24,118-question pool. Across 260 open-weight models, our answer-text encoder scores 0.48 +/- 0.10 on the new tasks against 0.35 +/- 0.10 for the best prior encoder. Our encoders that read answer text keep at least 91% of their full-pool skill from only 400 questions (1.3% of the inference compute of the full question pool), while a correctness-only baseline keeps 83% with the same 400 questions. Because these embeddings need only model outputs, they support behavioral auditing and low-cost profiling, and could extend to API-only models. Code and data: https://github.com/wcquesera/bullpen

**comment/C1：** 28 pages, 7 figures, 12 tables. Code and data: https://github.com/wcquesera/bullpen

</details>

<a id="paper-174"></a>

### 174. [DUGO: Dual-Scale Gaussian Memory for Online Temporal Action Localization in Egocentric Procedural Videos](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41402v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41402

**作者：** Mohammed Aman Bhuiyan、Md. Sazzad Hossain Adib、Samiul Basir Bhuiyan、Mir Faiyaz Hossain、Syed Mohammed Shamsul Islam、Md Zakir Hossain、Shafin Rahman

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41402v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41402v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出在线时序动作定位框架 DUGO：用双尺度时序编码器结合深度可分离局部表示与全局自注意力，并以高斯原型记忆库存储动作记忆，在编码器和 anchor-query 阶段通过门控交叉注意力调用，在自中心程序视频数据集上取得领先结果。

**相关性简评：** 其在无未来帧的流式条件下维护紧凑的随机原型记忆并门控取用，可能与 LVR 的潜在状态更新、长程证据保留及实时预算下的决策相关，可供核查；但任务为纯视频动作定位，与 VLM 推理联系较远，迁移需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DUGO: Dual-Scale Gaussian Memory for Online Temporal Action Localization in Egocentric Procedural Videos

**abstract/A1：** Online Temporal Action Localization (OnTAL) aims to detect and localize action instances from untrimmed streaming videos in real time. Unlike offline approaches, OnTAL models have no access to future frames and cannot go back and refine earlier predictions, which makes the task considerably harder given the strict real-time constraints and narrow temporal window available. Most existing OnTAL methods lean on either brief anchor-based context or a single-scale memory of past frames, which limits how well they can capture actions unfolding across multiple time scales or requiring long-range reasoning in Procedural Videos. To address these gaps, we present DUGO (Dual-Scale Encoding and Gaussian Memory Online), a new OnTAL framework that jointly models local motion dynamics and long-range temporal dependencies. At its core, DUGO uses a Dual-Scale Temporal Encoder, pairing depth-wise local representations with global self-attention so it can pick up both fine-grained temporal patterns and broader contextual cues. We also introduce a Gaussian Memory Bank, which represents action memory as stochastic, input-conditioned Gaussian prototypes, allowing for richer temporal memory through gated cross-attention applied at both the encoder and anchor-query stages. Extensive experiments on procedural egocentric datasets (EGTEA, EPIC-Kitchens-100) demonstrate that DUGO achieves state-of-the-art performance while also providing substantial improvements on non-egocentric benchmarks such as Muses. The project page is available at: https://aman554-eq.github.io/DUGO

**comment/C1：** This paper has been accepted at the 2026 IEEE International Conference on Big Data (IEEE BigData 2026)

</details>

<a id="paper-175"></a>

### 175. [Certifying Retraining Regret through Residual Structure](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41410v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41410

**作者：** Peida Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41410v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41410v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究区间值表格所有连续补全下，固定 ridge 预测器相对全特征重训练的 regret 认证：给出基于原始行残差的可验证一致误差界、可计算的原始表见证，并在真实任务与定点预测器契约上测试认证宽度与停止决策。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉或多模态推理无直接联系，但其「用残差/能量条件给出可计算上界与见证来认证固定点停止决策」的思路，可能为 latent 推理中的 STOP 决策验证与证据充分性判断提供启发性工具，可供核查迁移空间。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Certifying Retraining Regret through Residual Structure

**abstract/A1：** We certify a fixed ridge predictor's regret against full-feature retraining over every continuous completion of an interval-valued table. Our main theorem gives verifiable original-row residual conditions for a uniform error bound independent of nuisance-feature scale and rank, with an asymptotically sharp leading constant and quantitative leakage stability. Full-rank constructions separate certification dimension from feature dimension, including examples requiring directions beyond the candidate. Observed-row anchors make the conditions computable, while exact row geometry and global search return valid upper bounds and feasible original-table witnesses. A finite-scale contraction and common-domain moment fallback implement the structural result. Four real-world tasks retain all model features and certify twenty rank-three instances to width $10^{-5}$. Their primary median widths improve over centered moments. Joint residual refinement additionally certifies all twenty new response-independent masks to the same precision within 76.21 seconds. For a concrete fixed-point predictor contract, pure energy certifies thirteen of fifteen stopping decisions in ten seconds, versus five for moments. Refinement certifies all fifteen within 91.68 seconds at the same objective threshold.

**comment/C1：** 46 pages, 5 figures. Code and reproduction materials: https://github.com/Peida-Lee/certifying-retraining-regret

</details>

<a id="paper-176"></a>

### 176. [A Unified XAI-NSAI Analytical Framework for Trustworthy IoT Anomaly Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41424v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41424

**作者：** Hugues Leger、Giovani R. Abuaitah

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41424v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41424v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出一个统一分析框架，从五个互补维度同时刻画 XAI 与神经符号（NSAI）方法，用三篇代表性研究演示其分析用途，并在医疗 IoT 异常检测系统中展示其设计用途。

**相关性简评：** 其将解释性与神经符号推理合并考察、并在资源约束下权衡证据与人类决策的维度划分，可能为 latent 推理的证据筛选与可解释核查提供问题表述上的参照，但领域较远，需核查是否真有可迁移线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Unified XAI-NSAI Analytical Framework for Trustworthy IoT Anomaly Detection

**abstract/A1：** The widespread deployment of Internet of Things (IoT) devices in critical environments makes anomaly detection essential for identifying abnormal behaviors and supporting timely investigation and response. However, accurate detection alone is insufficient. Practical IoT deployments must also provide explanations, incorporate domain knowledge and reasoning, operate under IoT constraints, and support human decision making. Although recently proposed approaches increasingly incorporate Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) or Neuro-Symbolic Artificial Intelligence (NSAI), XAI and NSAI methods are often considered independently. We argue that they should be considered together to better understand their complementary roles in supporting trustworthy IoT anomaly detection. To address this gap, we propose a unified analytical framework organized around five complementary dimensions. The framework provides a structured basis for characterizing existing approaches, identifying capability gaps and design trade-offs, and guiding the design of new anomaly detection systems for different IoT applications. We demonstrate the framework's analytical utility by applying it to three representative studies and illustrate its design utility through its application to a Medical IoT anomaly detection system.

**comment/C1：** 8 pages, 3 figures, accepted for publication in the Proceedings of the IEEE Annual Congress on Artificial Intelligence of Things (AIoT), 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-177"></a>

### 177. [Evidence-Guided Recovery for AI Agents with Ambiguous Tool Outcomes and Partial Execution](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41453v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41453

**作者：** Genliang Zhu、Xiaobo Chen、Ziyuan Wang、Chu Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41453v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41453v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究 AI agent 在工具调用结果不明确（超时可能失败、已提交或仍在执行）且流程部分完成时的恢复问题，提出基于已批准义务、提供方能力与公开证据相容状态的证据引导恢复框架，并用「终止证书」区分真正达成目标与公开证据支持的安全完成，配以可行性检查与效应历史裁定；在有限任务集与预注册对照中比较规划建议与证据提示。

**相关性简评：** 其「终止证书」与证据相容性检查可为 LVR 的 STOP 决策与证据充分性判据提供概念线索，但对象是工具型 agent 而非视觉推理，需核查是否能映射到 latent 状态下的停止与残余效应判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Evidence-Guided Recovery for AI Agents with Ambiguous Tool Outcomes and Partial Execution

**abstract/A1：** Tool timeouts can leave AI agents facing ambiguous tool outcomes and partial execution: an action may have failed, committed or remained in flight while other workflow steps have already completed. We formulate evidence-guided task recovery over approved obligations, provider capabilities and states compatible with public evidence. A shared admissibility check handles key expiry, authorization timing, dependencies and residual effects; a terminal certificate separates actual goal satisfaction from publicly supported safe completion. The known-model implementation combines standard observation-contingent planning with a separately implemented effect-history adjudicator. In a finite study of 37 assignments, planning and three active controls each certify 28 completions; four saved synthetic fee units incur additional computation. A preregistered assignment-held-out comparison then evaluates planning advice against evidence prompting with two local model bundles. Across 58 paired units in 19 previously developed task families, advice certifies 30 completions versus 9, a 36.2-percentage-point difference (exact family sign-flip p=0.0078), using 272 proposals versus 399. All 116 trajectories pass independent replay. Both models exhibit a positive completion difference. The comparison measures the evaluated advice interface under a common shield; search-versus-rule advice requires a separate matched comparison. Unknown and handoff outcomes remain explicit, with guarantees tied to the declared provider capabilities rather than arbitrary third-party exactly-once execution.

**comment/C1：** 24 pages, 1 figure, and 10 tables. Includes a finite recovery model, model-facing confirmation study with two local-model bundles, independent replay, and an explicit provider-capability scope

</details>

<a id="paper-178"></a>

### 178. [When Everyone Can Build a Model: Mechanistic Inference in the Age of AI](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41454v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41454

**作者：** Marvin van Aalst、Adèle H. Ribeiro、Anna Matuszyńska

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41454v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41454v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文讨论 AI 辅助建模降低了生物系统灰箱模型的构建门槛，但预测成功不能保证所推断机制为真；作者主张用因果推断判断哪些关系可识别，并用集成分析刻画同等拟合下仍开放的响应函数范围。

**相关性简评：** 其「可识别性、多个模型同等拟合、学习组件吸收机制部分误差」的讨论，可能为 LVR 中 latent 是否真正使用视觉证据、贡献分配与干预式验证提供问题表述上的类比，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Everyone Can Build a Model: Mechanistic Inference in the Age of AI

**abstract/A1：** Modern deep learning and AI-assisted code generation are lowering the technical barrier to building mathematical models of biological systems that reproduce complex observations. This lets researchers with domain expertise build semi-mechanistic, hybrid models constrained by prior knowledge and augmented with learned structure without extensive training in modelling. However, easier construction does not make interpretation easier: predictive success of such so-called grey-box models does not establish that an inferred mechanism corresponds to the one that generated the data, and the learned component can absorb errors in the mechanistic part. Many biological modelling problems are underdetermined, so several, possibly infinitely many, models may reproduce the same data equally well. This ambiguity enters at two modelling choices: which variables can affect others, and which response function describes the underlying mechanism. For the first choice, causal inference provides a formal framework stating which causal relationships and intervention effects are identifiable from the data and available knowledge. For the second, ensemble analyses quantify the uncertainty and the range of behaviours still allowed by models that fit equally well. Together, they turn a single good fit into an explicit account of what is supported, what remains open, and which experiment would decide.

**comment/C1：** 8 pages, 2 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-179"></a>

### 179. [Improving the Optimization Landscape of Matrix Completion with $ε$-close Surrogates](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41455v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41455

**作者：** Ziye Ma

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、math.OC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41455v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41455v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文在矩阵补全设定下研究非凸优化景观：向观测掩码的零空间注入 ε 级扰动以构造带噪矩阵感知代理问题，证明其解与真值 O(ε) 接近且保持有效的 RIP 常数，从而在不对采样率和非相干性作显式要求的情况下给出恢复保证。

**相关性简评：** 其「从部分观测恢复潜在低秩状态，并向未观测方向注入噪声以改善过参数化优化景观」的视角，或可类比 LVR 中视觉证据不全时的 latent 状态补全与训练期正则化，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Improving the Optimization Landscape of Matrix Completion with $ε$-close Surrogates

**abstract/A1：** Non-convex optimization is central to machine learning and AI, yet unlike its convex counterpart, it still lacks a general and principled framework. In this work, we try to highlight the importance of combining noisy perturbations with over-parametrization mathematically under the classic setting of matrix completion (MC). Matrix completion is an important non-convex recovery problem in which only a subset of a matrix's entries is observed, and the remaining entries must be recovered using low-rank constraints. This problem is notoriously difficult because observations are limited. However, we show that by injecting $ε$-level perturbations into the nullspace of the observation mask, we can construct noisy matrix sensing surrogates whose solutions remain $\\mathcal{O}(ε)$-close to the ground truth with valid restricted isometry property (RIP) constants. The existence of RIP further enables powerful tensor frameworks to be applied with theoretical guarantees. This approach reflects a broader empirical trend in which stochasticity or noise improves the curvature of the optimization landscape, allowing over-parameterized (a.k.a large) models to better separate signal from noise. Assuming each matrix entry is observed independently, we establish quantitative recovery guarantees without explicit requirements on sampling rate or incoherence, and discuss how such assumptions can further strengthen our results.

**comment/C1：** 30 pages, accepted at NeurIPS'26

</details>

<a id="paper-180"></a>

### 180. [RetryRoute: Retry-Aware Model Routing for Agentic Image Editing Services](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41460v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41460

**作者：** Hanshuai Cui、Zhiqing Tang、Zhi Yao、Jun Luo、Weijia Jia

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41460v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41460v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文发现图像编辑模型重试存在「粘性失败」（失败后再试的成功率仅为首次尝试的 17–53%），据此提出 RetryRoute：在失败状态上规划重试、升级到其他模型或放弃，并把实时排队等待计入成本。

**相关性简评：** 将失败历史当作状态做重试/升级/放弃的序贯决策并显式权衡成本，与 LVR 中的停止决策、计算预算和成功信念估计可能有概念联系，可供核查；但研究对象是服务路由，不涉及视觉推理内部状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RetryRoute: Retry-Aware Model Routing for Agentic Image Editing Services

**abstract/A1：** Web agents increasingly edit images by calling open editing models in a critique-and-retry loop, and these models differ in cost by more than an order of magnitude. After every rejected edit, a service must decide whether to retry the same model, escalate to another or give up. Existing routers pick one model per request, and cascades escalate in a fixed order. However, both treat a retry as an independent draw. We identify sticky failures, meaning that a retry after a failure succeeds only 17-53% as often as a first try across six open models, three benchmarks and two judges. Based on this finding, we propose RetryRoute, a retry-aware router that estimates each model's chance of success given the request's failure history. It plans retries, escalations and abandonment over failure states and adds live queue waits to the cost, so the same plan becomes load-aware dispatch. At the main budget, RetryRoute's success under the in-loop judge exceeds the strongest of eleven prior baselines by 2.8 points on average over eight groups. On a GPU serving testbed it raises the share of edits delivered within the service-level objective by 5.0-6.3 points over the best prior baseline at every load.

</details>

<a id="paper-181"></a>

### 181. [VoxLution: Evolving Voxels for High-Fidelity 3D Scene Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41501v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41501

**作者：** Daoyi Gao、Xinyi Zhang、Alexey Bokhovkin、Lei Li、Angela Dai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41501v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41501v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** VoxLution 用基于 transformer 的由粗到细体素序列建模生成高保真室内 3D 场景：先生成低分辨率空间布局，再分块局部精化几何，并以分辨率无关的八分体编码和邻块边界信息保持整体一致性。

**相关性简评：** 其多分辨率序列化、由粗到细的状态逐步细化与边界一致性约束，可能为 LVR 中分层潜在表示如何先定全局再补局部细节、避免局部块间证据割裂提供类比线索，但原文并不涉及视觉推理或答案使用，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** VoxLution: Evolving Voxels for High-Fidelity 3D Scene Generation

**abstract/A1：** High-resolution 3D indoor scene generation is crucial for immersive applications such as virtual reality, spatial computing, and robotics simulation. We introduce VoxLution, a coarse-to-fine transformer-based approach that generates high-fidelity indoor scenes with rich details, overcoming the overly smooth geometry typical of existing generative models. First, we generate a low-resolution spatial layout; then, based on this structural information, we progressively refine the scene geometry locally in chunks. Key to our approach is formulating 3D super-resolution as a sequence modeling problem over multi-resolution sparse voxel grids. To achieve this, we introduce a training-free, resolution-invariant tokenization scheme that compactly encodes the geometric information of eight octants into a single decimal number. We formulate the transformer sequence to include multiple resolutions, allowing the coarse layout to guide the generation of fine local details. To ensure coherence across local chunks, we incorporate adjacent chunk boundary information directly into the transformer sequencing. VoxLution achieves scene generation at a finer voxel resolution than existing baselines, demonstrating state-of-the-art performance on standard benchmarks with 59.6% lower FPD and 51.1% lower FID on 3D-FRONT, and 70.5% lower FPD and 73.8% lower FID on Scannet++.

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://daoyig.github.io/VoxLution/; Video: https://youtu.be/0DAaquV1xK8

</details>

<a id="paper-182"></a>

### 182. [Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning of Transformer Encoders for Audio-Image-Symbolic Music Retrieval](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41512v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41512

**作者：** Eliseo Fuentes-Martinez、Antonio Hidalgo-Centeno、Jorge Calvo-Zaragoza、Antonio Javier Gallego

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.SD、eess.AS

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41512v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41512v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文比较全参微调、LoRA 与 QLoRA 在九种音频、乐谱图像与符号乐谱编码器上的效果，并用 2x2 设计交叉考察编码器适配与共享投影对多模态音乐检索的影响。

**相关性简评：** 其「单模态判别提升未必迁移到多模态组织」的观察，可能与 VLM 中视觉编码器冻结/适配及跨模态投影对齐的关系相通，但任务与推理机制无关，借鉴价值需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning of Transformer Encoders for Audio-Image-Symbolic Music Retrieval

**abstract/A1：** Score-based multimodal music retrieval requires representations that are both discriminative within each modality and compatible across audio recordings, sheet music images, and symbolic scores. This paper investigates the effectiveness of parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) for adapting pretrained music encoders and examines whether modality-specific improvements translate into better multimodal representations. We compare full fine-tuning, LoRA, and QLoRA across nine pretrained audio, image, and symbolic score encoders on TriScore and the Musical Theme Dataset. Encoders are first evaluated and selected through supervised unimodal classification, using frozen-encoder linear probing as a reference. We then introduce a controlled 2x2 design crossing encoder adaptation with task-specific shared projection, comparing frozen and adapted representations before and after projection. LoRA or QLoRA is selected in 14 of 18 modality-target combinations, frequently matching or outperforming full fine-tuning while optimizing substantially fewer parameters. However, these unimodal gains do not transfer uniformly to multimodal classification. Adaptation and projection are complementary for composer classification on TriScore, largely redundant for TriScore instrument and the Musical Theme Dataset instrument and ensemble tasks, and partially interfering for composer classification on the Musical Theme Dataset. The final adapted-and-projected title representation on TriScore supports piece-level retrieval, reaching average Top-5 and Top-10 accuracies of 55.32 and 64.68, respectively. Cross-modal exclusion and neighborhood analyses further reveal strong modality-specific local structure in the projected spaces. Overall, PEFT provides an effective and computationally efficient alternative to full fine-tuning, but improved unimodal discrimination alone does not guarantee better multimodal organization.

</details>

<a id="paper-183"></a>

### 183. [TACO: Trajectory-Aware Curvature Observation for Memorization Localization and Mitigation in Diffusion Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41520v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41520

**作者：** Ruxi Gu、Zilei Wang、Wei Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41520v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41520v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 TACO，在扩散模型后期去噪轨迹上聚合与提示相关的曲率响应，并结合 latent 引导的空间细化生成记忆区域图，用作定位信号与推理期干预的空间控制信号。

**相关性简评：** 纯图像生成任务，但「单步信号存在瞬时性、需沿轨迹聚合」与「用空间定位图引导干预」这两点，可能为 LVR 中逐步证据累积、选择性 LOOK 与空间证据筛选提供类比线索，需核查其聚合与重加权方式是否可迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TACO: Trajectory-Aware Curvature Observation for Memorization Localization and Mitigation in Diffusion Models

**abstract/A1：** Text-to-image diffusion models can reproduce memorized training content within localized image regions, making precise localization essential for targeted mitigation. However, curvature observations at a single denoising state can contain transient responses, while spatially uniform mitigation may disrupt prompt-relevant content. We present Trajectory-Aware Curvature Observation (TACO), a unified framework for local memorization detection and mitigation in diffusion models. Our empirical analysis reveals that memorization-related curvature becomes pronounced during late-stage denoising and persists at consistent spatial locations across neighboring states. Motivated by these observations, TACO aggregates prompt-dependent curvature responses along the late denoising trajectory and applies latent-guided spatial refinement to improve spatial coherence. The resulting memorization map serves both as a localization signal and as a spatial control signal for mitigation. Specifically, TACO uses this map to steer existing inference-time interventions toward memorization-related regions through spatial reweighting and localized guidance correction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TACO consistently outperforms competing localization methods, and improves the Pareto trade-off between memorization suppression, semantic alignment, and perceptual quality. These results establish trajectory-aware spatial analysis as an effective link between memorization localization and targeted mitigation.

**comment/C1：** 19 pages, 10 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-184"></a>

### 184. [Verify--Backtrack Loop: Structured Precondition Recovery for Long-Horizon Language Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41564v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41564

**作者：** Mingyang Liu、Qingcan Kang、Shixiong Kai、Tao Zhong、Mingxuan Yuan、Xin Li、Linqi Song

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41564v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41564v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Verify–Backtrack Loop，用有界目标栈维护任务分解，在目标激活、执行失败或状态变化时做「是否服务于总目标」与「当前是否可行」的双视野验证，并结合类型化失败归因触发 Push、Pop、retry 或回退父目标的结构化控制。

**相关性简评：** 其显式控制状态、目标贡献验证与失败回退机制，可能为 LVR 中何时继续思考、获取视觉证据或停止/回溯提供概念参照；但该工作为纯文本交互智能体、不涉及视觉或 latent 状态，能否迁移尚需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Verify--Backtrack Loop: Structured Precondition Recovery for Long-Horizon Language Agents

**abstract/A1：** Language agents increasingly tackle long-horizon tasks in interactive environments, where actions often depend on latent preconditions. The core difficulty is not trajectory length but recovery: when a prerequisite is missing or an earlier decomposition fails, trying another plausible action rarely helps. ReAct and later reflection or adaptive-planning methods support local correction, but express recovery as free-form text, with no explicit control state linking the terminal goal, the active goal, and the failure source. We introduce the Verify--Backtrack Loop (VBL), a training-free extension of ReAct that turns precondition discovery and failure diagnosis into structured planning-state updates. VBL maintains the task decomposition as a bounded goal stack and, on goal activation, execution failure, or material state change, jointly verifies two horizons---whether the active goal contributes to the terminal goal and whether it is feasible in the current state. These dual-horizon judgments, combined with typed failure attribution, trigger explicit operations---Push, Pop, retry, or BacktrackTo the parent. We evaluate VBL with Qwen3.5-9B, GPT-4o, and DeepSeek-V3.2 on AppWorld, ALFWorld, and ScienceWorld. VBL improves over ReAct and reflection-based control on every backbone--benchmark pair. On the challenging AppWorld setting, it improves task success by up to 22.07 percentage points over ReAct. Ablations show that removing either verification or diagnosis degrades performance, in several settings below ReAct, confirming that the two mechanisms are complementary. These results show that reliable long-horizon interaction benefits from revising the planning state, not merely the next action.

</details>

<a id="paper-185"></a>

### 185. [Patch-Based Deep Kernel Learning for Data Assimilation and Bias Correction with Extremely Sparse Observations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41596v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41596

**作者：** Emanuele Mele、Angelo Coluccia、Massimo Cafaro、Italo Epicoco、Paola Nassisi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41596v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41596v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CNN 与可学习核高斯过程耦合的 patch 化混合框架，在极稀疏观测下联合完成数据同化与偏差校正，以边际似然端到端训练，输出 MAP 分析及其不确定度，并将训练复杂度由 O(N³) 降到 O(N)。

**相关性简评：** 其“稀疏观测＋模型先验→带校准不确定度的后验状态估计”与 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选、信念状态维护在问题表述上可能相通，可供核查证据加权与不确定度校准思路，但领域相距较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Patch-Based Deep Kernel Learning for Data Assimilation and Bias Correction with Extremely Sparse Observations

**abstract/A1：** Data Assimilation (DA) reconstructs the state of a spatio-temporal system by combining sensor observations with the output of a numerical model. Since model states are high-dimensional while observations cover only a small fraction of the domain, DA is a challenging large-scale estimation problem, central to weather forecasting and oceanography. Classical variational methods such as 3D-Var rely on a hand-crafted background error covariance that assumes unbiased errors, and therefore cannot capture state-dependent correlations or systematic model biases. Moreover, their cost grows with the size of the domain. Deep learning (DL) methods can perform DA and bias correction (BC) non-linearly, but typically offer limited interpretability and no calibrated uncertainty estimates. We propose a hybrid framework that couples a convolutional neural network (CNN) with a learnable-kernel Gaussian Process (GP) to jointly perform DA and BC from extremely sparse observations. The model is trained end-to-end by maximizing the marginal log-likelihood, learns a data-driven covariance over the whole grid, and provides both the Maximum A Posteriori analysis and its uncertainty by design. A patch-based strategy reduces the $O(N^3)$ training cost of exact GPs over $N$ grid points to $O(N)$ for a fixed patch size. Inference remains exact and only requires solving a linear system whose $O(m^3)$ cost depends on the number of observations $m \\ll N$, not on the domain. The learned covariance can also replace the hand-crafted one in 3D-Var, improving its accuracy without changing the variational formulation. Experiments on sea surface temperature and salinity, with both synthetic and real, extremely sparse in-situ observations, show that the framework outperforms 3D-Var, a classical GP and a competing DL model, while reducing inference time by more than 98% with respect to 3D-Var.

</details>

<a id="paper-186"></a>

### 186. [Chance-Constrained Inspection Viewpoint Sequencing for On-Orbit Servicing via Safe Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41639v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41639

**作者：** Chloé Carer、Adam Abdin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、math.OC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41639v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41639v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文把在轨服务中的近距离观测视角排序建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程，用带拉格朗日松弛的 PPO 结合 PID 调节的机会约束，在覆盖、Δv 与禁飞区风险预算下联合高层视角序列与 CW 方程下的转移评估。

**相关性简评：** 其「在覆盖率、燃料与碰撞风险预算下顺序选择视角并显式权衡停止时机」的决策表述，可能为 LVR 中 LOOK 的开销调度与带预算的停止/取证策略提供概念类比，但论文本身不涉及内部推理状态或视觉表征，联系较远，仅供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>
