**title/T1：** Chance-Constrained Inspection Viewpoint Sequencing for On-Orbit Servicing via Safe Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Autonomous inspection of a target spacecraft is typically the initial step in on-orbit servicing. Planning close-proximity observation viewpoints involves a complex combinatorial problem constrained by coverage, fuel, and safety considerations. This paper models the viewpoint selection as a Constrained Markov Decision Process (CMDP) and applies Proximal Policy Optimization with Lagrangian relaxation to solve it. The system combines a high-level planner, which sequences camera viewpoints on an observation sphere around the target, with a low-level model that assesses each transfer using the Clohessy--Wiltshire (CW) equations for relative motion. The deterministic constraints on single-impulse reachability and $Δv$ cap are enforced directly on the action set. Safety margins are affected by navigation and manoeuvre errors, making the actual clearance stochastic. To address this, a chance constraint on the realised trajectory is implemented, with a Lagrangian multiplier governed by PID control to adapt the penalty during training. The approach is tested on a Galileo-class navigation satellite in medium Earth orbit, modelled as a point cloud where visibility from each viewpoint considers field-of-view and line-of-sight occlusion. Across various risk budgets, the trained policy maintains the keep-out violation rate within the specified limit, achieving coverage close to its maximum and staying within the mission $Δv$ budget. Compared to the usual practice of applying a fixed margin to the keep-out radius and planning deterministically, the chance-constrained policy attains less than half the violation rate at comparable coverage. This makes the collision risk an explicit design parameter, eliminating the need for re-tuning per case. The learned policy selects a viewpoint without the repeated Monte Carlo risk estimates required by a greedy, chance-constrained planner at every decision point.

</details>

<a id="paper-187"></a>

### 187. [A Constructive Orbit-Based Theory of Learnability](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41641v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41641

**作者：** Yunied Puig

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41641v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41641v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出有限维「orbit」框架：用 wrapped weighted shift 构造数据自适应的字典与块插值映射，把学习分解为支架发现、稀疏实现与基于稀疏坐标的预测三步，并证明 orbit 独立性、插值范数控制、稀疏近似偏差界与扰动稳定性，配以合成与分类实验。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理无直接关系，但其「稀疏实现偏差界」与块插值扰动下的路径稳定性，可能为刻画 latent 状态压缩中的信息损失与推理轨迹稳定性提供可核查的分析视角；是否可迁移仍待判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Constructive Orbit-Based Theory of Learnability

**abstract/A1：** We develop a finite-dimensional orbit framework for constructing data-adapted dictionaries, sparse realizations, and predictive extensions. The method uses wrapped weighted shifts to generate orbit dictionaries and local interpolation maps from independent orbit representatives, turning the learning problem into three explicit tasks: scaffold discovery, sparse realization on the discovered dictionary, and prediction through learned sparse coordinates. We introduce CCDS as the main constructive ingredient: a deviation-controlled sparse realization procedure that recovers MP/OMP-type behavior at the minimal-deviation boundary while allowing feasible interior paths with prescribed residual geometry that is inaccessible to MP/OMP. We prove generic orbit independence, exact block interpolation with norm control, observable bounds on sparse approximation distortion, sparse realization guarantees on selected supports, and fixed-path stability under admissible perturbations of the block interpolants. These results connect sparse approximation, interpolation, and finite-dimensional operator dynamics through the construction and analysis of data-induced orbit dictionaries. Experiments on synthetic geometry, controlled realization tests, and standard classification benchmarks show that selected orbit scaffolds have substantially better matched-rank projection behavior than random orbit or unstructured alternatives, that CCDS attains near-zero realization error at the minimal-deviation boundary, and that predictors trained from the sparse CCDS certificates give competitive performance.

</details>

<a id="paper-188"></a>

### 188. [TADP: Dual-Branch Temporally Adaptive Differential Privacy for Expression-Aware Video Anonymization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41642v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41642

**作者：** Feng Xu、Xun Li、Lars Petersson、Yulei Sui、David Ahmedt-Aristizabal、Dadong Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41642v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41642v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 TADP，用双分支分别提取身份与表情表示，在推理期对两分支施加时间自适应高斯噪声并做 RDP 组合，实现视频人脸匿名化，同时保留可用的表情识别效用与时间稳定性。

**相关性简评：** 其「显式分离任务必要的表情信息与应抑制的身份信息，对 latent 表示加噪并以下游识别效用衡量信息保留」的思路，可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与信息坍缩的度量方式，但该文不涉及推理状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TADP: Dual-Branch Temporally Adaptive Differential Privacy for Expression-Aware Video Anonymization

**abstract/A1：** We present TADP, a temporally adaptive differential privacy (DP) framework for inference-time privacy-preserving video generation under fixed-length clip-replacement adjacency. TADP uses a dual-branch design to extract identity and expression representations, clips and privatizes both branches with temporally adaptive Gaussian mechanisms whose noise scales depend only on preceding privatized releases within the same clip and are bounded below by history-uniform calibrated minima, and accounts for their joint release via adaptive Rényi Differential Privacy (RDP) composition. The clip-level RDP guarantee is converted to $(\\varepsilon,δ)$-differential privacy, while soft quantization and subsequent temporal processing are applied only after privatization as DP post-processing. Experiments on facial expression datasets show reduced identity leakage under evaluated attacks, usable perceptual quality and temporal stability, and high facial expression recognition utility relative to the evaluated privacy-preserving baselines. TADP formally satisfies clip-level DP and empirically provides a favorable privacy--utility trade-off under the evaluated threat models and datasets.

**comment/C1：** WACV2027 accepted

</details>

<a id="paper-189"></a>

### 189. [Risk-Sensitive Mission Planning for Multi-Target On-Orbit Inspection via Distributional Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41647v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41647

**作者：** Adam Abdin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、math.OC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41647v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41647v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究低轨多目标在轨检查任务的规划问题，用 QR-DQN 学习任务回报的分布，并以均值-方差准则调节风险偏好来决定下一个要检查的目标，再用枚举得到的有效前沿检验所学策略。

**相关性简评：** 「任务时间预算有限时按风险偏好选择目标」的分布式回报表述，可能为 LVR 中 LOOK/STOP 的动态决策与风险敏感停止提供概念借鉴，但该工作不含视觉或 latent 推理，迁移性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Risk-Sensitive Mission Planning for Multi-Target On-Orbit Inspection via Distributional Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Multi-target on-orbit servicing missions require planning tools that can adapt to evolving operational conditions while accounting for the outcome variability inherent to safety-critical spaceflight. This paper presents an adaptive planning approach for multi-target servicing campaigns in low Earth orbit and applies it to inspection missions flown by a low-thrust servicer. The approach is based on Distributional Reinforcement Learning, which learns the full distribution of mission returns and thereby supports explicit risk-sensitive decision-making at each planning step. Specifically, a Quantile-Regression Deep Q-Network (QR-DQN) agent selects which target to inspect next by jointly considering the expectation and the variance of the predicted return through a mean-variance criterion. The mission environment models circular orbits with a spread in inclination and uses Edelbaum's combined-manoeuvre low-thrust transfer estimates. The observation comprises the orbital elements, the visit history, the observed risk state of each target and the remaining mission-time budget. The risk state of each target changes stochastically during the campaign, making the outcome of an inspection uncertain and creating operationally meaningful distinctions between risk-neutral and risk-averse planning strategies. Because the mission-time budget generally admits only a subset of the candidate targets, the planner must also select which targets to inspect. Numerical experiments on scenarios with five target satellites show that one risk-aversion weight moves the policy across a range of mean return and return variance, over an unchanged environment and reward. Risk-averse agents reduce the variance of the episodic return by approximately one half relative to risk-neutral agents and lower the probability of a mission loss, at a cost in mean return. To validate the learned policies, the efficient frontier, which gives the highest mean return attainable at each level of return variance, is computed exactly by enumerating the admissible visit orders. At equal variance, the learned policies lie close to this frontier in mean return, and the risk-averse policies have a lower probability of a mission loss than the frontier policies.

</details>

<a id="paper-190"></a>

### 190. [Does Reasoning Make Language Models Agree?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41691v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41691

**作者：** Mila Gorecki、Moritz Hardt

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL、cs.CY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41691v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41691v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把「推理作为干预」与「推理作为观察行为」区分开，在 9 个模型、3 个分类任务上，以准确率校准后的期望一致性为基准，比较受控提高推理努力与观察到的推理长度分别对应的模型间一致性。

**相关性简评：** 其「干预 vs 观测长度」的混淆及「更长推理反而预示不一致」的发现，可能为 LVR 中把生成长度当作收益代理、以及 STOP 决策与计算量归因的评估设计提供核查线索；但工作为纯文本、无视觉或 latent 状态，仅属概念启发。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Does Reasoning Make Language Models Agree?

**abstract/A1：** Language models face two opposing concerns: monoculture, in which models err in correlated ways, and multiplicity, in which equally accurate models make conflicting decisions. The rise of reasoning models has complicated this debate: On the one hand, reasoning may increase model agreement if models anchor on the same salient features or follow similar reasoning paths. On the other hand, stochastic reasoning traces may introduce additional branch points leading to more diverse predictions. We resolve this tension by distinguishing reasoning as an intervention from reasoning as an observed behavior. We compare agreement under controlled changes in reasoning effort with agreement across observed reasoning lengths, measuring both against the agreement expected from model accuracy. Across nine models from two developers and three classification tasks, enabling reasoning increases agreement between models evaluated on the same individuals. Yet the individuals who elicit longer reasoning traces receive less consistent predictions across models. Both patterns persist after accounting for differences in accuracy. Asking models to reason more thus promotes agreement, while observing them reason longer signals disagreement.

</details>

<a id="paper-191"></a>

### 191. [Retrieval-Augmented Initialization with Dual-Guided Inversion for Flow-Based Inverse Problem Solvers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41695v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41695

**作者：** Hansam Cho、Seoung Bum Kim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41695v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41695v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文固定下游 flow 逆问题求解器，系统比较不同噪声初始化策略，并提出 RA-DGI：借助检索到的代理干净图像与双引导反演构造更优的初始噪声，在多个逆问题上优于随机初始化与部分推理时扩展方法，且计算开销更低。

**相关性简评：** 其「初始 latent 状态显著影响后续生成/重建质量」的结论，可能为 LVR 中推理工作区如何初始化、以及用检索到的视觉证据播种初始状态提供类比线索，可供核查；但任务属于图像逆问题重建，与多模态推理距离较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Retrieval-Augmented Initialization with Dual-Guided Inversion for Flow-Based Inverse Problem Solvers

**abstract/A1：** Flow models have recently emerged as powerful generative priors for solving inverse problems. Despite their success, most existing flow-based inverse problem solvers initialize the reconstruction process with random Gaussian noise. However, recent studies in image generation have shown that the choice of initial noise can significantly influence generation outcomes. The role of initialization as a distinct design choice in flow-based inverse problem solving remains comparatively underexplored. In this work, we systematically evaluate noise initialization strategies while holding the downstream flow-based inverse solver fixed. We propose Retrieval-Augmented Initialization with Dual-Guided Inversion (RA-DGI), a method that constructs improved initial noise by leveraging retrieved proxy clean images and dual-guided inversion. Extensive experiments across multiple inverse problems demonstrate that the quality of the initial noise substantially affects reconstruction fidelity and perceptual performance. RA-DGI outperforms standard random initialization and achieves superior results compared to inference-time scaling strategies, while requiring less computational overhead. These findings highlight initialization as a critical yet previously overlooked factor in flow-based inverse problem solvers.

**comment/C1：** WACV 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-192"></a>

### 192. [From fleet trajectories to single-cell promises: hierarchically weighted conformal prediction for battery SOH monitoring](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41696v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41696

**作者：** Xiangyu Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.AP

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41696v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41696v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在124个LFP电池上按「车队—批次—单体」三层报告协议测量保形预测区间覆盖率，发现汇总覆盖健康而最差单体几乎不被覆盖，并用仅依赖预测时冻结的侧信息（签名特征、批次标签、循环序号）的分层加权保形方法修复部分差距，同时报告协议漂移下的失效与修复容量受标定池多样性限制。

**相关性简评：** 电池SOH与视觉推理无关，但其「汇总覆盖≠单体覆盖」的诊断、侧信息加权而不触碰残差的做法以及零延迟率（deferral share）报告，可能可供核查 LVR 中按样本/情景评估 latent 状态可靠性与停止或延迟决策时的分层校准问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From fleet trajectories to single-cell promises: hierarchically weighted conformal prediction for battery SOH monitoring

**abstract/A1：** Interval predictions from conformal prediction are usually read as "90% coverage", but on nested battery data that number promises the fleet average, not the individual cell. We measure the gap on 124 public LFP cells under a preregistered three-tier reporting protocol (fleet, batch, cell): pooled coverage looks healthy (0.875-0.906 across splits) while the worst cell is covered 0.1-2% of the time, with exact binomial tests against the nominal level. We then repair part of the gap with a hierarchically weighted conformal engine whose weights consume only side information frozen at prediction time - signature features, batch labels, cycle indices - and never touch residuals. The preregistered repair criterion passes: 10th-percentile cell coverage moves 0.750 to 0.830 (53% of the gap), the worst cell 0.642 to 0.694, with zero deferrals and a flat sensitivity spectrum. What the engine does not repair is localized rather than left open: under protocol drift the frozen weights harm monotonically on the SOH scale (the absolute scale is reported in full), a structural dead end we report instead of tuning away; and repair capacity is bounded by calibration-pool battery diversity, measured on both datasets. On a cross-chemistry external set the calibrated intervals collapse (0.286), while the within-domain skeleton replicates (pooled 0.933, worst cell 0.686). The deliverable is a reporting protocol - three tiers of promise anchored to measured regimes, worst-cell statistics, deferral share - that makes per-cell interval claims auditable before deployment.

**comment/C1：** Under review at Applied Energy

</details>

<a id="paper-193"></a>

### 193. [Leakage-Controlled Structure-Preserving Augmentation and Template-Aware Evaluation for Bangla Symptom-Based Disease Prediction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41733v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41733

**作者：** Sabah Nushra、Shovan Samanta Turzo、Moinul Hossain、Md Kishor Morol

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41733v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41733v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在 725 个孟加拉语症状档案（85 类疾病、172 个二值症状）上检验仅基于训练集的不对称 Bernoulli 扰动增强，采用重复真实档案留出与父组内层交叉验证，并辅以 Hamming 距离泄漏审计、1-bit 剔除挑战集及缺失/虚假症状鲁棒性和解释忠实度评估。

**相关性简评：** 其「模板邻近」泄漏审计与删除/添加症状的扰动评测，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选、捷径泄漏及证据缺失下的评价设计提供核查线索，但领域与方法均相距较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Leakage-Controlled Structure-Preserving Augmentation and Template-Aware Evaluation for Bangla Symptom-Based Disease Prediction

**abstract/A1：** Bangla symptom-based disease prediction is commonly evaluated on a small, imbalanced structured benchmark containing many profiles that differ by only one or two symptoms. We test whether train-only asymmetric Bernoulli perturbation improves classification using 725 unique profiles spanning 85 diseases and 172 binary symptoms. Experiments use ten repeated real-profile holdouts and parent-grouped inner cross-validation. The 2 x 2 design separates augmentation from hyperparameter tuning, with exact duplication, random oversampling, class weighting, and SMOTEN as balancing controls. For Bernoulli Naive Bayes (BNB), the gain over tuned raw training does not withstand Holm correction, although BNB attains the highest mean accuracy (97.50%). The boosted-tree models benefit substantially: XGBoost, LightGBM, and CatBoost improve by 18.10, 28.45, and 10.09 percentage points, respectively. A Hamming-distance audit places 91.0% of standard held-out profiles within one symptom bit of the training data. Accuracy decreases on a same-disease 1-bit-purged challenge; nevertheless, XGBoost and LightGBM retain large augmentation gains. We also evaluate robustness to missing and spurious symptoms and the faithfulness of BNB explanations. Overall, Bernoulli perturbation is useful for some model families, but benchmark accuracy should be interpreted together with template proximity.

**comment/C1：** 6 pages, 5 figures, 5 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-194"></a>

### 194. [Locality-Controlled Out-of-Distribution Guidance for Robust Regulatory DNA Sequence Design](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41736v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41736

**作者：** Dabin Jeong、Marwane Bourdim、Mo Lotfollahi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、q-bio.GN

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41736v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41736v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究调控 DNA 序列的性质条件生成，指出单奖励评估会掩盖 reward hacking，将评估拆分为性质、新颖性与合理性三维，并提出 LOCOGEN：用序列级不变性学习得到 motif mask 保护敏感位点，仅在采样时对背景位置施加 OOD 抑制，且不重训扩散骨干。

**相关性简评：** 其「空间非均匀」的引导视角——先由不变性学习定位敏感位置，再只对可探索区域施加扰动——可能为 LVR 中按位置分配 latent 状态更新、保留关键视觉证据提供概念线索；多目标分解诊断也可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Locality-Controlled Out-of-Distribution Guidance for Robust Regulatory DNA Sequence Design

**abstract/A1：** Designing regulatory DNA sequences for targeted gene-expression control is an important challenge. Recent approaches frame this task as property-conditioned generation, where diffusion models trained on natural sequences are guided toward high predicted activity through reward-based fine-tuning or sampling-time guidance. We show that single-reward evaluation can obscure reward-hacking behavior. By decomposing evaluation into property, novelty, and plausibility, we identify an empirically underexplored region of the evaluation space in which all three objectives are satisfied, but no existing baseline succeeds. We postulate that this gap arises because prior methods treat guidance primarily as a matter of magnitude. In regulatory DNA, however, sequence grammar is spatially nonuniform: motif positions are sensitive to perturbation, while background regions allow more exploration. We therefore propose LOCOGEN by reformulating guidance as a problem of locality. LOCOGEN learns an invariant motif mask through sequence-level invariance learning to protect motif positions, while applying OOD damping only to background positions. It operates at sampling time on a frozen diffusion backbone, without retraining the backbone. Across HepG2, K562, and SK-N-SH, LOCOGEN reaches this previously underexplored region of the property-novelty-plausibility space. Code is available at https://github.com/Lotfollahi-lab/LocoGen.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-195"></a>

### 195. [Do LLMs Know When They Are Wrong About Molecules? In-Context Property Prediction with Self-Reported Uncertainty](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41745v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41745

**作者：** Matthias Busch、Marius Tacke、Tamadur Albaraghtheh、Sviatlana V. Lamaka、Mikhail L. Zheludkevich、Roland C. Aydin、Christian Feiler、Christian J. Cyron

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41745v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41745v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 在 in-context 示例下评测 7 个前沿 LLM 的分子性质预测与自报不确定性，与同分子训练的经典基线对比，并筛查训练数据污染（ESOL 被记忆作为对照）。

**相关性简评：** 其自报不确定性对错误排序较弱但可与基线不确定性相当的发现，以及污染筛查设计，可能为 LVR 中基于置信度的验证、STOP 决策与评测泄漏控制提供参照。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Do LLMs Know When They Are Wrong About Molecules? In-Context Property Prediction with Self-Reported Uncertainty

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) are used in many domains, but molecular property prediction with them is still little explored. In this work we evaluate the capability of 7 frontier LLMs to predict molecular properties together with a self-reported uncertainty from in-context examples, and compare them with classical baselines trained on the same molecules. We use 4 datasets and screen each for contamination of the training data of the LLMs: three are clean, and the fourth, ESOL, is memorised by most of the models and serves as a comparison. On the clean datasets every LLM improves with the context, at 30 examples the most accurate one beats every baseline. The requested reasoning effort changes accuracy little. The self-reported uncertainty ranks the prediction errors weakly, with Spearman rank correlations of up to 0.46, about the level reached by the uncertainties of the baselines. Combining uncertainty and prediction, on lipophilicity the most certain half of the best LLM is the most accurate at small context sizes; on BACE and antiviral, and on lipophilicity at large context sizes, the best LLMs are on par with the best baseline. On the contaminated ESOL dataset the LLMs show no improvement with the context size, as expected for a memorised dataset, and their stated uncertainty is smaller on the molecules whose value they recall.

</details>

<a id="paper-196"></a>

### 196. [Implicit Assumptions About Tasks in Continual Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41792v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41792

**作者：** Kathrin Korte、Christian Metzner、Akshay Bedhotiya、Viet Anh Khoa Tran、Eleni Nisioti、Sebastian Risi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41792v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41792v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文在持续学习中区分「底层数据」「数据划分」「学习器」三个层级，指出任务相似度、变化速率、呈现顺序等指标常被跨层级混用，并用 teacher–student 实验说明切分粒度、顺序与相似结构会改变测量结果。

**相关性简评：** 其「报告量属于哪个层级」的区分可能启发 LVR 中对 latent 相似度与轨迹贡献测量的核查：token/片段/层深度/缓存位置等不同层级上的量是否被混为一谈，值得对照原文确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Implicit Assumptions About Tasks in Continual Learning

**abstract/A1：** Continual learning is the study of how a model accumulates knowledge from data received over time, rather than all at once. In practice, this is typically achieved by dividing a dataset into tasks and presenting them sequentially. Explanations of the resulting measurements take into account factors such as the similarity between segments, the rate at which the data changes and the order of presentation. Each of these factors can be defined in terms of the underlying data, the partition imposed on it, or the learner that encounters it. These levels do not necessarily coincide, and it is usually left implicit which level a reported quantity refers to. We separate these and identify which properties acquire a value at each level. In controlled teacher--student experiments, we demonstrate that partitioning the same underlying trajectory at different levels of granularity, changing the order of presentation, and varying the similarity structure of the underlying data all result in different measured outcomes. We review representative benchmarks to determine which of these properties are stated. Where the underlying data cannot be inspected, as in foundation models built on undisclosed pre-training corpora, its properties are inherited by the model's initial state, and the training applied on top of it can be described.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to the Workshop on Continual Learning in the Era of Foundation Models and Embodied Agents at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-197"></a>

### 197. [Whose Values, Which Contexts, and What Signals? Grounding Cultural Value Alignment with Re3Culture](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41793v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41793

**作者：** Jing Yao、Hyunjin Hwang、Weihua Zheng、Bryan Chen Zhengyu Tan、Jia Wang Peh、Dylan Raharja、JinYeong Bak、Roy Ka-Wei Lee、Rui Cao、Yuki Arase、Jacki O'Neill、Zhicheng Dou、Xing Xie、Xiaoyuan Yi、Furu Wei

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41793v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41793v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文构建 Re3Culture 数据集，以 700 余名跨四种文化的参与者、社区选定的情境场景和显式价值优先序问卷，讨论文化价值对齐的目标应如何在真实人类数据上被界定，并分析"代表谁、在哪观察、用何种信号"三类选择对估计结果的影响。

**相关性简评：** 其核心观察——仅由回答偏好等行为信号难以反推内在价值优先序，相似输出可能源于不同内部状态——与 LVR 中仅用答案监督难以约束 latent 推理状态、需显式获取内部信号的困境结构相似，可作为"显式状态监督 vs 行为监督"的问题表述线索供核查，但领域相距较远，与视觉或多模态推理无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Whose Values, Which Contexts, and What Signals? Grounding Cultural Value Alignment with Re3Culture

**abstract/A1：** Cultural value alignment requires not only model optimization but also a well-specified target grounded in real-human evidence. Yet how such targets should be grounded in human data remains largely unexplored, raising three fundamental questions: (1) whose values should be represented? (2) where should cultural values be observed? and (3) what signals reveal underlying values? We investigate these questions through Re3Culture, a large-scale dataset linking (i) Representative communities: 700+ human participants across four cultures recruited through cultural-affiliation screening and demographic-aware sampling; (ii) Realistic scenario: culturally sensitive scenarios selected by community members, e.g., family and kinship; and (iii) Revealed value signals: 1,400+ value surveys, 20,000+ topic-grounded behavioral principles and explicit value priorities with responses for 6,000+ situated questions. Our analysis shows why these choices matter for defining the target, with three implications. First, estimating cultural values requires both community affiliation and broad internal coverage. Substituting other cultures or restricting subgroup coverage shifts value estimates. LLM simulations also disproportionately reflect particular subgroups. Second, values must be observed in concrete, community-guided situations. Abstract questionnaire profiles weakly recover situated priorities, and communities differ in which scenarios they identify as culturally value-revealing. Third, value priorities should be elicited explicitly, not learned from response preferences or bottom-line answers alone. Preferences conflate value agreement with other response-level qualities, and similar answers can be derived from different value priorities. By addressing these requirements, Re3Culture provides the first human-grounded resource that explicitly informs target definition for cultural value alignment.

</details>

<a id="paper-198"></a>

### 198. [Towards Out-of-Distribution Uncertainty Quantification for Machine-Learned Interatomic Potentials via Pre-Training](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41801v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41801

**作者：** Stefan P. Schmid、Tobias Setzer、Maximilian Scheurer

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、physics.chem-ph

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41801v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41801v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在大型材料科学数据集上预训练多个不确定性估计器，用于量化不同 machine-learned interatomic potentials 的预测不确定性，并在分布内与 OOD 吸附能任务上评估其零样本与微调表现。

**相关性简评：** 与研究主线距离较远，但其「预训练不确定性估计器 + 检验不确定度与误差相关性」的做法，或可启发 LVR 中 STOP 决策与证据充分性/可靠性判定的训练与评价方式，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Towards Out-of-Distribution Uncertainty Quantification for Machine-Learned Interatomic Potentials via Pre-Training

**abstract/A1：** Informative uncertainty quantification (UQ) is essential for the practical deployment of machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs), as it provides insight into their reliability across chemical space. Although numerous post-hoc uncertainty estimation methods for MLIPs have recently been proposed, most have been trained in low-data regimes, restricting their applicability to narrow domains of interest. Inspired by the emergence of foundational MLIPs, we propose pre-training uncertainty estimators on large, diverse datasets to enable broader applicability. We pre-train several UQ models on large materials science datasets to quantify the uncertainty of different MLIPs, and evaluate their performance on both in-distribution and out-of-domain (OOD) adsorption-energy predictions. We find that pre-trained uncertainty estimators provide viable zero-shot uncertainty estimates across multiple datasets, while fine-tuning consistently yields positive correlations between predicted uncertainty and prediction error, even on the most challenging datasets. These results lay the groundwork for foundational UQ models for MLIPs, ultimately facilitating their deployment in practical settings by providing users with a priori information about model reliability and applicability.

</details>

<a id="paper-199"></a>

### 199. [Continual Reinforcement Learning Is an Integration Problem](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41809v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41809

**作者：** Nis Meinert、Jakob Gawlikowski、Edward Grant、Max Cyprys、Nathan Schurr、Neil Boston、Alfredo Ibias、Milind Tambe、Janusz Marecki

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41809v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41809v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文以致述方式提出持续强化学习应满足的十项设计需求（desiderata），用其审视若干代表性方法，并指出尚无已发表评估证明这些性质能在单一智能体的连续生命周期内同时成立；为此另提供一个可交互的持久世界场景与相应基准设想。

**相关性简评：** 其核心主张——多机制需在同一智能体内整合、并由同一生命周期内的统一评估检验——可能启发 LVR 中 THINK/LOOK/STOP 的收益成本与支持组合、以及长度泛化的联合评测设计；但该文不涉及视觉状态表示，具体可迁移的评估程序仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Continual Reinforcement Learning Is an Integration Problem

**abstract/A1：** This paper presents an opinionated set of ten desiderata intended to inform the design and evaluation of reinforcement-learning agents that learn continually throughout a continuing lifetime. We use these desiderata to examine a representative selection of existing approaches. Progress on individual desiderata is valuable, but the longer-term aim should be to integrate all ten within a single agent. To our knowledge, no published evaluation has yet provided evidence for such an integration. Likewise, the representative benchmarks we review probe different parts of this target, but separate evaluations do not establish that the corresponding properties coexist within one agent throughout a continuing lifetime. As a secondary contribution, we therefore present a compact persistent-world scenario as a playable companion to the desiderata and outline a proposed benchmark that examines a broad range of associated behavioral capabilities together within one such lifetime.

**comment/C1：** 39 pages, 2 figures, 5 tables. Playable companion and source code: https://github.com/fractalbrain-ai/10d-game

</details>

<a id="paper-200"></a>

### 200. [DIFFNAS: Predictor-Free Neural Architecture Search via Diffusion Model and Zero-Cost Evaluation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41818v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41818

**作者：** Yize Sun、Hanzhang Ma、Zixin Wu、Xuechun Li、Volker Tresp、Yunpu Ma

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41818v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41818v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 DIFFNAS，用离散图扩散模型生成网络架构，并以 MeCo、SWAP 等零成本代理指标代替学习到的性能预测器来引导生成过程，在 NAS-Bench-201 与 MobileNetV3 空间上验证搜索效率与精度。

**相关性简评：** 「免预测器、以廉价训练无关指标条件化生成过程」的搜索范式，或可启发性地类比到 LVR 中免学习验证器的 latent 轨迹搜索与候选筛选，但领域相距较远，仅可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DIFFNAS: Predictor-Free Neural Architecture Search via Diffusion Model and Zero-Cost Evaluation

**abstract/A1：** Neural Architecture Search (NAS) has advanced the automated design of deep networks, but remains computationally expensive due to reliance on model training or performance predictors. We introduce DIFFNAS, a predictor-free NAS framework that leverages a discrete graph diffusion model guided by zero-cost performance proxies. Unlike prior diffusion-based NAS methods that require learned performance predictors, DIFFNAS conditions the generative process on training-free metrics such as MeCo and SWAP, enabling efficient architecture search with minimal computational overhead. Experiments on NAS-Bench-201 show that DIFFNAS finds the optimal architectures for CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 (94.37% and 73.51%) without a pretrained predictor, while reducing search cost by up to 17.5x compared to DiffusionNAG. On the MobileNetV3 search space, its accuracy is lower than that of DiffusionNAG. These results highlight the promise of diffusion models combined with zero-cost proxies for scalable and effective architecture search.

**comment/C1：** 13 pages, 1 figure, 6 tables. Yize Sun and Hanzhang Ma contributed equally

</details>

<a id="paper-201"></a>

### 201. [PGGT-Loc: Primitive-Grounded Geometry Transformer for Feed-Forward Camera Localization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41833v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41833

**作者：** Hanqiao Ye、Yuzhou Liu、Mengqi Rong、Jian Liu、Yangdong Liu、Shuhan Shen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41833v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41833v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PGGT，用单张查询图像与平面地图基元直接回归 6-DoF 相机位姿，避免依赖复杂多阶段地图，在密集表面地图与极简 layout 模型上取得领先定位性能。

**相关性简评：** 其「视觉观测对齐到紧凑几何基元」的思路，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据与结构化任务要素如何绑定进内部表征提供参照；但该文属前馈位姿回归、无 latent 推理过程，内部表征是否编码对应关系仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PGGT-Loc: Primitive-Grounded Geometry Transformer for Feed-Forward Camera Localization

**abstract/A1：** Camera localization methods are mostly multi-stage pipelines relying on dedicated and complex maps that are costly to construct, store, and maintain over time. Alternatively, well-structured maps that organize scenes with compact yet expressive primitives are often readily available, providing rich structural and semantic cues that humans can interpret. However, their potential for direct camera localization remains largely unexplored due to the significant modality gap between map primitives and visual observations. This motivates a new concept for localization, which estimates camera poses by grounding query observations to map primitives. In this paper, we instantiate the concept in the context of planar primitives with PGGT, the Primitive-Grounded Geometry Transformer network. Given a single query image and a set of planar map primitives, the network is trained to predict multiple localization targets for feed-forward 6-DoF camera localization that generalizes to unseen environments. With this straightforward and scalable architecture, our method pushes the state-of-the-art localization performance on both dense surface maps and minimal layout models. The code and models are available at https://github.com/3dv-casia/PGGT-Loc

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026. 24 pages, 14 figures, including appendices. Code and models: https://github.com/3dv-casia/PGGT-Loc

</details>

<a id="paper-202"></a>

### 202. [ObjectGuidedMap: An Object-Aware Model for Robust Bird's Eye View Map Prediction in High-Occlusion Scenarios](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41834v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41834

**作者：** Christian Bohn、Harsh Yadav、Klaus Friedrichs、Hasan Tercan、Tobias Meisen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41834v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41834v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 ObjectGuidedMap，在 Mask2Map 基础上增加 3D 目标检测分支，并在 BEV 特征层与实例 query 层用 deformable attention 和 cross-attention 做跨任务融合，使 BEV 地图预测能借助目标上下文抵抗交通参与者遮挡。

**相关性简评：** 其「观测被遮挡时用实例级 query 与特征层融合补偿视觉证据」的设计，可能为 LVR 中保留任务必要视觉细节、筛选与补全视觉证据提供可核查的结构线索，但属于感知任务而非推理，联系较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ObjectGuidedMap: An Object-Aware Model for Robust Bird's Eye View Map Prediction in High-Occlusion Scenarios

**abstract/A1：** Accurate BEV map prediction is a critical component of autonomous driving systems, enabling downstream planning and control. Recent approaches such as Mask2Map achieve strong performance by predicting vectorized, instance-level map elements directly from multi-view images. However, existing BEV map prediction models operate largely independently of object-level scene understanding, despite the fact that traffic participants frequently occlude or implicitly encode information about road geometry. In this work, we propose a multitask architecture that jointly predicts BEV maps and 3D objects, explicitly enabling information exchange between the two tasks. Building upon Mask2Map, we introduce an object detection branch and incorporate cross-task feature fusion at both the BEV feature level and the instance-query level via deformable attention and cross-attention mechanisms. This design allows map instances to leverage object context while remaining robust to occlusions caused by traffic participants. Experimental results on the nuScenes and Argoverse 2 datasets demonstrate that our approach consistently improves BEV map prediction accuracy, with particularly significant gains of 21% mAP in scenes containing heavy object occlusion.

</details>

<a id="paper-203"></a>

### 203. [Binary Sparse Codes: Where are we on Energy Efficient Representations?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41933v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41933

**作者：** Jose Miguel Ramos、Luis SaCouto、Andreas Wichert

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41933v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41933v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文梳理产生二值稀疏编码的三类范式（高效编码理论、反向传播训练、局部 Hebbian 生物可行学习），提出以「相似性保持」定量刻画信息保持的度量，并在匹配条件下比较四种代表性方法，揭示稀疏性与信息保持之间的权衡。

**相关性简评：** 其相似性保持度量可能可供核查，用于量化 LVR 内部状态是否保留输入的关系几何，以及稀疏压缩与视觉细节信息坍缩之间的取舍；但论文本身不涉及视觉或多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Binary Sparse Codes: Where are we on Energy Efficient Representations?

**abstract/A1：** Biological neural systems achieve impressive computational capabilities under tight metabolic constraints, relying on sparse cortical representations that minimise total neural activity. This combination of efficiency and representational power has motivated growing interest in sparse coding as a computational strategy for artificial systems. Artificial models designed to produce binary sparse codes span three broad paradigms, theoretical methods grounded in efficient coding, deep learning methods trained via backpropagation, and biologically plausible methods that learn through local Hebbian rules, each yielding codes whose quality depends not only on achieving low activation density but also on preserving the informational structure of the input. The latter property, however, lacks an agreed-upon quantitative definition, leaving the field without a principled basis for comparing methods. We propose a set of metrics that formalise information preservation as similarity preservation, measuring how faithfully a binary sparse code reproduces the relational geometry of the input space, and apply this framework to compare four representative methods under matched conditions. The results reveal a consistent trade-off in which models that achieve high information preservation do so at the cost of sparsity, while models approaching the uniformity of an optimal random sparse code tend to sacrifice it. Competitive Sparse Codes, a biologically plausible approach, is the only architecture to occupy a region of near-maximal sparsity while retaining meaningful information preservation. These findings frame the simultaneous satisfaction of both factors as an open challenge for the field and provide a reusable evaluation framework for measuring progress toward it.

**comment/C1：** 38 pages, 8 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-204"></a>

### 204. [Observable-Specific Validity Tests for Scientific AI: Controlled Approximations and Intervention Response](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41939v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41939

**作者：** Xiaoshn Nee、Haobo Zhong、Xiaomin Ni

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.DL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41939v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41939v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文为科学 AI 提出「面向特定可观测量的有效性记录」，把近似或归约与参考模型、误差度量、容差、参数域及干预变换绑定，并用奇异摄动振子、Hubbard 二聚体、重复神经拟合与因果模型案例分别检验能量与响应是否被独立验证。

**相关性简评：** 其「不只复现数值、还要复现干预响应」的判定框架与配对变换检验，可能为核查 VLM 内部 latent 是否真正承载任务必需信息、答案是否依赖 latent 内容而非仅拟合输出提供评价思路，但需较多迁移核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Observable-Specific Validity Tests for Scientific AI: Controlled Approximations and Intervention Response

**abstract/A1：** An approximation can reproduce an energy while missing its response, or satisfy an error tolerance without being certified by a given bound. We formulate observable-specific validity records for scientific artificial intelligence (AI) that link a reduction to its reference model, error measure, tolerance, parameter domain, and transformations or interventions. Explicit displacement and velocity certificates for a singularly perturbed oscillator recover distinct convergence orders and provide sufficient validity guarantees. Exact Hubbard dimer relations resolve the different coupling domains accepted by energy and response criteria. Across 16 repeated neural fits, imposing dimensional homogeneity reduces energy scale transfer error from 52.3% to 0.0768% and raises the median fraction jointly satisfying gap and response tolerances from zero to 100%. Paired transformations verify the protected scaling relation, while a coupling crossover identifies the additional information needed for response transfer. A causal model recovers a positive intervention response beneath a strong negative observational association. For two mechanisms observed with Gaussian noise whose responses first differ at order $x^k$, we derive an exact fixed control query requirement scaling as $x^{-2k}$ and verify its full success curve numerically. Together with a 256-task evaluation interface, these results provide independently checkable tests of controlled approximation, physical transfer, and mechanism discrimination in scientific AI.

</details>

<a id="paper-205"></a>

### 205. [Robust Learning of Multi-Index Models under Logconcave Distributions via Weak Partial Learners](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41961v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41961

**作者：** Ilias Diakonikolas、Daniel M. Kane、Mingchen Ma

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41961v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41961v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在 logconcave 分布下给出学习 Boolean 概念类（含多指标模型）的算法框架：用「标签可部分未定义」的弱部分分类器做 boosting，配合递归矩分区与低维子空间矩差异检测，得到更快的鲁棒学习器。

**相关性简评：** 部分标签弱学习器与矩差异检测可能为 LVR 中稀疏/部分监督的轨迹训练、以及判断内部状态相对参考分布是否偏移提供概念线索，但与视觉推理状态生成无直接联系，迁移性待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Robust Learning of Multi-Index Models under Logconcave Distributions via Weak Partial Learners

**abstract/A1：** We develop a new algorithmic framework for learning Boolean concept classes under logconcave distributions. Our framework applies to broad families of multi-index models and yields significantly faster robust learners than were previously known in these settings. It extends to partial classification and testable learning. Consequently, we obtain improved learners for several fundamental concept classes, including functions of halfspaces and multiclass linear classifiers, under logconcave marginals. Our starting point is a new boosting framework based on weak partial learners. Classical boosting approaches require often-intractable distribution-free weak learners or yield weak approximation guarantees. We show instead that weak learners for partial classification, where labels may be undefined on part of the domain, suffice for strong agnostic learning. The key challenge is that boosting requires a weak learner that succeeds under conditioned distributions that are not guaranteed to be logconcave. To address this, we develop a recursive moment-based partitioning procedure: given a logconcave distribution $X$ and a conditional version $X'$, we partition the space into polynomially many convex regions so that, on most regions, the low-degree moments of $X$ and $X'$ approximately agree. On each good region, a well-behaved multi-index model correlates with a low-degree polynomial, giving a weak hypothesis. Combining these hypotheses yields the required weak learner. A key ingredient of our approach is an efficient method for detecting moment discrepancy. Specifically, we show how to find a low-dimensional subspace that captures any non-trivial discrepancy between $X$ and $X'$, if one exists. Our approach combines spectral analysis of moment tensors with recent progress on isoperimetry for logconcave measures. This discrepancy-finding procedure may be of independent interest.

**comment/C1：** Full version of the paper appearing in FOCS 2026. The abstract was shortened due to requirements from arXiv

</details>

<a id="paper-206"></a>

### 206. [GeoFlip: Benchmarking Geometry-Sensitive Physics in Video Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42002v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.42002

**作者：** Yuqi Li、Junming Liu、Bowen Hu、Ruize Niu、Huiran Duan、Yingli Tian、Hao Wu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42002v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.42002v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文构建 GeoFlip，一个以几何干预引发物理结果翻转的视频生成基准：固定物理设置与文本提示，仅改变支撑范围、物体位置和机构配置，形成 482 组配对场景与 964 段参考视频，并对 16 个图像到视频模型在事件正确性、结构完整性与参考相似度上评测。

**相关性简评：** 配对干预可核查模型是否真正使用首帧几何证据、还是被先验或文本旁路主导，与 LVR 中视觉信息保留和证据筛选的诊断思路相通；但它是视频生成基准，能否迁移为 latent 推理状态的评价工具尚待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GeoFlip: Benchmarking Geometry-Sensitive Physics in Video Generation

**abstract/A1：** Video generation models synthesize realistic motion, but can they respond correctly to changes in initial geometry? We introduce GeoFlip, a video generation benchmark centered on physical outcome flips induced by geometry. With shared physical settings and textual prompts held fixed, GeoFlip applies controlled interventions to support extent, object placement, and mechanism configuration, constructing paired scenes with contrasting reference events, such as remaining stable versus falling or tipping. These pairs test whether models translate geometric differences in the first frame into correct physical evolution. The benchmark comprises 482 scene pairs and 964 reference videos across six physical categories, with reference outcomes established through procedural scene construction, physics simulation, and numerical validation and verification of physical events. We evaluate 16 models for image to video generation along three dimensions: event correctness, structural integrity, and similarity to reference videos. We examine evaluation consistency with independent vision language judges. On the 442 conditions shared by all models, the highest event correctness averaged across tasks is 27.98%, revealing substantial limitations in physical generation conditioned on geometry. Model strengths vary across physical mechanisms, and the three evaluation dimensions capture distinct capabilities. Typical failures include ignoring geometric changes, generating incorrect object motion, bending rigid objects, and introducing additional components. Rank correlations between judges reach 0.906 for event correctness and 0.929 for structural integrity. By combining controlled geometric interventions, verifiable physical references, and multidimensional evaluation, GeoFlip assesses progress from visual realism toward physical consistency conditioned on geometry.

**comment/C1：** 14 pages, 7 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-207"></a>

### 207. [TradeWar: A Benchmark for Execution-Grounded Trade Negotiation with Adaptive LLM Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42004v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.42004

**作者：** Xiangyu Kong、Ruirui Yang、Xia Hou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42004v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.42004v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 TradeWar 多国贸易谈判仿真基准，把对话承诺转成关税与贸易份额策略并在结算中执行，用经济健康指数评价；同时给出 TACTIC 智能体，在不更新权重的情况下跨回合利用对手行为与结算结果迭代改进策略。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理无直接联系，但其「把对话产出落到可执行结算、以实际执行结果而非表面一致评分」的思路，可能可供核查 latent 内容是否真正因果影响最终答案，以及延迟反馈下的跨回合状态更新与贡献分配问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TradeWar: A Benchmark for Execution-Grounded Trade Negotiation with Adaptive LLM Agents

**abstract/A1：** Multi-turn negotiation remains a challenging problem for large language model (LLM) agents. While LLMs can conduct natural-language dialogue, existing benchmarks predominantly assess only agreement rates, overlooking whether negotiated commitments actually create value once executed. To address this gap, we introduce TradeWar, a multi-country simulation benchmark that translates negotiation dialogues into concrete tariff and trade-share policies, enforces capacity and network constraints during settlement, and evaluates outcomes via an Economic Health Index (EHI). To solve this execution-grounded task, we propose TACTIC (Trade-aware Adaptive Consolidation and Theory-of-mind for International Commerce), an agent that iteratively refines its negotiation strategy across episodes-without updating base LLM weights-by leveraging prior experience, opponent behavior, and past settlement outcomes. Despite using zero-shot chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting, PromptLLM attains a 75% agreement rate but only 57.1 final EHI (\*100), whereas TACTIC reaches 68.8. Across 20 sequential episodes and three seeds, TACTIC's final EHI improves from 58.2 to 73.2, compared with 65.2 for an ablation without cross-episode updates. Under technology-controls and energy-crisis shocks, TACTIC also exhibits smaller performance degradation than PromptLLM and MemoryLLM. These results indicate that CoT deliberation and interaction memory alone do not fully address delayed execution feedback, whereas settlement-grounded adaptation improves long-horizon negotiation in TradeWar.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to DAI'26

</details>

<a id="paper-208"></a>

### 208. [CausalXRL: Explainable Reinforcement Learning through Causal Graph Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42028v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.42028

**作者：** Yanming Zhang、Eric Papenhausen、Klaus Mueller

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42028v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.42028v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CausalXRL 框架，利用因果图推理为强化学习智能体的决策生成结构化、多层次的解释，并在基准 RL 环境与 RTS 环境 MicroRTS 上通过保真度、因果结构学习效率的定量分析及专家定性研究进行评估。

**相关性简评：** 非多模态、无视觉证据，但因果图式的状态抽象与「解释保真度」评估思路，可能为 latent 推理状态中的因果贡献分配与答案是否真正使用 latent 内容的核查提供借鉴。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CausalXRL: Explainable Reinforcement Learning through Causal Graph Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement learning is a powerful paradigm for training autonomous agents and has achieved impressive performance in complex environments. However, this success often comes at the cost of interpretability, diminishing trust and complicating efforts to debug and improve agent behavior. To address these challenges, we introduce CausalXRL, a novel framework for explainable reinforcement learning (XRL). A key feature of CausalXRL is its use of causal graph reasoning, which provides transparent, structured, multi-level explanations of agent decision-making. We validate CausalXRL through comprehensive case studies and a two-part evaluation: (1) a quantitative analysis of explanation fidelity and causal-structure learning efficiency in benchmark RL environments, and (2) a qualitative expert study assessing explainability in the real-time strategy (RTS) benchmark MicroRTS. The quantitative results show that CausalXRL can provide faithful explanations while efficiently learning causal structures, and the qualitative expert study suggests that participants found CausalXRL useful for inspecting high-level RTS strategies.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to ICML 2026 - full paper - main track

</details>

<a id="paper-209"></a>

### 209. [The Knowledge Layer Model (KLM): Evidence-Visible AI Output, Routing Diagnostics, and Negative Results](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42066v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.42066

**作者：** Omer Asim Ozturk、Cihan Aydiner

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42066v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.42066v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Knowledge Layer Model（KLM）这一开放规范，用带版本的认知评估、证据关系和显式未知项记录 AI 推理依据，并以 SEDIM 为参考实现；同时报告路由诊断与多项内部负结果，尚无端到端验证。

**相关性简评：** 其「证据可见」记录与显式未知项可能为核查 LVR 答案是否真正依赖 latent 证据、如何做贡献归属提供概念线索，但论文明确不证明因果依赖且非视觉推理，关联有限。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Knowledge Layer Model (KLM): Evidence-Visible AI Output, Routing Diagnostics, and Negative Results

**abstract/A1：** AI answers require more than apparent correctness: reviewers need to inspect their recorded basis. We introduce the Knowledge Layer Model (KLM), an open, implementation-neutral specification for inference records with versioned epistemic assessments, evidence relations, and explicit unknowns. SEDIM, the knowledge and evidence architecture of Nage AI OS, is a reference implementation. We reference KLM v0.3 (repository tag v0.3.0). Empirical material consists of separate internal experiments, not end-to-end validation. A historical platform-domain routing benchmark reports 137/150 classifications across 15 classes (91.3%; nominal 95% Wilson interval 85.7-94.9%); the numerator is recovered from a rounded summary, and historical example membership is unverified. A native-model weight-path configuration yielded 0/15 successful answers in a corrected two-source test. A capacity-growth comparison could not establish a reliable effect because of a defective legacy scorer and missing original outputs. Lexical and scoped diagnostics each yielded AUC 0.008 for silent-versus-contradicted examples, below the registered minimum of 0.65. Legacy packing lost gold-containing text in 118/221 selected cases; repaired packing retained it in 221/221. These measure text presence, not answer quality. Python and TypeScript verifiers reportedly agreed on six checks per synthetic fixture; a separate production disclosure was not accepted. Restoration, re-derivation, human-rubric support, boundary isolation, and bounded-response evaluations remain proposed. Findings are internal and author-reported; historical observations are distinguished from later reproductions. Evidence visibility does not establish correctness, causal reliance, security, or regulatory compliance. Independent replication and a complete public audit package remain future work.

**comment/C1：** 47 pages, 1 figure, 14 tables. Includes Appendix A

</details>

<a id="paper-210"></a>

### 210. [Hyperbolic Inverse Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42104v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.42104

**作者：** Iulia Rusu、Anoop Praturu、Sergei Gepshtein、Tatyana Sharpee

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42104v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.42104v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Hyperbolic IRL，将奖励参数化为特征向量与学习参数向量之间的双曲距离（并给出欧氏距离变体），在线性与非线性 IRL 基线、确定性与随机策略、标准基准及博物馆导航数据上比较其预测精度与可解释性。

**相关性简评：** 其核心是特征空间的几何假设与不可分离的合取奖励结构，可能为 LVR 中视觉特征表示的几何组织与组合结构如何被读取提供概念线索，但论文不含视觉推理状态或 latent 生成机制，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Hyperbolic Inverse Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) seeks to infer the reward function underlying observed behavior, but its predictive accuracy and interpretability depend on how that reward is computed from the underlying features. While prior IRL reward functions have been implemented through a variety of techniques including linear weights, neural networks, or other function approximators, none make assumptions about the geometry of the underlying feature space. Inspired by geometric organization observed in neural circuits, we introduce Hyperbolic inverse reinforcement learning (Hyperbolic IRL), which parameterizes reward as a function of Hyperbolic distance between the feature vector and a learned parameter vector, as well as its Euclidean distance variant. We tested our methods against a range of linear and nonlinear IRL baselines under both deterministic and stochastic policies. Our Hyperbolic approach achieved comparable performance to state-of-the-art models on established benchmarks and excelled in the low data limit and across dense environments. Applied to human navigation among multiple exhibits in a museum room, Hyperbolic IRL reveals reward structure that depends not only on individual exhibit-distance features, but also on non-separable conjunctions between them. Together, these results demonstrate that a Hyperbolic reward parameterization can improve both the predictive accuracy and interpretability of IRL, providing a framework for studying how non-separable, conjunctive structure can begin to explain behavior.

</details>

<a id="paper-211"></a>

### 211. [Gradient Descent on Two ReLU Neurons: Global Landscape and Bifurcation Dynamics](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42118v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.42118

**作者：** Binghua Li、Mengzhe Li、Denny Wu、Tianhao Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42118v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.42118v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在双神经元 ReLU teacher-student 模型（各向同性高斯输入、小初始化、population gradient flow）下分析平方损失景观与梯度流轨迹，证明两权重均非零的临界点只能是全局最优或鞍点，并刻画学生神经元先向角平分线鞍点坍缩、随后逃逸并各自专门化的多阶段对称破缺过程。

**相关性简评：** 其「先坍缩至鞍点、再分化解耦」的多阶段对称破缺与鞍点逃逸分析，可能为理解多条 latent 推理轨迹或潜在状态槽在训练中如何形成分工、初始化与宽度如何影响专门化提供概念与诊断线索；但该工作纯属优化理论、无视觉或多模态成分，迁移需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Gradient Descent on Two ReLU Neurons: Global Landscape and Bifurcation Dynamics

**abstract/A1：** Understanding how gradient descent learns features remains a central challenge in neural network theory. We study this question in a minimal multi-index setting that already exhibits rich multi-phase gradient dynamics: a well-specified two-neuron ReLU teacher-student model under isotropic Gaussian inputs, trained by population gradient flow from small initialization. We show that the dynamics are organized by two directions: the "easy" bisector direction, which carries the leading signal, and the "hard" splitting direction, which governs specialization. To characterize the loss structure and learning behavior, we analyze the population squared-loss landscape and show that every critical point with both student weights nonzero is either a global minimum or a saddle. We then track the gradient-flow trajectory, showing that student neurons first collapse toward a bisector saddle before escaping and specializing to teacher neurons. Our results provide both a landscape and dynamics account of the multi-phase symmetry-breaking behavior that arises in a simple multi-index model and under standard algorithmic and architectural choices.

**comment/C1：** 85 pages, 2 figures. Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-212"></a>

### 212. [Instance-dependent sequential limits as an exact information ledger](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42133v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.42133

**作者：** Akshay Balsubramani

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.IT、math.ST、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42133v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.42133v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把 bandit regret、序贯检验、最佳臂识别与 MDP 的 instance-dependent 极限统一写成一条从散度分解、数据处理到检验与优化的“精确信息账本”，并给出一族鞅恒等式及可计算的缺陷项，用于在多个应用数据源上定位算法与经典极限的差距。

**相关性简评：** 其序贯证据累积与 Wald 检验式停止、信息账本和缺陷分解，可能为 LVR 中 THINK/LOOK/STOP 的停止决策与信息—计算成本核算提供概念工具，但需核查如何映射到 latent 状态与视觉证据筛选。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Instance-dependent sequential limits as an exact information ledger

**abstract/A1：** Instance-dependent limits for bandit regret, sequential tests, best-arm identification and Markov decision processes are proved by one chain. It runs from a divergence decomposition through data processing to a statistic, a testing step that turns a class guarantee into a requirement on the history, and optimization over alternatives and allocations. Only the first step is an identity. This work writes the chain as an exact ledger. By the chain rule, data processing loses exactly a conditional divergence, which vanishes for arms in natural exponential families at the per-arm counts and reward sums. A one-parameter family of martingale identities extends the decomposition to every tilt between two instances. The classical limits follow by relaxations with named defects. All defects but the asymptotic step's can be computed from an algorithm's own runs, the uniform quantification's only when the class guarantee is a number. On seven published sources, from movie ratings to engine replacement, the ledger locates the difference between standard algorithms and the classical limits. For identification among trained models and for Wald's test the largest term is usually the uniform quantification's.

</details>

### 213. [Hear No Evil: Detecting Gradient Leakage by Malicious Servers in Federated Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2506.20651v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2506.20651

**作者：** Fei Wang、Baochun Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CR、cs.DC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2506.20651v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2506.20651v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究联邦学习中恶意服务器操纵全局模型以诱发梯度泄漏，并提出客户端检测机制；其安全与隐私攻防问题不涉及视觉细节表征、latent 推理状态生成或视觉证据使用，也缺少可供核查的状态或监督借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Hear No Evil: Detecting Gradient Leakage by Malicious Servers in Federated Learning

**abstract/A1：** Recent work has shown that gradient updates in federated learning (FL) can unintentionally reveal sensitive information about a client's local data. This risk becomes significantly greater when a malicious server manipulates the global model to provoke information-rich updates from clients. In this paper, we adopt a defender's perspective to provide the first comprehensive analysis of malicious gradient leakage attacks and the model manipulation techniques that enable them. Our investigation reveals a core trade-off: these attacks cannot be both highly effective in reconstructing private data and sufficiently stealthy to evade detection -- especially in realistic FL settings that incorporate common normalization techniques and federated averaging. Building on this insight, we argue that malicious gradient leakage attacks, while theoretically concerning, are inherently limited in practice and often detectable through basic monitoring. As a complementary contribution, we propose a simple, lightweight, and broadly applicable client-side detection mechanism that flags suspicious model updates before local training begins, despite the fact that such detection may not be strictly necessary in realistic FL settings. This mechanism further underscores the feasibility of defending against these attacks with minimal overhead, offering a deployable safeguard for privacy-conscious federated learning systems.

</details>

<a id="paper-214"></a>

### 214. [Leaner Training, Lower Leakage: Revisiting Memorization in LLM Fine-Tuning with LoRA](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2506.20856v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2506.20856

**作者：** Fei Wang、Baochun Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.CR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2506.20856v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2506.20856v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究 LoRA 微调中的记忆与数据泄露风险，属于参数高效训练与隐私评估，未涉及视觉推理状态、证据使用或 latent 推理机制，也无可直接借鉴的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Leaner Training, Lower Leakage: Revisiting Memorization in LLM Fine-Tuning with LoRA

**abstract/A1：** Memorization in large language models (LLMs) makes them vulnerable to data extraction attacks. While pre-training memorization has been extensively studied, fewer works have explored its impact in fine-tuning, particularly for LoRA fine-tuning, a widely adopted parameter-efficient method. In this work, we re-examine memorization in fine-tuning and uncover a surprising divergence from prior findings across different fine-tuning strategies. Factors such as model scale and data duplication, which strongly influence memorization in pre-training and full fine-tuning, do not follow the same trend in LoRA fine-tuning. Using a more relaxed similarity-based memorization metric, we demonstrate that LoRA significantly reduces memorization risks compared to full fine-tuning, while still maintaining strong task performance.

</details>

### 215. [Spatial Atlas-Informed Loss Ensembles for Whole-Body PSMA PET/CT Metastasis Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40834v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40834

**作者：** Grant N. Chau、Bashirul A. Biswas、Biratal Wagle、Matthew E. Maeder、Michael S. Leapman、James B. Yu、Indrani Bhattacharya

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40834v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40834v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 构建群体转移灶空间图谱，并将其统计量嵌入 nnU-Net 的交叉熵损失，以提升全身 PSMA PET/CT 转移灶分割与病灶检出。

**排除原因：** 属于医学影像分割中引入空间先验权重的任务型应用，不涉及内部推理状态、证据获取或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Spatial Atlas-Informed Loss Ensembles for Whole-Body PSMA PET/CT Metastasis Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Existing whole-body PSMA PET/CT prostate metastasis segmentation methods generally rely on images alone, without explicitly incorporating where metastases tend to occur. We investigate whether a population metastasis atlas can improve lesion detection when integrated into model training. We constructed voxel-wise mean and standard deviation maps from 375 expert-annotated training scans and used them to weight the cross-entropy loss of an nnU-Net. The additional penalties for missed lesions and false positives depend on spatial atlas statistics and diminish as predictions become confidently correct. Seven configurations were compared on validation data; three were combined by majority voting into SAILS (Spatial Atlas-Informed Loss enSemble). We compared SAILS with a baseline nnU-Net and inference-time atlas fusion on AutoPET~III (110 disease-positive cases) and Deep-PSMA (100 external cases). No atlas or registration is needed at inference. Results: On AutoPET~III, SAILS improved whole-body Dice (0.531 vs. 0.520; p=0.006), reduced missed-lesion volume (9.5 vs. 12.1 cc/case; p&lt;0.01), and increased pooled lesion sensitivity (0.857 vs. 0.843; p=0.004). On Deep-PSMA, SAILS detected 126 additional reference lesions (sensitivity 0.769 vs. 0.751; p&lt;0.001), without a significant whole-body Dice change (-0.009; p=0.36). High-risk bone Dice improved in both cohorts (0.576 vs. 0.552 and 0.667 vs. 0.653; both p&lt;0.01). False-positive volume increased by 2.3 and 18.2 cc/case, respectively (p&lt;0.001). Atlas-weighted training improves lesion detection, especially in high-risk bone, without test-time atlas registration, but increases false positives.

**comment/C1：** 21 pages, 6 figures

</details>

### 216. [TSFLab: A Self-Improving Agent-Agnostic Workbench for Time Series Forecasting Research](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40901v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40901

**作者：** ChengAo Shen、Yanjun Zhao、Haoxin Liu、Bonnie Chen、Miles Ma、Dongjin Song、Hanghang Tong、Qingsong Wen、Jingrui He、Jingchao Ni

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40901v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40901v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 TSFLab：一个与具体编码智能体解耦的「工作台」，为 Claude Code、Codex 等提供共享资源与工具，用于时序预测论文的复现、基准测试和方法开发，并降低 token 成本。

**排除原因：** 该工作属时序预测领域的智能体辅助研究与基准工程，关注复现成本与资源维护，未涉及视觉/多模态推理的内部状态、证据筛选或 latent 生成，其 token 指标也只服务研究自动化，故缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TSFLab: A Self-Improving Agent-Agnostic Workbench for Time Series Forecasting Research

**abstract/A1：** As publications and products on time series forecasting (TSF) proliferate, researchers face a growing burden of analyzing existing work. First, not all publications release code. For instance, only about 20% of arXiv TSF papers have a code link in the surveyed period. Second, released code also requires manual maintenance. Thus, TSF researchers must constantly adapt or implement existing works. However, independent implementations can differ in experimental settings and are difficult to maintain. We therefore propose to use AI agents for benchmarking TSF research and even supporting autoresearch, e.g., developing new TSF models. Yet directly applying existing coding agents, such as Claude Code and Codex, is suboptimal: prohibitive costs, limited scalability to large research projects (e.g., benchmarking), and lack of self-maintenance. To address these challenges, we propose TSFLab, a self-improving agent-agnostic "workbench" that equips compatible coding agents with shared resources and tools for low-cost TSF research and continuous maintenance of research resources. Our experiments cover reproduction, benchmarking, and new method development. In reproduction and benchmarking, TSFLab improves task completion by 20-163% and cuts fresh tokens per completed task by 11-66% across baselines. A pilot study of agent-driven method development also shows improved forecasting accuracy via TSFLab, with 32.8-73.6% fewer fresh tokens and 9.2-68.8% less development time than the other settings.

**comment/C1：** Code: https://github.com/D2I-Group/TSFLab

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40938

**作者：** Yunbai Zhang、Yunyi Gao、Han-Tai Shiao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40938v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40938v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DiRAFT，用聚合的概念转移、起始概念与计划长度先验采样新的概念图计划，再由话语层生成金融电话会合成文本，并从语料结构重叠与人工核查非公开事实残留两方面评估隐私风险。

**排除原因：** 该工作聚焦合成金融文本的隐私与结构保真评估，不涉及 VLM 推理中的视觉状态表示、latent 内容使用或停止决策，其先验采样与话语实现也难以给出具体可迁移的 LVR 方法线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DiRAFT: Privacy-Aware Financial Transcript Synthesis from Aggregate Discourse Priors

**abstract/A1：** Using customer conversations to train and evaluate financial language models raises confidentiality concerns because non-public financial facts can remain after direct identifiers are masked. Synthetic transcripts offer an alternative data source, but their discourse fidelity and residual confidentiality risks require assessment. We introduce DiRAFT (Discourse Realization from Aggregate First-order Transitions), which extracts financial concept graphs and records the order in which concepts are first introduced. It pools adjacent concept transitions across training records and samples new graph plans from aggregate transition, starting-concept, and plan-length priors. A discourse layer guides text realization without supplying an individual source passage or graph. We assess privacy-related risks through two complementary evaluations: corpus-level structural overlap and source-grounded checks for specific non-public investor facts. The structural assessment covers two financial corpora and three generation backends. The confidential-fact assessment uses human review of 1,400 investor-call outputs across seven strategies and two backends. We also evaluate transition fidelity, content recoverability, and trajectory diversity. Stochastic sampling provides a favorable balance between transition fidelity and structural overlap, with consistent advantages over greedy and top-3 on investor calls. The confidential-fact assessment likewise detects fewer matches for stochastic sampling than these alternatives on both backends covered by the fact assessment. These complementary assessments provide empirical evidence on residual confidentiality risks without establishing a formal guarantee.

**comment/C1：** 21 pages. Accepted at the Financial AI Security, Privacy and Safety Workshop at ICAIF 2026 (non-archival)

</details>

### 218. [CAMEL: Cross-Architecture Masked Embedding Learning for Ultra-Lightweight Retinal Foundation Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40954v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40954

**作者：** Andre Guarnier De Mitri、Giovani Decico Lucafo、Miller Matheus Lima Anacleto Rocha、Diego Furtado Silva

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40954v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40954v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CAMEL，一种跨架构蒸馏框架，用非对称掩码的嵌入学习把大型视网膜基础模型的知识迁移到轻量学生模型，以降低临床部署成本。

**排除原因：** 属医学影像模型压缩与蒸馏工作，既无 latent 推理状态生成，也未涉及视觉证据筛选、停止决策或多步推理，与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CAMEL: Cross-Architecture Masked Embedding Learning for Ultra-Lightweight Retinal Foundation Models

**abstract/A1：** Recent progress in deep learning has substantially advanced automated grading of ophthalmologic pathologies, supported by large annotated datasets and improved representation learning. However, a significant gap remains between state-of-the-art performance and clinical scalability. In particular, foundation models have reshaped image-based medical screening and diagnosis. These large-scale architectures trained on extensive datasets learn rich, generalizable feature representations that can be adapted to a wide range of downstream tasks. In ophthalmology, RETFound, one of the most relevant foundation models for retinal images, performs strongly across classification, segmentation, and risk prediction tasks. Despite their effectiveness, foundation models are often computationally prohibitive, hindering their deployment in real-world clinical environments where hardware constraints and latency are critical. To bridge this gap, we introduce CAMEL (Cross-Architecture Masked Embedding Learning), a novel cross-architecture distillation framework that uses an asymmetric masking strategy during embedding learning to effectively transfer knowledge from massive teacher foundation models to compact, lightweight student models. Our experimental evaluation across four datasets shows that CAMEL preserves accuracy while drastically reducing computational complexity, enabling lightweight yet highly accurate models suitable for resource-limited clinical settings.

**comment/C1：** 8 pages, 6 figures, pre-print

</details>

### 219. [RADI3N: GeometRic ImAge EDiting via ParametrIc 3D CoNtrol](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40973v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.40973

**作者：** Abdul Wasi、Akhil Gorugantu、Mahesh Bhosale、Anjan Dutta、Junsong Yuan、Sauradip Nag、David Doermann

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40973v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.40973v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究基于参数化 3D 控制的部件级图像编辑与纹理迁移，属于图像生成与几何变形领域，未涉及 VLM 内部视觉表示、推理状态生成或证据获取与停止决策，缺乏可核查的实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RADI3N: GeometRic ImAge EDiting via ParametrIc 3D CoNtrol

**abstract/A1：** Recent diffusion-based methods have substantially advanced image editing, yet parametric 3D control at the part level remains challenging. Existing approaches either operate on whole objects with manual rigging, or revert to 2D handles at the part level. They also struggle to model appearance and texture under large transformations, leading to unrealistic outputs in geometry-intensive edits. To address these limitations, we introduce RADI3N, a framework for semantically-grounded part-aware object manipulation with parametric 3D control. Our CS-ARAP deformation module suppresses rotation-field discontinuities at part boundaries via neighborhood covariance smoothing, producing structurally consistent deformations under complex part-level edits. We further introduce an inversion-free selective texture transfer mechanism that suppresses high-frequency positional biases and scales up low-frequency semantic cues within cross-image attention. This eliminates strict spatial bias and supports semantic matching, allowing source appearance to follow the edited part onto its new geometry. Experiments demonstrate that RADI3N achieves state-of-the-art performance in geometric fidelity, viewpoint consistency, and controllability.

</details>
