### 220. [When Can Unlabeled Data Pools Replace Membership Queries?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41001v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41001

**作者：** Dravyansh Sharma

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41001v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41001v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究半空间学习下，未标注数据池能否替代 membership query，并在角分布假设、无标注预算限制和 s-concave 边际下给出标签复杂度上界与下界。

**排除原因：** 属纯学习理论的标签复杂度刻画，未涉及视觉/多模态表示、latent 推理状态或证据筛选，与 LVR 主线缺乏可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Can Unlabeled Data Pools Replace Membership Queries?

**abstract/A1：** Membership queries let a learner label arbitrary points and give near-optimal $\\tilde O(n\\log(1/\\eps))$ label complexity for halfspaces under every marginal. A pool learner may label only sampled points. We ask when unlabeled data can replace membership queries. For homogeneous halfspaces the answer depends only on the angular law of $X/\\\|X\\\|$. If this law assigns zero mass to every hyperplane through the origin and positive mass to every nonempty open set of directions together with its reflection, a pool learner uses $O(n\\log^3n\\log(n/(\\epsδ)))$ labels, uniformly over all such marginals and targets, without density, moment, concavity or isotropy assumptions. The same label bound holds for non-homogeneous halfspaces under every marginal with full support and no mass on affine hyperplanes, including Gaussians. Dropping either angular assumption admits $Ω(1/\\eps)$ pool versus $O(\\log(1/\\eps))$ membership-query separations, even with unlimited unlabeled data. The price is paid in unlabeled data: in dimension $n\\ge3$, for every unlabeled budget fixed in advance, some marginal satisfying both assumptions forces $Ω(1/\\eps)$ labels. For isotropic $s$-concave marginals in dimension $n\\ge3$, unlimited pools keep logarithmic label rates for every finite $s$. With an unlabeled budget fixed in advance, class-uniform polylogarithmic rates hold exactly for $s&gt;-1/(n+1)$, or $s&gt;-1/n$ under central symmetry, and in the general class the optimal rate between these thresholds is $\\tildeΘ(\\eps^{-(n-2)/n})$. This extends the range $s\\ge-1/(2n+3)$ of previous sample-based guarantees, in the realizable setting.

</details>

**排除原因：** 论文用视频世界模型为无人机航拍检测扩充训练数据，属数据合成与检测刷分，未涉及内部推理表示、latent 状态生成或证据筛选决策，与 LVR 主线缺少具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Dreams Are Cheaper Than Flights: Curated World-Model Data Improves Aerial Detection

**abstract/A1：** Robot perception is increasingly data driven, yet field data is slow, costly, and sometimes disruptive to collect. Video world models offer a cheaper source: seeded with real frames, they extend a recorded scene into new footage with the deployment's sensor, altitude, and environment. We ask whether such synthetic data matches the utility of real field data and whether detectors trained with it perform in the field. Seeding a 7B-parameter world model with wildlife drone video, we more than double the training set with curated frames. On held-out videos from the same drones, mAP@50--95 rises 4.3\\% when these frames are added to real data and 10.3\\% when they replace it, and up to 33.5\\% on an urban benchmark. Although an expert rejects an estimated 87.5\\% of generated clips, the data costs about \\$1,100 of GPU time, against more than \\$3,200 of field labor alone for a less useful real training set. Synthetic data thus shifts data costs from fieldwork to compute.

**comment/C1：** 6 pages, Corl 2026

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41055

**作者：** Hojat Allah Salehi、Devrim Aras、Md Jueal Mia、M. Hadi Amini、Imtiaz Karim、Mashrur Chowdhury、Bhavani Thuraisingham

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41055v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41055v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究车联网联邦强化学习中的相关私有量化与后量子认证通信：利用车辆对之间的共享随机性降低聚合误差，并用 Rényi DP 做向量级局部隐私核算，在协同路由仿真中评估。

**排除原因：** 主题为联邦学习通信压缩与 DP 隐私保护，不涉及多模态推理、latent 状态或视觉证据使用，与 LVR 主线无可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Communication-Efficient Private Federated Reinforcement Learning with Post-Quantum Authentication for Vehicular Networks

**abstract/A1：** Federated reinforcement learning supports cooperative vehicular routing without centralizing trajectory data, but model updates may reveal private information and incur communication overhead over intermittent vehicle-to-infrastructure links, particularly when post-quantum authentication is required. We study correlated private quantization, using complementary shared randomness between paired vehicles to reduce aggregation mean squared error while preserving the marginal parameter-level local differential privacy and one-bit communication cost of independent quantization. A tunable extension mixes shared and independent randomness to bound privacy leakage given paired outputs and shared-randomness side information. For this extension, Rényi differential privacy accounting establishes vector-level local differential privacy for one model upload even when pairings are revealed. Under matched vector-level privacy and identical local and global parameters, the extension yields 16.5% lower analytical aggregation mean squared error than independent quantization. We evaluate cooperative routing by coupling traffic simulation with packet-level simulation of post-quantum-authenticated vehicle-to-infrastructure communication. Compared to full-precision updates, one-bit uploads reduce authenticated uplink size by 92.4% and increase mean verified updates aggregated per round from 19.6 to 26.5. Under mobility-constrained communication at a parameter-level privacy budget of 1, correlated private quantization reduces mean travel time by 14.3% relative to independent quantization at the same budget and communication cost. At a parameter-level privacy budget of 0.3, a vehicle-origin inference attack trained on independent simulations reaches 30% accuracy with correlated private quantization and 22% with independent quantization after 100 rounds, versus 91% without privacy and 10% for random guessing.

**comment/C1：** 46 pages, 12 figures, 12 tables. Code: https://github.com/solidlabnetwork/cpq-vehicular-frl

</details>

### 223. [LanG 2.0: Energy-Efficient Agentic AI for Semi-Automated Security Operations at Scale](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41072v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41072

**作者：** Anes Abdennebi、Nadjia Kara、Laaziz Lahlou、Hakima Ould-Slimane

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41072v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41072v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该工作面向安全运营中心的多步威胁检测与规则生成，核心是小模型替代前沿模型的能耗与结构 safeguard，未涉及视觉或多模态推理中的内部状态、证据筛选或停止决策，缺少可核查的 LVR 联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LanG 2.0: Energy-Efficient Agentic AI for Semi-Automated Security Operations at Scale

**abstract/A1：** Security operations centers (SOCs) need to turn terabytes of alerts, logs and flows into trusted, affordable decisions to unveil hidden threats. We present LanG 2.0, an agentic system that detects threats, correlates evidence and writes rules, investigating whether small local models can replace frontier models affordably and safely when structural safeguards, not model accuracy, provide the guarantees. It incorporates entity-anchored correlation, human approval of rule changes and a tamper-evident ledger. Tests span eight flow datasets from five networks, real XDR alerts and multistage campaigns. Against its predecessor, campaign correlation precision rises from 18.75% to 51.81%, and rule acceptance from at most 60.00% to 98.33%. Against Claude Opus 5.5, scoring flows against each network's normal traffic detects attacks on unseen networks at 73.42% F1 without false positives, versus 58.10% with 49.00% false positives, for at least 650 times less energy. Finetuned models statistically match it on alert classification and rule writing for 5.6 to 59 times less energy. The work demonstrates LanG's practical scalability, processing 3.33 million records in 6.4 minutes, over 30 million hourly, and its safeguards resisted prompt injection, label poisoning and ledger tampering, so small local models can carry most SOC detection affordably and safely.

</details>

**内容简述：** 提出 QUAL，一种无需标签的时间序列基础模型组合框架：以模型池作为概率参照对各成员预测做分位数对齐，再用 Huber 聚合与 rearrangement 得到稳健组合预测。

**排除原因：** 该工作是纯时间序列预测的免训练集成与分位数校准，不涉及视觉或多模态推理，也没有内部状态生成、证据获取或停止决策等可与 LVR 对接的具体线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** QUAL: Quantile Alignment Improves Zero-Shot Time-Series Foundation Models

**abstract/A1：** Time-series foundation models (TSFMs) provide zero-shot forecasts for unseen series, but their accuracy varies across datasets. Combining several models can reduce this variability, yet zero-shot deployment provides no target labels for selecting or weighting them. We find that a model pool is useful not only as a final ensemble, but also as a probability reference for transforming each member forecast. Aligning quantiles through this pool-relative reference improves every member forecast in aggregate. Motivated by this finding, we propose QUAL (Quantile alignment), a simple label-free framework for improving and combining diverse TSFMs. QUAL first produces aligned member forecasts, then applies Huber aggregation and rearrangement to obtain a robust combined forecast without training or model modification. On GIFT-Eval, quantile alignment reduces average member MASE and CRPS by 7.4% and 9.8%. The full framework remains robust when weak forecasters enter a candidate pool whose quality cannot be verified in advance. Code is available at https://github.com/junhyeokkang/QUAL.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Foundation Models for Temporal Systems: From Forecasting to World Modeling

</details>

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Calibration of Large Model Quantization based on HiFloat4 Format

**abstract/A1：** Quantization of Large Language Models (LLMs) has be- coming increasingly important for reducing the computa- tional costs and resource requirements for practical deploy- ment.Growinginterestinthisareahasmotivatedthecommu- nity to move beyond 8-bit quantization towards 4-bit preci- sion, leading to the development of customized 4-bit formats such as MXFP4 and NVFP4. Recently, HiFloat4 (HiF4) has emerged as a competitive alternative which adopts a Block Floating-Point(BFP)formatwithathree-levelscalinghierar- chy, thereby achieving a significantly larger dynamic range. While quantization can substantially reduce memory usage and accelerate inference, it frequently introduces accuracy degradation. Calibration is therefore essential for narrowing theperformancegapbetweenlow-precisionquantizedmodels and their full-precision counterparts. Nevertheless, existing calibration methods are mainly designed and validated us- ing integer-precision formats, such as INT4, lacking support for HiF4 due to its hierarchical BFP structure. In this paper, weinvestigatethreecalibrationapproaches,namelyHiGPTQ, HiAutoRound, and HiFlatQuant, to further enhance model quantizationbasedontheHiF4format.Extensiveexperiments demonstratethatallthreeproposedapproachesimproveinfer- ence accuracy compared with direct HiF4 conversion, reduc- ing the gap between HiF4 and BF16 to less than 1% and out- performing the state-of-the-art NVFP4 format across various models and downstream tasks.

</details>

**排除原因：** 研究对象是调度规则演化与强化学习式知识保留，与 LVR 的视觉证据表征、latent 状态生成和推理停止决策无实质联系，代理模型与相似度加权也缺少可核查的迁移线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Lifelong Learning of Scheduling Rules with Surrogate-assisted Genetic Programming for Dynamic Flexible Job Shop Scheduling

**abstract/A1：** Genetic programming (GP) has been widely used to learn scheduling rules for dynamic flexible job shop scheduling (DFJSS). However, trained rules tend to only generalise to tasks of similar scales and distributions, and when task characteristics change, learned rules often suffer significant performance degradation. Moreover, fine-tuning pre-trained rules on new tasks often leads to catastrophic forgetting, where prior knowledge is lost. This motivates the need for GP methods that can adapt to new tasks while preserving past knowledge, leading to a lifelong learning paradigm. However, lifelong learning in GP for DFJSS remains largely under-explored. To address this gap, this paper proposes a surrogate-assisted GP method for lifelong learning to handle sequential heterogeneous DFJSS tasks with job or batch arrivals. The framework includes three key components: an adaptive two-stage training strategy to balance plasticity and stability, a task-similarity-based weighting mechanism to regulate knowledge reuse, and an improved surrogate model with distance constraints to enhance estimation accuracy. Experimental results show that the proposed method effectively mitigates catastrophic forgetting while maintaining strong adaptability, outperforming several compared methods and demonstrating the potential of lifelong GP in dynamic scheduling environments.

</details>

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

**内容简述：** 提出 FlowMoDE，用由粗到细的 flow matching 解码框架做单目深度估计，仅在单一合成室内数据上训练，并在多个真实室内外数据集和机器人场景上验证 sim-to-real 泛化与推理效率。

**排除原因：** 属密集回归式深度估计应用，flow matching 解码层只是任务输出头，缺少推理状态、证据筛选或停止决策层面的线索，与 LVR 关注点无实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FlowMoDE: Coarse-to-fine Flow Matching for Detail-aware Sim-to-Real Monocular Depth Estimation

**abstract/A1：** Monocular depth estimation is a fundamental capability for 3D spatial perception in robotic and intelligent systems. While data-driven discriminative methods have achieved compelling performance for monocular depth estimation, generative approaches can reconstruct fine structural details with stronger generalization. However, most existing generative diffusion models suffer from low inference efficiency caused by multi-step sampling, whereas single-step diffusion models struggle to recover high-fidelity depth maps. To mitigate these drawbacks, this paper presents FlowMoDE, a coarse-to-fine flow-matching-based framework for efficient, structure-aware monocular depth estimation supporting sim-to-real and cross-scene generalization. The core architecture first conducts coarse-level decoding conditioned on diffused features extracted from a pre-trained semantic encoder, and subsequently restores fine-grained structural patterns via expanded decoding layers. Trained exclusively on a single synthetic indoor dataset, our proposed method outperforms prior methods across five real-world indoor and outdoor datasets, yielding an average 14.1% reduction in relative error against state-of-the-art works as well as 9.9\\% reduction in chamfer distance for point cloud reconstruction. We further validate the generalization capability of FlowMoDE on realistic robotic scenes, with qualitative comparisons against state-of-the-art methods. The project page is https://flowmode-shao.netlify.app/

**comment/C1：** 9 Pages, 7 Figures, Under Review

### 228. [Beyond CLIP: Calibrated (Few-Shot) Defect Detection With System One Decision Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41199v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41199

**作者：** Fadel M. Megahed、Ying-Ju Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41199v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41199v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 面向高良率、小批量制造场景，研究以每类仅 2 张参考图的 k-shot 方式使用结构化决策模型（Jev、Clef-Flash）做缺陷检测，并配套文本描述自动精炼与概率校准。

**排除原因：** 属于工业视觉缺陷检测的少样本分类与概率校准应用，聚焦分类准确率与可靠性，未涉及视觉细节保留、推理状态生成或证据获取与停止决策，缺少可迁移到 LVR 的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond CLIP: Calibrated (Few-Shot) Defect Detection With System One Decision Models

**abstract/A1：** Cameras and vision sensors are widely used for product and process inspection in manufacturing environments. Traditionally, this process required thousands of images of both nominal and defective products to train such models. Recent research shows that vision transformer (ViT) models, such as CLIP, can reduce this requirement to 50-100 images per nominal and defective class. However, collecting even 50 defective images can be difficult in high-yield or low-volume manufacturing processes. In this paper, we examine the utility of two recently proposed structured decision models (Jev and Clef-Flash) in applications using as few as k=2 reference images per class at inference. In the process, we provide three main contributions: (a) a framework for k-shot learning of these models for image classification/inspection applications; (b) an agentic approach for automatically refining the text descriptions that guide image classification in these decision models; and (c) an approach to calibrate the resulting defect probabilities to improve their reliability for inspection decisions. We test these contributions in five manufacturing case studies. Using two reference images per class, both Jev and Clef-Flash outperform CLIP's test accuracies with the same references (0.75-1.00 \[Jev\] and 0.71-0.99 \[Clef-Flash\] vs 0.67-0.84 \[CLIP\]). Furthermore, we show that our agentic refinement of text descriptions significantly improves accuracy across 6 of 8 model-application combinations, and our calibration approach reduces the Brier score in 13 of the 15 model-application configurations. To support the reproducibility of our work, we provide our replication code and have created the R "decisions" package for evaluating additional models. We also provide a publicly available web app that allows practitioners to inspect their own images with no code.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 5 figures

</details>

### 229. [RTL-Interact: Training EDA-Interactive Models for Multi-Task RTL Design](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41225v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41225

**作者：** Wenxuan Shi、Yaoyu Zhu、Di Huang、Chongxiao Li、Xiaoyun Zhang、Muxin Song、Yutong Wu、Ziyuan Nan、Pengwei Jin、Tian Zhi、Xishan Zhang、Rui Zhang、Xing Hu、Zidong Du、Qi Guo、Yunji Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41225v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41225v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 面向 RTL 设计，提出多任务后训练框架 RTL-Interact，使单一模型学会生成 RTL、构造 EDA 检查并依据工具反馈修改，另提出 Pattern-Aware Policy Optimization 强化学习方法。

**排除原因：** 论文属纯文本代码/EDA 交互式生成与修复，虽涉及工具反馈迭代，但无视觉或多模态内部状态、证据筛选与 latent 推理的实质联系，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RTL-Interact: Training EDA-Interactive Models for Multi-Task RTL Design

**abstract/A1：** Large language models offer a promising route to register-transfer-level (RTL) design, but reliable implementations require checking and revision based on electronic design automation (EDA) tools' feedback. Existing RTL-specialized models often lack learned interaction with such EDA tools or rely on separate generation and repair components. We introduce RTL-Interact, a multi-task post-training framework that trains a single RTL-specialized model to generate RTL, construct task-appropriate EDA checks, and revise implementations using tool feedback. The framework combines automatically constructed multi-task demonstrations with staged supervision from chain-of-thought to tool-integrated reasoning. We further introduce Pattern-Aware Policy Optimization (PAPO) for multi-task reinforcement learning. PAPO augments Group Relative Policy Optimization with correctness-gated rewards that favor concise successful checking while rewarding effective revision, and uses task grouping to preserve coverage across objectives. On CVDP, RTL-Interact-32B achieves 48.7% weighted Pass@1 and 68.9% Pass@5, surpassing its teacher model, DeepSeek-V3.2, while RTL-Interact-8B achieves 44.9% weighted Pass@1. Additionally, we show that PAPO can reduce average tool calls by 63.8% while improving Pass@1. Both models also outperform all RTL-specialized baselines on VerilogEval v2 and RTLLM v2, demonstrating the effectiveness of our method.

**comment/C1：** Under review at ICLR 2027

</details>

**title/T1：** rl\_rob: A transparent, from-scratch Python library for teaching reinforcement learning from tabular methods to deep policy gradients

**abstract/A1：** rl\_rob is an open-source Python library for teaching reinforcement learning. It implements the arc of a modern course, from tabular temporal-difference learning through linear function approximation to deep value-based and policy-gradient methods, with every algorithm written from first principles so that its update rule is visible in a few lines of code. All agents share one interface, so learners can compare methods directly across stages and trace a deep reinforcement learning update from the temporal-difference error to the weight change. The library supports teaching and assessment in which students implement, modify and explain update rules, rather than only produce working code.

</details>

**内容简述：** 提出 SERIAL-7 韩英双语基准，用可执行校验框架评测七种表示上的结构化输出生成与跨表示保持。

**排除原因：** 工作聚焦文本结构化输出的格式与语义校验，不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、证据筛选与停止决策，跨表示保持也仅是文本产物间的转换，缺少可借鉴的 latent 状态线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SERIAL-7: Deriving, Realizing, and Preserving Machine-Verifiable Outputs Across Representations

**abstract/A1：** Large language models increasingly generate structured outputs consumed by programs and downstream systems, where semantic correctness alone does not guarantee reliable execution. We introduce SERIAL-7, a Korean-English benchmark built around an executable verification framework for machine-verifiable generation across seven representations. Using 177 validated canonical artifacts, SERIAL-7 separates semantic derivation (Derive), constrained artifact generation (Realize), and cross-representation preservation (Preserve). Its 43 atomic representation constraints are paired with executable checks over format, structure, and task-specific semantics, enabling progressively stricter verification from representation compliance to full artifact validity. Across ten models, REALIZE-L3 HSR ranges from 42.0% to 93.9%, while Full Compliance exposes additional artifact-level failures beyond correct final answers and valid representations. These results show that reliable structured generation requires explicit, executable verification of how artifacts are realized and preserved across representations.

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on AI for Verifiable Coding

</details>

### 232. [Generated storm lifecycles fill the rare-event tail of short rainfall records](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41365v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41365

**作者：** Valerie Tsao、Nathaniel W. Chaney、Manolis Veveakis、Marta Zaniolo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41365v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41365v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 StormGen 生成式模型，将实测风暴最初几小时的降雨转化为完整的 24 小时逐时降雨生命周期，用于补足短观测记录中极端事件的稀疏尾部，并以德州东南部数据及 Harvey 事件做评估。

**排除原因：** 属于气象领域的条件生成与极端事件外推应用，虽涉及由部分观测生成完整序列，但未涉及多模态视觉表示、推理状态更新、证据筛选或停止决策等 LVR 问题，缺乏可核查的方法借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Generated storm lifecycles fill the rare-event tail of short rainfall records

**abstract/A1：** Historically rare ''one-in-a-generation'' storms are occurring with unprecedented frequency, causing catastrophic damage and mounting casualties, and exposing the vulnerability of infrastructure designed around short observational records. To overcome the limitations of sparse data, we present StormGen, a generative machine-learning model that transforms an observed storm's initial hours into complete, physically plausible hourly rainfall lifecycles in minutes. Evaluated on a decade of southeast Texas rainfall, StormGen produced close counterparts for 4\\5% of the most severe 2\\% of storms--compared to just 3\\% for stochastic storm transposition--and maintained spatial coherence over full 24-hour sequences. Crucially, from a single hour of initial rainfall, the model successfully reconstructed the specific lifecycle, spatial pattern, and extreme catchment totals of Hurricane Harvey itself, despite the event being entirely absent in training. Because storm timing alone can more than double peak flows, these complete generated scenarios provide a vital mechanism to stress-test flood protections against unprecedented extremes.

</details>

### 233. [RECITE: Revising Eligibility Criteria Incorporating Textual Evidence](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41368v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41368

**作者：** Russell Ro、Reza Abbasi-Asl

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41368v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41368v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该工作用 RAG 与 LLM-as-judge 处理临床试验入排标准的文本修订，属纯文本证据检索与改写基准，未涉及视觉状态表示、latent 推理或多模态证据筛选，缺乏可迁移到 LVR 的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RECITE: Revising Eligibility Criteria Incorporating Textual Evidence

**abstract/A1：** Overly restrictive eligibility criteria contribute to widespread enrollment failure in clinical trials, yet published recommendations for how specific criteria should be broadened remain disconnected from trial design at scale. We introduce RECITE (Revising Eligibility Criteria Incorporating Textual Evidence), a framework for automated discovery, extraction, and implementation of evidence-based eligibility criteria revisions. RECITE uses protocol amendment documents from ClinicalTrials.gov to construct a dataset of 11,913 curated instances across 5,735 trials, each pairing original criteria with documentary evidence and target revisions. We benchmark fifteen LLM configurations under truncated-to-context-window and retrieval-augmented generation conditions, on a 3,116-instance sample under an LLM-as-judge protocol validated against ten domain experts (judge vs. human quadratic-weighted Cohen's $κ\_{qw} = 0.694 \\pm 0.096$). The best open-weight model, Gemma 2 9B, achieves $κ\_{qw}$-aligned ordinal score $3.0 \\pm 0.9$ on a 0--4 scale (normalized 0.742) and the best closed-weight model, GPT-4o, reaches $3.0 \\pm 0.8$ (normalized 0.745). Using an agentic literature discovery system, we then identified 44 paper--trial matches across five publications providing trial-design and EC-modification evidence. Our findings suggest that existing clinical knowledge can be operationalized into actionable trial design recommendations.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages, 4 figures, 4 tables. Extended version of a paper in KDD 2026 (32nd ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining). Code and data: https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.20487342

</details>

### 234. [Anomalies as Residuals: Cauchy Displacement Networks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41387v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41387

**作者：** Yang Cao、Zihao Guo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41387v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41387v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 将 KNN、LOF 等邻域型表格异常检测器的邻域几何编译为单隐层 Cauchy 激活网络，以预测位移长度作为异常分数，实现单次前向的低开销检测。

**排除原因：** 研究对象是表格异常检测与邻域均值位移的近似，不涉及多模态推理、latent 状态生成或证据获取，也未提供可核查的 LVR 方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Anomalies as Residuals: Cauchy Displacement Networks

**abstract/A1：** Neighbourhood-based detectors such as KNN, LOF and LUNAR remain among the most accurate methods for tabular anomaly detection, but they compare every query with the stored training set, so their inference cost and memory grow with the data and the data must be kept at deployment. We ask whether the neighbourhood geometry these detectors exploit can be compiled into a small network that scores a query with a single forward pass. We propose the Cauchy Displacement Network (CDN), which regresses each normal point onto the mean of its $k$ nearest training neighbours with a single-hidden-layer network of Cauchy activations and scores a query by the length of its predicted displacement. This displacement is an empirical mean-shift vector, whose fixed-radius population counterpart locally approximates a scaled gradient of the log density. We show that Cauchy units exactly represent the score fields of a structured family of Student-$t$ expert densities, and establish a constructive approximation bound for the corresponding population neighbourhood-mean map using a single-hidden-layer Cauchy network. We further show that the bounded network output guarantees asymptotically linear residual-score growth along every fixed direction. On 46 benchmark datasets against 28 baselines, CDN obtains the best mean rank under both AUC-ROC and AUC-PR, while scoring $10^4$ queries in a few milliseconds on a single CPU thread, up to three orders of magnitude faster than neighbourhood-based detectors, with a model whose size does not depend on the training set.

</details>

### 235. [Cross-Interface Authorization for AI Computer-Use Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41397v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41397

**作者：** Genliang Zhu、Chu Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41397v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41397v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出一种受 profile 约束的跨接口授权协议，为计算机使用代理的每条可产生影响的路由签发绑定 effect 的 prepare ticket，并在 commit 时重新校验路由、effect、授权与权威状态，配有形式化保证与大规模 trace、变异测试验证。

**排除原因：** 论文属于代理安全授权与协议验证，其中的「状态」指授权与系统状态而非 VLM 推理的 latent 状态，也未涉及视觉证据、推理生成或停止决策，缺乏可迁移到 LVR 的具体线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Cross-Interface Authorization for AI Computer-Use Agents

**abstract/A1：** Computer-use agents act through graphical interfaces, command lines, APIs, scripts, and Model Context Protocol servers. A policy decision can be interface-invariant while its executable authorization is not: another route can bypass mediation, alter a security-relevant default, or commit after the authorized state has changed. We present a profile-bounded protocol for a fixed, versioned computer-use deployment. It enumerates effect-bearing routes and durable sinks, checks a route-to-sink enforcement cut, normalizes each concrete request to a canonical effect, issues a route-bound prepare ticket, and verifies the route, effect, grant, trace, profile, inventory, and authoritative state again at commit. A committed result requires an agreeing sink receipt. Under explicit assumptions, we prove profile-bounded refinement, matched-effect decision invariance, resistance to alternate-route laundering and declared cross-surface recomposition, commit-time state safety, and receipt-grounded outcomes. The reference profile registers 12 effect families, five interfaces, and 60 routes. Two structurally distinct runtime mappings produce 3,464 traces over 1,732 scenarios each: 960 commits, 2,312 denials, and 192 commits after re-preparation. An independent checker accepts every retained trace and pair and rejects 26/26 semantic regressions; coverage and normalizer suites reject 24/24 and 720/720 mutants; and 168/168 commit probes match their declared outcomes. A frozen-source audit passes 52 checks over 12 files from two independent hybrid runtimes and one public authorization substrate. Source-shaped adapter refinements commit 48/48 instrumented calls and reject 15/15 unbound cases. The resulting guarantees bind the declared profile and its effect semantics.

**comment/C1：** 31 pages, 2 figures, and 7 tables. Includes formal results, two reference runtime mappings, mutation tests, commit-time race probes, source-shaped adapter checks, and a frozen external-source audit. Guarantees bind the registered deployment profile and declared effect semantics

</details>

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41411v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41411v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文构建了 cryo-EM 密度图引导蛋白原子模型搭建的大规模筛选数据集与统一评测基准，并对比评测现有学习方法与 Phenix、AlphaFold3，分析分辨率、序列长度等因素的影响。

**排除原因：** 属于特定科学任务的数据集与基准工作，不涉及 VLM 推理中的视觉证据筛选、latent 状态生成或停止决策等机制，缺乏可供核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CryoAtlas: A Large Curated Dataset and Unified Benchmark for Cryo-EM Atomic Model Building

**abstract/A1：** Deep learning has accelerated automated atomic model building from cryo-EM maps, yet progress remains limited by the lack of large, high-quality datasets and standardized evaluation benchmarks. We address this gap by introducing CryoAtlas, a rigorously curated dataset and unified benchmark for density-guided atomic model building of protein chains. By integrating and filtering EMDB and PDB entries (up to March 2026), CryoAtlas provides 17,809 map-model-sequence entries, including a curated 16,657-entry training split. From this resource, we construct a 618-target benchmark with a shared evaluation protocol that jointly assesses structural accuracy, model-to-map fit, and stereochemical validity. Using this framework, we benchmark state-of-the-art learning-based methods against Phenix and AlphaFold3, comprehensively analyzing the impact of map resolution, sequence length, protein category and sequence identity on modeling accuracy. As a case study, retraining the ModelAngelo CNN component on CryoAtlas improves performance on the benchmark. CryoAtlas provides an open, reproducible foundation for future cryo-EM atomic model building research. Project details and resource release updates are available at https://oulling.github.io/cryoatlas/.

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS 2026 E&amp;D Track

</details>

### 237. [NormDetect: Anchored Calibration for Low-False-Positive Detection of Machine-Generated Web Text](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41459v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41459

**作者：** Hanshuai Cui、Fanshuai Meng、Zhiqing Tang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41459v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41459v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 NormDetect：将按章节学习的判别头与经过 Unicode 归一化的零样本 Binoculars 分数混合，并仅在人类文本上校准单一阈值，以在极低误报率下提升对机器生成网络文本的召回。

**排除原因：** 该工作面向文本来源取证与 FPR 校准，不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、证据获取与停止决策，混合打分与阈值校准也未给出可核查的 LVR 借鉴线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** NormDetect: Anchored Calibration for Low-False-Positive Detection of Machine-Generated Web Text

**abstract/A1：** Web platforms screen posts for text written by large language models, and every false flag hits a human writer. Detectors must therefore run at a very low false positive rate (FPR). Zero-shot detectors such as Binoculars score text with frozen language models, but invisible edits such as homoglyphs shatter tokenization and push machine text into the human range. Unicode normalization undoes these edits, yet the normalized score still misses half of the machine text at a strict FPR, under attacks that leave nothing to clean. We find that with the Falcon-7B scoring pair, heads on token statistics recall less than the normalized score at this FPR, and several flag too many human posts in some sections. We propose NormDetect, which anchors a learned per-section head to the normalized zero-shot score. A blend weight chosen on training folds under a human FPR cap combines the two scores, and one threshold is calibrated on human text only. On eight sections of the RAID benchmark with eleven attacks, NormDetect raises recall at a 0.1% calibrated FPR by 5.0 points over normalized Binoculars. Its human FPR bound stays at most 1% in every section, and no other detector within this bound recalls as much.

</details>

### 238. [Spectral Haar Binarizer: Compact Boolean Contrast Representations for Hyperspectral Classification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41478v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41478

**作者：** Michał Romaszewski、Przemysław Głomb

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41478v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41478v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Spectral Haar Binarizer，用 Haar 式区间对比构造布尔谓词，并以 AdaBoost 式选择得到紧凑光谱表示，用于 Tsetlin Machine 等分类器的高光谱分类。

**排除原因：** 属于高光谱遥感分类的特征离散化应用，不涉及 VLM 推理、latent 状态生成或证据筛选，与 LVR 主线及可迁移的方法线索均缺少具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Spectral Haar Binarizer: Compact Boolean Contrast Representations for Hyperspectral Classification

**abstract/A1：** Tsetlin Machines offer an interpretable, hardware-friendly alternative to conventional machine learning models, but applying them to continuous hyperspectral measurements requires converting spectral signals into Boolean representations. Standard binarisation methods typically discretise each spectral band independently, potentially discarding important relationships between neighbouring wavelengths. In this paper, we introduce the Spectral Haar Binarizer (SHB), a contrast-based spectral representation method that constructs Boolean predicates from differences between selected spectral intervals. Inspired by Haar-like contrast features, the method uses an AdaBoost-style selection procedure to obtain a compact set of discriminative spectral predicates. We evaluate SHB on five heterogeneous hyperspectral datasets covering forensic blood detection, agricultural and urban remote sensing, and rainforest tree-species classification. Across binary and multiclass tasks, SHB consistently outperforms standard per-band binarisation strategies and provides the strongest or near-strongest results across classifiers. In binary tasks, TM-SHB achieves the best average performance, while RF-SHB is strongest in multiclass settings. Bayesian and rank-based analyses confirm that SHB's advantage is robust across datasets and not driven by isolated cases. The results show that representing continuous hyperspectral measurements is a central processing step for efficient classification. That contrast-based Boolean features provide an effective bridge between information-rich sensor measurements and compact, logic-based processing.

</details>

<a id="paper-239"></a>

### 239. [Multi-Test Nation Bias Benchmarking of LLMs with Explicit Unbiased Baselines](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41486v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41486

**作者：** Jonghyeon Choi、Yeonjun Choi、Beakcheol Jang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41486v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41486v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文用五项测试对 LLM 在国际关系场景中的国别偏向做量化评测，并借助投票行为构造伪无偏基线；既不涉及视觉输入或 latent 推理状态，也没有关于证据筛选、动态计算或停止决策的具体可借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Multi-Test Nation Bias Benchmarking of LLMs with Explicit Unbiased Baselines

**abstract/A1：** While prior work has highlighted that systematic nation-level bias in large language models (LLMs) can pose operational risks for international relations (IR) applications, many existing evaluations still lack a clearly specified unbiased reference (ground truth), limiting fully quantitative and cross-setting measurement. Using an expanded corpus of 722 official UN Security Council (UNSC) records, we measure nation-level bias with five complementary tests, thereby extending prior approaches to cover a broader set of bias expression channels: opinion ranking (WOW), association sorting (AK+-), vote simulation (PVS), opposition attribution (F-Oppose), and framing (Headliner). To enable grounded bias evaluation, we explicitly define an unbiased status for each test; in particular, for WOW and AK+- where ground truth is difficult to specify, we propose an action-based pseudo unbiased status derived from observable voting behavior to construct a quantifiable baseline. Benchmarking 4 LLMs (GPT-4o-mini, Llama 3.3-70B, Mistral-22B-Small, and Qwen3-30B) across the five permanent members of the UNSC, we find a test-agnostic macro-pattern: relative to the reference baselines defined for each test, model outputs lean toward the opposition (red) side for China and Russia and toward the supportive (blue) side for the U.K., the U.S., and France. These results underscore the need to audit IR-facing LLM deployments with grounded, multi-test benchmarks tied to explicit reference points.

**comment/C1：** Published in Transactions of the Association for Computational Linguistics (TACL), 2026. 17 pages. Equal contribution: Jonghyeon Choi and Yeonjun Choi

</details>

### 240. [Infinite ResNets are Gaussian Mean ODEs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41514v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41514

**作者：** Louis-Pierre Chaintron、Lénaïc Chizat、Javier Maass

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41514v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41514v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究 ResNet 在大宽度、深度与特征维度联合极限下的训练动力学，用带高斯初始权重的「Neural Mean ODE」上的梯度下降替代 DMFT 的非马尔可夫描述，并给出依赖网络形状与数据协方差谱的近似标度律。

**排除原因：** 内容属纯优化的深度网络极限理论，既不涉及视觉/多模态内部表示，也未讨论推理期状态生成、证据获取或停止决策，未见可核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Infinite ResNets are Gaussian Mean ODEs

**abstract/A1：** Understanding the training dynamics of deep neural networks in large-scale limits is a question both of foundational and of practical importance. Prior works have obtained such descriptions through dynamical mean field theory (DMFT), yielding a system of non-Markovian dynamics that are challenging to analyze and that obscure the optimization structure of the original dynamics. In this paper, we obtain instead a simple description of the large-scale limit training dynamics for ResNets in standard shape scalings and in the $1/\\text{depth}$ branch scaling that allows maximal (local) feature updates. For a model trained with gradient descent (GD) from a random initialization, the limit dynamics is simply GD on a Neural Mean ODE with Gaussian initial weights. Our analysis covers population or empirical risk GD in the joint limit of large width, depth and embedding, input, and output dimensions. We obtain approximation scaling laws that depend on the shape of the ResNet and the covariance spectrum of the data. We also illustrate the flexibility of our approach by discussing Mixture of Expert models, where likewise the limit admits an intuitive (nested) Mean ODE description.

</details>

### 241. [Enhancing Sentiment Classification on Small Datasets through Data Augmentation and Transfer Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41518v1>)

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

**内容简述：** 该文在小规模情感分类数据集上比较 EDA、回译和上下文替换三种数据增强，并对照传统分类器与 BERT，给出按模型类型选择增强策略的经验结论。

**排除原因：** 纯文本情感分类的数据增强对比，不涉及视觉或多模态内部状态、证据筛选、latent 生成与停止决策，也未提出可供核查的状态或监督线索，与 LVR 研究主线缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Enhancing Sentiment Classification on Small Datasets through Data Augmentation and Transfer Learning

**abstract/A1：** Small-scale sentiment classification often suffers from data scarcity, which limits the generalization ability of the models. This study evaluates and compares the effectiveness of three data augmentation strategies: Easy Data Augmentation (EDA), back-translation, and contextual token substitution (nlpaug-style), with both traditional machine learning classifiers (Logistic Regression, Random Forest) and transformer-based models (BERT). We perform a comprehensive empirical comparison with low-resource sentiment datasets by summarizing the results of recent studies and performing targeted head-to-head experiments. Our findings indicate that all augmentation methods improve performance. Contextual augmentation yields the most consistent gains for BERT models, while EDA and back-translation provide greater benefits for traditional classifiers. These insights help guide the selection of data augmentation techniques tailored to model type and dataset size, filling a critical gap in research on data augmentation for sentiment classification on small datasets.

</details>

### 242. [Connectivity-Aware Client Selection in Decentralized Federated Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41570v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41570

**作者：** Usevalad Milasheuski、Monica Nicoli、Stefano Savazzi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41570v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41570v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究去中心化联邦学习中的客户端选择，利用图拉普拉斯谱特征与热核/扩散核相似度，让每个客户端只向结构上相关的邻居广播更新，以减少通信量。

**排除原因：** 材料聚焦通信开销与收敛精度的分布式训练问题，未涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部表示、生成与证据使用，也无可迁移到 LVR 的具体状态或决策线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Connectivity-Aware Client Selection in Decentralized Federated Learning

**abstract/A1：** Decentralized federated learning (DFL) has emerged as a scalable alternative to centralized federated learning, removing the single point of failure and communication bottleneck at the server. However, most existing decentralized approaches rely on full-neighborhood broadcasting, where each client transmits its model update to all of its neighbors in each communication round. While effective, this results in significant communication overhead and high energy consumption. This paper applies spectral graph tools as lightweight client selection methods that leverage the graph topology by analyzing the spectral properties of the graph Laplacian over a fixed communication graph. Specifically, we exploit eigenvector embeddings and heat/diffusion kernel similarities to guide each client in selecting only a subset of structurally relevant neighbors, i.e., those that are most beneficial for the convergence of the federation process. This topology aware randomization matches or outperforms uniform neighborhood subsampling and approaches the accuracy of full broadcasting. The largest gains, about $3$ percentage points, emerge under strong data heterogeneity on larger, structurally rich graphs, while the total number of transmitted messages is reduced by $50\\%$ compared to naive broadcasting.

</details>

### 243. [DrawSplat: Atlanta World-Guided Implicit Gaussian SLAM for Indoor Reconstruction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41602v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41602

**作者：** Fan Zhu、Zhisong Xu、Ziyu Chen、Zhenjun Zhao、Takayuki Okatani

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41602v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41602v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DrawSplat，一个结合 Atlanta World 结构先验与锚点式隐式神经高斯表示的 RGB-D SLAM 框架，用于室内重建中的位姿跟踪、渲染与几何一致性优化。

**排除原因：** 属于 3D 重建与 SLAM 的表示和正则化工作，不涉及 VLM 推理状态、视觉证据筛选或动态计算决策，也未提供可迁移的 latent 推理线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DrawSplat: Atlanta World-Guided Implicit Gaussian SLAM for Indoor Reconstruction

**abstract/A1：** RGB-D SLAM requires accurate online tracking, high-quality rendering, and geometrically consistent reconstruction. Existing Gaussian SLAM methods mainly optimize Gaussian primitives with photometric, depth, or local frame-to-model constraints, which are often insufficient in indoor scenes with weak textures, large planar structures, noisy depth, and long trajectories. The resulting maps may suffer from pose drift, surface thickness artifacts, noisy normals, and blurred structural boundaries. We propose DrawSplat, an Atlanta World-guided implicit Gaussian SLAM framework for indoor reconstruction. DrawSplat follows a drawing-like process: explicit point-line geometry first sketches the structural layout, and implicit neural Gaussians then fill in high-fidelity appearance and continuous geometric details. It estimates camera poses, sparse geometry, and gravity direction through point-line joint tracking, infers structural planes from tracked lines using the shared-gravity component of the Atlanta World prior, and converts them into differentiable regularization terms on Gaussian centers and normals. To improve compactness and efficiency, DrawSplat further combines an anchor-based implicit neural Gaussian representation with adaptive structural sampling, which allocates Gaussian capacity to structurally informative regions. Experiments on multiple datasets show that DrawSplat achieves robust tracking, high-fidelity rendering, and strong geometric reconstruction among Gaussian-based SLAM systems, while maintaining a practical balance among quality, efficiency, and model size.

</details>

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出一个基于 SimCLR 对比学习的自监督三阶段模型，用于从真实图像估计挖掘机等施工机械的骨架姿态，并通过仿真数据与少量真实数据完成迁移。

**排除原因：** 研究对象是机械关键点姿态估计的标注效率问题，不涉及多模态推理、latent 状态、证据筛选或停止决策，其中对比预训练与视觉特征表示的联系过于泛化，缺少可供核查的 LVR 方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Self-Supervised Pose Estimation Approach for Construction Machines

**abstract/A1：** Pose estimation is a computer vision task used to estimate a skeleton of dynamic systems to predict future movements. Most of the research in this direction is based on a supervised learning approach which requires a massive amount of labeled datasets. In this paper, a self-supervised three-stage model based on a contrastive learning approach is introduced for estimating a skeleton of dynamic construction machines; such as excavators without using any labeled images for the first stage. The whole model structure is divided into three stages: the pre-train stage using the SimCLR contrastive approach, and two fine-tuning stages for the transfer learning and the downstream task. The model can leverage the features and learn from a huge unlabeled dataset called ACID to two small datasets generated from NVIDIA Isaac and MATLAB Simscape simulators as well as transfer the knowledge to a smaller dataset with a ratio of 3.5% from the original ACID dataset. The results show that the proposed approach can improve the accuracy of pose estimation for heavy construction machines in real images by 11% and 13% in comparison to the normal self-supervised approach with two backbones ResNet-50 and HRNet-W32, respectively.

**comment/C1：** 12 Pages, 12 figures, submitted for the International Symposium on Visual Computing conference

</details>

### 245. [From WSIs to Bulk, Spatial and Single-Cell Transcriptomics via Multi-Scale Graph Neural Networks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41692v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41692

**作者：** Miguel Andrade、José Almeida、Carlos Santiago、Catarina Barata

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41692v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41692v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文属计算病理学任务应用：用病理基础模型特征与多尺度 GNN 从 WSI 预测 bulk、空间与单细胞转录组，仅涉及跨尺度一致性损失等通用表示学习手段，未涉及 VLM 推理状态生成、证据使用或停止决策，缺乏可供核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From WSIs to Bulk, Spatial and Single-Cell Transcriptomics via Multi-Scale Graph Neural Networks

**abstract/A1：** Understanding the spatial distribution of gene expression is key to studying complex diseases like cancer. While Spatial Transcriptomics (ST) and Single-Cell Transcriptomics (scT) provide high-resolution insights, they remain expensive and scarce. In contrast, standard histopathology Whole-Slide Images (WSIs) paired with bulk RNA sequencing are widely available. This work introduces a Multi-Scale Graph Neural Network (GNN) capable of simultaneously predicting bulk, spatial, and single-cell transcriptomics directly from WSIs. By extracting morphological features using a Pathology Foundation Model (PFM) (UNI2-h) and constructing spatial graphs at both patch and cell levels, the model learns the hierarchical relationships within the tissue. The network is trained on a combined dataset of The Cancer Genome Atlas Program (TCGA), Visium, and Xenium samples across lung, kidney, and breast tissues. A hybrid training strategy is employed, leveraging direct supervision alongside weakly supervised consistency losses to ensure predictions align across biological scales. Results show the model accurately predicts gene expression at the slide level and moderately at the patch level (strongest on Xenium), and achieves moderate correlation at the challenging single-cell level. Furthermore, downstream tasks explore the clinical relevance of these predictions, demonstrating their utility in survival analysis, spatial hazard mapping, and tumor classification via molecular signatures.

</details>

**内容简述：** 提出 Nereus 水下图像数据集，含 88K 图像、2.68M 问答对与细粒度属性、物种标注及点标注的 grounded counting QA，用于细粒度海洋生物感知评测。

**排除原因：** 属于特定领域（水下）的细粒度识别与计数数据集，未涉及 latent 状态、推理过程监督或证据获取/停止机制，与 LVR 的状态、训练和监督问题缺少具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Nereus: A Large-Scale Underwater Dataset for Fine-Grained Attribute Understanding and Grounded Counting Perception

**abstract/A1：** Underwater imagery records rich ecological information and supports applications such as science communication, fishery monitoring, ecological conservation, and oceanographic research. Beyond general scene understanding, these applications require models to recognize fine-grained biological traits and reason over spatially grounded evidence. However, existing underwater datasets often provide coarse category labels, generic captions, or total-count supervision, which limits their use for organism-level analysis and ecological reasoning. We introduce Nereus, a large-scale dataset for Underwater Fine-grained Perception that jointly supports Fine-Grained Object Attribute Understanding and Grounded Counting Perception. Nereus contains 88K images and 2.68M question-answer pairs, including annotations for 136K marine organism objects across 2,690 species and 1,515 genera, as well as 65K point annotations and 67K grounded counting QA pairs. Experiments with representative multimodal models show that Nereus improves downstream adaptation over existing underwater-domain data and provides a foundation for future research on fine-grained marine perception.

**comment/C1：** Accepted by NeurIPS 2026 Evaluations and Datasets Track

### 247. [Advancing Clinical Trial Outcome Prediction with Domain-Specific Large Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41710v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41710

**作者：** Burak Bakkar、Hana Moalla、Mohamed Salem Jedidi、Haifa Ben Messaoud、Nicolas Lopez Carranza

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41710v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41710v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在 TOP 基准上比较 BioBERT、BioMistral 在临床试验分期结局预测中的微调与冻结嵌入加轻量分类器（probing）两种用法。

**排除原因：** 属纯文本临床预测任务，无视觉或跨模态推理、无推理期状态生成与停止决策，probing 仅为常规分类器协议，缺少可供 LVR 借鉴的具体机制线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Advancing Clinical Trial Outcome Prediction with Domain-Specific Large Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Clinical trial failures are a major barrier to efficient drug development, incurring substantial costs and delaying patient access to effective therapies. Structured approaches such as the Hierarchical Interaction Network (HINT) predict trial outcomes by explicitly modelling interactions between drugs, diseases and protocols, but make limited use of the free text in trial registrations. We evaluate two domain-specific language models, BioBERT (108M parameters) and BioMistral (7B parameters), for phase-level outcome prediction on the TOP benchmark (12,477 trials), representing each trial as text built from ten ClinicalTrials.gov fields. We compare fine-tuning (full fine-tuning for BioBERT, low-rank adaptation for BioMistral) with probing, in which the pretrained model is frozen and only a lightweight classifier is trained on its embeddings. On the held-out test set of each phase, all four language-model variants achieved higher ROC-AUC (0.613--0.772) and PR-AUC (0.649--0.901) than the published HINT results, corresponding to relative PR-AUC gains of 10--20\\%. F1 differences were small and mixed, and no model, including HINT, clearly exceeded the F1 of a trivial classifier that predicts success for every trial. Under the configurations tested, probing performed comparably to fine-tuning: fine-tuned models scored highest on ROC-AUC and PR-AUC in Phases I and II, and frozen BioMistral embeddings in Phase III (ROC-AUC 0.772; PR-AUC 0.901), although most differences between variants were below 0.03 and were not tested for significance. These results suggest that pretrained biomedical representations already encode signal relevant to trial outcome, and that probing is a computationally cheaper alternative to fine-tuning for this task.

</details>

### 248. [SAR-TS-BERT: Physics-Guided Self-Supervised Pre-Training With Polarimetric Features for SAR Time-Series Crop Classification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41728v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41728

**作者：** Jieqiu Wang、Yuhan Zhang、Bo Zheng、Shaoming Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41728v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41728v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SAR-TS-BERT，将后向散射强度与极化分解特征分双分支编码，并用基于时序变化率的物理引导 span 掩码做自监督预训练，用于 SAR 时序作物分类。

**排除原因：** 该工作属遥感时序分类的预训练与特征融合，不涉及视觉语言模型推理、latent 推理状态或答案对视觉证据的使用，也无可核查的状态更新或停止决策线索，故不入选。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SAR-TS-BERT: Physics-Guided Self-Supervised Pre-Training With Polarimetric Features for SAR Time-Series Crop Classification

**abstract/A1：** Multitemporal synthetic aperture radar (SAR) imagery provides continuous, cloud-free observations of crop phenology for agricultural monitoring in cloudy and rainy regions, yet high-accuracy classification typically requires extensive labeled samples. Although self-supervised pre-training mitigates label dependency in remote sensing, existing frameworks primarily target optical imagery or generic spatial SAR scenes, underutilizing temporal backscatter dynamics and polarimetric scattering mechanisms. We propose SAR-TS-BERT, a physics-guided BERT-style self-supervised pre-training framework for SAR time-series crop classification. SAR-TS-BERT integrates backscatter intensities and polarimetric ratio features (VV, VH, and VV/VH) with dual-polarimetric Cloude-Pottier decomposition parameters ($H/α/A$) into a six-band input representation through two core designs: 1) a physics-guided span masking strategy (SpanPhysics) that adaptively selects contiguous masked spans based on temporal backscatter change rates to prioritize key phenological transitions; and 2) a dual-branch cross-modal fusion encoder that models backscatter and polarimetric decomposition features through separate Transformer branches followed by bidirectional cross-attention fusion. On independent test sets in Estonia (EuroCropsML) and the USA (California CDL), SAR-TS-BERT achieves overall accuracies (OAs) of 96.78% and 87.29%, respectively, and attains a mean monthly OA of 99.37% on Chongming Island, Shanghai. On the Estonia benchmark, pre-training improves OA and Macro F1 by 1.17 and 1.59 percentage points over training from scratch under full supervision, and by 2.58 and 5.19 percentage points using only 1% labeled data. Incorporating SAR scattering physics into temporal self-supervised pre-training effectively enhances crop classification accuracy, parcel-level mapping consistency, and few-shot transferability.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages, 13 figures, 18 tables. Jieqiu Wang and Yuhan Zhang contributed equally to this work

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41772

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文构建自动驾驶高速公路施工区的真实与仿真双域基准数据集，包含360°多模态标注数据与24类场景仿真，并评测3D检测与端到端规划模型。

**排除原因：** 属于自动驾驶感知与规划的数据集和基准工作，未涉及视觉推理的内部状态、证据筛选、停止决策或 latent 表示，与本主线缺少具体可借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** WorkIng: A Real-World and Synthetic Highway Work-Zone Benchmark Dataset for Autonomous Navigation

**abstract/A1：** Highway work zones are critical edge cases for autonomous vehicles (AVs), combining atypical geometries and jurisdiction-specific regulations that existing benchmarks fail to capture: real-world datasets lack 360° multi-modal, full-scene 3D work zone annotation, and no single recorded site can span the layouts and regulatory variants needed for generalizable models. We present WorkIng, a dual-domain benchmark and training resource. WorkIng-Highway provides a 360° multi-modal work zone benchmark with synchronized 128-channel LiDAR, six RGB cameras, and RTK-GPS across 32 km, yielding 12,000+ annotated frames and 227,399 tracked 3D bounding boxes. WorkIng-CARLA complements this with procedural simulation spanning 24 regulation-aware scenarios across six canonical topologies and six international traffic standards, populated by 100+ custom 3D assets. Benchmarking representative 3D detection and end-to-end planning architectures shows that real-world data remains the primary performance driver, and that scenario-stratified synthetic augmentation improves architectures across 2D recognition, 3D detection, and trajectory planning. Resources: https://github.com/cvims/WorkIng

**comment/C1：** Accepted to WACV 2027. 19 pages including supplementary material. (c) 2027 IEEE. Personal use of this material is permitted

**作者：** Pranav Patel、Siva Kannathasan、Prerak Patel、Jigar Patel、Vansh Patel、Sheel Patel

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CE、cs.ET、cs.NI、eess.SY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41784v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41784v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出面向联网临床技术的临床协议感知网络韧性框架，用有界临床微网络与受控互联设计预防、隔离和响应措施，并以参数化蒙特卡洛仿真比较技术暴露与建模临床后果。

**排除原因：** 主题为医疗网络安全架构与风险建模仿真，不涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部表示、证据获取、状态更新与停止决策，也未提供可迁移到 LVR 的方法线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Clinical-Protocol-Aware Cyber Resilience for Connected Clinical Technologies Under AI-Enabled Adversarial Conditions

**abstract/A1：** Healthcare increasingly relies on connected medical devices, clinical information systems, cloud services, and distributed-care technologies, creating cybersecurity risks across clinical workflows. AI-enabled capabilities can accelerate vulnerability discovery and attack-path analysis, increasing the need to limit compromise propagation. This paper proposes a clinical-protocol-aware cyber-resilience framework based on bounded clinical micro-networks and controlled interconnections. The framework integrates clinical factors into cybersecurity design, management, and evaluation, covering prevention, containment, and incident response. A parameterized Monte Carlo simulation distinguishes technical cyber exposure from modeled clinical consequence. Experiments investigate architectural connectivity, adversarial capability, security controls, protocol dependency, risk alignment, communication directionality, lifecycle integration, and response timing. Under the simulation assumptions, connected architecture increases mean cyber exposure by 56.3% relative to segmented architecture, while combined prevention and containment reduce exposure by 60.1% relative to no controls. Increasing protocol dependency raises the modeled clinical-impact index from 1.021 to 7.567 without changing mean cyber exposure, demonstrating that technical exposure and clinical consequence can diverge. Risk-alignment experiments further indicate higher modeled clinical impact when greater technical risk corresponds to greater clinical significance. The findings support comparative architectural analysis, not calibrated estimates of real-world cyber risk, control effectiveness, or patient harm. The framework provides a clinical-context-aware approach to security-boundary design, control prioritization, and coordinated containment in connected healthcare environments.

**comment/C1：** This work has been submitted to the IEEE for possible publication. Copyright may be transferred without notice, after which this version may no longer be accessible

</details>

### 251. [On-device Detection of Wearer-induced NLOS on Chest-worn UWB Tags: Machine Learning, Link Indicators, or Both?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41799v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41799

**作者：** Markus Gallacher、Vu Anh Minh Le、Matteo Trobinger、Davide Vecchia、Carlo Alberto Boano、Gian Pietro Picco

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41799v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41799v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究胸挂 UWB 标签上佩戴者引起的非视距（WNLOS）检测，在 150 万样本上比较 21 种机器学习与链路指标分类器，并提出混合方法与约束下的分类器推荐工具。

**排除原因：** 属于无线定位中的 NLOS 分类与嵌入式能耗权衡，不涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部表示、latent 状态生成或证据筛选，无可核查的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** On-device Detection of Wearer-induced NLOS on Chest-worn UWB Tags: Machine Learning, Link Indicators, or Both?

**abstract/A1：** Ultra-wideband (UWB) enables high-accuracy distance estimation (ranging), but performance degrades in non-line-of-sight (NLOS) conditions. When UWB tags are worn, the human body inevitably causes a wearer-induced NLOS (WNLOS), yielding significant errors; still, despite its relevance, WNLOS remains understudied. Moreover, WNLOS detection typically must occur on-device at the tag, requiring classification accuracy to be balanced against tight runtime and energy constraints often hard to meet with machine learning (ML) -- the dominating NLOS classification approach. Meanwhile, UWB radio link indicators (LI) offer a faster and lightweight alternative that has rarely been explored. We study existing on-device ML and LI classifiers when applied to WNLOS, and propose novel hybrid ones exploiting their complementarity. We focus on chest-worn tags and analyze 21 classifiers on a dataset of 1.5 million samples across channels, PHY settings, environments, subjects, and orientations. Results show that while all of them achieve good classification accuracy, relative performance, including ranging errors due to misclassification, depends heavily on channel and PHY configuration. Runtime and energy efficiency also vary dramatically; some techniques offer marginal accuracy gains while consuming up to 39.6x more energy. Therefore, we also introduce a recommender tool to identify optimal classifiers under user-defined constraints, publicly released with our dataset.

**comment/C1：** Submitted to ACM Transactions on Sensor Networks

</details>

**内容简述：** 该硕士论文研究迁移学习中源任务泛化与目标任务可迁移性之间的关系，通过三类对抗攻击构造反例，考察源任务表现良好是否必然带来迁移成功。

**排除原因：** 论文聚焦迁移学习的坏极小值与对抗性构造，未涉及视觉或多模态推理中的 latent 状态、证据使用或停止决策，也缺乏可直接核查的表示或训练线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Exploring Bad Minima in Transfer Learning

**abstract/A1：** Transfer learning is a highly successful learning paradigm in deep learning which leverages knowledge from one task and applies it to another. Despite its success, the necessary conditions for knowledge transfer are unclear and require further research. In this dissertation, our aim is to understand transferability a little better by answering the following question: Does good generalization on a source task necessarily imply transferability to a related target task? We hypothesize the answer is negative, and attempt to find counterexamples via adversarial attacks on the transfer learning procedure. Over three types of adversarial attacks, our empirical results show a strong relationship between generalization on the source task and transferability. These results do not rule out generalization on the source task as a sufficient condition for successful transfer.

**comment/C1：** 57 pages, 13 figures, 3 tables. MPhil thesis, University of Cambridge, 2022. Supervised by Ferenc Huszár

</details>

### 253. [Learning to Interact and Direct: Co-Evolving Video Director Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41865v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41865

**作者：** Yingzhao Jian、Zhongan Wang、Yuxuan Hou、Hehe Fan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41865v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41865v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 LIT 框架，由视频导演 agent、模拟用户与元工程师组成协同进化闭环，通过对任务轨迹的反思与外部知识获取改进导演与交互策略。

**排除原因：** 该工作聚焦多阶段视频生产流程与用户交互策略的自演化，属多智能体工作流与任务应用，未涉及 VLM 内部视觉状态表示、latent 推理生成或证据筛选与停止决策，缺少可核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning to Interact and Direct: Co-Evolving Video Director Agents

**abstract/A1：** Video director agents orchestrate multistage production to realize users' creative visions. Emerging research on their self-evolution advances screenplay planning effectively. However, the mutual reinforcement between directing and user interaction remains largely overlooked. Improved interaction clarifies user intent and informs workflow refinement, while more reliable execution shifts collaboration from troubleshooting to creative decisions. To this end, we introduce LIT (Learning to Interact and DirecT), a framework comprises a director agent, a simulated user, and a meta engineer. Simulated user assigns creative tasks to probe the director's weaknesses, while the meta engineer uses the task traces to revise the director's interaction and production strategy. Although this approach can address discovered interaction and production issues, it still relies on passive attribution by the meta agent. This limits its ability to adapt to broader user interaction patterns and production techniques. To solve this problem, we equip LIT with a co-evolution paradigm. The meta agent proactively learns external knowledge, thereby improving its ability to discover problems and propose solutions. The meta agent, simulated user, and director together form an evolutionary closed loop, collaboratively enhances the system's directing and interaction capabilities. Across the Test and OOD Test splits, LIT improves production and Interaction scores by 19.1% and 32.0%, respectively, relative to the initial pipeline. Controlled ablations reveal the correlation between improvements in production and interaction, where the co-evolution mechanism further encourage directing skill and interaction mode exploration.

</details>

### 254. [On k-nearest-neighbor regression in metric spaces](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41896v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41896

**作者：** László Györfi、Vladimir G. Pestov、Harro Walk、Roi Weiss

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41896v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41896v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究度量空间取值特征的 k 近邻回归的均方一致性，通过刻画邻居选择密度对概率质量的再分配，分别给出近似误差与响应条件方差的充分条件，并构造反例说明 sigma-finite Nagata 维数不足以保证无界响应下的均方一致性。

**排除原因：** 这是纯渐近统计学习理论，讨论估计量的相合性与维数条件，不涉及 latent 状态生成、证据使用、训练监督或停止决策，其邻居选择密度的结论过于抽象，难以对 LVR 的相似度与证据筛选问题提供具体线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** On k-nearest-neighbor regression in metric spaces

**abstract/A1：** We study mean squared consistency of k-nearest-neighbor regression for metric space valued features. For a fixed feature distribution mu with atomless distance distributions, we describe how neighbor selection redistributes probability through the density of a uniformly chosen neighbor of an independent query. This gives separate conditions for the approximation error and the conditional response variance, and weakens Stone's domination condition when stronger response moments are available. In particular, when k\_n tends to infinity and k\_n/n tends to zero, a uniform L2(mu) bound on the selection densities suffices under a finite fourth response moment. We give a corresponding condition in terms of neighborhoods of fixed population mass and recover the Euclidean density bound. Finite global Nagata dimension gives Stone's domination. While sigma-finite Nagata dimensionality is known to ensure universal consistency of k-NN classification in complete separable spaces, we show that this is not the case for mean squared consistency with unbounded responses: we construct a space in this class on which one fixed noiseless response with all finite moments has diverging mean squared error.

</details>

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

**排除原因：** 该文面向纯文本优化建模的技能库检索与仲裁（原型残差重排、结构签名加权 Jaccard 召回），不涉及视觉证据、latent 推理状态或其生成与训练，路线替换规则也只是技能选择器之间的工程仲裁，暂无可核查的借用线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Structure-Aware Skill Retrieval and Arbitration for Optimization Modeling

**abstract/A1：** Large Language Models (LLMs) can turn natural-language optimization tasks into solver-checked answers, but current agents remain brittle under narrative rewrites and often store reusable experience at the instance level. We introduce \\textsc{ArchOR}, an archetype-centric skill agent that builds on \\textsc{OptSkills} by adding two retrieval-focused modules. \\emph{Centroid-Aware Representation Learning} (CARL) refines the ordering of cached skill representations through a low-rank prototype residual. \\emph{Signature-Guided Retrieval} (S\\textsuperscript{3}R) attaches open-dimensional structural signatures to skills, prioritizes candidates by weighted Jaccard overlap, and bounds semantic re-ranking to a top-$K$ set. A baseline-preserving arbitration rule determines when the structural route should replace the original selector. With the same DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash backbone and released skill library, \\textsc{ArchOR} reaches 65.00\\% Micro-Avg.\\ Pass@1 across five benchmarks, improving over \\textsc{OptSkills} by 1.64~pp Micro-Avg.\\ and 2.68~pp Macro-Avg.

</details>

We present Sensorium, an open-source Python library for recording, processing, annotating, and curating multimodal surgical data from configurable robot and sensor streams using the Robot Operating System (ROS) and ROS2 middleware. The library combines operator-facing data acquisition, configurable processing, semantic annotation, and episodic dataset generation. ### 257. [Chronos-2: A foundation model for multivariate time series forecasting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41956v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41956

**作者：** Abdul Fatir Ansari、Oleksandr Shchur、Jaris Küken、Andreas Auer、Boran Han、Pedro Mercado、Syama Sundar Rangapuram、Huibin Shen、Lorenzo Stella、Xiyuan Zhang、Mononito Goswami、Shubham Kapoor、Danielle C. Maddix、Pablo Guerron、Florian Saupe、Markus Reichstein、Tony Hu、Junming Yin、Nick Erickson、Prateek Mutalik Desai、Hao Wang、Huzefa Rangwala、George Karypis、Yuyang Wang、Michael Bohlke-Schneider

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41956v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41956v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Chronos-2，一个面向多变量时间序列与异质协变量的零样本概率预测基础模型，从历史上下文推断序列间关系，并在多领域基准与科学案例中验证精度与效率。

**排除原因：** 该工作属时间序列预测基础模型，不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、证据筛选、动态计算与停止决策；其协变量融合虽与异质输入整合有形式相似，但缺乏可供核查的具体机制联系，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Chronos-2: A foundation model for multivariate time series forecasting

**abstract/A1：** Accurate forecasting with uncertainty quantification is critical in a variety of scientific applications ranging from epidemiology and economics to energy systems and earth sciences. Yet most forecasting models still need to be developed and trained separately for each domain-specific dataset. Time series foundation models (TSFMs) aim to remove this limitation and enable the application of the same model across domains through large-scale pretraining. However, most existing TSFMs treat each time series independently and therefore cannot model interactions among time series and external drivers. Here, we introduce Chronos-2, a foundation model for zero-shot forecasting that models the interactions between multivariate time series and heterogeneous covariates, which commonly arise in scientific applications. The model infers relationships among time series directly from historical context and generates probabilistic forecasts without task-specific treatment. Across various benchmarks, Chronos-2 achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on average by improving skill scores by 5.2 percentage points relative to the strongest baselines and increasing win rates by 10 percentage points. Chronos-2 is also computationally efficient, generating forecasts for 100 time series in under one second on a single mid-range GPU, 50x and 1000x faster end-to-end than the best task-specific point and probabilistic forecasters, respectively. In addition, we demonstrate its effectiveness in diverse scientific settings -- including infectious-disease forecasting, macroeconomic modeling, carbon-flux prediction, and runoff simulation -- where the model matches or surpasses specialized methods developed for each domain. These results show that a single pretrained model can capture structure shared across temporal systems.

**comment/C1：** 41 pages, 9 figures. Extended version of the Chronos-2 technical report (arXiv:2510.15821), adding scientific case studies (epidemiology, macroeconomics, carbon flux, hydrology, hierarchical forecasting), additional baselines, ablation studies, and calibration analysis. Code: https://github.com/amazon-science/chronos-forecasting

</details>
