### 258. [Prune Gradually, Adapt Sparsely: Efficient Pruning of Large Language Models at Extreme Sparsity](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41968v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41968

**作者：** Jean Lucien Randrianantenaina、Hung Nguyen、Nasib Ullah、Jinbin Zhang、Nilesh Gupta、Inderjit S. Dhillon、Rohit Babbar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41968v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41968v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ETAPS，用分阶段收紧全局稀疏约束并结合小固定预算的稀疏增量训练，在极端稀疏度下以更低成本完成 LLM 权重剪枝。

**排除原因：** 该工作关注权重稀疏化带来的存储与推理成本，不涉及视觉或多模态推理中的状态表示、证据使用或停止决策，缺乏可核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Prune Gradually, Adapt Sparsely: Efficient Pruning of Large Language Models at Extreme Sparsity

**abstract/A1：** Weight pruning can reduce the storage and inference cost of large language models (LLMs), but preserving quality at high sparsity levels remains challenging. Existing methods face a quality-cost trade-off in this regime. One-shot pruning is cheap but degrades sharply as sparsity increases, even when followed by post-pruning recovery. A growing line of work instead optimizes the mask directly from the dense model under the end-to-end objective; among these methods, \[Lee et al.,2026\], the recent state of the art at extreme sparsity, reaches high quality but at the cost of model-sized optimizer state and auxiliary variables. To address this gap, we propose ETAPS, which prunes gradually and adapts sparsely: it tightens a global sparsity constraint in progressive stages while training a sparse delta with a small, fixed budget. Our key observation is that gradual weight removal keeps each pruning step small enough for sparse adaptation to compensate, allowing the adapted model to provide end-to-end gradients that guide the next pruning decision without model-sized optimizer state. Across six models from 125M to 13B parameters, ETAPS achieves lower perplexity than ELSA at every evaluated sparsity from 50% to 90%, with perplexity reductions of up to 42%. ETAPS is also far cheaper: at 90% sparsity, it prunes LLaMA2-7B on a single H200, where ELSA uses four, with $5.0\\times$ smaller total persistent state and 49% fewer GPU-hours. Together, our findings point to a promising direction for achieving high quality in demanding regimes such as extreme sparsity at substantially lower cost.

</details>

<a id="paper-259"></a>

### 259. [ID2Pattern: Pretraining Generalizable Identity Behavior Patterns for Transaction Risk Foundation Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41988v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41988

**作者：** Ze Zhao、Haoyu Jiang、Weiyu Han、Ruobing Zuo、Xiao Tan、Shuai Chen、Tianyi Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41988v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41988v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 ID2Pattern，用 LAID tokenizer 编码交易序列中卡、设备、账号等身份字段的出现与复现方式，再预训练多尺度离散模式码本用于交易风险预测。

**排除原因：** 研究面向支付风控中的高基数 ID 表示，与视觉细节保留、latent 推理状态生成、证据筛选或停止决策无实质联系；离散码本抽象属于通用做法，未提供可核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ID2Pattern: Pretraining Generalizable Identity Behavior Patterns for Transaction Risk Foundation Models

**abstract/A1：** Large-scale behavioral pretraining offers a promising route to generalizable representations in online payment systems. However, high-cardinality identity fields such as card, device, and account IDs, though rich in behavioral signals, remain underexploited. Their continually growing vocabularies and limited semantic meaning make existing vocabulary-based and semantics-based encodings ill-suited. Their generalizable signal lies not in specific values, but in the identity behavior patterns they exhibit across a transaction sequence. Building on this insight, we propose ID2Pattern, a pretraining framework that brings these patterns into transaction foundation models. ID2Pattern first encodes identity fields with a Local Anonymous ID (LAID) tokenizer, which captures how each identity appears and recurs within the sequence, independent of its specific value. It then pretrains a multi-scale pattern codebook on unlabeled sequences, which abstracts LAID sequences into discrete behavior patterns for downstream prediction. Experiments on an industrial dataset with over 100M sequences and six public datasets show that ID2Pattern consistently outperforms strong baselines, improving industrial by 4.13% and public datasets by 8.83% on average. Notably, ID2Pattern remains equally effective even when all identity values are unseen during training, highlighting its potential to generalize to new users and markets.

**comment/C1：** 13 pages, 6 figures

</details>

### 260. [GhostPrefix: Synchronization-Free Universal Prefix Attacks for ASR Manipulation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42026v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.42026

**作者：** Shengding Liu、Ashwin Innuganti、Qiben Yan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42026v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.42026v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究对 ASR 的免同步通用音频前缀对抗攻击，目标是无视上下文地操控转写结果，不涉及视觉或多模态推理中的内部表示、证据获取或停止决策，也未提供可直接核查的 LVR 方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GhostPrefix: Synchronization-Free Universal Prefix Attacks for ASR Manipulation

**abstract/A1：** We present GhostPrefix, the first synchronization-free universal adversarial prefix attack against automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems. Unlike prior audio attacks, which are typically crafted per utterance, require synchronization with the victim waveform, or target narrow objectives, GhostPrefix learns a short, reusable audio segment prepended to arbitrary live speech to exert real-time control over ASR outputs without knowledge of the future utterance or temporal alignment. A key challenge is maintaining stable adversarial effects across unknown, evolving speech contexts. GhostPrefix combines token-level losses with a novel representation-level objective for stable, input-agnostic effects. GhostPrefix supports three attack modes: (i) prompted prefix attacks for semantic steering, (ii) targeted prefix attacks for transcript hijacking, and (iii) untargeted prefix attacks for transcription degradation. To remain perceptually inconspicuous, we regularize prefixes toward benign environmental-sound distributions and improve robustness against over-the-air distortions. We evaluate GhostPrefix across 13 open-source ASR models from four families. In digital settings, it achieves average attack success rates of 97.6% for prompted steering and 93.4% for targeted hijacking, while driving word error rate to 1.18 under untargeted attacks. Over the air, it achieves success rates of 91.5% and 89.5% for prompted and targeted modes, respectively. These manipulations propagate into downstream AI-assisted pipelines, including voice assistants and AI note-taking tools, causing systematic semantic misinterpretations. GhostPrefix exposes synchronization-free universal prefixes as a practical, under-explored threat to real-world ASR and downstream applications. Artifacts and demos are available at https://ghostprefix.github.io/.

</details>

### 261. [Learning Clean Generative Processes from Noisy Measurements](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42036v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.42036

**作者：** Fabian Kruse、Lorenzo Baldassari、Ivan Dokmanić

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42036v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.42036v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究如何在仅有噪声观测的情况下学习干净图像的生成模型：先在训练噪声水平之上用 Noise2Noise 回归学后验预测，再以「采样延续后预测应等于其期望」的一致性约束补足更低噪声水平的预测，并在 CIFAR-10、光子计数显微与 SAR 数据上验证。

**排除原因：** 属于噪声测量下的扩散式图像生成，关注的是去噪后验预测与采样延续，不涉及视觉推理状态、证据获取或停止决策；其中的一致性关系与 latent 轨迹监督的类比过于宽泛，缺乏可核查的具体方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning Clean Generative Processes from Noisy Measurements

**abstract/A1：** We study how to learn a generative model of clean images using only noisy observations. Diffusion processes and their generalizations to non-Gaussian noise require posterior predictions at every noise level. We first show that it is possible to learn these predictions from independent noisy views using Noise2Noise regression, but only at or above the noise level of the training data. To learn the predictions below that level, needed to generate clean images, we impose consistency: a prediction should equal its expectation after continuing the sampling process. We derive conditions under which consistency uniquely determines the continuation to the clean image distribution and verify them for Poisson, binomial, Gamma, and negative-binomial measurements. Experiments on CIFAR-10, photon-counting microscopy, and synthetic aperture radar show that the required posterior predictions can be learned from noisy views alone and that the consistency relation repairs the continuation beyond the noise level of the data, which severely deteriorates without consistency.

</details>

### 262. [Same Question, Different Answers: Auditing Persona-Dependent Variation in Six LLMs on Smoking-Cessation Advice](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42039v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.42039

**作者：** Artin Yousefi、Aizierjiang Aiersilan、Lorien C Abroms、David A Broniatowski

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42039v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.42039v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 论文审计六款 LLM 在戒烟建议上因人设（年龄、专业表述、吸烟史）产生的回答差异，并测试「事实下限」系统提示的干预效果，属于健康咨询公平性与提示工程研究；未涉及视觉或多模态信息、内部状态表示、证据获取或推理计算分配，缺乏可供 LVR 核查的状态或决策线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Same Question, Different Answers: Auditing Persona-Dependent Variation in Six LLMs on Smoking-Cessation Advice

**abstract/A1：** Hundreds of millions of people now ask large language model (LLM) chatbots for health advice, which raises a question that is simple to pose and hard to answer: does everyone get the same advice? We audit six widely deployed LLMs from four providers on smoking cessation, a domain with codified clini- cal guidance and substantial public-health stakes, by putting the same four cessation questions to twelve user personas that differ in life stage, self-described expertise, and smoking history, for a total of 2,880 responses. What the models say depends on who appears to be asking. Every model named FDA-approved prescription cessation medications less often to teen personas than to adult personas, by 32 to 67 percentage points, and in five of six models a persona presenting health- care expertise received fewer facts than a persona presenting none. We then evaluate one deployable intervention: a short system prompt specifying a factual floor, the set of core med- ical facts every user should receive when they are relevant, while leaving tone, vocabulary, and framing free to adapt. The prompt narrows persona differences in every model, but its effect on overall fact coverage varies in both magnitude and direction across models, so a prompt validated on one system cannot be assumed to transfer to another. Two cautions frame these results. Equal mention rates are not always the correct clinical target, because prescription cessation pharmacother- apy is not recommended for minors, so a defensible factual floor must be age-aware rather than uniform. And because our personas vary on several attributes at once, we report scenario- level differences rather than isolated effects of age or expertise. We release all 2,880 responses and the analysis code.

</details>

### 263. [A richness-sensitive Acoustic Biodiversity Index from species-level detections: classifier benchmarking, false-positive control and validation against standard soundscape indices in an African savanna and an urban zoo](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42042v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.42042

**作者：** Muhammad Saeed、Dmitry Mikhaylov、Mohamed H. Alhosani、Yasser F. Al Wahedi、Rory Pilossof、Ahmed Arabi、Walid Shaaba

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.SD、q-bio.QM

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42042v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.42042v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究基于物种级检测的声学生物多样性指数构造、分类器基准与假阳性控制，属生态声学监测，未涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部状态、证据使用或停止决策，无可核查的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A richness-sensitive Acoustic Biodiversity Index from species-level detections: classifier benchmarking, false-positive control and validation against standard soundscape indices in an African savanna and an urban zoo

**abstract/A1：** Acoustic indices are widely used as rapid proxies for biodiversity, but they summarise the distribution of sound energy rather than the species producing it and often track richness poorly. We revise and test an Acoustic Biodiversity Index (ABI) built from species-level detections. Its primary form, ABI\_E, is Shannon diversity of detections normalised by the regional species pool expected at the site and season (H / ln S\_ref); temporal stability T is reported as a separate component. We first benchmarked four classifiers on a strongly labelled tropical dataset (1,216 calls, 24 classes) with recording-grouped cross-validation. BirdNET embeddings + logistic regression performed best (macro-F1 0.750 +/- 0.029), and random frame-level splits inflated accuracy from 0.41 to 0.70. We then applied BirdNET v2.4 with a location and season filter to recordings from a riverine savanna in the Zambezi Valley, Zimbabwe, and from three habitats at Emirates Park Zoo and Resort, Abu Dhabi, United Arab Emirates. A stricter post-hoc location filter removed 517 high-confidence detections of 9 implausible species, all at the zoo. After diel-matched rarefaction to equal effort (6 h), the savanna site held 21 species (Hill number 1D = 9.83; ABI\_E = 0.38) against 1.3-13 species (ABI\_E = 0.03-0.21) at the zoo sites. Across 149 recorded hours, ABI\_E correlated strongly with richness (Spearman rho = 0.93), whereas six standard indices did not (best: ACI, rho = 0.48). The stability term was lower at the savanna site (0.39) than at the zoo (0.65-0.67), showing that it penalises the natural diel cycle of bird activity. Species-level indices with explicit false-positive control, a declared regional reference pool and effort standardisation provide a more interpretable biodiversity indicator than energy-based acoustic indices.

**comment/C1：** 35 pages, 16 figures, 7 tables; supplementary tables and figures included

</details>

### 264. [Differentially Private Synthetic Data from Sliced Chebyshev Moments](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42081v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.42081

**作者：** Lucas Rosenblatt、R. Teal Witter、Christopher Musco

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CY、cs.DS

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42081v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.42081v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出差分隐私合成数据算法 SPLICE：在随机方向上测量带噪的切片 Chebyshev 多项式矩并拟合匹配分布，给出与数据维度 d 无关的 Sliced Wasserstein-1 误差界，并在基准上取得领先性能。

**排除原因：** 研究目标是隐私保障下的分布级合成数据生成，不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态，切片矩匹配与 LVR 的表示、证据筛选或停止决策之间也没有具体可核查的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Differentially Private Synthetic Data from Sliced Chebyshev Moments

**abstract/A1：** Research on practical differentially private (DP) synthetic data has largely focused on methods that approximately preserve low-dimensional marginals of a target distribution, and can thus only hope to preserve higher-dimensional, global structure as a lucky by-product. At the same time, existing methods that target global accuracy guarantees pay a price exponential in the data dimension, $d$. In this work, we address this \`\`curse of dimensionality'' by presenting a new DP synthetic data algorithm, SPLICE, that comes with a compelling global distributional guarantee, even in high-dimensions, and moreover, is practically state-of-the-art. Our algorithm is based on measuring noisy sliced Chebyshev polynomial moments of the target distribution and fitting a distribution to match, a twist on recent polynomial methods for 1-D synthetic data. We prove that, given a dataset of $n$ examples, SPLICE returns a private distribution with Sliced Wasserstein-1 distance $\\tilde{O}(1/\\sqrt{n})$ from the target, for constant privacy parameters. Notably, this bound is independent of the data dimension, $d$. Along the way, we prove a moment matching result of independent interest: for a distribution on the unit ball, matching the first $O(1/α)$ Chebyshev moments along $\\tilde{O}(d/α^2)$ random directions guarantees $α$ Sliced Wasserstein error. Finally, we provide a practical implementation of the algorithm that borrows tools from the tensor decomposition literature and achieves state-of-the-art performance compared to prior DP synthetic data methods on an extensive benchmark.

</details>

### 265. [Fast Privacy Loss Accounting for Random Allocation via Multiscale FFT](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42117v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.42117

**作者：** Vitaly Feldman、Moshe Shenfeld

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42117v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.42117v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究差分隐私下 random allocation 的隐私损失核算，用 multiscale FFT 加速 PLD 计算并改进舍入误差，与 VLM/latent 推理的表示、生成、监督或停止决策均无实质联系，也不存在可核查的方法借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Fast Privacy Loss Accounting for Random Allocation via Multiscale FFT

**abstract/A1：** In the random allocation sampling scheme each user's data is used in $k$ steps chosen randomly and uniformly from a sequence of $t$ steps. This scheme is known to improve on Poisson sampling in private optimization and in communication-efficient aggregation. The existing privacy loss distribution (PLD) accountant for this scheme runs in time quadratic in the inverse of the accuracy parameter $α$ (Feldman and Shenfeld, 2026). We give an algorithm, \\emph{multiscale FFT}, that represents likelihood ratios on a grid that is uniform within each dyadic interval and computes the exact rounding of every pairwise sum using FFTs within each interval. It keeps the guarantees of the accountant and improves the dependence of its running time on $α$ from $O(α^{-2})$ to $O(α^{-1}\\log(1/α))$. In addition, we demonstrate that rounding can be performed via the mean-preserving connect-the-dots projection of (Doroshenko et al., 2026). This reduces the errors that rounding introduces in the mean of the privacy loss and in the $χ^2$ divergence of the computed pair from the order of $α$ to $O(α^2/\\log(t))$. In our experiments, our algorithm achieves speedups of several orders of magnitude at high accuracy and gives improved privacy bounds for DP-SGD with PREAMBLE.

</details>

### 266. [Physiological Trajectories and Observation Patterns in ICU Mortality Prediction: Transportability Under Changing Measurement Conditions](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42126v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.42126

**作者：** Benjamin T. Pike

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、q-bio.QM

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42126v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.42126v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究 ICU 死亡预测中生理轨迹与测量模式的跨数据集迁移性，属临床时间序列预测，与 LVR 的视觉状态表示、生成、选择性证据获取等机制无实质联系，也无可具体核查的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Physiological Trajectories and Observation Patterns in ICU Mortality Prediction: Transportability Under Changing Measurement Conditions

**abstract/A1：** Objective: To distinguish the transferable predictive value of physiological trajectories from that of observation patterns in ICU mortality prediction, and to assess whether measurement timing affects performance independently of observation quantity. Materials and Methods: In-hospital mortality was predicted at 48 hours using MIMIC-III, MIMIC-IV, and eICU. Nested representations progressively integrated latest-value snapshots (R1), longitudinal physiological summaries (R2), and measurement frequency and timing features (R3). Models fitted on 7,762 MetaVision patients were evaluated on 21,682 MIMIC-IV patients and 43,878 eICU patients across 204 hospitals. The effect of measurement timing was assessed while holding the number of retained observations constant. Results: Physiological trajectories improved external prediction in eICU (R2-R1 log-loss difference -0.00922, 95% CI -0.01148 to -0.00704). Observation-pattern features improved locally developed eICU models (R3-R2 -0.00257, 95% CI -0.00370 to -0.00146), but not clearly after transfer from MetaVision (-0.00012, 95% CI -0.00206 to +0.00208). R3 was favoured in 10 of 30 source bootstrap refits. At matched observation counts, retaining measurements spread throughout the window reduced log loss relative to random retention by 0.03280 for R3 versus 0.00703 for R2. Removing the final 12 hours increased log loss relative to count-matched random removal by 0.01947 for R3 versus 0.00822 for R2. Under reduced availability, the local R3 increment changed from beneficial to harmful. Discussion: Physiological trajectories transferred more consistently, while observation-pattern features were particularly sensitive to the timing and availability of measurements. Conclusion: Clinical prediction models should be evaluated not only in new patient populations but also under the measurement conditions that supply their inputs.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 3 figures, 3 tables. Supplementary material available as an ancillary file

</details>

### 267. [Counting on Dummies: Privacy Amplification with Observable Counts and Truncated Batches](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42139v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.42139

**作者：** Vitaly Feldman、Audra McMillan、Kunal Talwar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42139v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.42139v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究差分隐私 SGD 中批次大小被观测及批次截断导致的隐私损失问题，提出用随机数量的「dummy points」隐藏真实批次大小，并为 Poisson 采样与 random allocation 给出通用隐私放大分析与紧致记账，实验表明在截断场景下隐私—效率权衡优于直接记账。

**排除原因：** 主题为差分隐私记账与采样机制，与 LVR/VLM 推理中的表示、证据筛选、状态更新或停止决策均无具体联系，也未提供可迁移的监督或计算控制线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Counting on Dummies: Privacy Amplification with Observable Counts and Truncated Batches

**abstract/A1：** In many practical deployments of Differentially Private Stochastic Gradient Descent (DP-SGD), the server processes data in batches. Randomly sampling these batches allows for better privacy bounds via privacy amplification results. Most sampling schemes with provable privacy amplification, including Poisson sampling and random allocation, result in variable batch sizes. The privacy guarantee of these schemes requires that the batch sizes are not revealed. In distributed settings, the server performing the aggregation or any party observing the communication traffic may learn these batch sizes. Furthermore, in both centralized and distributed settings, memory and computation constraints may require each batch to be of some maximum size $s$, necessitating truncation of the batch when it exceeds this limit. It is known that this truncation can result in a significant increase in privacy loss. In this work, we address both of these challenges via a simple mechanism that uses a random number of \\emph{dummy points} to privatize the batch size and maintain the privacy guarantee even under truncation. We demonstrate that our mechanism is easy to implement in a distributed setting, and provide a general analysis of this mechanism for both Poisson sampling and random allocation. Our analysis is designed to support efficient and relatively tight privacy accounting. It also effectively amplifies the privacy of the count releases by subsampling, making the expected number of dummy points asymptotically smaller than what is needed to directly privatize the (sensitivity-one) batch sizes with non-negative noise. For Poisson sampling with truncated batches, we demonstrate empirically that our mechanism achieves better privacy--efficiency tradeoffs than the direct accounting for truncation recently given by (Ganesh, 2025).

</details>

</details>
